(co-ḷ) 1-4. vāṉ kaṭal parappil tūvaṟku etiriya - periya kaṭaṟparappil eḻum tirit tivalikaḷi ēṟṟuk koṇṭa, mīṉ kaṇṭaṉṉa - viṇmīṉik kaṇṭālotta, mel arumpu ūḻtta - melliya arumpukaḷ malarnta, muṭavumutir puṉṉi - muṭam paṭṭa mutirnta puṉṉi marattiṉ, taṭavunili māciṉi - periya niliyi yuṭiya kariya ciṉiyil, puḷ iṟikūrum mel am pulampa - puṭkaḷ mikat taṅkiyirukkum meṉṉilamākiya kaṭaṟ karikkut talivaṉē!
7. peruma - perumāṉē!
5-7. neytal uṇkaṇ pitala kaluḻa - neytaṟ pūvi otta miyuṇṭa ivaḷ kaṇkaḷ varuntiṉavāy aḻa, pirital eṇṇiṉi yāyiṉ - ivaḷip pirintu cellutali niṉittāyāyiṉ, naṉṟum aritu tuṟṟaṉi - peritum ariyataṉi mēṟkoḷvāyāyiṉi,
7-13. koṇṭaloṭu kurūu tirip puṇari uṭitarum ekkar - kīḻkāṟṟāl viḷakkam poruntiya kaṭaliṉ alikaḷ uṭikkum maṇal mēṭṭiṟ (kiṭakkum), paḻantimil koṉṟa putuvalip paratavar - paḻiya paṭakiṉ citivu pōkkip putukkiya  putiya valiyiṉiyuṭiya paratavarkaḷ, mōṭṭu maṇal aṭikari kōṭṭumīṉ koṇṭi - uyarnta maṇaliyuṭiya aṭikariyil vantu kiṭakkum cuṟā mīṉiṉ koḷḷiyiṉi, maṇam kamaḻ pākkattup pakukkum - maṇam nāṟukiṉṟa pākkattiṉkaṇ palarkkum pakuttuk koṭukkum, vaḷam keḻu toṇṭi aṉṉa ivaḷ nalaṉ - vaḷam mikka toṇṭi eṉṉum paṭṭiṉatti yotta ivaḷatu aḻaku, uritiṉil koṇṭu āṅkup peyartal vēṇṭum - ivaṭkē urittāka niṉṉūrkku varintu koṇṭu pōtal vēṇṭum.
(muṭipu) pulampa! peruma! pirital eṇṇiṉiyāyiṉ aritu tuṟṟaṉi; toṇṭi aṉṉa ivaḷ nalaṉ, uritiṉil koṇṭu āṅkup peyartal vēṇṭum.
(vi-ri) etiriya ūḻtta puṉṉi eṉka. tūvaṟku - tūvali; vēṟṟumi mayakkam. mācciṉi : mā - kariya eṉalumām. pital - viṉiyeccamuṟṟu. koṇṭi - koḷḷappaṭṭavi. pākkam - paratavarcēri. toṇṭi eṉpatoru pati cōḻanāṭṭuk kaṭaṟkarikkaṇṇiruppiṉum, ivvāciriyar piṟa iṭaṅkaḷil cēraṉuṭiya toṇṭi, maranti eṉpaṉavaṟṟip pāṭiyiruttaliṉ iṅkuk kuṟitta toṇṭiyum cērar kaṭaṟkarip paṭṭiṉamākiya toṇṭiyē yātal vēṇṭum.  `tōḷum kūntalum pala pārāṭṭi, vāḻta lollumō maṟṟē ceṅkōṟ kuṭṭuvaṉ toṇṭi yaṉṉa, eṟkaṇṭu nayantunī nalkākkālē'1 eṉpatu kāṇka. nalam ivaṭku urittāka varintukoṇṭu pōkavēṇṭum eṉṟatu; aṉṟāyiṉ ivaḷ iṟantupaṭuvaḷ eṉpatu tōṉṟak kūṟiyapaṭi.
(u-ṟi) `paḻantimil ....... koṇṭi' eṉpataṟkup "paratavar tām aḻivu kōtta timilāṉum paṇṇiṉa valiyāṉum tamatu toḻilākiya vēṭṭimēṟ cellātu tēṭāmal vanta iḻinta cuṟāmīṉi akappaṭuttu, ataṉi aḻittuk kūṟuvittu ellāriyum aḻittāṟpōla, nīyirum numakku uṟutiyāka ākkik koḷḷappaṭṭa naṉmikaḷāṉ numakku oḻukkamākiya nalvaḻiyiṉ oḻukātu, kaṇṭō rikaḻnta kaḷavoḻukkilē oḻuki, ik kaḷaviṉip parappip palarum alar kūṟumpaṭi paṇṇāniṉṟīr" eṉpar, kuṟippurikārar.

11. pāli
(talimakaṉ poruḷvayiṟ pirintaviṭattu āṟṟāḷāya talimakaḷ vēṟupāṭu kaṇṭu āṟṟāḷāya tōḻikkut talimakaḷ āṟṟuval eṉpatu paṭac colliyatu.)
		vāṉam ūrnta vayaṅkoḷi maṇṭilam 		neruppeṉac civanta uruppavir amkāṭ
		ṭiliyila malarnta mukiyil ilavam
		kalikoḷ āyam maliputoku peṭutta
	5.	añcuṭar neṭuṅkoṭi poṟpat tōṉṟik
		kayantukaḷ ākiya payantapu kāṉam
		emmoṭu kaḻintaṉa rāyiṉ kammeṉa
		vampu virittaṉṉa poṅkumaṇaṟ kāṉyāṟṟup
		paṭuciṉi tāḻnta payiliṇar ekkar
	10.	meypuku vaṉṉa kikavar muyakkam
		avarum peṟukuvar maṉṉē nayavara
		nīrvār nikarmalar kaṭuppa ōmaṟan
		taṟukuḷa niṟikkuna pōla alkalum
		aḻutaṉ mēvala vākip
	15.	paḻitīr kaṇṇum paṭukuva maṉṉē.
-oḷaviyār.
(co-ḷ) 1-7. vāṉam ūrnta vayaṅku oḷi maṇṭilam - vāṉil ūrntēkum viḷaṅkum oḷiyiṉatākiya ñāyiṟṟu maṇṭilam, neruppeṉac civanta uruppu avir am kāṭṭu - tī yeṉac ciṉanteritta veppam viḷaṅkum kāṭṭakattē, iliila malarnta muki il ilavam - iliyilavāy malarntuḷa arumpillāta ilavam pūkkaḷ, kalikoḷ āyam malipu tokupu eṭutta - āravārattik koṇṭa makaḷir kūṭṭam makiḻntu kūṭi eṭutta, am cuṭar neṭuṅkoṭi poṟpat tōṉṟi - aḻakiya kārttiki viḷakkiṉ neṭiya oḻuṅku pōlat tōṉṟa, kayam tukaḷ ākiya payam tapu kāṉam - kuḷaṅkaḷ nīraṟṟut tukaḷpaṭṭirukkum vaḷam tappiya kāṭṭil, kammeṉa emmoṭu kaḻintaṉarāyiṉ - nam talivar virivāka nammi uṭaṉkoṇṭu kaḻintaṉarāyiṉ,
8-11. vampu viritta aṉṉa poṅku maṇal kāṉyāṟṟu - kacciṉi virittup parappi vittālaṉṉa viḷaṅkum maṇal mikka kāṭṭāṟṟiṉatu, payil iṇarpaṭu ciṉi tāḻnta ekkar - mikka pūṅkottukkaḷiyuṭiya periya kiḷikaḷ tāḻntuḷa maṇal mēṭṭil, mey pukuvu aṉṉa - meykaḷ oṉṟiṉ oṉṟu pukuvati otta, ki kavar muyakkam - kivirumpum muyakkattiṉi, nayavara avarum peṟukuvar maṉ - aṉpu tōṉṟa avarum aṭivar,
12-15. paḻi tīr kaṇṇum - ematu kuṟṟamaṟṟa kaṇkaḷum, nīr vār nikar malar kaṭuppa - nīr coriyum oḷi poruntiya malariyoppa, ō maṟantu - oḻitaliṉṟi, aṟu kuḷam niṟikkuna pōla - nīraṟṟa kuḷattiṉip perukkum maṭi pōla, alkalum aḻutal mēvala ākip paṭukuva maṉ - nāḷum aḻutalip poruntāvākit tuyilappeṟum.
(muṭipu) (nam talivar) kāṉam emmoṭu kaḻintaṉarāyiṉ, muyakkam avarum peṟukuvar; (nam) kaṇṇum paṭukuva.
(vi-ri) ilavam - ilavam pū. tōṉṟi - tōṉṟa eṉat tirikka. kammeṉa - viriya, tēya vaḻakku; `kammeṉa veḻutaru perumpaṭi'1 eṉpatūuṅ kāṇka. kārttikit tiṅkaḷiṉ kārttiki nāḷil illantōṟum variciyāka viḷakkukaḷi ēṟṟivittu viḻā ayartal, caṅkac ceyyuṭkaḷ palavaṟṟiṟ kūṟap peṟutaliṉ, tamiḻnāṭṭil paṇṭu toṭṭu iv vaḻakkam iruntu varuvateṉpatu peṟṟām; "amma vāḻi tōḻi kimmika" 2 eṉṉum pāṭṭiṉuḷ kārttiki viḻāk koṇṭāṭum muṟimiyum, "elvaḷi ñekiḻac cāay"3 eṉṉum pāṭṭiṉuḷ, "peruviḻā viḷakkam pōlap palavuṭaṉ, iliyila malarnta ilavamoṭu" eṉa ivvuvamiyum in nūluṭ piṉṉar varuvatuṅ kāṇka. emmoṭu kaḻintaṉarāyiṉ, kaṇṇum aḻutal mēvalavākip paṭukuva eṉa etirvu paṟṟik kūṟaliṉ, āṟṟuval eṉpatu paṭac colliya teṉṉum tuṟiyāyiṟṟu. maṉ kaḻiviṉkaṇ vantatu eṉak koḷḷiṉ, āṟṟātu iraṅkutal nikaḻntatām. nacciṉārkkiṉiyar itaṉip4 pālikkaṇ iraṅkal nikaḻntataṟku eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyavāṟum kāṇka.
(mē-ḷ) `uripporu ḷallaṉa mayaṅkavum peṟumē'5 eṉṉum cūttiravurikkaṇ, "vāṉa mūrnta....... amkāṭṭu" eṉak kāṭuṟi yulakattup pāli vantatu" eṉṟum, `koṇṭutalik kaḻiyiṉum'6 eṉṉum cūttiravurikkaṇ, `vāṉamūrnta' eṉṉum akappāṭṭi ṉuḷ `meypukuvaṉṉa..............maṉṉē' eṉak kūṟi aḻutalmēvā vāyk kaṇṇum tuyilumeṉa irakkam mīkkūṟiyavāṟum uṇarka' eṉṟum urittaṉar, nac.
12. kuṟiñci
(pakaṟkuṟi vārāniṉṟa talimakaṉ tōḻiyāṟ ceṟippaṟivuṟukkappaṭṭu, iravukkuṟi vārā varival eṉṟāṟku, atuvum maṟuttu varivu kaṭāyatu.)
		yāyē kaṇṇiṉum kaṭuṅkā talaḷē
		entiyum nilaṉuṟap poṟāaṉ cīṟaṭicivappa
		evaṉila kuṟumakaḷ iyaṅkuti eṉṉum
		yāmē, piriviṉ ṟiyinta tuvarā naṭpiṉ
	5.	irutalip puḷḷiṉ ōruyi rammē
		ēṉalaṅ kāvalar āṉā tārttoṟuṅ
		kiḷiviḷi payiṟṟum veḷilāṭu peruñciṉi
		viḻukkōṭ palaviṉ paḻuppayaṉ koṇmār
		kuṟavar ūṉṟiya kurampi putiya
	10.	vēṅki tāaya tēmpāy tōṟṟam
		pulicettu verīiya pukarmuka vēḻam
		maḻipaṭu cilampiṟ kaḻipaṭap peyarum
		nalvari nāṭa nīvariṉ
		melliyal ōrun tāṉvā ḻalaḷē.
- kapilar.
(co-ḷ) 1-5. yāyē kaṇṇiṉum kaṭuṅ kātalaḷ - em tāy taṉ kaṇṇiṉum ivaḷpāl mikka kātaluṭiyāḷ, entiyum nilaṉ uṟap poṟāaṉ - em tantiyum (ivaḷ eṅkēṉum celvatu kāṇiṉ) nilattē ivaḷ aṭi porunti naṭakkap poṟātavaṉāki, ilakuṟumakaḷ - ēṭi! iḷiya makaḷē!, cīṟaṭi civappa - niṉ ciṟiya aṭi civappuṟa, evaṉ iyaṅkuti eṉṉum - eṉ ceyac celkiṉṟāy eṉṟu kūṟum, yāmē - yāṅkaḷum, pirivu iṉṟu iyinta tuvarā naṭpiṉ - piritalillātu kūṭiya uvarttalillāta naṭpiṉāl, irutalip puḷḷiṉ ōr uyiram - irutalip paṟaviyip pōla iraṇṭu uṭaṟku ōr uyiriṉam āvēm,
6-14. ēṉal am kāvalar āṉātu ārttoṟum - tiṉip puṉam kākkum makaḷir ōyātu ārkkuntōṟum, kiḷi viḷi payiṟṟum veḷilāṭu peruñciṉi - kiḷikaḷ tam iṉatti aḻikkum aṇil āṭum periya kiḷikaḷil, viḻukkōḷ palaviṉ paḻuppayaṉ koṇmār - periya kāykaḷik koṇṭa palāmarattiṉ paḻamāya payaṉik koḷḷutaṟku, kuṟavar ūṉṟiya kurampi putiya - kuṟavarkaḷ nāṭṭiya kuṭici maṟiya , vēṅki tāya tēmpāy tōṟṟam - tēṉ oḻukum vēṅkip pūkkaḷ paranta tōṟṟatti, puli cettu verīiya pukarmuka vēḻam - puliyeṉṟu karuti añciya puḷḷikaḷ poruntiya mukattiṉiyuṭiya yāṉi, maḻi paṭu cilampil kaḻipaṭap peyarum - mēkam poruntiya pakkamaliyiluḷḷa mūṅkilkaḷ muṟipaṭap peyarntu cellum, nal vari nāṭa - nalla mali poruntiya nāṭaṉē, nī variṉ - nī iravukkuṟi variṉ, melliyal vāḻalaḷ - meṉmittaṉmi vāynta it talivi uyir vāḻntirāḷ.
(muṭipu) vari nāṭa! yāy kātalaḷ; enti evaṉ iyaṅkuti eṉṉum; yām ōr uyiram; nī variṉ melliyal vāḻalaḷ.
kiḷi viḷipayiṟṟum nalvari eṉavum, vēḻam peyarum nalvari eṉavum kūṭṭuka.
(vi-ri) tōḻiyāṉavaḷ, yāy kātalaḷ; enti evaṉ iyaṅkuti yeṉṉum eṉpavaṟṟāl iṟceṟippiṉiyum, yām ōruyiram eṉpataṉāl talivi yeytum varuttattiṉit tāṉ naṉku aṟiyumāṟṟiṉiyum, nī variṉ melliyal  vāḻalaḷ eṉpataṉāl iravuk kuṟiyiṉ ētam añcutaliyum aṟivuṟuttu varivu kaṭāyiṉaḷām eṉka. ila - ēṭi :  viḷippeyar. kiḷi viḷi payiṟṟum eṉṟatu, kiḷiyōppum makaḷir kuralik kiḷiyiṉ kuraleṉak karutip puṉattiluḷḷa kiḷikaḷ tam iṉatti aḻittalic ceyyum eṉṟapaṭiyām; "koṭicci yiṉkural kiḷicet taṭukkattup, piṅkura lēṉaṟ paṭartaruṅ kiḷi"1 eṉak kapilar kūṟumāṟuṅ kāṇka. iṉi, kāvalar kuṟavar eṉak koṇṭu avarkaḷ marattiṉ ciṉimī tiruntu ārkkuntōṟum kiḷikaḷ olittalic ceyyum eṉṟu urittalumām. veḷil - aṇil. kōḷ - kāy; kāyttalumām. paḻuppayaṉ - paḻutta paḻamumām. kurampiyil vēṅkimalar paranta tōṟṟattip puliyeṉak karuti vēḻam añciṟṟu eṉṉum ik karuttu  `naṟamaṉi vēṅkiyiṉ pūppayil pāṟiyi nākanaṇṇi, maṟamaṉi vēṅki yeṉanaṉi yañcumañ cārcilampā'2 eṉat tirucciṟṟampalak kōvi yāriṉum varutal aṟiyaṟpālatu. tēm - tēṉ. ōrum tāṉ eṉpaṉa acikaḷ.
(u-ṟi) yāṉiyāṉatu veruvat takāta kurampiyi veruvattakka puliyeṉṟu karutit taṉak kuṇavākiya kaḻi muṟiyap pāyum eṉpatu, veruvat takāta eṅkaḷ tamari veruvi variya muyalātu nī tiritaliṉ, yāṅkaḷ iṟantupaṭumpaṭiyāyiṟṟu eṉṟa paṭiyām.
(mē-ḷ) `poḻutum āṟum'3 eṉṉum cūttirattu, `nal vari...... vāḻalaḷ' eṉṉum ic ceyyuṭ pakutiyik kāṭṭi, `eṉpatu iravum pakalum vāraleṉṟatu, varital vēṭkip poruḷatāya vaḻuvamiti, eṉṟaṉar, nac.'
`muṭṭu vayiṟ kaḻaṟal'4 eṉṉum cūttirattu, varinteytuṅ kūṭṭattiṟku ētuvākiya meyppāṭukaḷuḷ oṉṟākiya avaṉ puṇarvu maṟuttal eṉpataṟku, `nalvari nāṭa........ vāḻalaḷ' eṉṟa pakutiyi eṭuttukkāṭṭi, "eṉpatu talimakaḷ kuṟippiṉit tōḻi kūṟiyatākalāṉ, iḵtu avaṉ puṇarvu maṟuttal eṉappaṭum. iḵtu `oṉṟit tōṉṟum tōḻi mēṉa' 5 eṉṉum ilakkaṇattāṟ ṟōḻi kuṟippāyiṉum, talimakaḷ kuṟippeṉavē paṭum eṉpatu koḷka" eṉṟurittaṉar, pērā.
13. pāli
(poruḷvayiṟ piriyaluṟṟa talimakaṉit tōḻi 
celavaḻuṅku vittatu;  uṭampaṭṭatūumām.)
		taṉkaṭaṟ1 piṟanta muttiṉ āramum
		muṉitiri koṭukkun tuppiṉ taṉmalit
		teṟalarum marapiṉ kaṭavuṭ pēṇik
		kuṟavar tanta cantiṉ āramum
	5.	irupē rāramum eḻilpeṟa aṇiyum
		tiruvīḻ mārpiṉ teṉṉavaṉ maṟavaṉ
		kuḻiyiṟ koṇṭa marāa yāṉi
		moḻiyiṉ uṇarttuñ ciṟuvari yallatu
		varinili yiṉṟi iravalark kīyum
	10.	vaḷvāy ampiṟ kōṭip porunaṉ
		paṇṇi tiiya payaṅkeḻu vēḷviyiṉ
		viḻumitu nikaḻva tāyiṉun teṟkērpu
		kaḻimaḻi poḻinta poḻutukoḷ amiyattuc
		cāya liṉ tuṇi ivaṭpirin tuṟiyiṉ
	15.	nōyiṉ ṟāka ceyporuḷ vayiṟpaṭa
		mācil tūmaṭi virinta cēkkik
		kavaviṉ puṉmik kaḻika vaḷavayal
		aḻaṉuti yaṉṉa tōki īṉṟa
		kaḻaṉi nelliṉ kavimutal alaṅkal
	20.	nirampakaṉ ceṟuvil varampaṇiyāt tuyalvarap
		pulampoṭu vanta poḻutukoḷ vāṭi
		ilaṅkupūṅ karumpi ṉērkaḻi irunta
		veṇkuruku narala vīcum
		nuṇpal tuvaliya taṇpaṉi nāḷē.
- peruntalic cāttaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 1-12. taṉ kaṭal piṟanta muttiṉ āramum - taṉatu teṉkaṭalil tōṉṟiya muttākiya āramum, muṉi tiṟi koṭukkum tuppiṉ - pakivar tiṟikoṭukkum valimiyi yuṭiyatāṉa, taṉmalit teṟal arum marapiṉ kaṭavuḷ pēṇi - taṉatu potiyil maliyil uḷḷa aṭiyārkaḷit tuṉpuṟuttalil lāta murukavēḷi vaḻipaṭṭu, kuṟavar tanta cantiṉ āramum - kuṟavarkaḷ koṇṭu vantu tarum cantaṉamākiya āramum āya, iru pēr āramum - ivviru periya āraṅkaḷiyum, eḻil peṟa aṇiyum - aḻakuṟa aṇiyum, tiru vīḻ mārpiṉ teṉṉavaṉ maṟavaṉ - ilakkumi virumpum mārpiṉiyuṭiya pāṇṭiyaṉatu paṭittalivaṉum, kuḻiyiṟ koṇṭa marāa yāṉi - payampil piṭitta paḻakāta yāṉikaḷi, moḻiyiṉ uṇarttum ciṟu vari allatu - uriya moḻikaḷāl toḻili aṟivikkum ciṟu poḻutallatu, varinili iṉṟi - taṉakkeṉac ciṟitum uṇarntu koḷḷum nilimiyiṉṟi, iravalarkku īyum - iravalarkaḷukku vaḻaṅkum, vaḷvāy ampiṉ kōṭipporunaṉ - kūrmi vāynta ampiṉiyuṭiya kōṭikkut talivaṉumākiya, paṇṇi tiiya payam keḻu vēḷviyiṉ - paṇṇi eṉpāṉ ceyta payaṉ mikka kaḷavēḷviyip pōla, viḻumiyatu nikaḻvatāyiṉum - nīr tēṭivarum poruḷāṟ ciṟanta payaṉ nikaḻu māyiṉum,
17-24. vaḷavayal aḻalnuti aṉṉa - vaḷam mikka vayalil tīyiṉ koḻuntiṉi yotta, tōki īṉṟa - tōṭukaḷi īṉṟa, kaḻaṉi nelliṉ navi mutal alaṅkal - vayal nelliṉ palavākak kiḷitta mutaliliruntu tōṉṟiya neṟkatir, nirampu akaṉ ceṟuvil - nirampiya akaṉṟa vayaliṉiṭattu, varampu aṇiyāt tuyalvara - varappukkaḷi aṇiyākak koṇṭu kiṭantu aliya, pulampoṭu vanta poḻutu koḷ vāṭi - taṉimit tuṉpiṉik koṇṭu varum paṉikkālat taṉmi koṇṭa vāṭik kāṟṟu, ilaṅku pūṅkarumpiṉ ēr kaḻi irunta - viḷaṅkum pūkkaḷi yuṭiya karumpiṉ ōṅkiṉa taṇṭiṉ mītirunta, veṇkuruku narala vīcum - veṇṇāri olikkum paṭi vīcukiṉṟa, nuṇ pal tuvaliya taṇpaṉi nāḷ - nuṇṇiya palavākiya tuḷikaḷik koṇṭa taṇṇiya paṉikkālattē.
12-14. teṟku ērpu kaḻi maḻi poḻinta - (mēkaṅkaḷ) teṟkē eḻuntupōy mikka maḻiyip poḻiyum, poḻutukoḷ amiyattu - maḻikkālat taṉmi vāyntiṭum kūtirkālattiṟku, cāyal iṉ tuṇi ivaḷ - iṉiya tuṇiyākiya meṉmi vāynta ivaḷi, pirintu uṟiyiṉ - pirintupōy vēṟṟunāṭṭut taṅkuvīrāyiṉ,
15-17. vayiṟpaṭa mācu il tū maṭi virinta - takka iṭattē kuṟṟamaṟṟa tūya aṟuvi virikkappeṟṟa, cēkki kavavu iṉpu uṟāmik kaḻika - paṭukkikkaṇ muyaṅkum iṉpam uṟāmal (appaṉināḷ) kaḻiyiṉum kaḻika, ceyporuḷ nōy iṉṟu āka - nī tēṭukiṉṟa poruṭku ekkuṟiyum nērā toḻivatāka.
(muṭipu) taliva! paṇṇi tiiya vēḷviyiṉ viḻumitu nikaḻvatāyiṉum, kūtirp poḻutiṟkut tuṇiyāya ivaḷit taṇpaṉi nāḷil pirintuṟiyiṉ, ap paṉi nāḷ kavavu iṉpuṟāmik kaḻika; ceyporuḷ nōyiṉṟāka.
(vi-ri) iru pērāramum aṇiyum teṉṉavaṉ maṟavaṉum iravalarkku yāṉi īyum kōṭip porunaṉumāya paṇṇi, eṉṟum vayalil nelliṉ alaṅkal tuyalvara vanta vāṭi, kuruku narala vīcum paṉināḷ eṉṟum kūṭṭuka. muttiṉ, cantiṉ eṉpuḻi, iṉ cāriyi alvaḻikkaṇ vantaṉa. piṟarāl teṟutalariya kaṭavuḷ eṉṟumām. aṇiyum maṟavaṉ eṉka; aṇiyum teṉṉavaṉ eṉṟumām. moḻi - yāṉikkut toḻil aṟivikkum kuṟippu moḻikaḷ. kōṭi - kōṭṭūr eṉpar kuṟippuriyāciriyar. vāṭi vīcaliṉ, mēkam teṟku ērpu eṉṟār. poḻutu koḷ amiyam - kūtirppoḻutu. ceyporuḷ nōyiṉṟāka eṉṟatu, ataṟku iṭiyūṟu uṇṭām eṉṉum karuttāl. iṭiyūṟāvatu talivi talivaṉip pirintu uyir vāḻāmiyāṉāvatu. aṇiyā - aṇintu eṉṟumām.
14. mulli
(pāṇaṉ taṉakkup pāṅkāyiṉār kēṭpac colliyatu)
		arakkat taṉṉa cennilap peruvaḻik
		kāyāñ cemmal tāayp palavuṭaṉ
		īyaṉ mūtāy varippap pavaḷamoṭu
		maṇimiṭin taṉṉa kuṉṟaṅ kaviiya
	5.	aṅkāṭ ṭāriṭi maṭappiṇi taḻīit
		tirimarup pirali pullarun tukaḷa
		mulli viyaṉpulam parappik kōvalar
		kuṟumpoṟi maruṅkiṉ naṟumpū vayarap
		patavumēyal aruntu matavunaṭi nallāṉ
	10.	vīṅkumāṇ ceruttal tīmpāl piliṟṟak
		kaṉṟupayir kurala maṉṟuniri pukutaru
		māliyum uḷḷā rāyiṟ kāli
		yāṅkā kuvaṅkol pāṇa veṉṟa
		maṉiyōḷ colletir collal cellēṉ
	15.	cevvaḻi nalyāḻ iciyiṉeṉ piyeṉak
		kaṭavuḷ vāḻttip piyuḷ meynniṟut
		tavartiṟañ celvēṉ kaṇṭaṉeṉ yāṉē
		viṭuvicik kutiri vilaṅkupari muṭukak
		kalporu tiraṅkum pallār nēmik
	20.	kārmaḻi muḻakkici kaṭukkum
		muṉinal lūraṉ puṉineṭun tērē.
- okkūr mācāttaṉār.
 (co-ḷ) 1-12. arakkattu aṉṉa cennilap peruvaḻi - cevvarakkiṉi yotta civanta nilattiṟ cellum peru neṟiyil, kāyā cemmal tāay - kāyāviṉ vāṭiya pūkkaḷ paravikkiṭakka, īyal mūtāy pala uṭaṉ varippa - tampalap pūccikaḷ palavum oruṅkē varivariyāka ūrntu cella, pavaḷamoṭu maṇi miṭintaṉṉa kuṉṟam kaviiya - (avi) pavaḷattoṭu nīlamaṇi neruṅki yiruntā lokkum kuṉṟam cūḻnta, am kāṭṭu ār iṭi - kāṭṭiṉ akattē ariya iṭaṅkaḷil, maṭappiṇi taḻīi - maṭappattiyuṭiya peṇmāṉit taḻuvi, tirimaruppu irali - tirittu viṭṭāl pōṉṟa kompiṉiyuṭiya āṇmāṉ, pul aruntu ukaḷa - pulli aruntit tāvic cella, mulli viyaṉpulam parappi - (āṉiṉaṅkaḷi) akaṉṟa mulli nilattilē paravi mēyaviṭṭu, kōvalar kuṟumpoṟi maruṅkiṉ naṟumpū ayara - āyarkaḷ ciṟiya kuṉṟukaḷiṭattē naṟiya pūkkaḷi māliyākak kaṭṭi makiḻntirukka, patavu mēyal aruntu matavu naṭi nalāṉ - aṟukam pullāya uṇaviṉi aruntiya valiya naṭiyiṉiyuṭiya nalla āṉiṉaṅkaḷ, vīṅku māṇ ceruttal - parutta māṇpuṟṟa maṭi, tīmpāl piliṟṟa - iṉiya pālic coriya, kaṉṟu payir kurala maṉṟu niṟi pukutarum - kaṉṟi niṉintu aḻikkum kuraliṉavāy maṉṟukaḷil niṟiyac cellum, māliyum uḷḷārāyiṉ - immālik kālattum nam talivar nammi niṉittu vārārāyiṉ,
12-14. kāli yāṅku ākuvam kol pāṇa eṉṟa - pāṇaṉē ikkālattu yāṅkaḷ ennili yuṟuvēm eṉṟu kūṟiya, maṉiyōḷ col etir collal cellēṉ - taliviyiṉ colliṟku maṟumoḻi kūṟa iyalātavaṉāki,
15-17. cevvaḻi nalyāḻ iciyiṉeṉ piyeṉa - yāḻil nalla cevvaḻippaṇṇi melleṉa icittu, kaṭavuḷ vāḻtti - kaṭavuḷi vāḻtti, piyuḷ mey niṟutti - tuyariṉikkoṇṭa meyyiṉaṉāy, avartiṟam celvēṉ yāṉ - avarmāṭṭuc celvēṉākiya yāṉ,
18-21. viṭu vicik kutiri - tūṇṭappeṟum vēkaṅ koṇṭa kutiriyiṉ, vilaṅku pari muṭuka - etirppaṭumavaṟṟi vilaṅki muṉ cellum celavu mika, kal porutu iraṅkum pal ār nēmi - kaṟkaḷil mōti olikkum pala āraṅkālkaḷiyuṭiya uruḷiyiṉ oli, kār maḻi muḻakkici kaṭukkum - kārkālattu maḻiyiṉ iṭi muḻakkiṉi okkum, muṉinallūraṉ puṉineṭuntēr - muṉiyākiya nalla ūriṭattāṉākiya talivaṉuṭiya aṇiceyappeṟṟa nīṇṭa tēriṉi,
17. kaṇṭaṉeṉ - kaṇṭēṉ.
(muṭipu) `pāṇa! em talivar, māliyum uḷḷārāyiṉ, kāli yāṅkākuvam' eṉṟa (talivi) colletir collēṉ kaṭavuḷ vāḻtti, avartiṟam celvēṉ muṉi nallūraṉ neṭuntēr kaṇṭaṉeṉ; (eṉap pāṇaṉ pāṅkāyiṉārkkuk kūṟiṉāṉ)
irali ukaḷa, kōvalar naṟumpū ayara, nallāṉ takutaru māli eṉka.
(vi-ri) arakkattaṉṉa : attuccāriyi. kāyāñ cemmal eṉa melimikkatu, `yāmarakkiḷavi'1 eṉṉum cūttirattu ilēcāṟ koḷka. kāyāñ cemmalukku nīla maṇiyum, īyal mūtāykkup pavaḷamum uvamiyām; etir niraṉiṟi. īyal mūtāy - tampalap pūcci; intira kōpam. pullaruntu - pulli arunti. niṟi - niṟiya. kāli - kālam. cevvaḻi - mullippaṇ. muṉi - pakippulam.
(mē-ḷ)  `nilam peyarntuṟital'2 eṉṉum cūttirattup pāṇarkkuk kūṟṟu nikaḻvataṟku ic ceyyuḷi eṭuttuk kāṭṭiṉar, nac.
`maṉiyōḷ kiḷaviyum'3 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, `māliyum........ pāṇa' eṉṉum icceyyuṭ pakutiyi, pāṇaṉ kēṭpat talivi kūṟṟu vantataṟku eṭuttuk kāṭṭiṉar, pērā.

15. pāli
(makaṭ pōkkiya tāy colliyatu.)
		emveṅ kāmam iyiva tāyiṉ
		meymmali perumpūṭ cemmaṟ kōcar
		kommiyam pacuṅkāyk kuṭumi viḷinta
		pāka lārkip paṟikkaṇ pīlit
	5.	tōkik kāviṉ tuḷunāṭ ṭaṉṉa
		vaṟuṅki vampalart tāṅkum paṇpiṟ
		ceṟinta cēric cemmal mūtūr
		aṟinta mākkaṭ ṭākuka tilla
		tōḻi mārum yāṉum pulampac
	10.	cūḻi yāṉic cuṭarppūṇ naṉṉaṉ
		pāḻi aṉṉa kaṭiyuṭi viyaṉnakarc
		ceṟinta kāppikan tavaṉoṭu pōki
		atta iruppi ārkaḻal putuppūt
		tuytta vāya tukaḷnilam parakkak
	15.	koṉṟi yañciṉik kuḻaṟpaḻaṅ koḻuti
		vaṉki yeṇkiṉ vayaniri parakkum
		iṉṟuṇip paṭarnta koḷkiyō ṭorāṅkuk
		kuṉṟa vēyil tiraṇṭeṉ
		meṉṟōḷ aññi ceṉṟa āṟē.
- māmūlaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 9-19. tōḻimārum yāṉum pulampa - taṉ tōḻi mārum yāṉum taṉimiyāṉ varunta, cūḻi yāṉic cuṭarppūṇ naṉṉaṉ pāḻi aṉṉa - mukapaṭām aṇinta yāṉiyiṉiyum oḷiviṭum aṇiyiṉiyum uṭiya naṉṉaṉatu pāḻi eṉṉum patiyi otta, kaṭiyuṭi viyaṉ nakar ceṟinta kāppu ikantu - kāval mikka tantiyatu periya maṉiyiṉ mikka kāvalit tāṇṭi, avaṉoṭu pōki - talivaṉoṭu puṟappaṭṭu, iṉtuṇip paṭarnta koḷki yoṭu orāṅku - taṉ iṉiya tuṇivaṉi niṉinta koḷkiyāl oruṅkukūṭi, kuṉṟavēyil tiraṇṭa mel tōḷ eṉ aññi ceṉṟa - kuṉṟattu mūṅkilpōlat tiraṇṭa melliya tōḷiyuṭiya eṉ aṉṉi ceṉṟaṭintatum, attam iruppi ār kaḻal putu pū - aruñcurattil uḷḷa iruppi marattiṉ ārkkuk kaḻaṉṟa putiya pūkkaḷi, tuytta vāy - tiṉṟa vāyiṉavāy, tukaḷ nilam parakka - nilattilē tūḷi parakkac ceṉṟu, koṉṟi amciṉi kuḻal paḻam koḻuti - koṉṟiyiṉatu aḻakiya kiḷikaḷiluḷḷa kuḻal pōlum paḻattik koḻuti, vaṉ ki eṇkiṉ vayaniri parakkum - valiya kiyiṉiyuṭiya karaṭikaḷiṉ valiya kūṭṭam parantu celvatumāya, āṟu - neṟi.
1-8. em vem kāmam iyivatāyiṉ - ematu mikka viruppam kikūṭuvatāyiṉ, mey mali perumpūṇ cemmal kōcar - meym moḻiyāṟ ciṟanta periya aṇikaḷiyuṭiya talimi koṇṭa kōcarkaḷatu, kuṭumi kommi am pacuṅkāy viḷinta pākal ārki - ārkkiṉit taliyiluṭiya tiraṇṭa paciya kāy muṟṟiṉa pākaṟpaḻattit tiṉṉutaluṭiya, paṟik kaṇ pīlit tōkik kāviṉ - paṟi pōla vaṭṭamāṉa kaṇkaḷik koṇṭa tōki kaḷiyuṭiya mayilkaḷ mikka cōlikaḷiyuṭiya, tuḷunāṭṭu aṉṉa - tuḷunāṭṭi yotta, vaṟuṅki vampalar tāṅkum paṇpiṉ - poruḷiṉṟi varum putiyarkaḷip purakkum naṟpaṇpiṉi yuṭiya, ceṟinta cēri cemmal mūtūr aṟinta mākkaṭṭuākuka - neruṅkiya cērikaḷik koṇṭa talimi vāyntirukkum mutiya ūrkaḷil mukamaṟinta makkaḷi uṭiyatu ākuka.
(muṭipu) em veṅkāmam iyivatāyiṉ avaṉoṭu pōki eṉ aññi ceṉṟa āṟu, tuḷu nāṭṭaṉṉa cemmaṉ mūtūril aṟinta mākkaṭṭu ākuka.
kōcar tuḷu nāṭu eṉka. aññi ceṉṟa āṟu, vayaniri parakkum āṟu eṉat taṉittaṉi kūṭṭuka.
(vi-ri) kōcar meymmiyiṟ ciṟantavar eṉpatu, `oṉṟu moḻik kōcar'1 eṉavum, `vāymoḻi niliiya cēṇviḷaṅku nallici, vaḷaṅkeḻu kōcar'2 eṉavum varuvaṉavaṟṟāṉ aṟiyappaṭum. iṉi, meymmali eṉpataṟku uṭampilē niṟinta eṉalumām. pīli - tōki, ciṟi. tōki - mayil. til - viḻivu. pāḻi - naṉṉaṉatu ūr. koṉṟip paḻattiṉit turuvit tuḷittuk kuḻalākak koḷḷutal uṇṭākaliṉ, kuḻaṟpaḻam eṉṟār. aññi - aṉṉi. cevili, taliviyi arumi pārāṭṭi aññi eṉṟāḷ.
(u-ṟi) `karaṭi ārkaḻal putuppūvi nukarnta vāyi yuṭiya vāy atilē niṟintu, koṉṟip paḻattik kōtiṉāṟpōla, avaḷum talivaṉoṭum kūṭiya iṉpattilē niṟinta cerukkilē, kūṭi vaḷarnta tōḻimāriyum, peṟṟu vaḷartta eṉṉiyum puṟakkaṇittup pōṉāḷ' eṉpatu.
16. marutam
(parattiyar cēriyiṉiṉṟum vanta talimakaṉ yāriyum 
aṟiyēṉ eṉṟāṟkut talimakaḷ colliyatu.)
		nāyuṭi mutunīrk kalitta tāmarit
		tātiṉ alli ayalitaḻ puriyum
		mācil aṅki maṇimaruḷ avvāy
		nāvoṭu navilā nakipaṭu tīñcol
	5.	yāvarum viḻiyum polantoṭip putalvaṉit
		tērvaḻaṅku teruvil tamiyōṟ kaṇṭē
		kūreyiṟ ṟarivi kuṟukiṉaḷ yāvarum
		kāṇunar iṉmiyiṉ cettaṉaḷ pēṇip
		polaṅkalañ cumanta pūṇtāṅ kiḷamuli
	10.	varuka māḷeṉ ṉuyireṉap perituvantu
		koṇṭaṉaḷ niṉṟōḷ kaṇṭunilic cellēṉ
		mācil kuṟumakaḷ evaṉpē tuṟṟaṉi
		nīyun tāyi ivaṟkeṉa yāṉtaṉ
		kariya vantu virivaṉeṉ kaviik
	15.	kaḷavuṭam paṭunaril kaviḻntu nilaṅ kiḷiyā
		nāṇi niṉṟōḷ nilikaṇ ṭiyāṉum
		pēṇiṉeṉ allaṉō makiḻna vāṉat
		taṇaṅkaruṅ kaṭavu ḷaṉṉōḷ niṉ
		makaṉtā yātal purivatāṅ keṉavē.
-cākalācaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 17. makiḻna - talivaṉē!
1-5.  nāyuṭi mutu nīr kalitta tāmarit tātiṉ alli ayal itaḻ puriyum - nīrnāykaḷiyuṭiya paḻiya nīril taḻitta tāmariyiṉ pūntātākiya alliyiṉ aṭutta itaḻiṉi yotta, mācu il aṅki maṇi maruḷ avvāy - kuṟṟamillāta akaṅkiyiṉiyum pavaḷam pōṉṟa aḻakiya vāyiṉiyum, nāvoṭu navilā naki paṭu tīñcol - nāvāṟ payiṉṟu pēcappaṭāta kēṭṭārkku makiḻcciyit tarum iṉiya kutalic coṟkaḷiyum uṭiya, yāvarum viḻiyum polam toṭip putalvaṉi - kaṇṭār aṉivarum virumpum poṟṟoṭi yaṇinta putalvaṉi,
6-11. polam kalam cumanta - poṟkalaṉkaḷit tāṅkiya, kūr eyiṟṟu arivi - kūriya paṟkaḷiyuṭiya niṉ paratti, tēr vaḻaṅku teruvil tamiyōṉ kaṇṭu - avaṉ ciṟutēr ōṭṭik koṇṭirunta teruvil taṉiyaṉāy niṟpak kaṇṭu, cettaṉaḷ pēṇi - niṉ oppumi karutip pōṟṟi, yāvarum kāṇunar iṉmiyiṉ - yāruṅ kāṇpōr iṉmiyiṉ, kuṟukiṉaḷ - kuṟuki, peritu uvantu - mika makiḻntu, varuka eṉ uyireṉa - eṉ uyirē varuvāyāka eṉa, pūṇ tāṅku iḷamuli koṇṭaṉaḷ niṉṟōḷ kaṇṭu - pūṇkaḷiyaṇinta iḷamulikaḷil aṇittukkoṇṭu niṉṟavaḷik kaṇṭu,
11-14. nilic cellēṉ - niṉṟa niliyiṉiṉṟu mīḷēṉāy, virivaṉeṉ vantu kavii - virintu vantu avaḷi aṇittu, mācil kuṟumakaḷ - kuṟṟamaṟṟa iḷiya makaḷē, evaṉ pētuṟṟaṉi - ēṉ mayaṅkiṉi, nīyum tāyi ivaṟku eṉa - ivaṉukku nīyum oru tāyal liyō eṉṟu, yāṉ taṉ kariya - yāṉ avaḷiṭam kūṟa,
15-16. kaḷavu uṭampaṭunaril kaviḻntu - tām ceyta kaḷavik (kaṇṭu koṇṭār muṉ) uṭaṉpaṭṭu niṟpārpōla mukam kaviḻntu, nilam kiḷiyā nāṇi niṉṟōḷ nili kaṇṭu - nilattik kālviralāṟ kīṟi nāṇi niṉṟa avaḷ taṉ niliyiṉik kaṇṭu,
16-19. vāṉattu aṇaṅku aruṅ kaṭavuḷ aṉṉōḷ - vāṉattiṉ ariya teyvamākiya aruntati pōṉṟāḷ, niṉ makaṉ tāyātal purivatu eṉavē - niṉ makaṉukkut tāyātal okkum eṉa eṇṇi, yāṉum pēṇiṉeṉ allaṉō - yāṉum avaḷi virumpiṉēṉ allaṉō?
(muṭipu) makiḻna! kūr eyiṟṟarivi, putalvaṉik kaṇṭu koṇṭaṉaḷ niṉṟōṭ kaṇṭu, vantu kavii, nīyum tāyi ivaṟkeṉa yāṉ kariya, nāṇi niṉṟōḷ nilikaṇṭu, niṉmakaṉ tāyātal purivatu eṉavē pēṇiṉeṉ allaṉō?
arivi kuṟukiṉaḷ pēṇi muli (yiṭattuk) koṇṭaṉaḷ niṉṟōḷ eṉavum, yāṉ virivaṉeṉ vantu kaviik kariya eṉavum kūṭṭuka.
(vi-ri) alli eṉṟatu īṇṭut tātiṉi uṇarttiṟṟu. aviritaḻ eṉṉum pāṭattiṟku alliyākiya itaḻeṉa irupeyaroṭṭākak koḷka; alli - akavitaḻ. maṇi - pavaḷam. nakipaṭu tīñcol eṉpataṟku, nakiyit tōṟṟuvikkum tiruntāta col eṉṟumām; iḵtu iḷami poruḷākat tōṉṟiya nakiyām. pakivarum uṭpaṭa yāvarum viḻiyum eṉṟāḷ; `ceṟunarum viriyum ceyirtīr kāṭcic ciṟuvar' eṉa (66) in nūluṭ piṉṉar varutaluṅ kāṇka. talivaṉatu tēr cellum teru, eṉṟumām; talivaṉ parattivayiṉ tērūrntu celvāṉ ākaliṉ. cettu - karuti ; oppumām. polaṅkalañ cumanta pūṇ tāṅku iḷamuli eṉṟatu, talivaṉi vayamākkum avaḷatu iḷami karutik kūṟiyatu. māḷa: aci. putalvaṉi uyireṉṟatu, kātal paṟṟiyatu. tāyi; `ai' muṉṉilikkaṇ vantatu. eppoḻutum niṉṉiyē uḷḷattuk koṇṭiruppāḷ eṉa eḷḷuvāḷāy aruntatiyaṉṉāḷ eṉṟāḷ. iṉi, aṇaṅku aruṅ kaṭavuḷ eṉpataṟkup piṟari varuttukiṉṟa eytaṟku ariya teyvamakaḷ eṉṟumām.
(mē-ḷ) `avaṉaṟivu āṟṟa' 1 eṉṉum cūttirattu `kāmak kiḻatti taṉmakat taḻīi ēmuṟu viḷiyāṭ ṭiṟutikkaṇ' talivi kūṟṟu nikaḻvataṟku, icceyyuḷiyum, ac cūttirattu `avaṉ vayiṟ pirippiṉum' eṉpataṟku `niṉmakaṉ ṟāyātal' eṉṉum icceyyuṭ pakutiyiyum, `kaṟpu vaḻippaṭṭavaḷ'2 eṉṉum cūttirattu, talivi parattiyi ēttutal vaḻuvamitiyām eṉpataṟku `nāṇi........ purivatāṅ keṉavē' eṉṉum ic ceyyuṭ pakutiyiyum utāraṇam kāṭṭiṉar, nac.
"eḷḷaliḷami" 1 eṉṉum cūttirattu, `nāvoṭu navilā naki' eṉpatu, piṟar iḷami poruḷāka naki piṟantatu eṉṟum, `perumiyuñ ciṟumiyum' 2 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, `kaḷavuṭam paṭunariṟ kaviḻntu nilaṅ kiḷiyā' eṉpatu naki yeṉṉum meyppāṭu paṟṟiya uvamam eṉṟum, ciṟumi paṟṟi vantateṉṟum, `uvamap poruḷi'3 eṉṉum cūttirattu, kaḷavuṭam paṭunarkkuḷḷa vēṟupāṭu ulakattu aṭippaṭa vanta vaḻakkātalāṉ aḵtu ētuvāka aṟintu koḷḷappaṭuvatāyiṟṟu eṉṟum kūṟuvar, pērā.

17. pāli
(makaṭpōkkiya cevilittāy colliyatu.)
		vaḷaṅkeḻu tirunakarp pantuciṟi teṟiyiṉum
		iḷantuṇi yāyamoṭu kaḻaṅkuṭa ṉāṭiṉum
		uyaṅkiṉ ṟaṉṉiyeṉ meyyeṉ ṟacii
		mayaṅkuviyar poṟitta nutalaḷ taṇṇeṉa
	5.	muyaṅkiṉaḷ vatiyum maṉṉē iṉiyē
		toṭimāṇ cuṟṟamum emmum uḷḷāḷ
		neṭumoḻit tanti aruṅkaṭi nīvi
		notuma lāḷaṉ neñcaṟap peṟṟeṉ
		ciṟumutuk kuṟivi cilampār cīṟaṭi
	10.	vallakol cellat tāmē kalleṉa
		ūreḻun taṉṉa urukeḻu celaviṉ
		nīri lattat tāriṭi maṭutta
		koṭuṅkō lumaṇar pakaṭuteḻi teḷviḷi
		neṭumperuṅ kuṉṟat timiḻkoḷa iyampuṅ
	15	kaṭuṅkatir tirukiya vēypayil piṟaṅkal
		peruṅkaḷi ṟuriñciya maṇṇari yāat
		taruñcurak kavaliya atarpaṭu maruṅkiṉ
		nīḷari ilavat tūḻkaḻi paṉmalar
		viḻavut talikkoṇṭa paḻaviṟal mūtūr
	20.	neyyumiḻ cuṭaril kālporac cilki
		vikuṟu mīṉil tōṉṟum
		mipaṭu māmali viḷaṅkiya curaṉē.
- kayamaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 1-5. vaḷamkeḻu tirunakar - celvam poruntiya aḻakiya maṉiyiṉkaṇṇē, pantu ciṟitu eṟiyiṉum - ciṟitu poḻutu pantu eṟintu āṭiṉum, iḷantuṇi āyamoṭu - taṉṉi yotta iḷiyarāya āyattārōṭu, kaḻaṅku uṭaṉ āṭiṉum - oruṅkiruntu kaḻaṅku āṭiṉum, aṉṉi eṉ mey uyaṅkiṉṟu eṉṟu acii - aṉṉiyē! eṉ uṭal varuntiṟṟu eṉṟu kūṟit taḷarntu, mayaṅku viyar poṟitta nutalaḷ - ceṟiyum viyarvu arumpiya neṟṟiyiṉaḷāy, taṇṇeṉa muyaṅkiṉaḷ vatiyum - (eṉ uṭal) taṇṇeṉṟu kuḷira eṉṉit taḻuvi amarvaḷ (muṉpu), maṉ - antō atu kaḻintatē!
5-7. iṉi - ippoḻutu, toṭimāṇ cuṟṟamum emmum uḷḷāḷ - toṭiyiyuṭiya ciṟanta āyattāriyum emmiyum niṉiyāḷāy, neṭumoḻit tanti aruṅkaṭi nīvi - mikka pukaḻiyuṭiya taṉ tantiyiṉatu ariya kāvaliṉik kaṭantu ceṉṟu;
10-22. kal eṉa ūr eḻuntaṉṉa urukeḻu celaviṉ - kalleṉṉum oliyuṭaṉ ōr ūrē eḻuntu celvatokkum accam mikka celkiyiyuṭiya, koṭuṅkōl umaṇar - koṭiya kōlkaḷiyuṭiya uppuvāṇikar, nīril attattu āriṭi - nīraṟṟa kāṭṭiṉ ariya iṭaṅkaḷilē, maṭutta pakaṭu teḻi teḷviḷi - taṭiyuṟum kaṭākkaḷi urappiyōṭṭum teḷḷiya olikaḷ, neṭum peru kuṉṟattu imiḻ koḷa iyampum - neṭiya periya maliyil etiroli uṇṭāka vanticippatum, kaṭuṅkatir tirukiya vēypayil piṟaṅkal - kaṭumi mikka ñāyiṟṟiṉ katirkaḷ muṟukiya mūṅkil aṭarnta pakkamalikaḷiyuṭiyatum, peru kaḷiṟu uriñciya maṇari - yāttu - periya kaḷiṟukaḷ uriñciyatāl cēṟṟu maṇṇip poruntiya aṭiyiṉiyuṭiya yā maraṅkaḷi yuṭiya, aruñ curak kavaliya atarpaṭu maruṅkiṉ - ariya curattē kavartta neṟikaḷ poruntiya pakkaṅkaḷil, nīḷ ari ilavattu ūḻkaḻi pal malar - nīṇṭa aṭiyiṉiyuṭiya ilava marattiṉ mutircci mikka palavāya pūkkaḷ, viḻavutalik koṇṭa paḻaviṟal mūtu ūr - viḻākkaḷit taṉṉiṭattē koṇṭa paḻiya veṟṟiyik koṇṭa mutiya ūril, ney umiḻ cuṭaril - neyyip peyta viḷakkiṉ cuṭar viḻuvatupōla viḻumpaṭi, kālporac cilki - kāṟṟup porutalāl eñciyavi cilavāki, vikuṟu mīṉil tōṉṟum - viṭikiṉṟa kālattu mīṉpōlac cilavāyt tōṉṟuvatu mākiya, mi paṭu māmali vilaṅkiya curaṉ - mēkaṅkaḷ poruntiya periya malikaḷ kuṟukkiṭṭu niṟkum curaneṟiyiṉil;
8-10. cella - cellutaṟku, notumalāḷaṉ neñcu aṟap peṟṟa - ētilāḷaṉatu neñcu taṉakkē yurittākap peṟṟa, eṉ ciṟu mutukkuṟivi - eṉatu ciṟiya mūtaṟivuṭiyavaḷatu, cilampu ār cīṟaṭi tām - cilampu poruntiya ciṟiya aṭikaḷ, vallakol - vallunavō?
(muṭipu) ciṟu mutuk kuṟiviyāya eṉ makaḷ. muṉpu pantu ciṟitu eṟiyiṉum kaḻaṅku āṭiṉum acii muyaṅkiṉaḷ vatiyum, iṉi, avaḷ cilampu ār cīṟaṭi tanti aruṅkaṭi nīvi, mali vilaṅkiya curaṉ cellavalla kol.
urukeḻu celaviṉ umaṇar eṉavum, āriṭi maṭutta pakaṭu eṉavum kūṭṭuka. teḷviḷi iyampum mali eṉavum, piṟaṅkali yuṭiya maliyeṉavum, paṉmalar cilkit tōṉṟum mali yeṉavum iyiyum.
(vi-ri) maṉ kaḻiviṉkaṇ vantatu. neṭumoḻi - pukaḻ; vañciṉamumām. aṟap peṟutal - urittākap peṟutal; `taṉṉuyir tāṉaṟap peṟṟāṉi' (268) eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvarum. ciṟumutuk kuṟivi - iḷamiyilē aṟivu mutirntavaḷ; `ciṟumutukkuṟivikkuc ciṟumiyuñ ceytēṉ'1 eṉṟār iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷum. umaṇar kūṭṭattu mikutiyik kuṟittaṟku ūreḻuntaṉṉa eṉṟār. āriṭi - ēṟṟiḻivuṭiya iṭaṅkaḷ. imiḻ koḷa - iṉimi porunta eṉṟumām. kaḷiṟu, mārikkālattu uṇṭāṉa cēṟu ularntu viṟuviṟutta uṭali uriñcutalāl yāmaram maṇṇiyuṭiya ariyāyiṟṟu. viṭikiṉṟa kālattu, mīṉkaḷ pala maṟiyac cilavē tōṉṟumākaliṉ, vikuṟu mīṉiṟ ṟōṉṟumeṉṟār.
18. kuṟiñci
(tōḻi iravu varuvāṉip pakal vāveṉṟatu.)
		nīrniṟam karappa ūḻuṟu putirntu
		pūmalar kañaliya kaṭuvaraṟ kāṉyāṟṟuk
		karāan tuñcuṅ kalluyar maṟicuḻi
		marāa yāṉi matantapa oṟṟi
	5.	urāa īrkkum uṭkuvaru nīttaṅ
		kaṭuṅkaṭ paṉṟiyiṉ naṭuṅkātu tuṟantu
		nāma aruntuṟip pērtan tiyāmat
		tīṅkum varupavō ōṅkal veṟpa
		orunāḷ viḻumam uṟiṉum vaḻināḷ
	10.	vāḻkuvaḷ allaḷeṉ tōḻi yāvatum
		ūṟil vaḻikaḷum payila vaḻaṅkunar
		nīṭiṉ ṟāka iḻukkuvar ataṉāl
		ulamaral varuttam uṟutumem paṭappik
		koṭuntēṉ iḻitta kōṭuyar neṭuvarip
	15.	paḻaṅtūṅku naḷippil kāntaḷam potumpil
		pakaṉī variṉum puṇarkuvi akaṉmali
		vāṅkamik kaṇṇiṭi kaṭuppayāy
		ōmpiṉaḷ eṭutta taṭameṉ tōḷē.		- kapilar
(co-ḷ) 8. ōṅkal veṟpa - uyarnta maliyiyuṭiya talivaṉē!,
1-5. nīr niṟam karappa ūḻ uṟupu utirntu pūmalar kañaliya kaṭuvaral kāṉyāṟṟu - nīriṉ niṟam maṟiya mutirpuṟṟu utirntu aḻakiya malarkaḷ neruṅkiya nīr kaṭuki varutaliyuṭiya kāṭṭāṟṟil, karā am tuñcum kal uyar maṟicuḻi - mutali taṅkum uyarnta kallil mōti mīḷum cuḻikaḷiyuṭiyatum, marāa yāṉi matam tapa oṟṟi urāa īrkkum uṭkuvaru nīttam - iṉattōṭu maruvāta kaḷiṟṟi yāṉiyi ataṉ matam keṭa mōti valiyuṟṟu iḻuttaliṉ accam tōṉṟuvatumākiya veḷḷattiṉi;
6-8. kaṭuṅkaṇ paṉṟiyiṉ naṭuṅkātu tuṟantu - añcāmiyi yuṭiya paṉṟiyippōla naṭuṅkutaliṉṟik kaṭantu ēṟi, nāma aruntuṟi pēr tantu yāmattu īṅkum varupavō - (aṇaṅku uṟitaliṉ) accam tarum ariya tuṟiyiṉit tāṇṭi naḷḷiravil ittakiya iṭattum makkaḷ varuvāruḷarō;
9-10. oru nāḷ viḻumam uṟiṉum vaḻināḷ eṉtōḻi vāḻkuvaḷ allaḷ - orunāḷ nī tuṉpam uṟiṉum piṉṉāḷ eṉ tōḻi uyirvāḻvāḷ allaḷ;
10-12. yāvatum ūṟu il vaḻikaḷum payila vaḻaṅkunar nīṭu iṉṟāka iḻukkuvar - iṭiyūṟu ciṟitum illāta vaḻikaḷilum aṅkup palakālum pōy varuvār nīṭutal iliyākat tavaṟu eytuvar;
12-13. ataṉāl ulamaral varuttam uṟutum - ataṉāl yāṅkaḷ maṉam cuḻalum varuttattiṉi yaṭivōm;
13-18. akal mali vāṅku amik kaṇ iṭi kaṭuppa - akaṉṟa maliyiluḷḷa vaḷinta mūṅkiliṉ kaṇukkaṭku naṭuvāṉa iṭatti yokka, yāy ōmpiṉaḷ eṭutta taṭamel tōḷ - yāy pōṟṟi vaḷartta taliviyiṉ periya meṉmi vāynta tōḷi, em paṭappi - em tōṭṭattiṉi yaṭuttuḷḷa, koṭutēṉ iḻitta - vaṭṭamākiya tēṉiṟāl vikkappeṟṟa, kōṭu uyar neṭu vari - cimiyam uyarnta nīṇṭa malikkaṇ, paḻam tūṅku naḷippil - paḻaṅkaḷ toṅkukiṉṟa maracceṟiviṉuḷ, kāntaḷ am potumpil - kāntaḷiṉuṭiya putariṭattu, pakal nī variṉum puṇarkuvi - pakaṟkaṇ nī variṉum poruntuvi.
(muṭipu) veṟpa! kāṉyāṟṟu nīttam naṭuṅkātu tuṟantu yāmattu īṅkum varupavō; eṉtōḻi vāḻkuvaḷ allaḷ; ūṟil vaḻikaḷum vaḻaṅkunar iḻukkuvar; ataṉāl varutta muṟutum; taṭameṉṟōḷ kāntaḷam potumpiṟ pakal variṉum peṟukuvi.
kāṉyāṟṟuc cuḻiyiyuṭiya nīttam eṉavum, yāṉiyi yuṭiya nīttam eṉavum iyiyum.
(vi-ri) urāa - aliya eṉṟumām. nāma : nām eṉṉum uriccol īṟu tirintatu. pērtantu - pērntu. īṅku - taṉmik kaṇ vantatu. aṉṟē vāḻāḷ eṉṉātu vaḻināḷ vāḻāḷ eṉṟatu, aṟivatu piṉṉāḷilākaliṉ. yāy- cevili.
`koṭuntēṉ ......... potumpiṉ' eṉṟamiyāl, iravukkuṟi vilakkip pakaṟkuṟi uṭaṉpaṭṭāḷ pōlak kūṟiṉum, tēṉ aḻikka varuvārālum, paḻameṭukka varuvārālum, pūppaṟikka varuvārālum pakaṟkuṟiyum aritām eṉṟu kuṟippāl maṟuttāḷām.
(mē-ḷ) `poḻutum āṟum'1 eṉṉum cūttirattu, `avaṉūṟañcal' eṉṟataṟku, `oru nāḷ viḻumamuṟiṉum vaḻināḷ, vāḻkuvaḷallaḷ eṉṟōḻi' eṉṉum pakutiyi eṭuttuk kāṭṭiṉar. nac.
19. pāli
(neñciṉāṟ poruḷ valikkappaṭṭup pirinta talimakaṉ talimakaḷ 
nalaṉ nayantu uḷḷiya neñciṉik kaḻaṟiyatu.)
		aṉṟavaṇ oḻintaṉṟum iliyē vantunaṉi
		varuntiṉi vāḻiyeṉ neñcē paruntirun
		tuyāviḷi payiṟṟum yāvuyar naṉantali
		uruḷtuṭi makuḷiyiṉ poruḷterin ticikkuṅ
	5.	kaṭuṅkuraṟ kuṭiñiya neṭumperuṅ kuṉṟam
		emmo ṭiṟattalum cellāy piṉṉiṉ
		ṟoḻiyac cūḻntaṉi yāyiṉ tavirātu
		celliṉic ciṟakkaniṉ ṉuḷḷam vallē
		maṟaval ōmpumati emmē naṟaviṉ
	10.	cēyitaḻ aṉiya vākik kuvaḷi
		māyitaḻ puriyum malirkoḷ īrimi
		uḷḷakaṅ kaṉala uḷḷuto ṟulaṟip
		paḻaṅkaṇ koṇṭa1 kataḻntuvīḻ aviraṟal
		veyya ukutara verīip piyeṉac
	15.	cilvaḷi corinta melliṟi muṉki
		pūvī koṭiyiṟ pulleṉap pōki
		aṭarcey āyakal cuṭartuṇi yāka
		iyaṅkātu vatintanaṅ kātali
		uyaṅkucāy ciṟupuṟam muyaṅkiya piṉṉē.
- poruntil iḷaṅkīraṉār.
(co-ḷ) 1-2. eṉ neñcē vāḻi -, aṉṟu avaṇ oḻin taṉṟum ili - nām puṟappaṭum aṉṟu yāṉ vārēṉ eṉṟu kūṟi nam maṉiyil taṅkiviṭṭāyu malli, vantu naṉi varuntiṉi - ittuṇiyum vantu mikavum varuttamuṟṟāy.
2-6. paruntu iruntu uyā viḷi payiṟṟum - paruntu taṅki vaḻiccelvōr varuttatti yaṭiyum ōciyip palakālum ceyyum, yā uyar naṉantali - yā maraṅkaḷ uyarnta akaṉṟa iṭaṅkaḷil, uruḷ tuṭi makuḷiyiṉ - (kaṭippu) uruḷkiṉṟa iḻuku paṟiyiṉ ōciyippōla, poruḷ terintu icikkum - poruḷ teriya olikkum, kaṭuṅkural kuṭiñiya - kaṭiya kurali yuṭiya āntikaḷiyuṭiya, neṭum peruṅ kuṉṟam - nīṇṭa periya kuṉṟatti, emmoṭu iṟattalum cellāy - emmoṭu toṭarntu kaṭattalum ceyyāy;
6-8. piṉ niṉṟu oḻiyac cūḻntaṉi yāyiṉ - piṉṉē niṉṟu oḻiyak karutiṉiyāyiṉ, tavirātu cel iṉi vallē - taṭiyiṉṟi ippoḻutē virintu celvāyāka, ciṟakka niṉ uḷḷam - niṉ uḷḷatteṇṇam ciṟappatāka;
9-19. kuvaḷi mā itaḻ puriyum - muṉ kuvaḷiyiṉatu kariya itaḻi yokkum, malirkoḷ īr imi - nīr mikutal koṇṭa kuḷirnta kaṇkaḷiṉ imi, naṟaviṉ cēy itaḻ aṉiyavāki - piṉpu naṟavampūviṉ civanta itaḻ aṉiyavāki, uḷḷakam kaṉala - uḷḷam kotittalālē, uḷḷutōṟu ulaṟi - niṉiyuntoṟum vaṟṟi, paḻaṅkaṇ koṇṭa kataḻntu vīḻ avir aṟal - tuṉpaṅ koḷaṟku ētuvāṉa virintu viḻum viḷaṅkum aṟalnīr, veyya ukutara - veppaṅ koṇṭavāyc coriya, verīi - añci, cil vaḷi corinta mel iṟi muṉki - cila vaḷikaḷum coriyappeṟṟa melliya cantiṉiyuṭiya muṉkiyuṭiyāḷum, pū vī koṭiyiṟ pulleṉap piyeṉap pōki - pūkkaḷ oḻiyappeṟṟa koṭipōlap polivaṟa melleṉac ceṉṟu, aṭar cey āy akal cuṭar tuṇiyāka - poṟṟakaṭṭā ṉiyaṉṟa aḻakiya akaliṭattut tāṉ ēṟṟiya viḷakkē tuṇiyāka, iyaṅkātu vatinta nam kātali -eṅkaṇum iṟaṅka valiyaṟṟu ōriṭattē taṅkiyuḷḷavaḷumākiya nam kātaliyiṉ, uyaṅku cāy ciṟupuṟam muyaṅkiya piṉ - varunti melinta piṭariṉit taḻuviya piṉ;
9. maṟaval ōmpumati emmē - emmi maṟattali nīkkuvāyāka.
(muṭipu) neñcē! aṉṟu oḻintaṉṟumili; vantu naṉi varuntiṉi; kuṉṟam emmōṭiṟattalum cellāy; oḻiyac cūḻntaṉiyāyiṉ cel iṉi; niṉ uḷḷam ciṟakka; nam kātali ciṟupuṟam muyaṅkiya piṉṉē emmi maṟaval ōmpumati.
(vi-ri) makuḷi - ōci. makuḷiyiṉ eṉa oppu urittatu ōci māttiram karuti; poruḷ terital kuṭiñi ōcikkē uḷḷatu. terintu - teriya eṉat tirikka. poruḷ teriya icittal, kuttipputi eṉpatōr poruḷ teriya icittal eṉpar. iṉi, poruḷ - pōvār kāriyamumām; āntiyiṉ kural pōvār kāriyatti uṇarttum nimittamātaliṉ. uḷḷam - neñciṉatu uḷḷam. naṟaviṉ cēyitaḻic cevvarikku uvamiyākavum, kuvaḷiyik kaṇ vaṭiviṟku uvamiyākavum kūṟuvārum uḷar. koṇṭa - koḷḷutaṟku ētuvāṉa. kuṟippurikārar virintu viḻukiṉṟa eṉap poruḷ ceytiruttaliṉ `kataḻntu vīḻ' eṉpatē avar koṇṭa pāṭamākum `kaliḻntu' eṉum pāṭattiṟkuk `kalaṅki' eṉka. aṟal - aṟṟu viḻukiṉṟa nīr. verīi - piṟar kāṇpareṉa añci. vaḷi palavum muṉpē kaḻaṉṟoḻintaṉa eṉpāṉ cilvaḷi corinta eṉṟāṉ. muṉkiyiyuṭiya kātali, vatinta kātali eṉak kūṭṭuka. maṟiya niṉṟu piṉṉē taḻuvutalāṉ, ciṟupuṟam muyaṅkiya eṉṟāṉ. muyaṅkiyapiṉ eṉṟatu muyaṅkuka eṉak kūṟiyavāṟu māyiṟṟu.
(mē-ḷ) `puṇartal pirital'1 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, ic ceyyuḷik kāṭṭi, `itu maṟavalōmpumati yeṉap pirivu kūṟiṟṟu' eṉṟum,  `nōyu miṉpamum'2, eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, `kikaviyāc ceṉṟu.... neñcē' eṉpatu, neñciṉi uṟuppuṭiyatupōl uvanturittatu eṉṟum, `varuntiṉi vāḻiyeṉ neñcē.... ciṟakka niṉṉuḷḷam' eṉpatu, neñciṉi aṟivuṭiyatupōla aḻuki paṟṟik kūṟiyatu eṉṟum kūṟiṉar, nac.

20. neytal
(pakaṟkuṟi vanta talimakaṉ ciṟippuṟattāṉākat tōḻi talimakaṭkuc colluvāḷāyt talimakaṉ kēṭpac colliyatu.)
		perunīr aḻuvat tenti tanta
		koḻumīṉ uṇaṅkal paṭupuḷ ōppi
		ekkarp paṉṉi iṉṉiḻal aciic
		cekkar ñeṇṭiṉ kuṇṭaḷi keṇṭi
	5.	ñāḻal ōṅkuciṉit toṭutta koṭuṅkaḻit
		tāḻi vīḻkayiṟ ṟūcal tūṅkik
		koṇṭal iṭumaṇal kuravi muṉiyiṉ
		veṇṭalip puṇari āyamo ṭāṭi
		maṇippūm pintaḻi tii yaṇittakap
	10.	palpūṅ kāṉal alkiṉam varutal
		kavvi nallaṇaṅ kuṟṟa ivvūrk
		kaṭikoṇ ṭaṉaḷē tōḻi peruntuṟi
		elliyum iravum eṉṉātu kalleṉa
	15.	valavaṉ āynta vaṇpari
		nilavumaṇal koṭkumōr tēruṇ ṭeṉavē.
- 1 ulōccaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 13. tōḻi -,
1-10. ekkarp puṉṉi iṉniḻal acii - kaṭaṟkarikkaṇ maṇal mēṭṭiluḷḷa puṉṉiyiṉ iṉiya niḻalil taṅki, perunīr aḻuvattu - kaṭaṟparappiṉiṉṟu, enti tanta koḻumīṉ uṇaṅkal paṭupuḷ ōppi - nam tanti tanta koḻumiyākiya mīṉiṉ vaṟṟalik kavaravarum paṟavikaḷi akaṟṟiyum, cekkar ñeṇṭiṉ kuṇṭu aḷi keṇṭi - civanta naṇṭiṉ āḻamāṉa vaḷikaḷit tōṇṭiyum, koṭuṅkaḻi tāḻi vīḻkayiṟṟu ñāḻal ōṅku ciṉit toṭutta ūcal tūṅki - vaḷinta kaḻiyiluḷḷa tāḻi viḻutālāya kayiṟṟāl pulinakak koṉṟiyiṉ uyarnta kiḷiyil kaṭṭittoṅkaviṭṭa ūcalil āṭiyum, koṇṭal iṭumaṇal kuravi muṉiyiṉ - kīḻkkāṟṟuk kuvitta maṇalil kuraviyāṭi (yum ivi) veṟuppiṉ, veṇtalip puṇari āyamoṭu āṭi - veḷḷiya taliyiyuṭiya kaṭal nīril āyattārōṭu viḷiyāṭi, maṇi pū pintaḻi aṇittaka tii - aḻakiya pūkkaḷōṭu mēviya paciya taḻiyuṭiyiṉi aḻaku porunta uṭuttik koṇṭum, pal pū kāṉal alkiṉam varutal - (iṅṅaṉa mellām) pala pūkkaḷiyum uṭiya kaṭaṟkaric cōliyil nām talivaroṭu taṅki varutalippaṟṟi;
11-16. ivvūr - ivvūril, kavvi nal aṇaṅku uṟṟa - alar kūṟalākiya naṟpēy piṭittirukkum, koṭitu aṟi peṇṭir coṟ koṇṭu - koṭumi kūṟalē aṟiyum peṇṭir kūṟum collik kēṭṭu, aṉṉi - nam aṉṉi, perum tuṟi - periya tuṟiyakattu, elliyum iravum eṉṉātu - pakalum iravum eṉṟillātu, valavaṉ āynta vaṇpari - pākaṉ āyntu koṇṭa aḻakumikka kutiri pūṭṭappeṟṟu, nilavu maṇal kal eṉa koṭkum ōr tēr uṇṭu eṉavē - nilāppōṉṟa maṇalil kalleṉṉum oliyuṭaṉ cuḻaṉṟu tiriyum oru tēr uṇṭu eṉṟu eṇṇi, kaṭikoṇṭaṉaḷ - illiṉ kaṇ kāvaṟpaṭuttiṉaḷ; (eṉ ceyvām)
(muṭipu) tōḻi, nām palpūṅkāṉal talivaṉoṭu acii, oppik, keṇṭit, tūṅki, āṭi, muṉiyiṉ, tii, alkiṉam varutalip paṟṟi aṉṉi peṇṭir kūṟum coṟkoṇṭu peruntuṟiyiṭattuk koṭkum tēr uṇṭu eṉavē nammik kaṭikoṇṭaṉaḷ; (eṉ ceyvām.)
(vi-ri) koḻumīṉ - oruvakimīṉumām. kuraviyi veṟuppiṉ puṇari yāṭiyum eṉṟumām. aṇaṅku - īṇṭup pēy; nal aṇaṅku eṉṟatu ikaḻccik kuṟippu.
(mē-ḷ) `maṟintavaṟ kāṇṭal'1 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, piṟar kūṟṟāl tamar taṟkāttamikku ic ceyyuḷiyum, `tērum yāṉiyum'2 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, talivaṉ tērūrntu varutaṟku, `nilavumaṇaṟ koṭkumōr tēruṇ ṭeṉavē' eṉpataṉiyum eṭuttuk kāṭṭiṉar, nac.

21. pāli
(poruḷ valikkappaṭṭup pirinta talimakaṉ iṭiccurattu 
niṉṟu mīḷaluṟṟa neñciṉik kaḻaṟiyatu.)
		maṉiyiḷa nocci muval vāṉmukit
		tuṇinirit taṉṉa māvīḻ veṇpal
		avvayiṟ ṟakaṉṟa alkul tiit
		tāḻmeṉ kūntal taṭameṉ paṇittōḷ
	5.	maṭanti māṇalam pulampac cēynāṭṭuc
		cellal oṉṟiyāṉ collavum ollāy
		viṉinayan tamintaṉi āyiṉ maṉinakap
		palvēṟu veṟukki tarukam vallē
		eḻuviṉi vāḻiyeṉ neñcē puriyiṇar
	10.	mellaviḻ añciṉi pulampa vallōṉ
		kōṭaṟi kompiṉ vīyukat tīṇṭi
		marāa malitta maṇavāyt teṉṟal
		curañcel maḷḷar curiyal tūṟṟum
		eṉṟūḻ niṉṟa puṉtali vippiṟ
	15.	paruntiḷip paṭūum pāṟutali ōmi
		iruṅkal viṭarakat tīṉṟiḷip paṭṭa
		meṉpuṉiṟ ṟampiṇavu pacitteṉap piṅkaṇ
		cennāy ēṟṟi kēḻal tākka
		iriyaṟ piṇaval tīṇṭaliṉ parīic
	20.	ceṅkāy utirnta piṅkuli īntiṉ
		paralmaṇ cuvala muraṇilam uṭitta
		valvāyk kaṇiccik kūḻār kūvalar
		ūṟā tiṭṭa uvalik kūval
		veṇkōṭu nayanta aṉpil kāṉavar
	25.	ikaḻntiyaṅ kiyaviṉ akaḻnta kuḻicet
		tiruṅkaḷiṟ ṟiṉaniri tūrkkum
		peruṅkal attam vilaṅkiya kāṭē.
- kāvaṉ mullip pūtaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 1-6. maṉi iḷa nocci - maṉip paṭappiyil uḷḷa iḷiya nocciyiṟ paṭarnta, mā vīḻ muval vālmuki - vaṇṭukaḷ virumpum mulliyiṉ veḷḷiya arumpukaḷil, tuṇi nirittaṉṉa - ottaṉavaṟṟi nirittu vittālotta, veṇpal - veḷḷiya paṟkaḷiyum, avvayiṟṟu - aḻakiya vayiṟṟiṉiyum, akaṉṟa alkul - paranta alkuliṉiyum, tii tāḻ mel kūntal - oppaṉi ceyyappeṟṟut tāḻntu toṅkum melliya kūntaliṉiyum, taṭamel paṇit tōḷ - periya meṉmi vāynta mūṅkiliyotta tōḷiṉiyum (uṭiya), maṭanti māṇ nalam pulampa - nam taliviyiṉ māṇpuṟum aḻaku keṭa, cēy nāṭṭuc cellal eṉṟu - cēymik kaṇṇatākiya nāṭṭiṟkuc cellātē eṉṟu, yāṉ collavum ollāy - avviṭattē yāṉ kūṟavum ataṟkup poruntāyāy;
7-9. viṉi nayantu amintaṉi yāyiṉ - av viṉiyi virumpi amintu pōntā yākaliṉ, maṉi naka palvēṟu veṟukki tarukam - nam talivi makiḻap palavēṟu vakiyiṉavāya celvaṅkaḷi īṭṭit taruvōm;
9-14. puri iṇar mel aviḻ am ciṉi pulampa - valam curinta pūṅkottukkaḷ melleṉa malarkiṉṟa aḻakiya kompukaḷ ap pūkkaḷi yiḻantu taṉimiyuṟa, vallōṉ kōṭu aṟi kompiṉ - pūkkoḷḷa vallōṉ marak kompiṉi aṭittu acikkum kōl pōla, marāam vī ukat tīṇṭi alitta maṇavāyt teṉṟal - marāmaratti malarkaḷ utirat tākki alikkum maṇattit taṉṉiṭattuṭiya teṉṟal, curam cel maḷḷar curiyal tūṟṟum - curaneṟiyiṟ cellum vīrarkaḷatu kuḻaṉṟa mayiril ammalarkaḷic coriyum, eṉṟūḻ niṉṟa puṉtali vippil - vemmi nilipeṟṟa pulliya iṭattiyuṭiya ūrkaḷil;
15-23. paruntu iḷippaṭum pāṟu tali ōmi - paruntu īṉṟu kāvaṟpaṭum citaṟiya taliyiṉavāya ōmi maraṅkaḷiyuṭiya, iruṅkal viṭarakattu - periya maliyiṉ muḻiñciṉiṭattu, īṉṟu iḷippaṭṭa - īṉṟu kāvaṟpaṭṭa, meṉ puṉiṟṟu am piṇavu pacitteṉa - meṉmiyum īṉṟa aṇmiyum uṭiya aḻakiya peṇ nāy pacittatāka, piṅkaṭ cennāy ēṟṟi - paciya kaṇkaḷiyuṭiya cennāy ēṟu, kēḻal tākka - āṇ paṉṟiyiṉit tākka, iriyal piṇaval tīṇṭaliṉ - atu kaṇṭu añciyōṭum peṇpaṉṟi mōtic cellutaliṉ, piṅkuli īntiṉ parīi utirnta ceṅkāy paral - paciya kulikaḷiyuṭiya īntiṉiṉṟum aṟupaṭṭu utirnta ceṅkāykaḷiṉ vittukkaḷuṭaṉ kūṭiya, maṇ cuvala muraṇ nilam - maṇmēṭākiya vaṉṉilatti, uṭitta - uṭittiṭṭa, val vāyk kaṇicci - valiya vāyiṉiyuṭiya kuntāliyiyuṭiya, kūḻ ār kūvalar - kūḻiṉi yuṇṇum kiṇaṟu veṭṭuvōr, ūṟātu iṭṭa uvalik kūval - akaḻntu pārttum nīr ūṟāmiyiṉ viṭṭēkiya taḻi mūṭiya kiṇaṟukaḷi;
24-27. iruṅ kaḷiṟṟu iṉaniri - periya kaḷiṟṟiṉamākiya kūṭṭam, veṇkōṭu nayanta aṉpuil kāṉavar - tamatu veḷḷiya kompiṉik kavara virumpiya irakkamaṟṟa vēṭarkaḷ, ikaḻntu iyaṅku iyaviṉ - tīṅkilli eṉṟu niṉittuk karuttiṉṟic cellum neṟikaḷil, akaḻnta kuḻi cettu - taṅkaḷi akappaṭutta akaḻntu maṟitta kuḻikaḷākak karuti, tūrkkum - avaṟṟit tūrkkum, peruṅkal attam vilaṅkiya kāṭu - periya kaṟkaḷiyuṭiya neṟikaḷ kuṟukkiṭum ikkāṭṭiṟ cēṟaṟku (īṇṭu niṉṟum mīḷātu);
9. iṉi vallē eḻu - ippoḻutu virintu eḻuntu eṉ piṉṉē varuvāyāka.
(muṭipu) neñcē! maṭanti nalam pulampa, cēy nāṭṭuc cellal eṉṟu yāṉ collavum, ollātu amintu pōntaṉiyākaliṉ, maṉi naka veṟukki tarukam; īṇṭu niṉṟum mīḷātu, kāṭṭiṟ cēṟaṟku ippoḻutu eḻuvāyāka.
teṉṟal tūṟṟum vippiṉ kāṭu eṉavum, uvalik kūvalik kaḷiṟṟiṉa niri tūrkkum kāṭu eṉavum iyiyum.
(vi-ri) piṇavu pacitteṉac cennāyēṟṟi, kēḻal tākkap piṇaval tīṇṭaliṉ parīi utirnta īntiṉ paraliyuṭiya maṭcuvalākiya vaṉṉilattil kūvalar uṭitta kūval eṉka.
maṉip paṭappiyil nocciyiṉkaṇ muval paṭarntiruttal, `mayilaṭi yiliya mākkural nocci, maṉinaṭu muvalō ṭūḻmuki yaviḻa' eṉṉum naṟṟiṇic (115) ceyyuḷāṉum, `maṉiyiḷa nocci muval vāṉmuki'(21) `maṉiya, tāḻvi ṉocci cūḻvaṉa malarum, muval mācciṉi(23)' eṉṉum innūṟ ceyyuṭkaḷāṉum aṟiyappaṭum. amintaṉi yāyiṉ - amintu pōntaṉiyāyiṉ eṉa virikka. maṉi naka - illam poliya eṉṟumām. kōṭu ali kompu - pū utirkkum kompu; atu teṉṟalukku uvami. curiyal - curuṇṭa talimayir. īṉṟaṇimipaṟṟip puṟattē cellalā kāmiyiṉ īriṭattum iḷippaṭṭa eṉṟār.
`paṉṟi pulvāy nāyeṉa mūṉṟum, oṉṟiya veṉpa piṇaveṉ peyarkkoṭi',1 `piṇava leṉiṉu mavaṟṟiṉ mēṟṟē'2 eṉṉum cūttiraṅkaḷāl, `piṇavu' eṉavum `piṇaval' eṉavum vantaṉa. kaḷiṟu tām ikaḻntu iyaṅku iyavil kāṉavar akaḻnta kuḻi eṉka.

22. kuṟiñci
(1. variviṭi vittup pirinta kālattut talimakaḷ āṟṟāḷākat tōḻi talimakaṉi iyaṟpaḻippat talimakaḷ iyaṟpaṭa moḻintatu.
2. talimakaṉ iravukkuṟi vantu ciṟippuṟattāṉākat tōḻiyāṟ colleṭukkappaṭṭut talimakaḷ colliyatūu mām.)
		aṇaṅkuṭi neṭuvari yucciyiṉ iḻitaruṅ
		kaṇaṅkoḷ aruvik kāṉkeḻu nāṭaṉ
		maṇaṅkamaḻ viyaṉmār paṇaṅkiya cellal
		ituveṉa aṟiyā maṟuvaraṟ poḻutil
	5.	paṭiyōrt tēytta palpukaḻt taṭakki
		neṭuvēṭ pēṇat taṇikuvaḷ ivaḷeṉa
		mutuvāyp peṇṭir atuvāy kūṟak
		kaḷanaṉ kiḻittuk kaṇṇi cūṭṭi
		vaḷanakar cilampap pāṭip palikoṭut
	10.	turuvac centiṉi kurutiyoṭu tūuy
		murukāṟṟup paṭutta urukeḻu naṭunāḷ
		āra nāṟa aruviṭar tatinta
		cāraṟ palpū vaṇṭupaṭac cūṭik
		kaḷiṟṟiri terīiya pārval otukkiṉ
	15.	oḷittiyaṅku marapiṉ vayappuli pōla
		naṉmaṉi neṭunakark kāvalar aṟiyāmit
		taṉṉaci uḷḷattu nannaci vāyppa
		iṉṉuyir kuḻiya muyaṅkutoṟum meymmalintu
		nakkaṉeṉ allaṉō yāṉē eytta
	20.	nōytaṇi kātalar varavīṇ
		ṭētil vēlaṟ kulantami kaṇṭē.
- veṟipāṭiya kāmak kaṇṇiyār.
(co-ḷ) 1-4. aṇaṅkuṭi neṭuvari ucciyiṉ iḻitarum kaṇam koḷ aruvik kāṉ keḻu nāṭaṉ - teyvamuṭiya nīṇṭa maliyucciyiṉiṉṟu viḻum kūṭṭamāya aruvikaḷiyuṭiya kāṭu poruntiya nāṭṭiyuṭiya nam talivaṉatu, maṇam kamaḻ viyaṉ mārpu aṇaṅkiya cellal itu eṉa aṟiyā maṟuvaral poḻutil - maṇam kamaḻum akaṉṟa mārpu varuntiya varuttatti itaṉāluṇṭāyateṉṟu aṟiyāta kalakkamuṟṟa kālattē;
5-11. paṭiyōrt tēytta palpukaḻ taṭakki neṭuvēḷ pēṇat taṇikuvaḷ ivaḷ eṉa - vaṇaṅkārit tēyttoḻitta palavakip pukaḻkaḷik koṇṭa periya kiyiṉiyuṭiya neṭuvēḷip pōṟṟiṉ ivaḷ tuyar taṇiyap peṟukuvaḷ eṉṟu, mutuvāyp peṇṭir atu vāy kūṟa - aṟivu vāyttaliyuṭiya peṇṭir ataṉi meyyākak kūṟa, kaḷam naṉku iḻittuk kaṇṇi cūṭṭi - veṟiyāṭuṅ kaḷam naṉku amittu (vēliṟkuk) kaṇṇi cūṭṭi, vaḷanakar cilampap pāṭip palikoṭuttu - vaḷam poruntiya kōyilil oliyuṇṭākap pāṭip palikoṭuttu, uruvac centiṉi kurutiyoṭu tūuy - aḻakiya centiṉiyik kurutiyuṭaṉ kalantu tūvi, muruku āṟṟuppaṭutta urukeḻu naṭunāḷ - murukaṉi varavaḻitta accam poruntiya naṭu iravil;
12-21. āram nāṟa - cantaṉam maṇam vīca, cāral aruviṭarttatinta palpū vaṇṭupaṭac cūṭi - pakka maliyil uḷḷa ariya muḻiñcukaḷiṟ ceṟinta pala pūkkaḷi vaṇṭu moyttiṭac cūṭiyum, kaḷiṟṟu iri terīiya pārval otukkiṉ oḷittu iyaṅkum marapiṉ vayap pulipōla - kaḷiṟākiya iriyit teritaṟ poruṭṭu otuṅkiya pārviyoṭu maṟintu iyaṅkum iyalpiṉi
yuṭiya valiya puliyiṉip pōla, nalmaṉi neṭunakark kāvalar aṟiyāmi - namatu nalla maṉiyiṉ kaṇṇuḷḷa neṭiya illiṉ kāvalāḷarum aṟiyāvaṇṇam, eytta nōytaṇi kātalar vara - nām melitaṟku ētuvāya in nōyit taṇittaṟkuriya nam kātalar vantu, taṉ naci nam uḷḷattu naci vāyppa - taṉṉi naccutaliyuṭiya nam uḷḷattiṉ viruppam niṟivēṟumpaṭi, iṉ uyir kuḻiya muyaṅkutoṟum - iṉiya uyir kuḻiyumpaṭi muyaṅkuntōṟum, īṇṭu ētil vēlaṟku ulantami kaṇṭu - iṅku namar yātum iyipillāta vēlaṉukku aḻintami kaṇṭu, yāṉ meymalintu nakkaṉeṉ allaṉō - yāṉ uṭal pūrittuc cirittēṉ allaṉō?
(muṭipu) nāṭaṉ mārpu aṇaṅkiya cellal aṟiyāp poḻutil, neṭuvēṭ pēṇat taṇikuvaḷ ivaḷeṉa mutuvāyp peṇṭir kūṟa, murukāṟṟup paṭutta naṭunāḷ, vayappulipōlak kātalar vara, naci vāyppa muyaṅkutoṟum vēlaṟ kulantami kaṇṭu, nakkaṉe ṉallaṉō?
(vi-ri) maṟuvaral - cuḻaṟci. paṭiyār - vaṇaṅkār; ā ōvāyiṟṟu, piratiyōr eṉṉum colliṉ citivumām. palpukaḻ - veṉṟi koṭi mutalāyavaṟṟāl varum pukaḻ. mutuvāy: mutu- aṟivu. atu vāyāka : vāy - uṇmi. murukaṉi āṟṟuppaṭuttal, `kurutic centiṉi parappik kuṟamakaḷ, murukiya niṟuttu muraṇiṉa ruṭka, murukāṟṟup paṭutta vurukeḻu viyaṉakar'1 eṉa ivvāṟē tiru murukāṟṟuppaṭiyilum varutal kāṇka. puli kaḷiṟṟiriyiṉip peṟutaṟkup patuṅki maṟintu varutal pōlat talivaṉ taliviyip peṟutaṟku maṟintu vantāṉ eṉka.
1. veṟiyeṭuttut tīrāta vaḻiyil aḵtiyārālē vanta teṉṟayirātapaṭi variyavanta pērutaviyiṉārip pollāṅku collak kaṭaviyō eṉat talivi iyaṟpaṭa moḻintāḷ eṉka.
2. īṇṭum talivi iyaṟpaṭa moḻintāḷāyiṉum veṟiyāṭṭu nikaḻcciyit talivaṟku veḷippaṭutti virintu variyat tūṇṭutal payaṉākum. veṟiyāṭṭiṉi aḻakupeṟak kūṟum icceyyuḷip pāṭiya ciṟappuppaṟṟi ivar veṟipāṭiya kāmak kaṇṇiyār eṉa vaḻaṅkappaṭṭār.
(mē-ḷ) 2`maṟintavaṟ kāṇṭal' eṉṉum talivi kūṟṟu nikaḻttumāṟu kūṟuñ cūttirattē `paḻitīr muṟuval ciṟitē tōṟṟal' eṉpataṟku ic ceyyuḷi eṭuttukkāṭṭi itaṉuṭ paḻitīra avaṉ vantu uyir taḷirppa muyaṅka, nakkaniliyit tōḻikkut talivi kūṟiyavāṟu kāṇka' eṉpar, nac.
`piṟappē kuṭimi'1 eṉṉum cūttirattu, `taṉṉaci yuḷḷattu, nannaci vāyppa' eṉpatu `iruvar uṇarvum ottavāṟu' eṉavum, `viṉipayaṉ meyyuru'2 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu `kaḷiṟṟiri........ vayappuli pōla' eṉpuḻi, pārva lotukkamākiya viṉi paṇpeṉappaṭātu. eṉṉi? paṇpeṉpatu kuṟippiṉṟi nikaḻum kuṇamākaliṉ eṉavum, pārvalotukka meṉappaṭṭa viṉippakutiyāṟ piḻiyāmaṟ kōṭaṟkup pārvalotukkiṉiṉṟā ṉeṉpatum piṟarkkañcip pārvalotuṅkukiṉṟāṉalla ṉeṉpatuñ colli, avaṉ talimikkēṟṟa uvamiyātaliṉ, atu poruṭṭōṟṟamāyiṟṟu eṉavum, kūṟiṉar, pērā.
23. pāli
(talimakaṉ piriviṉkaṇ talimakaḷ tōḻikkuc colliyatu.)
		maṇkaṇ kuḷirppa vīcit taṇpeyal
		pāṭulan taṉṟē paṟikkural eḻili
		putaṉmicit taḷaviṉ itaṉmuṭ cennaṉi
		neruṅkukulip piṭavamoṭu oruṅkupiṇi aviḻak
	5.	kāṭē kammeṉ ṟaṉṟē yavala
		kōṭuṭin taṉṉa kōṭaṟ pimpayirp
		pataviṉ pāvi muṉii matavunaṭi
		aṇṇal irali amarpiṇi taḻīit
		taṇṇaṟal parukit tāḻntupaṭ ṭaṉavē
	10.	aṉiyakol vāḻi tōḻi maṉiya
		tāḻviṉ nocci cūḻvaṉa malarum
		muval mācciṉi kāṭṭi
		avvaḷa veṉṟār āṇṭuccey poruḷē.
- ōrōṭōkattuk kantarattaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 10.  tōḻi vāḻi -,
1-2. paṟi kural eḻili - muḻaviṉ kural pōṉṟa oliyiṉiyuṭiya mēkam, maṇkaṇ kuḷirppa - maṇṇiṉiṭamellām kuḷira, taṇ peyal vīci - kuḷirnta maḻiyip peytu, pāṭu ulantaṉṟē - oli yaṭaṅkiṟṟu;
3-5. putal mici taḷaviṉ ital muḷ cen naṉi - putariṉ mītu paṭarnta cem mulliyiṉatu cival muḷḷip pōṉṟa civanta arumpu, neruṅku kuli piṭavamoṭu oruṅku piṇi aviḻa - neruṅkiya kuliyiṉiyuṭiya piṭāviṉ arumpuṭaṉ oruṅku piṇippu virintu malara, kāṭu kam eṉṟaṉṟē - kāṭu kammeṉṟu maṇam vīcukiṉṟatu;
5-9. avala kōṭu uṭintaṉṉa kōṭal pim payir - paḷḷaṅkaḷiluḷḷa caṅku uṭintāṟpōṉṟa veṇkōṭalatu paciya payirōṭu, pataviṉ pāvi muṉii - aṟukaṅ kiḻaṅkit tiṉṟu teviṭṭutaliṉ veṟuttu, matavu naṭi aṇṇal irali amar piṇi taḻīi - cerukkiya naṭiyiṉiyum talimiyiṉiyu muṭiya āṇmāṉ virumpiya peṇmāṉit taḻuvi, taṇ aṟal parukit tāḻntupaṭṭaṉavē - kuḷirnta nīrip paruki ōriṭattē cērntu taṅkiviṭṭaṉa;
10-13. maṉiya tāḻviṉ nocci cūḻvaṉa malarum muval mācciṉi kāṭṭi - maṉiyiṭattuḷḷa mulli cūḻntu malarum iṭamākiya kuṟiya nocciyatu kariya ciṉiyik kāṭṭi, āṇṭuc ceyporuḷ - tām vēṟṟu nāṭṭiruntu poruḷ īṭṭuṅ kālat telli, a aḷavu eṉṟār - am muval pūkkat toṭaṅkum akkālat telliyē eṉṟāraṉṟē, aṉiya kol - ikkālam avvaḷaviṟṟō, ataṉit tāṇṭi niṟpatoṉṟō kūṟuvāyāka!
(muṭipu) tōḻi vāḻi, eḻili maṇ kuḷirppap peyal vīcip pāṭu ulantaṉṟu; taḷaviṉ naṉi piṭavamoṭu piṇiya viḻak kāṭu kammeṉṟaṉṟu; irali piṇi taḻīit taṇṇaṟal parukit tāḻntu paṭṭaṉa; muval cūḻvaṉa malarum nocci mācciṉi kāṭṭi, āṇṭuc ceyporuḷ avvaḷaveṉṟār; aṉiyakol!
(vi-ri) ital - cival; kavutāriyumām. kam - maṇaṅkamaḻ kuṟippu. aṟuku, iñci mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ kiḻaṅkip pāvi eṉṟal marapu; `ceyyāp pāvi'1 eṉpuḻi, iñcik kiḻaṅkip pāvi eṉṟal marapeṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kūṟiyatuṅ kāṇka. kōṭaṟ pimpayir - kōṭalik kaḷiyākavuṭiya varaku mutaliya payirkaḷumām. aṉiya kol eṉpataṟku (talivaṉ kuṟitta kālaṅ kaṭantami kūṟuvāḷāy) poruḷ aṅṅaṉam ciṟantatoṉṟō eṉṟāḷ eṉalumām.
(mē-ḷ) `ēṉip pirivum avvayi ṉiliyum'2 eṉṉum cūttirattu, ic ceyyuḷi eṭuttukkāṭṭi, ituporuṭpiriviṉ kaṇ kārkuṟittu (varuva leṉṟamiyiṉ) āṟu tiṅkaḷ iṭi yiṭṭatu eṉṟu kūṟiṉar. nac.
24. mulli
(1. talimakaṉ paruvaṅ kaṇṭu colliyatu. 2. viṉimuṟṟum talimakaṉ taṉ neñciṟkuc colliyatūu mām.)
		vēḷāp pārppāṉ vāḷaran tumitta
		vaḷi1kaḷin toḻinta koḻunti ṉaṉṉa
		taḷipiṇi aviḻāc curimukap pakaṉṟi
		citaralan tuvali tūvaliṉ malarun
	5.	tii niṉṟa taṇpeyaṟ kaṭināḷ
		vayaṅkukatir karanta vāṭi vikaṟi
		vicumpuri vatupōl viyaliṭat toḻuki
		maṅkul māmaḻi teṉpulam paṭarum
		paṉiyiruṅ kaṅkulun tamiyaḷ nīntit
	10.	tammū rōḷē naṉṉutal yāmē
		kaṭimatiṟ katavam pāytaliṉ toṭipiḷantu
		nutimukam 2maḻukiya maṇṇiveṇ kōṭṭuc
		ciṟukaṇ yāṉi neṭunā oṇmaṇi
		kaḻippiṇik kaṟittōl poḻi 3 kaṇi yutipput
	15.	4taḻaṅkukural muracamoṭu muḻaṅkum yāmattuk
		kaḻittuṟi ceṟiyā vāḷuṭi yeṟuḻttōḷ
		iravut tuyil maṭinta tāṉi
		uravucciṉa vēntaṉ pācaṟi yēmē.
- āvūr mūlaṅ kiḻār.
(co-ḷ) 1-5. vēḷāp pārppāṉ - yākam paṇṇāta ūrppārppāṉ, vāḷ aram tumitta -kūriya arattāl aṟut teṭutta, vaḷikaḷintu oḻinta - vaḷikaḷ pōka eñciya, koḻuntiṉ aṉṉa - caṅkiṉ taliyippōṉṟa, taḷi piṇi aviḻāc curimukap pakaṉṟi - kaṭṭuṇṭa piṇippu viriyāta curinta mukattiṉiyuṭiya pakaṉṟiyiṉ arumpukaḷ, citaral am tuvali tūvaliṉ malarum - citaṟukiṉṟa aḻakiya maḻittuḷikaḷ vīcutalāl malarum (kālamākiya), taṇ peyal niṉṟa tii kaṭināḷ - kuḷirnta peyal niṉṟa tit tiṅkaḷākiya muṉpaṉiyiṉ kaṭi nāḷil;
6. vayaṅku katir karanta vāṭi vikaṟi- eḻukiṉṟa ñāyiṟu maṟintirukkum vāṭiyuṭaṉ kūṭiya viṭiyaṟkāliyil;
7-10. maṅkul mā maḻi - iruṇṭa periya maḻi, vicumpu urivatupōl- vicumpu tōlurivatupōla, viyal iṭattu oḻuki - akaṉṟa vāṉiṭattē yiyaṅki, teṉpulam paṭarum - teṉṟicikkaṇ pōyc cērum, paṉi irum kaṅkulum - paṉiyuṭaṉ kūṭiya kariya kaṅkuliṉ veḷḷattiyum, naṉṉutal tamiyaḷ nīntit tam ūrōḷē - ciṟanta neṟṟiyiṉaḷāya nam talivi taṉiyaḷāy nīntit tamatu ūriṉ kaṇ uḷḷāḷ;
10-18. yāmē - yām, kaṭi matil katavam pāytaliṉ - kāvali yuṭiya matiṟ kataviṉip pāytaliṉāl, toṭipiḷantu nutimukam maḻukiya maṇṇi veṇ kōṭṭuc ciṟukaṇ yāṉi - pūṇ piḷakkappaṭṭuk kūriya muṉi maḻuṅkiya moṭṭiyāṉa veḷḷiya kōṭṭiṉiyum ciṟiya kaṇṇiṉiyumuṭiya yāṉiyiṉ, neṭunā oṇmaṇi - nīṇṭa nāviṉiyuṭiya oḷi poruntiya maṇiyiṉ ōciyum, kaḻippiṇik kaṟittōl poḻikaṇi yutippu - kaḻikaḷuṭaṉ piṇikkappaṭṭa kariya tōlākiya kēṭakattuppoḻiyum ampukaḷ tittalāl eḻum ōciyum, taḻaṅkukural muracamoṭu muḻaṅkum yāmattu - muḻaṅkum ōciyiṉiyuṭiya muracoliyuṭaṉ cērntolikkum naḷḷiravil, kaḻittu uṟiceṟiyā - uṟi yiṉiṉṟum uruvi mīṇṭum uḷḷiṭāta, vāḷ uṭi eṟuḻt tōḷ - vāḷiṉik kōtta valiya tōḷiṉiyuṭiyavaṉum, iravut tuyil maṭinta tāṉi - iravil ayarntu tūṅkum cēṉikaḷik koṇṭavaṉumākiya, uravu ciṉavēntaṉ pācaṟiyēm - mikka ciṉam poruntiya vēntaṉatu pācaṟiyiṉkaṇ uḷḷēm; (eṉ ceyvām.)
(muṭipu) pakaṉṟi tūvaliṉ malarum ti niṉṟa kaṭināḷ vikaṟi maḻi teṉ pulam paṭarum kaṅkulum naṉṉutal tamiyaḷ nīntit tam ūrāḷ; yām, yāṉi maṇiyōciyum kaṇi yutippiṉ ōciyum muracamoṭu muḻaṅkum yāmattut tōḷiṉiyum tāṉiyiyu muṭiya vēntaṉ pācaṟiyēm, (eṉ ceyvām.)
(vi-ri) vēḷāp pārppāṉ vāḷaram tumitta vaḷi eṉṟamiyāl, vēḷvi ceyyāmiyāṉ tāḻnili yaṭinta pārppaṉarkkuc caṅkaṟukki mutaliya toḻilkaḷ uḷaveṉpatu peṟappaṭṭatu. koḻuntu - caṅkiṉ tali; taṇpeyal niṉṟa ti kaṭi nāḷ eṉa māṟuka. kaṅkulum; ummi ciṟappu. `vicumpurivatu pōl' eṉa ivvāciriyar uvamam kūṟiyatu pārāṭṭaṟkuriyatu; iḵtu il poruḷuvami. vāḷuṭi vēntaṉ eṉavum, tōḷiyum tāṉiyiyum uṭiya vēntaṉ eṉavum kūṭṭutalum ām. iravut tuyil maṭinta tāṉi eṉṟatu, palanāḷum tuyiliṉṟip pōr ceytu viṉimuṟṟuṅ kālit tuyiṉṟa tāṉi eṉṟapaṭi. kuṟitta paruvam vantum ceṉṟu taliviyik kūṭātu īṇṭu irukkiṉṟēmē eṉat talivaṉ iraṅkik kūṟiṉāṉām.
(mē-ḷ) `kiḻavi niliyē'1 eṉṉum cūttirattu, talimakaṉ pācaṟikkaṇ kiḻavi niliyurittup pulampal, veṉṟikkālattē viḷaṅkit tōṉṟum eṉpataṟkum, `vēntuṟu toḻilē'2 eṉṉuñ cūttirattup pakivayiṟ pirivu yāṇṭiṉatakam ām eṉpataṟkum icceyyuḷi eṭuttuk kāṭṭiṉar, nac.
25. pāli
(paruvaṅ kaṇṭaḻinta talimakaḷit tōḻi vaṟpuṟuttiyatu.)
		neṭuṅkarik kāṉyāṟṟuk kaṭumpuṉal cāay
		aviraṟal koṇṭa viravumaṇal akaṉtuṟit
		taṇkayam naṇṇiya poḻiltoṟuṅ kāñcip
		pintā taṇinta pōtumali ekkar
	5.	vatuvi nāṟṟam putuvatu kañala
		mānaṉi koḻutiya maṇiniṟa iruṅkuyil
		paṭunā viḷiyā ṉaṭuniṉ ṟalkalum
		urippa pōla ūḻkoḷpu kūva
		iṉaccitar ukutta ilavat tāṅkaṇ
	10.	ciṉippūṅ kōṅkiṉ nuṇtātu pakarnar
		pavaḷac ceppil poṉcorin taṉṉa
		ikaḻunar ikaḻā iḷanā ḷamiyañ
		ceytōr maṉṟa kuṟiyeṉa nīniṉ
		pita luṇkaṇ paṉivār puṟippa
	15.	vārā miyiṉ pulanta neñcamoṭu
		nōval kuṟumakaḷ nōyiyareṉ uyireṉa
		melliya iṉiya kūṟi vallē
		varuvar vāḻi tōḻi porunar
		celcamaṅ kaṭanta vilkeḻu taṭakkip
	20.	potiyiṟ celvaṉ polantērt titiyaṉ
		iṉṉici yiyattiṟ kaṟaṅkum
		kaṉmici aruviya kāṭiṟan tōrē.
- olliyūr tanta pūtap pāṇṭiyaṉ.
(co-ḷ) 18. tōḻi-, vāḻi-,
1-12. neṭuṅkarik kāṉyāṟṟuk kaṭumpuṉal cāay - nīṇṭa kariyiṉik koṇṭa  kāṭṭāṟṟiṉ vēkam mikka nīr aṟṟoḻiya, avir aṟal koṇṭa viravu maṇal akal tuṟi taṇkayam naṇṇiya poḻiltoṟum - viḷaṅkum aṟalān taṉmi koṇṭa viraviya maṇaliyuṭiya akaṉṟa tuṟiyiṭattuḷḷa kuḷirnta maṭukkaḷ poruntiya poḻiliṭaṅkaḷi lellām, kāñcip pacun tātu aṇinta pōtu mali ekkar - kāñci marattiṉatu aḻakiya tātukkaḷik koṇṭa pūkkaḷ mika utirntuḷa maṇal mēṭukaḷ, vatuvi nāṟṟam putuvatu kañala - putiya maṇanāṟṟam mikā niṟka, mānaṉi koḻutiya maṇiniṟa iruṅkuyil - māviṉ tātiṉik kōtiya nīlamaṇi niṟattiṉi yotta kariya kuyil, paṭu nā viḷiyāl naṭu niṉṟu alkalum urippa pōla ūḻkoḷpu kūva - olikkum taṉatu nāviṉ kūvutalāl naṭunilimēvi nāḷtōṟum cila kūṟuvaṉa pōla muṟikoṇṭu kūva, iṉacitar ciṉipū kōṅkiṉ ilavattu āṅkaṇ ukutta nuṇtātu - kūṭṭamākiya vaṇṭukaḷ pūkkaḷ niṟinta kiḷikaḷiyuṭiya kōṅka marattiṉ mītiruntu ilavam pūkkaḷāya avviṭattu utirtta nuṇṇiya tātukkaḷ, pakarnar pavaḷac ceppiṟ poṉ corintaṉṉa - poṉ viṟpōr pavaḷac cimiḻil poṟ poṭiyic corintu vittālaṉṉatāka, ikaḻunar ikaḻā iḷanāḷ amiyam - pirivāl eytum tuyarkaḷip puṟakkaṇittup pirivōrum aṅṅaṉam ikaḻntu piriyavoṇṇāta ivviḷavēṉiṟ kālatti yaṉṟō?
13-16. ceytōr maṉṟa kuṟi eṉa - tām mīṇṭu varuṅkālamāka uṟutiyākak kuṟittal ceytār eṉṟu kūṟi, nī niṉ pital uṇkaṇ paṉi vārpu uṟippa - nī niṉatu nōtaliyuṭiya miyuṇ kaṇkaḷ nīr vaṭintu corintu koṇṭirukka, vārāmiyiṉ pulanta neñcamoṭu nōval - talivar vārāmiyāl veṟutta neñcuṭaṉ varuntaṟka;
18-22. porunar celcamam kaṭanta vil keḻu taṭakki - pōrpuriyum pakivar etirntuvarum pōriṉi veṉṟoḻitta villiṉik koṇṭa periya kiyiṉiyuṭiyāṉum, potiyil celvaṉ polam tēr titiyaṉ - potiyil malikkuriya celvaṉum poṉṉālāya tēriṉiyuṭiyāṉumākiya titiyaṉ eṉpāṉatu, iṉṉici iyattiṟ kaṟaṅkum kalmici aruviya kāṭu iṟantōr - iṉiya veṟṟi muracupōla olikkum maliyucci yiṉiṉṟu vīḻum aruvi
kaḷiyuṭiya kāṭukaḷit tāṇṭip poruḷīṭṭac ceṉṟōrāya nam talivar;
16-18. kuṟumakaḷ nōyiyar eṉ uyireṉa - iḷiyāḷē, (niṉṉi iṅṅaṉam tuyaruṟac ceyta) eṉ uyir varuntuvatāka eṉṟāṟ pōlum, melliya iṉiya kūṟi - meṉmi vāynta iṉiya coṟkaḷik kūṟik koṇṭu, vallē varuvar - virintu vantuṟuvar.
(muṭipu) tōḻi, vāḻi, ikaḻunar ikaḻā iḷanāḷamiyam kuṟi ceytōr eṉa, vārāmiyiṉ pulanta neñcamoṭu kaṇ paṉi vārpu uṟippa nōval; kāṭiṟantōr, kuṟumakaḷ nōyiyar eṉ uyireṉa iṉiya kūṟi vallē varuvar.
(vi-ri) ekkar vatuvi nāṟṟam kañala, kuyil kūva, tātu poṉ corintaṉṉavākaliṉ ikaḻunar ikaḻā iḷanāḷamiyam eṉka. cāay - cāya eṉat tirikka. aṟal - nīr vaṟṟiya kālattu maṇal aṟṟaṟṟiruppatu. iḷavēṉiṟ poḻutilē āṭavarum makaḷirum cōlikaḷiluḷḷa ekkaril iṉpa viḷiyāṭṭu nikaḻttuva rākaliṉ vatuvi nāṟṟam kañala eṉṟār. ūḻ koḷpu kūvutalāvatu pēṭi kūvac cēval kūvutal. kuyil naṭuniṉṟu urippa pōla eṉpataṉi, `ūṭiṉī rellām uruvilāṉ ṟaṉṉāṇi, kūṭumi ṉeṉṟu kuyil cāṟṟa' 1 eṉpataṉāl aṟika. aṭuniṉṟu eṉap pirittu varuttāniṉṟu eṉṟu urittalumām. citar - vaṇṭu. kañala, kūva eṉum eccaṅkaḷ ikaḻāta eṉṉum vēṟu viṉikoṇṭu muṭintaṉa. iḷanāḷeṉṟār iḷavēṉili. kuṟumakaḷ eṉṟatu tōḻi tali makaḷi viḷittatumām. eṉṉuyir nōvatāka veṉṟu nī nōvātē eṉṟurittup piṉ, kāṭiṟantōr eṉṉuyir nōvatāka eṉa melliya iṉiya kūṟi varuvareṉa vēṟaṟuttu urittalumām.


26. marutam
(talimakaṉ tōḻiyi vāyil vēṇṭi, avaḷāl tāṉ vāyil peṟātu āṟṟāmiyē vāyilākap pukkuk 2kūṭiyavaṉ nīkkattukkaṇ pukka tōḻikkut talimakaḷ colliyatu.)
		kūṉmuḷ muḷḷik kuvikulik kaḻaṉṟa 3
		mīṉmuḷ ḷaṉṉa veṇkāl māmalar
		poytal makaḷir viḻavaṇik kūṭṭum
		avvayiṉ naṇṇiya vaḷaṅkē ḻūraṉip 
	5.	pulattal kūṭumō tōḻi alkal
		peruṅkatavu poruta yāṉi maruppiṉ
		irumpucey toṭiyi ṉēra vāki
		mākkaṇ aṭiya mārpakam porunti
		muyaṅkal viṭāal iviyeṉa mayaṅki
	10.	yāṉōm eṉṉavum ollār tāmaṟ
		ṟivipā rāṭṭiya paruvamu muḷavē, iṉiyē,
		putalvaṟ ṟaṭutta pāloṭu taṭiit
		titali aṇinta tēṅkoḷ meṉmuli
		naṟuñcān taṇinta kēḻkiḷar akalam 4
	15.	vīṅka muyaṅkal yāmvēṇ ṭiṉamē
		tīmpāl paṭutal tāmañ ciṉarē, āyiṭik
		kavavukki nekiḻntami pōṟṟi matavunaṭic
		cevili kiyeṉ putalvaṉi nōkki
		nallōrk kottaṉir nīyir iḵtō
	20.	celvaṟ kottaṉam yāmeṉa mellaveṉ
		makaṉvayiṟ peyartan tēṉē atukaṇ
		ṭiyāmuṅ kātalam avaṟkeṉac cāayc
		ciṟupuṟaṅ kaviyiṉa ṉāka uṟupeyal 1
		taṇtuḷik kēṟṟa palavuḻu ceñcey
	25.	maṇpōl nekiḻntavaṟ kaluḻntē
		neñcaṟi pōkiya aṟivi ṉēṟkē.
-pāṇṭiyaṉ kāṉap pēreyil tanta ukkirap peruvaḻuti.
(co-ḷ) 6-11.  tām - nam talivartām, peruṅ katavu poruta yāṉi maruppiṉ irumpu cey toṭiyiṉ ēra āki - periya matiṟ kataviṉip pāyntu piḷanta yāṉiyiṉ kōṭukaḷiṟ poruntiya irumpiṉāṟ ceyyappeṟṟa pūṇiṉ aḻakiṉiyuṭiyavāki, mā kaṇ ivi - kariya kaṇkaḷiyuṭiya ivi, mārpu akam aṭiya porunti muyaṅkal viṭāal eṉa - tam mārpakam aḻuntap porunti muyaṅku tali vilakkaṟka eṉṟu kūṟi, yāṉ mayaṅki ōm eṉṉavum ollār maṟṟu ivi pārāṭṭiya paruvamum uḷa - yāṉ varunti oḻivīrāka eṉavum tām ataṟkup poruntārākip piṉṉum ivikaḷip pārāṭṭiya kālaṅkaḷum uḷḷaṉa;
11-16. iṉi - ippoḻutō, putalvaṉ taṭutta pāloṭu taṭii - putalvaṉ uḷḷattip piṟitiṟ cellātu taṭuttuk koṇṭa pālāṟ carintu, titali aṇinta tēmkoḷ meṉmuli - tēmaliyaṇinta iṉimikoṇṭa melliya mulikaḷ, naṟum cāntu aṇinta kēḻ kiḷar akalam - avaratu naṟiya cantaṉam aṇiyappeṟṟa naṉṉiṟam viḷaṅkum mārpil, vīṅka muyaṅkal yām vēṇṭiṉam - vimma muyaṅkutali yām virumpiṉēm ākavum, tīmpāl paṭutal tām añciṉarē - tamatu mārpil iṉiya pāl paṭutali avar añciṉar;
16-21. āyiṭi kavavu ki nekiḻntami pōṟṟi - avviṭattu (muṉpu) aṇittali viṭāta avaratu kikaḷ (iṉṟu) nekiḻntami kaṇṭu, cevili ki matavunaṭi eṉ putalvaṉi nōkki - ceviliyiṉ kiyilirunta melliya naṭiyiṉiyuṭiya eṉ putalvaṉi nōkki, nallōrkku ottaṉir nīyir - nīyir num aḻakiya paratti mārkkup poruntiyavar āvīr, iḵtō celvaṟku ottaṉam yām eṉa - itō intac celvaṉukku nāṅkaḷ poruntiyavarkaḷ āvōm eṉṟu kūṟi, mella eṉ makaṉ vayiṉ peyar tantēṉ - melleṉa eṉ makaṉiṭattuc ceṉṟēṉ;
21-23. atukaṇṭu yāmum kātalam avaṟku eṉa - atu kaṇṭu talivar ac celvaṉpāl yāmum kātaluṭiyēm eṉṟu kūṟi, cāay ciṟupuṟam kaviyiṉaṉāka - paṇintu eṉatu mutukiṉi aṇittuk koṇṭaṉarāka;
23-25. uṟupeyal taṇtuḷikku ēṟṟa - mikka peyalāya kuḷirnta maḻiyi ēṟṟukkoṇṭa, pala uḻu ceñcey maṇpōl - pala muṟiyum uḻutiṭṭa cemmiyākiya ceyyiṉ maṇpōla, neñcu nekiḻntu kaluḻntu - eṉ neñcam nekiḻntu kalaṅki;
5. alkal - iravil;
25-26. avaṉ aṟi pōkiya aṟiviṉēṟku - avarpāl eṉṉi vañcittuc ceṉṟa aṟiviṉēṉākiya eṉakku;
1-5. kūṉmuḷ muḷḷi kuvikuli kaḻaṉṟa - vaḷinta muḷḷiṉi yuṭiya nīrmuḷḷiyatu kuvinta kulikaḷiṉiṉṟu vīḻnta, mīṉmuḷ aṉṉa veṇkāl māmalar = mīṉiṉatu muḷḷiṉi yotta veḷḷiya kāmpiṉiyuṭiya kariya malarkaḷi, poytal makaḷir viḻavu aṇi kūṭṭum - viḷiyāṭṭu makaḷir tām ceyyum viḻāviṟku aḻaku ceyvaṉavākac cērkkum, av vayal naṇṇiya vaḷam kēḻ ūraṉip pulattal kūṭumō - aḻakiya vayalkaḷ poruntiya vaḷamikka ūraṉākiya talivaṉip pulattal poruntumō. (itaṟku yāṉ eṉ ceyvēṉ.!
(muṭipu) tōḻi! tām mārpakam porunti, muyaṅkal viṭāl eṉṟu kūṟi, yām ōvum eṉṉavum ollār, ivar pārāṭṭiya paruvamum uḷa; iṉi tīmpāl paṭutal tām añciṉarē; āyiṭi eṉ putalvaṉi nōkki, nallōrk kottaṉir nīyir; iḵtō celvaṟ kottaṉam yāmeṉa makaṉ vayiṟ peyar tantēṉ; atukaṇṭu yāmum kātalam avaṟkeṉac cāayk kaviyiṉaṉāka, avaṉ kaṇṭu nekiḻntu kaluḻntu neñcaṟi pōkiya aṟiviṉēṟku ūraṉip pulattal kūṭumō.
alkal neñcaṟi pōkiya eṉka.
(vi-ri) kūrmuḷ eṉṉum pāṭattiṟkuk kūriya muḷḷeṉa urikka. ōm: ōvum eṉpataṉ iṭikkuṟi; ceyyum eṉṉum muṟṟu muṉṉilikkaṇ vantatu. tēmkoḷ - iṭaṅkoṇṭa eṉṟumām. inta eṉpatu iḵtō eṉat tirintatu.
(u-ṟi) `muḷḷiyiṉ pakkalilē tōṟṟi vāḻum pū, ataṉi viṭṭup piṟarkkup payaṉ paṭṭāṟpōla, nammuṭaṉ piṟanta neñcu avaruṭaṉ ēkukiyāyiṟṟu. avaruṭiya vatuvi viḻaviṟkum uṭalāyt tiriyāniṉṟāl pulattal kūṭumō eṉka' eṉpatu kuṟippuri.
(mē-ḷ) `aruṇmuntuṟutta' 1 eṉṉum cūttirattut taliviyiṉ aruṇmuntuṟutta aṉpupoti kiḷavikkum paṇinta moḻikkum itiluḷḷa `maṇ pōl nekiḻntu' `ivi pārāṭṭiya paruvamumuḷa' `neñcaṟi pōkiya aṟiviṉēṟku' eṉpavaṟṟiyum, `taṉ vayiṟ karattal' 2  eṉṉuñ cūttirattut talivi maṭaṉ aḻitaṟku, `kavavukki..... peyar tantēṉē' eṉṟataliyum kāṭṭiṉar, nac.
`viṉiyuyir melital'3 eṉṉum cūttirattup pārāṭ ṭeṭuttal mutaliya paṉṉiru nimittamumiṉṟi āṟṟāmi nimittamākak kaṟpiṉuṭ puṇarcci nikaḻntataṟku, `taṇṭuḷikkēṟṟa.... aṟiviṉēṟkē' eṉpataṉiyum, `teyva mañcal' 4 eṉṉum cūttirattu, `maṟintavi yurittal' eṉpataṟku, `muyaṅkal viṭāal..... añciṉarē' eṉpataṉiyum eṭuttuk kāṭṭiṉar, pērā.
27. pāli
(celavuṇarntu vēṟupaṭṭa talimakaṭkut tōḻi colliyatu.)
		koṭuvari irumpuli 5tayaṅka neṭuvari
		āṭukaḻi iruvetir kōṭik kolkuṅ
		kāṉaṅ kaṭiya veṉṉār nāmaḻa
		niṉṟatil poruṭpiṇic ceṉṟivaṇ tarumār
	5.	celpa eṉpa eṉpōy nalla
		maṭavi maṉṟa nīyē vaṭavayiṉ
		vēṅkaṭam payanta veṇkōṭ ṭiyāṉi
		maṟappōrp pāṇṭiyar aṟattiṟ kākkuṅ
		koṟkiyam peruntuṟi muttiṉ aṉṉa
	10.	nakippolin tilaṅkum eyiṟukeḻu tuvarvāy
		takippat taṅkalar āyiṉum ikappa
		yāṅṅaṉam viṭumō maṟṟē tēmpaṭat
		teṇṇīrk kēṟṟa tiraḷkāṟ kuvaḷip
		peruntaki citittum amiyāp paruntupaṭa
	15.	vēttamark kaṭanta veṉṟi nalvēl
		kurutiyoṭu tuyalvan taṉṉaniṉ
		arivē yuṇkaṇ amartta nōkkē.
- maturik kaṇakkāyaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 1-6. koṭuvari irumpuli tayaṅka - vaḷinta kōṭukaḷiyuṭiya periya puli veḷippaṭṭut tōṉṟa, neṭuvari āṭu kaḻi iru vetir kōṭikku olkum kāṉam - nīṇṭa maliyil aciyum taṇṭiṉiyuṭiya valiya mūṅkilkaḷ mēl kāṟṟiṉāl taḷarntu vaḷiyum kāṭṭu neṟi, kaṭiya eṉṉār - koṭiteṉṟu eṇṇārāy, nām aḻa - nām (pirintu) aḻutirukka, niṉṟatu ilporuṭpiṇi ceṉṟu ivaṇ tarumār celpa eṉpa eṉpōy - ōriṭattum nilipeṟuvatillāta poruṭ paṟṟiṉāl pirintu ceṉṟu ataṉi iṅku īṭṭivarac curam celvar eṉa ūrār kūṟuvar eṉṟu collum taliviyē, nī maṉṟa nalla maṭavi - nī uṟutiyāka nalla maṭamiyi yuṭiyi;
6-11. vaṭavayiṉ vēṅkaṭam payanta veṇ kōṭṭu yāṉi maṟappōrp pāṇṭiyar aṟattiṟ kākkum koṟki am perun tuṟi muttiṉ aṉṉa - vaṭaticikkaṇ vēṅkaṭamalip pakkattiluḷḷa aracar tiṟiyākak koṭutta veḷḷiya kōṭṭiṉiyuṭiya yāṉi
kaḷiyuṭiya vīram poruntiya pōril valla pāṇṭiyarkaḷ aṟaneṟi vaḻātu kākkum aḻakiya koṟkip peruntuṟiyil peṟum muttukkaḷip pōṉṟa, nakip polintu ilaṅkum eyiṟu keḻu tuvarvāy - muṟuvalāṟ ciṟantu viḷaṅkum paṟkaḷ poruntiya pavaḷam pōṉṟa niṉvāy, takippat taṅkalar āyiṉum - taṭuttalāl taṭippaṭṭut taṅkārāyiṉum;
13-17. tēmpaṭa teḷ nīrkku ēṟṟa tiraḷ kāl kuvaḷip peruntaki citittum amiyā - tēṉ uṇṭākat teḷinta nīriṉi ēṟṟa tiraṇṭa taṇṭiṉiyuṭiya kuvaḷippūviṉ ciṟanta aḻakiṉi veṉṟu keṭuttum amiyāmal, paruntupaṭa vēntu amarkkaṭanta veṉṟi nalvēl kurutiyoṭu tuyalvantaṉṉa - paruntukaḷ vantu cūḻa aracar tam pōrkaḷi veṉṟa veṟṟi poruntiya nalla vēl irattam tōyntu piṟaḻvatu pōṉṟa, niṉ ari vēy uṇ kaṇ amartta nōkku - niṉatu cevvari poruntiya mi yuṇṭa kaṇṇiṉ māṟupaṭṭa pārvi;
11-12. ikappa yāṅṅaṉam viṭumō - avari eṅṅaṉam kaṭantēka viṭṭuviṭum? (viṭātu kāṇ.)
(muṭipu) tōḻi, nam talivar kāṉam kaṭiya eṉṉār, nām aḻa, niṉṟatu il poruṭpiṇic ceṉṟu ivaṇ tarumār, celpa eṉpa eṉpōy, nī maṭavi maṉṟa; tuvarvāy takippat taṅkalarāyiṉum, niṉ uṇkaṇ amartta nōkku ikappa yāṅṅaṉam viṭum? viṭātukāṇ.
(vi-ri) putaruṭ kiṭanta puli veḷippaṭa mūṅkil aciyum eṉka. kaṭiyaveṉṉār tarumār nām aḻac celvar eṉṟār eṉṟu kūṭṭuka. niṉṟatu - nilipēṟu. vēṅkaṭam payanta yāṉi eṉṟatu am maliyiyuṭiya aracar tiṟiyākat tanta yāṉi eṉṟavāṟu. yāṉiyiyuṭiya pāṇṭiyar eṉavum, pōriṉil valla pāṇṭiyar eṉavum iyiyum. pāṇṭiyar pōrilē taṟukaṇmiyum nāṭu kāttalil taṇṇaḷiyum uṭiyareṉpār, maṟappōrp pāṇṭiyar aṟattiṟ kākkum eṉṟār. pāṇṭiyar kākkum koṟki; pāṇṭiyar nāṭu kāttaṟku iṭaṉākiya koṟki eṉalumām. tuvarvāy takippat taṅkalarāyiṉum eṉṟatu, atu takittal koṇṭē pōkkoḻivar eṉpatu tōṉṟa niṉṟatu. kōṭikku olkum eṉpataṉi mūṉṟaṉ urupākavum, teṇṇīrkku ēṟṟa eṉpataṉi iraṇṭaṉurupākavum virit turikka. amiyā eṉṉum etirmaṟi yeccam tuyalvantaṉṉa eṉṉum kuṟippu viṉikoṇṭu muṭintatu. vēntu, vētteṉa vikāramāyiṟṟu. amartta nōkku - poruntiya nōkkumām.
28. kuṟiñci
(talimakaṉ ciṟippuṟattāṉākat talimakaṭku colluvāḷāyt tōḻi colliyatu. itu pakalē ciṟippuṟam.)
		meyyil tīrā mēvaru kāmamo
		ṭeyyā yāyiṉum urippal tōḻi
		koyyā muṉṉum kuralvārpu tiṉiyē
		aruvi āṉṟa piṅkāl tōṟum
	5.	iruvi tōṉṟiṉa palavē nīyē
		murukumuraṇ koḷḷum tēmpāy kaṇṇip
		pariyal nāyoṭu paṉmalip paṭarum
		vēṭṭuvaṟ peṟalo ṭamintaṉi yāḻaniṉ
		pūkkeḻu toṭali 1nuṭaṅka veḻunteḻuntu
	10.	kiḷḷit teḷviḷi yiṭiyiṭi payiṟṟi
		āṅkāṅ koḻukā yāyiṉ aṉṉi
		ciṟukiḷi kaṭital tēṟṟāḷ ivaḷeṉap
		piṟart tantu niṟukkuva ḷāyiṉ
	15,	uṟaṟkari tākumavaṉ malarnta mārpē.
- pāṇṭiyaṉ aṟivuṭi nampi.
(co-ḷ) 2.  tōḻi,-
1-2. meyyil tīrā mēvaru kāmamoṭu - oruvar meyyiṉiṉṟum oruvar mey nīṅkātavāṟu poruntiya kāmattāl, eyyāy āyiṉum urippal - nī aṟiyāy āyiṉum (ataṉāl varum ētattiṉi) nāṉ urippēṉ kēṭpāyāka;
3-8. aruvi āṉṟa piṅkāl tōṟum - nīr illiyāṉa paciya mutaltōṟum, tiṉi kural vārpu - tiṉi katir mutirap peṟṟu, koyyā muṉṉum - koyvataṉ muṉṉum, pala iruvi tōṉṟiṉa - palavum taṭṭikaḷākat tōṉṟiṉa, nīyē - nītāṉ, muruku muraṇ koḷḷum tēm pāy kaṇṇi - vēṟupaṭṭa pala maṇaṅkaḷum kamaḻum tēṉ oḻukum kaṇṇiyiyuṭiyaṉāṉa, pariyal nāyoṭu palmali paṭarum vēṭṭuvaṟ peṟaloṭu amintaṉi - virin
tōṭum nāykaḷuṭaṉ pala malikaḷiyuṅ kaṭantu cellum vēṭṭuvaṉi eytumaḷavil amintaṉi;
8-11. niṉ pū keḻu toṭali nuṭaṅka eḻunteḻuntu - niṉ pūkkaḷ poruntiya māli yaciya aṭikkaṭi eḻuntu ceṉṟu, kiḷḷit teḷ viḷi iṭi iṭi payiṟṟi - kiḷikaḷi ōṭṭum teḷinta ōcikaḷi iṭiyiṭiyē (palakālum) eḻuppi, āṅku āṅku oḻukāy āyiṉ - aṅkaṅkē ceṉṟu vārātoḻiyiṉ;
11-13. aṉṉi ivaḷ ciṟukiḷi kaṭital tēṟṟāḷ eṉa - nam aṉṉi ciṟiya kiḷiyi ivaḷ ōṭṭutali yaṟiyāḷeṉa eṇṇi, piṟart tantu niṟukkuvaḷ āyiṉ - tiṉi kāttaṟkup piṟarik koṇarntu niṟuttuvaḷāyiṉ;
14. avaṉ malarnta mārpu uṟaṟku aritu ākum - talivaṉatu paranta mārpu piṉ nī aṭitaṟ kariyatākum.
(muṭipu) tōḻi! kāmattāl nī eyyāyāyiṉum urippal; tiṉi koyyā muṉṉum piṅkāltōṟum iruvi pala tōṉṟiṉa; nīyē vēṭṭuvaṟ peṟalō ṭamintaṉi; niṉ toṭali nuṭaṅka eḻuntu viḷi payiṟṟi oḻukāyāyiṉ, aṉṉi kiḷi kaṭital tēṟṟāḷeṉap piṟarttantu niṟukkuvaḷāyiṉ, avaṉ mārpu uṟaṟ karitām.
(vi-ri) tiṉi kural vārpu koyyā muṉṉum piṅkāl tōṟum iruvi tōṉṟiṉa eṉka. aruvi - aruvi nīr. āṉṟa - akaṉṟa; illiyāṉa. iruvi - katir  oḻinta taṭṭi.  māṟupaṭṭa pala pūkkaḷāl pala nāṟṟamā yiruttaliṉ muruku muraṇkoḷḷu meṉṟār. kaṇṇiyi yuṭiya vēṭṭuvaṉ, paṭarum vēṭṭuvaṉ eṉka. talivaṉiyē niṉintu kāttalik kiviṭuttamiyiṉ kiḷikaḷ kavara iruviyāyiṉa. iṭi iṭi - avaṟṟiṉ ōcikku iṭiyiṭiyē eṉalumām; iraṇṭōcikaṭkum vēṟṟumi yilli eṉṟapaṭi. āṅkāṅku - appaṭi appaṭiyē eṉṟumām; kiḷiyōṭṭuvatu pōṉṟu naṭittaluñ ceyyāyāyiṉ eṉṟavāṟu.
(mē-ḷ) `nāṟṟamum tōṟṟamum'1 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu ic ceyyuḷi eṭuttukkāṭṭi, itu variyum paruvam aṉṟeṉak kūṟiyatu eṉpar, nac.
29. pāli
(viṉimuṟṟi mīṇṭa talimakaṉ emmiyum niṉittaṟitirō 
eṉṟa talimakaṭkuc colliyatu.)
		toṭaṅkuviṉi tavirā acivil nōṉtāḷ
		kiṭantuyir maṟukuva tāyiṉum iṭampaṭiṉ
		vīḻkaḷiṟu miciyāp puliyiṉuñ ciṟanta
		tāḻvil uḷḷam talittalic ciṟappac
	5.	ceyviṉik kakaṉṟa kāli eḵkuṟ
		ṟiruvē ṟākiya teritaku vaṉappiṉ
		māviṉ naṟuvaṭi pōlak kāṇtoṟum
		mēval taṇṭā makiḻnōk kuṇkaṇ
		niṉiyātu kaḻinta vikal eṉiyatūum
	10.	vāḻaleṉ yāṉeṉat tēṟṟip paṉmāṇ
		tāḻak kūṟiya takicāl naṉmoḻi
		maṟantaṉir pōṟir emmeṉac ciṟantaniṉ
		eyiṟukeḻu tuvarvāy iṉṉaki aḻuṅka
		viṉaval āṉāp puṉiyiḻi kēḷiṉi
	15.	vemmi taṇṭā eriyuku paṟantalik
		kommi vāṭiya iyavuḷ yāṉi
		nīrmaruṅ kaṟiyātu tērmaruṅ kōṭi
		aṟunīr ampiyiṉ neṟimutal uṇaṅkum
		uḷḷunarp paṉikkum ūkkarum kaṭattiṭi
	20.	eḷḷal nōṉāp poruḷtaral viruppoṭu
		nāṇuttaḷi yāka viki māṇviṉik
		kuṭampāṇ ṭoḻintami allati
		maṭaṅkeḻu neñcam niṉṉuḻi yatuvē.
- 2veḷḷāṭiyaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 5-11. eḵku uṟṟu iru vēṟu ākiya teritaku vaṉappiṉ māviṉ naṟuvaṭi pōla - kattiyāl aṟukkappeṟṟu iru piḷavākiya viḷaṅkum vaṉappiṉiyuṭiya māviṉ naṟiya vaṭuppōla, kāṇtoṟum mēval taṇṭā makiḻ nōkku uṇkaṇ - kāṇuntoṟum kaḷippu mēvutal kuṟiyāta pārviyiṉiyuṭiya miyuṇṭa kaṇkaḷi, niṉiyātu kaḻinta vikal - niṉiyātu oḻinta nāḷil, eṉiyatūum vāḻaleṉ yāṉ eṉat tēṟṟi - yāṉ ciṟitum uyir tarittirēṉ eṉat teḷivittu, pal māṇ tāḻak kūṟiya taki cāl nalmoḻi - pala māṇpukaḷum tāḻntiṭak kūṟiya aḻakumikka nalla moḻiyi;
1-5. toṭaṅku viṉi tavirā acivu il nōṉtāḷ - tāṉ toṭaṅkiya viṉiyik kiviṭāta taḷarcciyillāta valiya muyaṟciyiṉiyuṭiyatum, kiṭantu uyir maṟukuvatāyiṉum iṭam paṭiṉ vīḻ kaḷiṟu miciyā - paṭṭiṉi kiṭantu uyir varuntuvatāyiṉum tāṉ vīḻttiya kaḷiṟu iṭappakkam vīḻiṉ ataṉit tiṉṉātatu mākiya, puliyiṉum ciṟanta tāḻvu il uḷḷam talittali ciṟappa - puliyik kāṭṭiṉum mēmpaṭṭa eṉṟum taḷartalillāta ūkkam mēṉmēl mika, ceyviṉikku akaṉṟa kāli - poruḷīṭṭum viṉikkup pirintu akaṉṟa kālattē;
12-14. maṟantaṉir pōṟir em eṉa - emmiṭattu maṟantu viṭṭīrpōlum eṉṟu, ciṟanta niṉ eyiṟu keḻu tuvarvāy iṉ naki aḻuṅka - ciṟanta niṉatu paṟkaḷ viḷaṅkum pavaḷam pōṉṟa vāyiṉ iṉiya nakikeṭa, viṉaval āṉāp puṉiyiḻi - viṉavutal nīṅkāta aḻakiya aṇikaḷi yuṭiyavaḷē, iṉikēḷ - ippoḻutu yāṉ kūṟuvatik kēṭpāyāka;
15-19. vemmi taṇṭā eri uku paṟantali - veppam kuṟiyāta eriparanta pāḻiṭattē, kommi vāṭiya iyavuḷ yāṉi - perumi yoḻinta vaḻiccellum yāṉi, nīr maruṅku aṟiyātu tēr maruṅku ōṭi - nīruḷḷa iṭam aṟiyāmal pēyttēr tōṉṟum iṭamellām ōṭi, aṟunīr ampiyiṉ neṟimutal uṇaṅkum - nīraṟṟa āṟṟiṟ kiṭakkum ōṭampōla vaḻiyiṭattu varuntik kiṭakkum, uḷḷunarp paṉikkum - niṉippōri varuttum, ūkku arum kaṭattiṭi - ūkkam oḻiyum kāṭṭiṉiṭattilē;
20-21. eḷḷal nōṉāp poruḷ taral viruppoṭu - piṟar ikaḻtalip poṟāta poruḷ īṭṭi varum viruppattuṭaṉ, nāṇutaḷiyāka viki - toṭaṅkiya viṉiyi muṭikkavēṇṭu meṉṉum māṉamē taḷiyākak kaṭṭuṇṭiruntu, māṇ viṉikku - māṇpuṟṟa viṉiyiṉ poruṭṭu, uṭampu āṇṭu oḻintami allati - eṉ uṭampu āṅkup pirintu niṉṟatallatu, maṭam keḻu neñcam niṉ uḻiyatuvē - aṟiyāmiyuṭiya eṉ neñcam niṉ kaṇṇatēyākum; (itaṉi yaṟiyāmiyiṉ iṅṅaṉam viṉavukiṉṟaṉi.)
(muṭipu) uṇkaṇ niṉiyātu kaḻinta vikal vāḻaleṉ yāṉeṉat tēṟṟik kūṟiya naṉmoḻiyi, puliyiṉuñ ciṟanta tāḻvil uḷḷam ciṟappac ceyviṉikku akaṉṟa kāli emmiṭattu maṟantaṉir pōṟir eṉa, iṉ naki aḻuṅka viṉaval āṉāp puṉi yiḻi kēḷ iṉi; uḷḷunarp paṉikkum aruṅkaṭattiṭip poruḷtaral viruppoṭu viki uṭampu āṇṭu oḻintati allati neñcam niṉṉuḻi yatuvē.
(vi-ri) nōṉṟāṭ puliyeṉavum miciyāp puli yeṉavum taṉittaṉi iyiyum. iṭam vīḻntataṉi uṇṇāta puliyiṉum ciṟanta ūkkam eṉṉum ik karuttiṉi, `kaṭuṅkaṭ kēḻal iṭampaṭa vīḻnteṉa, aṉṟava ṇuṇṇā tāki vaḻināṭ, perumali viṭarakam pulampa vēṭṭeḻun, tiruṅkaḷiṟ ṟorutta ṉalvalam paṭukkum, pulipacit taṉṉa melivi luḷḷat, turaṉuṭi yāḷar'1 eṉpataṉāṉ aṟika. (eḵku) uṟṟu - uṟutalāl. teritaku - ārāyattakka eṉavum, paṉmāṇ - palamuṟi eṉavum, iyavuḷ - talimi eṉavum urittalumām. yāṉi ōṭi uṇaṅkum kaṭam eṉka. neñciṉi, maṭaṅkeḻu neñcu eṉṟatu taliviyiṭat tiruntum taṉ āṟṟāmiyi aṟivittilatu eṉṟu.
(mē-ḷ) `karaṇatti ṉamintu'2 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, maṟantīr pōlum eṉṟa maṉiyōḷ ēmuṟu kiḷavikkut talivaṉ etir kūṟiyatākum ic ceyyuḷ eṉṟaṉar, nac.
30. neytal
(pakaṟkuṟi vanta talimakaṟkut tōḻi colliyatu.)
		neṭuṅkayiṟu valanta kuṟuṅkaṇ avvalik 		kaṭalpā ṭaḻiya iṉamīṉ mukantu
		tuṇipuṇar uvakiyar parata mākkaḷ
		iḷiyarum mutiyarum kiḷiyuṭaṉ tuvaṉṟi
	5.	uppoy umaṇar aruntuṟi pōkkum
		oḻuki nōṉpaka ṭoppak kuḻīi
		ayirtiṇi aṭikari olippa vāṅkip
		peruṅkaḷan tokutta uḻavar pōla
		irantōr vaṟuṅkalam malka vīcip
	10.	pāṭupala amittuk koḷḷi cāṟṟik
		kōṭuyar tiṇimaṇal tuñcun tuṟiva
		perumi eṉpatu keṭumō orunāḷ
		maṇṇā muttam arumpiya puṉṉit
		taṇṇaṟuṅ kāṉal vantunum
	15.	vaṇṇam evaṉō eṉṟaṉir celiṉē.
- muṭaṅkik kiṭanta neṭuñcēralātaṉ.
(co-ḷ) 1-11. neṭuṅ kayiṟu valanta kuṟuṅkaṇ avvali -neṭiya kayiṟu kaṭṭappeṟṟa kuṟiya kaṇkaḷiyuṭiya aḻakiya valiyil, kaṭal pāṭu aḻiya iṉamīṉ mukantu - kaṭaliṉ perumi kuṉṟa iṉamākiya mīṉkaḷi mukantu, tuṇi puṇar uvakiyar parata mākkaḷ iḷiyarum mutiyarum kiḷiyuṭaṉ tuvaṉṟi - tuṇiyuṭaṉ kūṭiya makiḻcci yuṭiyarāy iḷiyarum mutiyarumāya nuḷiyarkaḷ cuṟṟattuṭaṉ neruṅki, uppu oy umaṇar aruntuṟi pōkkum oḻuki nōṉ pakaṭu oppak kuḻīi - uppiṉic celuttum uppu vāṇikar ariya tuṟikaḷiṟ celuttum cakaṭukaḷiṟ pūṭṭappeṟṟa valiya erutukaḷi yoppak kūṭi, ayir tiṇi aṭikari olippa vāṅki - nuṇmaṇal ceṟinta pakkattuḷḷa kariyil āravāram peruka iḻuttu, peruṅkaḷam tokutta uḻavar pōla - periya kaḷattilē nellit tokutta uḻavarkaḷip pōṉṟu, irantōr vaṟuṅ kalam malka vīci - tammiṭam vantu irantōrkaḷuṭiya vaṟiya kalaṉkaḷ niṟiya am mīṉkaḷic corintu, pāṭu pala amittuk koḷḷi cāṟṟi - eñciyavaṟṟip pala kūṟukaḷākac ceytu vilikūṟi viṟṟu, kōṭu uyar tiṇi maṇal tuñcum tuṟiva - kari uyarnta tiṇṇiya maṇaṟ parappil tūṅkum tuṟivaṉē;
12-15. orunāḷ -, maṇṇā muttam arumpiya puṉṉit taṇ naṟuṅkāṉal vantu - tūymi ceyyappeṟāta muttukkaḷ arumpi
yirukkum puṉṉi maraṅkaḷiyuṭiya taṇṇiya naṟiya kaṭaṟkaric cōliyil vantu, num vaṇṇam evaṉō eṉṟaṉir celiṉ - nuṅkaḷ mēṉiyiṉ vaṇṇam ettakiyatō eṉṟu viṉāvic celliṉ;
12. perumi eṉpatu keṭumō - niṉperumi eṉpatu keṭṭup pōmō?
(muṭipu) parata mākkaḷ kiḷiyuṭaṉ tuvaṉṟip pakaṭoppak kuḻīi aṭi kari olippa vāṅki, uḻavar pōla vaṟuṅkalam malka vīcik koḷḷi cāṟṟi maṇaliṟṟuñcum tuṟiva! orunāḷ kāṉal vantu, num vaṇṇam evaṉō eṉṟaṉir celiṉ perumi eṉpatu keṭumō?
(vi-ri) iṉamīṉ; iṉam - vaki; kūṭṭamumām. tokutta
vaṟṟi vaṟuṅkalam malka vīcutal eṉṉum toḻil paṟṟiyatākaliṉ, uḻavarpōla eṉṟatu toḻil uvamam. pāṭu - kūṟu. perumi eṉpatu, nīr perumi eṉṟu niṉikkum atu. maṇṇā muttam - kaḻuvappaṭāta muttu; eṉṟatu, puṉṉi yarumpu; itu veḷippaṭi. `vaṇṇam evaṉō eṉṟaṉir celiṉē' eṉṟatu, vaṇṇam aḻivu paṭutalik kuṟittaṟku.
(u-ṟi) `peruṅkaṭaluṭ cikkik kiṭakkiṉṟa mīṉi nuḷiyar ataṉiṉiṉṟum nīkki uyir cekuttuk kaṇṭārellārkkuṅ kūṟu vittup parappip piṉpu uyir varuttiṉōmeṉṉum irakkamiṉṟi, maṇaṟkuṉṟilē uṟaṅkiṉāṟ pōla, peruṅkulattup piṟanta ivaḷi, nīyir num vacamāka nīkki varutti, vēṟupāṭṭāṉ ellārum ivaḷic cūḻumpaṭi alarākkip piṉpu nīr tuyaramiṉṟi uṟaṅkukiṉṟīr eṉṟavāṟu.'
(mē-ḷ) `nāṟṟamum tōṟṟamum'1 eṉṉum cūttirattu `vanta kiḻavaṉi māyañceppip poṟutta kāraṇam kuṟitta kāliyum' eṉṟa pakutikku, icceyyuḷi utāraṇamākak kāṭṭi, itaṉāl, `tammāl iṭiyūṟeyti varuntukiṉṟāṉi oru nāḷ vantilareṉa māyañ ceppiyavāṟum, nīr vārāmiyiṉ vaṇṇam vēṟu paṭumeṉa ēṟṟuk kōṭumeṉak kāraṇaṅ kūṟiyavāṟum kāṇka; tammēl tavaṟiṉṟākak kūṟuṅ kālattu itu kūṟuvar eṉṟataṟkuk kuṟitta kāli eṉṟār, eṉṟurittaṉar, nac.'

31. pāli
(piriviṭi āṟṟāḷāyiṉāḷeṉṟu piṟar collak kēṭṭu 
vēṟupaṭṭa talimakaḷ tōḻikkuc colliyatu.)
		neruppeṉac civanta uruppavir maṇṭilam
		pulaṅkaṭi maṭaṅkat teṟutaliṉ ñoḷki
		nilampuṭi peyarva taṉṟukol iṉṟeṉa
		maṉṉuyir maṭinta maḻimā ṟamiyat
	5.	tiliyila vōṅkiya niliyuyar yāattu
		mēṟkavaṭ ṭirunta pārppiṉaṅ kaṭkuk
		kalluṭik kuṟumpiṉ vayavar villiṭa
		niṇavarik kuṟinta niṟatta vatartoṟuṅ
		kaṇavira māli yiṭūukkaḻin taṉṉa
	10.	puṇṇumiḻ kuruti parippak kiṭantōr
		kaṇṇumiḻ kaḻukiṉ kāṉam nīntic
		ceṉṟār eṉpilar tōḻi veṉṟiyoṭu
		villalit tuṇṇum vallāṇ vāḻkkit
		tamiḻkeḻu mūvar kākkum
	15.	moḻipeyar tēetta paṉmali iṟantē.
- māmūlaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 12. tōḻi - tōḻiyē;
1-4. neruppu eṉac civanta - tīyip pōṉṟu ciṉanteḻunta, uruppu avir maṇṭilam - vemmi viḷaṅkum ñāyiṟu, pulam kaṭi maṭaṅkat teṟutaliṉ - viḷinilattiṭattēyuḷḷa payirkaḷ tīyn toḻiya aḻittaliṉ, iṉṟu nilam ñoḷkip puṭi peyarvatu aṉṟukol eṉa - iṉṟu nilavulakam kuṟivuṟṟu nilipeyarum kālam aṉṟō eṉṟu collumpaṭi, maṉṉuyir maṭinta maḻi māṟu amiyattu - nilipeṟum uyirkaḷ maṭitaṟ kētuvākiya maḻi peyyā toḻinta ik kālattilē;
5-11. iliila ōṅkiya uyarnili yāattu - ilikaḷ ilavāki mika ōṅkiya niliyiyuṭiya yāmarattiṉ, mēṟkavaṭṭu irunta pārppu iṉaṅkaṭku - mēluḷḷa kiḷikaḷilirunta pārppuk kaḷukku, kalluṭik kuṟumpiṉ vayavar villiṭa - kaṟkaḷiyuṭiya cīṟūril uḷḷa maṟavarkaḷ villāl ampiṉi eytalāl, kuṟinta niṟatta atartoṟum - polivaṟṟa niṟattiṉiyuṭiya vaḻitōṟum, kaṇaviramāli yiṭūuk kaḻintaṉṉa - cevvalari māli iṭappaṭṭu iṟantu kiṭantāloppa, niṇa vari - niṇa oḻuṅkum, puṇ umiḻ kuruti parippak kiṭantōr - puṇ coriyum kurutiyum tammic cūḻak kiṭan tōratu, kaṇ umiḻ - kaṇṇik kavarntu ceṉṟu umiḻntu koṭukkum, kaḻukiṉ kāṉam nīnti - kaḻukukaḷiyuṭiya kāṭṭit tāṇṭi;
12-15. vil alittu veṉṟiyoṭu uṇṇum vallāṇ vāḻkki - viṟpōril pakivarkaḷi aḻittu av veṟṟiyāleytum tiṟip poruḷkaḷit tuykkum valiya āṇmi poruntiya vāḻkkiyiṉiyuṭiya, tamiḻkeḻu mūvar kākkum - tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉiyāḷum mūvarālum kākkappeṟum, moḻipeyar tēetta paṉmali yiṟantu - vēṟṟu moḻiyiṉiyuṭiya tēyaṅ kaḷiluḷḷa pala malikaḷiyuṅ kaṭantu, ceṉṟār eṉpu ilar - talivar ceṉṟār eṉṟu kūṟutal ilar; (ivaḷ pirivāṟṟātu varuntukiṉṟāḷeṉṟu eṉṉiyē kuṟi kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.) 1
(muṭipu) tōḻi! maṇṭilam teṟutaliṉ, maṉṉuyir maṭinta amiyattu, yāattu mēṟkavaṭṭirunta pārppiṉaṅ kaṭku (uṇavākak) kuruti parippak kiṭantōratu kaṇkaḷik koṇṭu ceṉṟu umiḻum kaḻukukaḷiyuṭiya kāṉam nīntip paṉmaliyiṟantu talivar ceṉṟār eṉpu ilar. amiyattuc ceṉṟār eṉka.
(vi-ri) pulakkaṭi, pulaṅkaṭiyeṉa vikāramāyiṟṟu. nilam puṭi peyarvatu - ulakam aḻiyum ūḻikkālam; `nilam puṭi peyar vatāyiṉum'1 eṉap puṟanāṉūṟṟil varuvatuṅ kāṇka. ila : kuṟippu muṟṟu eccamāyiṟṟu. pārppu, paṟappavaṟṟiṉ iḷamip peyar. niṇavariyum kurutiyum parippak kiṭantōr eṉka. kaḻukiṉ kāṉam, urupum poruḷum tokkaṉa. eṉpu - eṉṟal; toḻiṟ peyar. mūvar kākkum tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ vēṟāya moḻi vaḻaṅkum tēyam eṉṟumām.
32. kuṟiñci
(1. piṉṉiṉṟa talimakaṟkuk kuṟinērnta tōḻi talimakaṭkuk kuṟi nayappak kūṟiyatu. 2. tōḻikkut talimakaḷ coṟṟatūumām.)
		nerunal elli yēṉal tōṉṟit
		tirumaṇi oḷirvarum pūṇaṉ vantu
		puravalaṉ pōlun tōṟṟam uṟaḻkoḷa
		iravaṉ mākkaḷiṟ paṇimoḻi payiṟṟic
	5.	ciṟutiṉip paṭukiḷi kaṭīiyar paṉmāṇ
		kuḷirkoḷ taṭṭi mataṉila puṭiyāc
		 cūrara makaḷiriṉ niṉṟa nīmaṟ
		ṟiyāri yōvem aṇaṅkiyōy uṇkeṉac
		ciṟupuṟaṅ kaviyiṉa ṉāka ataṟkoṇ
	10.	ṭikupeyal maṇṇiṉ ñekiḻpaña ruṟṟaveṉ
		uḷḷavaṉ aṟital añci uḷḷil
		kaṭiya kūṟik kipiṇi viṭāa
		verūumāṉ piṇiyiṉ orīi niṉṟa
		eṉṉurat takimiyil peyarttuppiṟi teṉvayiṟ
	15.	colla valliṟṟum ilaṉē allān
		tiṉantīr kaḷiṟṟiṟ peyarntōṉ iṉṟun
		tōlāvā ṟilli tōḻinām ceṉmō
		cāyiṟip paṇittōṭ kiḻami taṉakkē
		māciṉ ṟātalum aṟiyāṉ ēcaṟ
	20.	ṟeṉkuṟip puṟaṉili muyalum
		aṇka ṇāḷaṉi nakukam yāmē.
-2nalveḷḷiyār.
