7-13. kacciṉaṉ kaḻaliṉaṉ tēm tār mārpiṉaṉ - kacciṉiyum kaḻaliṉiyum tēṉoḻukum tāraṇinta mārpiṉiyum uṭiyavaṉum, vaki amip polinta vaṉappu ami teriyal - kūṟupāṭṭiṉ amitiyōṭu viḷaṅkiya aḻaku aminta māliyi yuṭiyavaṉum ākiya, curiyal am porunaṉi - kuḻaṉṟa mayiriṉiyuṭiya aḻakiya kūttiṉaṉākiya āṭṭaṉattiyi, kāṇṭirō eṉa - kaṇṭīrō eṉṟu (viṉāvi), ātimanti pētu uṟṟu iṉiya - avaṉ kātaliyākiya āti manti mayakkamuṟṟu varuntiṭa, ciṟi paṟintu urii - kariyiṉi mōtip paravi, ceṅkuṇakku oḻukum - nēr kiḻakkē ōṭum, am taṇ kāviripōla - aḻakiya kuḷirnta kāviri taṉ tirikkiyāl vuvik koṇṭamipōla, koṇṭu ki valittal - cūḷiṉi mēṟkoṇṭu kiyāṟ paṟṟik kōṭali, yāṉ cūḻnticiṉ - yāṉ eṇṇiyuḷḷēṉ.
(muṭipu) ūraṉ, yāṅkaḷ muḻavoṭu tūṅka, em cēri vanteṉa, avaṉ peṇṭir porunar paṟiyiṉ āṉātu kaḻaṟupa eṉpa; ātimanti porunaṉik kāṇṭirō eṉap pētuṟṟu iṉiya, kuṇakku oḻukum kāviri pōlac cūḷmēṟ koṇṭu kivalittali yāṉ cūḻnticiṉ.
(vi-ri) kaṉital - ceṟital; olittal eṉak koṇṭu, mārccaṉi yamintu olikkum eṉalumām. tūṅkal - āṭutal. peṇṭir eṉṟatu talimakaḷi.
antil : aci. kacciṉaṉ kaḻaliṉaṉ mārpiṉaṉ eṉṉum kuṟippu muṟṟukkaḷ eccamāyp porunaṉ eṉṉum peyarkoṇṭu muṭintaṉa. porunaṉ - āṭṭaṉatti. itaṉi, `āṭṭaṉatti nalaṉayan turiit, tāḻiruṅ katuppiṟ kāviri vavvaliṉ, mātiran tuḻii matimaruṇ ṭalanta, ātimanti'1 eṉpataṉā ṉaṟika.
urii - parantu. kāviripōla - kāviri taṉ tirikkiyāl vuvik koṇṭamipōla: itu toḻiluvamam.
āṭaṟ ṟakiyāṉātal pāṭaṟkuralāṉātal, peṟṟēṉeṉṟu colluvā ḷāyiṉ, yāṉ ippaṭic ceyvēṉ eṉṟāḷ.
(mē-ḷ) `pullutal mayakkum pulavik kaṇṇum'1 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, ic ceyyuḷ, talivaṉiṉ kāmak kiḻattiyāya iḷamip paruvattāḷorutti, taṉpāṟ ṟalivaṉ varuvatu paṟṟit talivi pulantatākak kēṭṭavaḻip perumitam koṇṭu kūṟiyatākum eṉpar, nac.
77. pāli
(talimakaṉ piriyak karutiya neñciṟkuc collic celavu aḻuṅku vittatu.)
		naṉṉutal pacappavum āḷviṉi tarīiyar
		tuṉṉaruṅ kāṉan tuṉṉutal naṉṟeṉap
		piṉṉiṉṟu cūḻntaṉi yāyiṉaṉ ṟiṉṉāc
		cūḻnticiṉ vāḻiya neñcē veytuṟa
	5.	iṭiyumiḻ vāṉam nīṅki yāṅkaṇum
		kuṭipatip peyarnta cuṭṭuṭi mutupāḻk
		kayiṟupiṇik kuḻici ōli koṇmār
		poṟikaṇ ṭaḻikkum āvaṇa mākkaḷiṉ
		uyirtiṟam peyara nallamark kaṭanta
	10.	taṟuka ṇāḷar kuṭar tarīit teṟuvarac
		ceñcevi eruvi yañcuvara ikukkuṅ
		kallatark kavali pōkiṉ cīṟūrp
		pullari yittip pukarpaṭu nīḻal
		elvaḷi yalikkum iruḷkūr māli
	15.	vāṉavaṉ maṟavaṉ vaṇaṅkuviṟ ṟaṭakki
		āṉā naṟaviṉ vaṇmakiḻp piṭṭaṉ
		poruntā maṉṉar aruñcamat tuyartta
		tiruntili yeḵkam pōla
		aruntuyar tarumivaḷ paṉivār kaṇṇē.
- maruta ṉiḷanākaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 4. neñcē vāḻiya.;
1-4. naṉṉutal pacappavum - nam taliviyiṉ oḷḷiya neṟṟi pacantiṭavum, āḷviṉi tarīiyar - muyaṟciyāl poruḷ īṭṭutaṟku, tuṉ aruṅ kāṉam - aṭitaṟkariya kāṭṭu neṟiyil, tuṉṉutal naṉṟeṉa - cellutal naṉṟeṉa eṇṇi, piṉṉiṉṟu - eṉ piṉṉē niṉṟu, cūḻntaṉiyāyiṉ - eṇṇutiyāyiṉ, naṉṟu iṉṉā cūḻnti ciṉ - mikavum koṭiyavaṟṟi āyntukoṇṭaṉiyāvāy; (etaṉāleṉiṉ,)
4-6. veytuuṟa iṭi umiḻ vāṉam - iṭikaḷi umiḻum mēkam vemmi mika, nīṅki - maḻi peyyātu nīṅkutalāl, yāṅkaṇum kuṭi patip peyarnta - evviṭattum kuṭikaḷ tattam patikaḷiṉiṉṟum peyarntu pōkaṟ kētuvāya, cuṭṭu uṭi mutu pāḻ - palarum cuṭṭik kūṟum mikka pāḻiṭamākiya pāliyil
7-8. kayiṟu piṇikkuḻici - kayiṟṟāṟ piṇittaliyuṟṟa kuṭatti luḷḷa, ōli koṇmār - ōliyi eṭuttuk kōṭaṟku, poṟi kaṇṭu aḻikkum - ak kuṭattiṉmē liṭṭa ilacciṉiyi āyntu nīkkum, āvaṇa mākkaḷiṉ - avvōliyit tērum mākkaḷ aviyiṟṟi veḷiyē īrtteṭuttal pōla;
9-11. uyir tiṟam peyara - tam uyir vēṟoru naṟpataviyiṟpuka, teṟuvara nal amark kaṭanta - māṟṟā rañca naṟpōri veṉṟu paṭṭa, taṟukaṇāḷar kuṭar - añcā vīrariṉ kuṭari, ceñcevi eruvi - civanta ceviyiṉiyuṭiya paruntu, añcuvara - kaṇṭār añcumāṟu, tarīi ikukkum - vāṅkip pōkaṭum;
12-14. kal atark kavali pōkiṉ - kaṟkaḷiyuṭiya kavartta neṟiyiṟ pōyiṉ, cīṟūr pul ari itti pukar paṭu nīḻal - āṅkuḷḷa cīṟūrkaḷiṉ pāṅkarp pulliya aṭiyiṉiyuṭiya ittiyiṉ puḷḷipaṭṭa niḻalilē, el vaḷi alikkum - peruṅ kāṟṟu alikkum, iruḷ kūrmāli - iruḷ mikka mālip poḻutil;
15-19. vāṉavaṉ maṟavaṉ - cēraṉ paṭittalivaṉākiya, vaṇaṅku vil taṭakki - vaḷinta villip periya kiyiṟ koṇṭa, āṉā naṟaviṉ vaṇ makiḻp piṭṭaṉ - amiyāta kaḷḷiṉatu mikka makiḻcciyiyuṭiya piṭṭaṉ eṉpāṉ, poruntā maṉṉar arum camattu uyartta - paki maṉṉaratu ariya pōril eṭutta, tiruntu ili eḵkam pōla - tiruntiya ilit toḻili yuṭiya vēl pōla, ivaḷ paṉivār kaṇ - nam taliviyatu nīr oḻukum kaṇkaḷ (tōṉṟi), arun tuyar tarum - ariya tuyariṉit tarum ātalāl eṉka.
(muṭipu) neñcē vāḻiya! nī āḷviṉi tarīiyar kāṉam tuṉṉutal naṉṟeṉac cūḻntaṉiyāyiṉ, iṉṉāc cūḻnticiṉ; (etaṉāleṉiṉ,) mutu pāḻākiya kavaliyik kaṭantu celluṅkāl māliyil, ivaḷ paṉivārkaṇ tōṉṟip, piṭṭaṉ poruntā maṉṉar camattu uyartta eḵkam pōla aruntuyar tarum ātalāl eṉka.
kuḻici ōli koṇmār poṟi kaṇṭaḻikkum āvaṇamākkaḷiṉ, eruvi taṟukaṇāḷar kuṭar tarīi ikukkum kavali eṉka.
(vi-ri) tarīiyar - poruḷtarīiya reṉa viritturikka. cūḻtalōṭu taṉṉit tūṇṭutaluṅ karutip piṉṉiṉṟu cūḻntaṉiyāyiṉ eṉṟāṉ. piṉṉiṉṟu - uṭaṉ niṉṟu eṉṟumām. naṉṟeṉak kūṟip pōntu ataṉālām ētattip piṉṉiṉṟu ārāyvāyāyiṉ eṉṟurittalumām. nīṅki - nīṅkalāl eṉat tirikka. cuṭṭuṭi mutupāḻ - palarālum cuṭṭappaṭutaluṭiya pāḻ eṉka. pāḻākiya kallatark kavali eṉak kūṭṭuka. `kayiṟu piṇik kuḻici  yōli koṇmār, poṟikaṇ ṭaḻikku māvaṇa mākkaḷiṉ' eṉṟatu, ūrāṇmi nāṭṭāṇmik kaḻakaṅkaṭku uṟuppiṉarit tērnteṭuttaṟ poruṭṭu, uṭaṉpāṭu terivikkum takutiyuṭiyār palarum eḻutik kuṭattiṉkaṭ pōkaṭṭa ōlikaḷi, āvaṇa mākkaḷ palar muṉ kuṭattiṉ mēliṭṭa ilacciṉiyik kaṇṭu, nīkki, uḷḷirukkum ōlikaḷi eṭuttu eṇṇit tērappaṭṭār ivareṉṉa muṭipu ceyvatōr vaḻakkattiṉik kuṟippatu. itu kuṭavōli eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum; paḻiya kalveṭṭukkaḷil immuṟi virivākac collappaṭṭuḷḷatu. āvaṇamākkaḷ - piramāṇam vāṅkukiṟa mākkaḷ eṉpārum uḷar. ōli koṇmār aḻikkum mākkaḷ eṉpataṉi, aḻittu ōli koḷḷum mākkaḷ eṉa māṟuka. el vaḷi - tōṟṟamuṭiya vaḷiyumām.
78. kuṟiñci
(kaḷavuk kālattup pirintuvanta talimakaṟkut tōḻi colliyatu.)
		naṉantalik kāṉat tāḷi yañci
		iṉantalit tarūu meṟuḻkiḷar muṉpiṉ
		variñimi ṟārkkum vāypuku kaṭāattup
		poṟinutaṟ polinta vayakkaḷiṟ ṟoruttal
	5.	irumpiṇart taṭakkiyiṉ ēmuṟat taḻuva
		kaṭuñcūṉ maṭappiṭi naṭuṅkuñ cāral
		tēmpiḻi naṟaviṉ kuṟavar muṉṟil
		muntū ḻāymalar utirak kāntaḷ
		nīṭitaḻ neṭuntuṭup pociyat taṇṇeṉa
	10.	vāṭi tūkkum varupaṉi yaṟciram
		nammil pulampiṟ ṟammūrt tamiyar
		eṉṉā kuvarkol aḷiyar tāmeṉa
		emviṭ ṭakaṉṟa ciṉṉāṭ ciṟitum
		uḷḷiyum aṟitirō ōṅkumali nāṭa
	15.	ulakuṭaṉ tiritarum palarpukaḻ nallici
		vāymoḻik kapilaṉ cūḻac cēyniṉṟu
		ceḻuñceyn nelliṉ viḷikatir koṇṭu
		taṭantāḷ āmpal malaroṭu kūṭṭi
		yāṇṭupala kaḻiya vēṇṭuvayiṟ piḻiyā
	20.	tāḷiṭūuk kaṭantu vāḷamar uḻakki
		ēntukōṭ ṭiyāṉi vēntar ōṭṭiya
		kaṭumparip puravik kivaṇ pāri
		tīmperum piñcuṉip pūtta
		tēṅkamaḻ putumalar nāṟumivaḷ nutalē.	
- maturi nakkīraṉār.
(co-ḷ) 14. ōṅku malināṭa - ōṅkiya mali poruntiya nāṭṭiyuṭiyavaṉē;
1. naṉantali kāṉattu āḷi añci - akaṉṟa iṭattiyuṭiya kāṭṭiṉiṭattē āḷiyiṉi yañci;
2-5. iṉam talit tarūum - taṉ iṉattit taṉṉiṭattē kūṭṭik koḷḷum, eṟuḻ kiḷar muṉpiṉ - viḷaṅkit tōṉṟum mikka valiyiṉiyum, vari ñimiṟu ārkkum vāypuku kaṭāattu - variyiṉiyuṭiya vaṇṭukaḷ olikkum vāyiṟpukum matattiṉiyum uṭiya, poṟi nutal polinta - puḷḷikaḷ poruntiya neṟṟiyāṟ polivuṟṟa, vayakkaḷiṟṟu oruttal - vali poruntiya āṇ yāṉikaḷiṉ talivaṉ, irum piṇart taṭakkiyiṉ - kariya caruccariyuṭiya periyakiyāl, ēm uṟat taḻuva - pātukāval tōṉṟat taḻuvavum;
6. kaṭum cūl maṭappiṭi naṭuṅkum cāral - mutaṟ cūliyuṭiya iḷiya piṭi naṭuṅkum pakka maliyiluḷḷa;
7. tēmpiḻi naṟaviṉ kuṟavar muṉṟil - iṉimiyuṟap piḻinta kaḷḷiṉiyuṭiya kuṟavarkaḷatu muṟṟattil;
8-10. muntūḻ āy malar utira - mūṅkiliṉ aḻakiya malarkaḷ utiravum, kāntaḷ nīṭuitaḻ neṭun tuṭuppu ociya - kāntaḷatu nīṇṭa itaḻiyuṭiya periya malar muṟiyavum, taṇeṉa vāṭi tūkkum - taṇṇeṉṟu vāṭikkāṟṟu vīcum, varupaṉi aṟciram - tōṉṟu
kiṉṟa paṉiyiyuṭiya muṉpaṉik kālattil;
11-13. emviṭṭu akaṉṟa ciṉṉāḷ - emmiviṭ ṭakaṉṟirunta cila nāṭkaḷuḷḷē, nam il pulampil - nammip pirintirukkum taṉimiyāl, tammūr - tammūriṉ kaṇṇē, tamiyar aḷiyar tām - taṉittirukkum iraṅkaṟkuriya ivar, eṉ ākuvar kol eṉa - ennili yaṭivarō eṉa eṇṇi iraṅki;
15-24. ulakuṭaṉ tiritarum palar pukaḻ nallici - ulakelām parakkum palarum pukaḻum nalla pukaḻiṉiyum, vāymoḻi - meymmoḻiyiṉiyumuṭiya, kapilaṉ cūḻa - kapilaṉ āyntu viṉiceya, cēy niṉṟu ceḻum cey nelliṉ viḷi katirkoṇṭu - neṭun toliviṉiṉṟu vaḷamporuntiya vayalkaḷil viḷinta neṟ katirkaḷik koṇṭuvantu, taṭantāḷ āmpal malaroṭu kūṭṭi - avaṟṟip periya tāḷiṉiyuṭiya āmpal malarālāya aviyaloṭu aṭṭu uṇpittu, yāṇṭu pala kaḻiya vēṇṭu vayiṉ piḻiyā - pala yāṇṭukaḷ kaḻiyavum pōri virumpic ceyyum iṭattiṉiṉṟum peyarāmal, tāḷ iṭūuk kaṭantu vāḷ amar uḻakki - pakivar vāṭ pōriṉik kalakki muyaṟciyāl veṉṟu, ēntu kōṭṭu yāṉi vēntar ōṭṭiya - nimirnta kompukaḷ poruntiya kaḷiṟṟiṉiyuṭiya mūvēntari yum piṟakkiṭac ceyta, kaṭum parip puravik kivaṇ pāri - mikka viriviṉiyuṭiya kutiriyiyum kivaṇmiyiyu muṭiya pāriyiṉ, tīm perum piñcuṉi pūtta - iṉiya periya paciya cuṉikkaṭ pūtta, tēm kamaḻ putumalar nāṟum ivaḷnutal - tēṉ maṇakkum putiya malareṉa maṇakkum ivaḷatu neṟṟiyiṉi;
13-14. ciṟitum uḷḷiyum aṟitirō - ciṟitaḷavēṉum niṉit taṟintīrō.
(muṭipu) mali nāṭa! āḷi yañci, oruttal taḻuvavum maṭappiṭi naṭuṅkum cāraliṭattuk kuṟavar muṉṟilil vāṭi tūkkum aṟciram, nammil pulampil aḷiyar eṉ ākuvar kol eṉa, pāriyiṉ cuṉipūtta putumalar nāṟum ivaḷ nutalic ciṟitum uḷḷiyum aṟitirō.
(vi-ri) iṉam tali : iṅkut tali aciyumām. eṟuḻ muṉpu : oruporu ḷiru col. miñiṟu eṉpatu ñimiṟu eṉṟāyiṟṟu. kaṭuñcūl - mutaṟ karu. `niṉṉayan tuṟivi kaṭuñcūṟ ciṟuvaṉ'1 eṉpatu kāṇka. tēmpiḻi naṟavu - tēṉāṟ piḻinta naṟavumām. tuṭuppu - tuṭuppup pōṟaliṉ pūviṟku ākupeyar. nam mil - nām illāta eṉka. `vāymoḻik kapilaṉ cūḻac cēy niṉṟu, ceḻuñcey nelliṉ viḷikatir koṇṭu, taṭantā ḷāmpal malaroṭu kūṭṭi' eṉṟatu, tamiḻnāṭṭu mūvēntarum pāriyiṉ paṟamparaṇi muṟṟiyiruppa, araṇiluḷḷār uṇaviṉṟi varuntāvāṟu, kapilar kiḷikaḷi vaḷarttuk katir koṇṭuvara viṭṭa varalāṟṟi uṇarttiyavāṟu. `pāri paṟampiṉ, niripaṟik kurīiyiṉam kālip pōki, muṭaṅku
puṟac cenneṟ ṟarīiya vōrāṅ, kiritēr koṭpiṉavākip poḻutupaṭa, paṭarkoḷ māli paṭartan tāṅku'1 eṉa iv varalāṟu oḷaviyārāluṅ kūṟappaṭṭuḷatu. malaroṭu kūṭṭi eṉpa taṉpiṉ, aṭṭu uṇpittu eṉa varuvitturikka. piḻiyā, īṟu keṭṭa etirmaṟi viṉiyeccam. vēntar ōṭṭiya eṉa iraṇṭaṉurupu uyartiṇi maruṅkiṟ ṟokku niṉṟatu uṭampoṭu puṇarttalāṟ koḷka.
(u-ṟi) `naṉantali..... maṭappiṭi naṭuṅkum' eṉṟatu, `yāṉi kākkavum piṭi naṭuṅkiṉāṟ pōla,' nīyir ivaḷip pātukākka vēṇṭumeṉṉuṅ karuttuṭiyarā yirukkavum, piriviṟku añcā
niṉṟāḷ eṉṟavāṟu.
79. pāli
(poruḷvayiṟ pirinta talimakaṉ iṭiccurattut taṉ neñciṟkuc colliyatu.)
		tōṭpataṉ amitta karuṅki āṭavar
		kaṉipoṟi piṟappa nūṟi viṉippaṭarntu
		kalluṟut tiyaṟṟiya valluvarp paṭuvil
		pāruṭi maruṅkiṉ ūṟal maṇṭiya
	5.	vaṉpulam tumiyap pōkik koṅkar
		paṭumaṇi yāyam nīrkkunimirntu cellum
		cētā eṭutta cennilak kurūuttukaḷ
		akaliru vicumpiṉ ūṉṟit tōṉṟum
		naṉantali aḻuvam nammoṭu tuṇippa
	10.	vallāṅku varutu meṉṉā talkuvara
		varuntiṉi vāḻiyeṉ neñcē iruñciṟi
		vaḷivāyp paruntiṉ vāṉkaṭ pēṭi
		āṭutoṟu kaṉiyum avvāyk kaṭuntuṭik
		koṭuvil eyiṉar kōṭcuram paṭara
	15.	neṭuviḷi payiṟṟum nirampā nīḷiṭi
		kalpiṟan tattam pōki
		nillāp poruṭpiṇip pirinta nīyē.
- kuṭavāyiṟ kīrattaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 11. vāḻi eṉ neñcē-;
11-17. āṭutoṟu kaṉiyum av vāyk kaṭuntuṭi - āṭuntōṟum olikkum aḻakiya vāyiṉiyuṭiya kaṭiya tuṭiyiṉiyum, koṭuvil eyiṉar kōḷ curam paṭara - vaḷinta villiṉiyumuṭiya maṟavar (pakivari vaḷittuk) koḷḷum curattiṉkaṭ cella, irum ciṟi vaḷivāyp paruntiṉ vāṉkaṇ pēṭi - periya ciṟiyiṉiyum vaḷinta vāyiṉiyumuṭiya paruntiṉ veḷḷiya kaṇṇiṉi yuṭiya pēṭi, neṭuviḷi payiṟṟum nirampā nīḷiṭi (taṉ tuṇiyiṉi nōkki) neṭuṅ kūppīṭu ceyyum cellat toliyāta nīṇṭa iṭamāya, kal piṟaṅku attam pōki - kaṟkaḷ viḷaṅkum kāṭṭil naṭantu, nillāp poruṭ piṇip pirinta nī - nili nillāta poruḷiṉ mēluḷḷa paṟṟiṉāl piritaluṟṟa nī;
1-9. tōṭpataṉ amitta karuṅ ki āṭavar - tōḷilē toṅkaviṭum cōṟṟu muṭiyik kōtta valiya kiyiṉiyuṭiya āṭavarkaḷ, viṉippaṭarntu - (kiṇaṟu veṭṭum) toḻiliṟ pukku, kaṉipoṟi piṟappa nūṟi - mikka tīp poṟi yuṇṭākap pāṟikaḷi veṭṭi, kalluṟuttu iyaṟṟiya - kallutal ceytu amitta, val uvarp paṭuvil - mikka uvariyuṭiya kiṇaṟṟil, pāruṭi maruṅkiṉ ūṟal maṇṭiya - pāriṉi uṭitta pakkattē ūṟiya nīri uṇṇa vēṇṭi, nīrkku nimirntu cellum - nīr parukak kalittuc cellum, koṅkar paṭu maṇi āyam cētā - koṅkaratu olikkum maṇi pūṇṭa āyamākiya cevviya ākkaḷ, vaṉpulam tumiyap pōki - vaṉṉilaṅkaḷ tuṇipaṭac ceṉṟu, eṭutta cennilak kurūuttukaḷ - kiḷampiya cem maṇṇākiya niṟam poruntiya puḻuti, akal iru vicumpiṉ ūṉṟit tōṉṟum - akaṉṟa periya vāṉiṉkaṇ mikkut tōṉṟum, naṉantali aḻuvam - iṭamakaṉṟa kāṭṭil ;
9-11. nammoṭu tuṇippa - nam talivi nammoṭu tuṇiyāka, vallāṅku varutum eṉṉātu - vallapaṭi celvōm eṉṉātu, alkuvara varuntiṉi - iṅkē vantu taṅkiya aḷavil varuntukiṉṟaṉi; iḵteṉṉi!
(muṭipu) neñcē! kalpiṟaṅku attam pōki nillāp poruṭpiṇi pirinta nī, naṉantali aḻuvam, nammoṭu (avaḷ) tuṇippa, vallāṅku varutum eṉṉātu alkuvara, varuntiṉi.
(vi-ri) cōṟum uṭaṉ koṇṭupōyk kiṇaṟu veṭṭutaliṉ, tōḷpataṉ amitta eṉṟār. kalluṟuttal - kallutal. val uvar - mikka uvar. paṭu - kiṇaṟu, pār - vaṉṉilam. nīrkku nimirntu cellum cētā maṇṭiya pōki eṭutta tukaḷ eṉka. varutum - pōvōm eṉṉum poruṭṭu. neṭuviḷi payiṟṟum eṉṟatu eyiṉar pōrkkuc cellutal aṟintu pēṭi tuṇiyi aḻikkum eṉṟapaṭi.

80. neytal
(iravukkuṟi vanta talimakaṟkut tōḻi colliyatu.)
		koṭuntāḷ mutaliyoṭu kōṭṭumīṉ vaḻaṅkum
		iruṅkaḻi yiṭṭuccuram nīnti yiraviṉ
		vantōy maṉṟa taṇkaṭaṟ cērppa
		niṉakkevaṉ ariyamō yāmē enti
	5.	puṇartirip parappakam tuḻiit tanta
		paṉmīṉ uṇaṅkaṟ paṭupuḷ ōpputum
		muṇṭakam kalitta mutunīr aṭikari
		oṇpal malara kavaṭṭili aṭumpiṉ
		ceṅkēḻ meṉkoṭi āḻi aṟuppa
	10.	iṉamaṇip puravi neṭuntēr kaṭii
		miṉṉilip polinta viḷaṅkiṇar aviḻpoṉ
		taṇnaṟum pintā tuṟikkum
		puṉṉiyaṅ kāṉal pakalvan tīmē.
- 1maruṅkūr kiḻār peruṅkaṇṇaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 3. taṇ kaṭaṟ cērppa - kuḷirnta kaṭaṟkariyiyuṭiya talivaṉē! nī;
 1-3. koṭun tāḷ mutaliyoṭu kōṭṭumīṉ vaḻaṅkum - vaḷinta kāliṉiyuṭiya mutaliyoṭu cuṟāmīṉ iyaṅkum, iruṅkaḻi iṭṭuc curam nīnti - kariya uppaṅ kaḻiyāya neruṅkiya ariya vaḻiyiṉik kaṭantu, iravil vantōy - iraviṉkaṇ vantuḷāy;
7-13. muṇṭakam kalitta mutunīr aṭikari - nīrmuḷḷi taḻitta kaṭaliyaṭinta kariyiluḷḷa, oṇ pal malara kavaṭṭili aṭumpiṉ - oḷḷiya palavāya malarkaḷiyuṭiya kavaṭupaṭṭa ilikaḷiyuṭiya aṭampiṉatu, ceṅkēḻ meṉkoṭi āḻi aṟuppa - civanta niṟamuṭiya melliya koṭikaḷi (niṉ) tēruruḷ aṟuttuvara, iṉamaṇip puravi neṭuntēr kaṭii - maṇikaḷip pūṇṭa ōriṉamākiya kutirikaḷ pūṭṭiya neṭiya tēriṉic celutti, miṉ ilip polinta viḷaṅku iṇar - oḷiyiyuṭiya iliyoṭu polinta viḷaṅkum pūṅkottukkaḷ, aviḻ poṉ taṇ naṟum pintātu uṟikkum - malarnta poṉpōlum taṇṇiya naṟiya cevvit tātukkaḷic coriyum, puṉṉi am kāṉal pakal vantīmē - puṉṉi maraṅkaḷiyuṭiya aḻakiya kaṭaṟkaric cōliyil pakalil varuvāyāka;
4-6. enti - em tanti, puṇartirip parappakam tuḻi it tanta - poruntum alikaḷiyuṭiya kaṭalakattē tuḻavik koṇarnta, palmīṉ uṇaṅkal paṭupuḷ ōpputum - palavaki mīṉkaḷiṉ vaṟṟaliṟ poruntum puṭkaḷi ōṭṭiyiruppōm; ākaliṉ;
4. yām niṉakku evaṉ ariyam - yāṅkaḷ niṉakku eṅṅaṉam ariyamāvēm?
(muṭipu) cērppa, iruṅkaḻi iṭṭuc curam nīnti iravil vantōy, neṭuntēr kaṭii, puṉṉiyaṅ kāṉal pakal vantīmē; mīṉ uṇaṅkal paṭupuḷ ōpputum; yām niṉakku evaṉ ariyam?
(vi-ri) kōṭṭu mīṉ - kompiyuṭiya mīṉ; cuṟāmīṉ. iṭṭuc curam - vaḻi ciṟitākiya curam. neṟiyarumi kūṟiyavāṟu.
malara aṭumpu eṉak kūṭṭuka.
81. pāli
(pirivuṇarttiya talimakaṟkut tōḻi talimakaḷ kuṟippaṟintu vantu colliyatu.)
		nāḷulā eḻunta kōḷval uḷiyam
		ōṅkuciṉi iruppit tīmpaḻam muṉiyiṉ
		pullaḷip puṟṟiṉ palkiḷic citali
		oruṅkumuyaṉ ṟeṭutta naṉivāy neṭuṅkō
	5.	ṭirumpūtu kurukiṉ iṭantiri tērum
		maṇpaka vaṟanta āṅkaṭ kaṇporak
		katirteṟak kaviḻnta ulaṟutali nōṉciṉi
		neṟiyayal marāam ēṟip pulampukoḷa
		eṟiparun tuyavum eṉṟūḻ nīḷiṭi
	10.	vemmuṉi aruñcuram nīntic ciṟanta
		cemmal uḷḷam turattaliṉ kaṟuttōr
		oḷiṟuvēl aḻuvam kaḷiṟupaṭak kaṭakkum
		māvaṇ kaṭalaṉ viḷaṅkil aṉṉem
		miyeḻil uṇkaṇ kaluḻa
	15.	aiya cēṟirō akaṉṟucey poruṭkē.
- ālampēri cāttaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 15. aiya-, akaṉṟucey poruṭku - emmip pirintu ceṉṟu tēṭum poruṭku;
10-11. ciṟanta cemmal uḷḷam turattaliṉ - ciṟanta talimi vāynta num uḷḷam tūṇṭutaliṉ;
11-14. kaṟuttōr - vekuṇṭeḻunta pakivaratu, oḷiṟu vēl aḻuvam - oḷirkiṉṟa vēliṉiyuṭiya pōrkkaḷatti, kaḷiṟu paṭak kaṭakkum - yāṉikaḷ maṭiya vellum, māvaṇ kaṭalaṉ viḷaṅkil aṉṉa - mikka vaṇmiyiyuṭiya kaṭalaṉ eṉpāṉatu viḷaṅkil eṉum patiyi yotta, em eḻil mi uṇkaṇ kaluḻa - ematu aḻakiya miyuṇṭa kaṇṇiṉaḷāya talivi aḻa ;
1-6. nāḷ ulā eḻunta kōḷ val uḷiyam - viṭiyaṟ kālattē eḻuntu ulāviya taṉ iriyik koḷḷutalil valla karaṭi, ōṅkuciṉi iruppit tīm paḻam muṉiyiṉ - uyarnta kiḷi
kaḷiyuṭiya iruppi marattiṉ iṉiya paḻattiṉi veṟuppiṉ, palkiḷic citali - pala kiḷikaḷāya kaṟiyāṉ, naṉivāy oruṅku muyaṉṟu eṭutta - naṉinta vāyāl oruṅkukūṭi varuntik kaṭṭiya, pulaḷip puṟṟiṉ - pulliya vaḷikaḷiyuṭiya puṟṟiṉatu, neṭuṅkōṭu - neṭiya ucciyiṉi, irumpu ūtu kurukiṉ iṭantu - iruppu uliyil ūtum turuttiyip pōṉṟu (uyirttup) peyarttu, iri tērum - iriyiṉi āynteṭuttuṇṇum, maṇpaka vaṟanta āṅkaṇ - maṇ piḷavupaṭa vaṟaṭciyuṟṟa pāli nilimāya avviṭattu;
6-10. kaṇ porak katir teṟa - veyyil kaṇkaḷip pārkka voṇṇātavāṟu pora ñāyiṟu kāytaliṉ, neṟiayal marāam - neṟiyiṉ ayalatākiya veṇkaṭampukaḷiṉ, kaviḻnta ulaṟutali nōṉciṉi ēṟi - (taḻikaḷ vāṭik) kaviḻntuḷa kāynta ucciyiṉiyuṭiya valiya kiḷikaḷil ēṟi, pulampukoḷa ēṟi paruntu uyavum - iriyiṉip pāynteṭukkum paruntu taṉimi koḷa varuntum, eṉṟūḻ nīḷ iṭi - veppam mikka nīṇṭa iṭaṅkaḷāya, vemmuṉi aruñcuram nīnti - vevviya muṉikaḷiyuṭiya ariya curattiṉit tāṇṭi;
15. cēṟirō - celluvīrō.
(muṭipu) aiya! poruṭku uḷḷam turattaliṉ, uṇ kaṇ kaluḻa, aruñcuram nīntic cēṟirō.
uḷiyam tīmpaḻam muṉiyiṉ neṭuṅkōṭiṭantu iri tērum āṅkaṇ paruntu uyavum nīḷiṭi aruñcuram eṉka.
(vi-ri) `atta viruppi yārkaḻal putuppūt, tuytta vāya tuṇikalam  parakka,.... vaṉki yeṇkiṉ vayaniri parakkum'1 eṉak karaṭi iruppippū uṇṭalum, `īyaṟ puṟṟat tīrmpuṟat tiṟutta, kurumpi valcip peruṅki yēṟṟi'2 eṉap puṟṟām paḻañcōṟu uṇṭalum muṉṉar vantuḷḷami kāṇka.
karaṭi akaḻkiṉṟa kālattu uyirttaliṉ, kurukiṉ iṭantu eṉṟār. muṉi - eyiṉar iruppu. aḻuvam - pōrkaḷap parappu. emi eḻil eṉap pirittu viyakkattakka aḻaku eṉṟurittalumām. uṇkaṇ : ākupeyar; taliviyi uḷappaṭutti em kaṇ kaluḻa eṉṟurittāḷumām. itu celavaḻuṅkuvittatu.
82. kuṟiñci
(tōḻikkut talivi aṟattoṭu niṉṟatu.)
		āṭamik kuyiṉṟa avirtuḷi maruṅkil
		kōṭi yavvaḷi kuḻalici yākap
		pāṭiṉ aruvip paṉinīr iṉṉicit
		tōṭami muḻaviṉ tutikura lākak
	5.	kaṇakkali ikukkum kaṭuṅkural tūmpoṭu
		malippūñ cāral vaṇṭiyā ḻāka
		iṉpal imiḻici kēṭṭuk kaliciṟantu
		manti nallavi maruḷvaṉa nōkkak
		kaḻivaḷar aṭukkat tiyaliyā ṭummayil
	10.	3naṉavuppuku viṟaliyil tōṉṟum nāṭaṉ
		uruva valvil paṟṟi amputerintu
		ceruccey yāṉi ceṉṉeṟi viṉāay
		pularkural ēṉaṟ puḻiyuṭi oruciṟi
		malartār mārpaṉ niṉṟōṟ kaṇṭōr
	15.	palartil vāḻi tōḻi avaruḷ
		āriruṭ kaṅkul aṇiyoṭu porunti
		ōriyā ṉākuva tevaṉkol
		nīrvār kaṇṇoṭu nekiḻtō ḷēṉē.
- kapilar.
(co-ḷ) 15. tōḻi vāḻi-;
1-15. āṭu amik kuyiṉṟa avir tuḷi maruṅkil - aciyum mūṅkilil tuḷikkappeṟṟa viḷaṅkum tuḷiyiṭattē, kōṭi avvaḷi kuḻal iciyāka - aḻakiya mēl kāṟṟiṉā leḻum oli kuḻaliṉ iciyākavum, pāṭu iṉ aruvip paṉi nīr iṉṉici - oli iṉiya aruviyiṉ kuḷirnta nīriṉ iṉiya ōci, tōṭu ami muḻaviṉ tuti kuralāka - tokutiyākiya muḻaviṉ neruṅkiya iciyākavum, kaṇakkali ikukkum kaṭuṅkural tūmpoṭu - kūṭṭamāya kalimāṉkaḷ tāḻa olikkum kaṭiya kural peru vaṅkiyattiṉ iciyākavum (ataṉoṭu), malip pūñcāral vaṇṭu yāḻāka - maliccāraliṭattē pūkkaḷi luḷḷa vaṇṭiṉ oli yāḻiṉ iciyākavum āka, iṉpal imiḻ ici kēṭṭu - iṅṅaṉam iṉiya palavāya olikkum icikaḷik kēṭṭu, kali ciṟantu - āravāram  mikku, manti nallavi maruḷvaṉa nōkka - mantikaḷāya nalla tiraḷ viyappuṟṟukkāṇa, kaḻi vaḷar aṭukkattu - mūṅkil vaḷarum pakka maliyil, iyali āṭum mayil - ulāvi āṭum mayilkaḷ, naṉavup pukum viṟaliyil tōṉṟum nāṭaṉ - kaḷattiṟ pukuntāṭum viṟali pōlat tōṉṟum nāṭṭiyuṭi yavaṉākiya, malar tār mārpaṉ- akaṉṟa tāriṉi yaṇinta mārpiyuṭiyavaṉ, uruva val vil paṟṟi - aḻakiya valiya villiṉik kiyiṟ paṟṟi, ampu terintu - ciṟanta ampiṉi āyntu koṇṭu, ceruc cey yāṉi cel neṟi viṉāay - taṉṉāl ampu eyyappeṟṟa yāṉi ceṉṟa neṟiyiṉi viṉavi, pular kural ēṉal puḻiyuṭi oruciṟi - mutirnta katiriṉiyuṭiya tiṉip puṉattiṉ vāyiliṉ oru pakkattē, niṉṟōṉ kaṇṭōr - niṉṟavaṉik kaṇṭōr, palar - palarāvar;
15-18. avaruḷ - avar tammuḷ, ār iruḷ kaṅkul - ariya iruḷ ceṟinta iravil, aṇiyoṭu porunti - aṇiyiṉkaṇ taṅki, nīrvār kaṇṇoṭu - nīr coriyum kaṇṇiṉoṭu, nekiḻ tōḷēṉ - melinta tōḷiyuṭiyēṉāka, ōr yāṉ ākuvatu evaṉ kol - yāṉoruttiyumē āyatu eṉṉiyō?
(muṭipu) tōḻi vāḻi! nāṭaṉ, mārpaṉ niṉṟōṉ kaṇṭōr palar; avaruḷ aṇiyoṭu porunti nīrvār kaṇṇoṭu, nekiḻ tōḷēṉ ōr yāṉ ākuvatu evaṉ kol?
(vi-ri) mūṅkilil vaṇṭu tuḷitta tuḷiyiṉ vaḻiyē mēl kāṟṟuc cellutalāl eḻum ōci, kuḻalici pōlum eṉṉuṅ karuttu, `āṭamit tumpi kuyiṉṟa, akalā vantuḷi kōṭi mukattaliṉ, nīrkkiyaṅ kiṉanirip piṉṟi vārkol, āykkuḻaṟ pāṇiyilituvan ticikkum' (225) eṉap piṉṉar varutaluṅ kāṇka. ivvuruvakam, `parutiyañ celvaṉ virikatirt tāṉik, kiruḷ vaḷippuṇṭa maruḷpaṭu pūmpoḻiṟ, kuḻalici tumpi koḷuttik kāṭṭa, maḻali vaṇṭiṉam nalliyāḻ ceyya, veyiṉuḻi paṟiyāk kuyiṉuḻi potumpar, mayilā ṭaraṅkiṉ manti kāṇ paṉakāṇ' 1 eṉa maṇimēkaliyil amintiruttal aṟintu iṉpuṟuttakkatu. uruva - eṉṟataṉi uriccol īṟu tirinta teṉak koṇṭu uṭkup poruntiya eṉṟalumām. `uruvak kutiri maḻavar ōṭṭiya'1 eṉṟataṉ uri kāṇka. ceruc cey yāṉi - pōr ceytu ampu eyyap peṟṟa yāṉi. malar mārpu eṉa iyiyum. malarnta tār eṉac ciṉikkēṟṟa aṭiyākkalu mām.  til : aci.
`āṭami kuyiṉṟa....... nāṭaṉ' eṉṟatu iṅṅaṉam piṟark kellām iṉpañ ceyyum nāṭaṉik kaṇṭār palaruḷḷum namakkē tuyarāvāṉ eṉṉeṉṟavāṟu. ic ceyyuḷ putumi poruḷākat tōṉṟiya viyappu eṉṉum meyppāṭu.
(mē-ḷ) `variviṭi vitta kālattu varuntiṉum, variyā nāḷiṭi vantōṉ muṭṭiṉum, uriyeṉat tōḻik kurittaṟ kaṇṇum tāṉē kūṟuṅ kālamu muḷavē'2 eṉṉum cūttirattu ic ceyyuḷi eṭuttukkāṭṭi, `avaṉi āyattār palaruṅ kaṇṭāreṉa vantōṉ muṭṭiyavāṟum, avaruḷ nekiḻ tōḷēṉ yāṉē yeṉat tāṉē kūṟiyavāṟuṅ kāṇka' eṉṟurittaṉar, nac.
`putumi perumi'3 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu `manti nallavi.... tōṉṟu nāṭaṉ' eṉṟavaḻi paṇṭu orukālum kaṇṭaṟiyātapaṭi āṭiṟṟu mayil eṉṟamiyiṉ, piṟa poruṭkaṭ ṭōṉṟiya putumiyāyiṟṟu eṉavum, `malartār mārpaṉ.... nekiḻ tōḷēṉē' eṉṟatu taṉkaṭṭōṉṟiya putumi paṟṟi viyappup piṟantatu. eṉṉi? taṉ karuttu veḷippaṭātu taṉ meykkaṭṭōṉṟiya putumiyit talivi viyantāḷ pōlat tōḻikku aṟattoṭu niṉṟamiyiṉ eṉavum, `iṉpatti veṟuttal'4 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu `kaṇṭuyil maṟutta leṉpatu - iravum pakalun tuñcāmi'; atu `pularkura lēṉal.... nekiḻtō ḷēṉē' eṉa varum eṉavum urittār, pērā.
83. pāli
(talimakaṉ iṭic curattut taṉ neñciṟkuc colliyatu.)
		valañcuri marāattuc curaṅkamaḻ putuvīc
		curiyā ruḷittali poliyac cūṭik
		kaṟiyaṭi maṭappiṭi kāṉat talaṟak
		kaḷiṟṟukkaṉ ṟoḻitta uvakiyar kaliciṟantu
	10	karuṅkāl marāattuk koḻuṅkompu piḷantu
		perumpoḷi veṇṇār aḻuntupaṭap pūṭṭi
		neṭuṅkoṭi nuṭaṅkum niyama mūtūr
		naṟavunoṭi nallil putavumutaṟ piṇikkuṅ
		kallā iḷiyar perumakaṉ pulli
	15.	viyaṉṟali naṉṉāṭṭu vēṅkaṭaṅ kaḻiyiṉum
		cēyar eṉṉā taṉpumikak kaṭii
		eytavan taṉavāl tāmē neytal
		kūmpuviṭu nikarmalar aṉṉa
		ēnteḻil maḻikkaṇem kātali kuṇaṉē.
- kallāṭaṉār.
(co-ḷ) neñcē!;
1-10. valam curi marāattu curam kamaḻ putuvī - valamākac curitta veṇkaṭampiṉatu curamellām kamaḻum putiya pūkkaḷi, curi ār uḷit tali poliyac cūṭi - curuḷ koṇṭa uḷipōṉṟa mayiriṉiyuṭiya taliyil viḷaṅkumpaṭi aṇintu, kaṟiaṭi maṭappiṭi kāṉattu alaṟa - uralpōṉṟa aṭiyiṉiyuṭiya iḷiya peṇ yāṉi kāṭṭiṟ (pirintu) alaṟa, kaḷiṟṟu kaṉṟu oḻitta uvakiyar - yāṉik kaṉṟiṉip pirittukkoṇṭa makiḻcciyi yuṭiyarāy, kali ciṟantu - cerukku mikku, karuṅkāl marāattuk koḻuṅ kompu piḷantu - valiya aṭimarattiṉi yuṭiya veṇ kaṭampiṉ vaḷaviya kompiṉip piḷantu, perum poḷi veṇnār - peritāka uritta veḷḷiya nārk kayiṟṟāl, aḻuntupaṭap pūṭṭi - ak kaṉṟiṉi aḻuttampeṟak kaṭṭik koṇarntu, neṭuṅkoṭi nuṭaṅkum niyamam mūtūr - nīṇṭa koṭikaḷ aciyum aṅkāṭikaḷiyuṭiya paḻimiyāṉa ūril, naṟavu noṭi nallil putavu mutal piṇikkum - kaḷviṟkum nalla illiṉ vāyiliṭattē piṇittiṭum, kallā iḷiyar - tam vēṭṭuvat toḻilaṉṟip piṟitu toḻil kallāta vēṭarkaṭku, perumakaṉ - talivaṉāṉa, pulli - pulli eṉpāṉatu, viyaṉtali nal nāṭṭu vēṅkaṭam kaḻiyiṉum - virinta iṭattiṉiyuṭiya nalla nāṭṭiṉ kaṇṇuḷḷa vēṅkaṭa maliyiṉik kaṭantu celliṉum;
11-14. neytal kūmpu viṭu nikar malar aṉṉa - neytaliṉatu muki yaviḻnta oḷi poruntiya malari yotta, ēntu eḻil maḻik kaṇ emkātali kuṇaṉ - aḻakiṉi ēntiya kuḷirnta kaṇṇiṉaḷāya nam kātaliyiṉ kuṇaṅkaḷ, cēyar eṉṉātu - cēymikkaṇ ceṉṟavar eṉṉātu, aṉpu mikak kaṭii - aṉpu mikacceluttiṭa, eytavantaṉa tām - tām nammāl aṇuka vantaṉa.
(muṭipu) neñcē! (nām) pulli vēṅkaṭam kaḻiyiṉum, em kātali kuṇaṉ tām cēyar eṉṉātu aṉpu mikak kaṭii eyta vantaṉa.
(vi-ri) uḷi - taliyāṭṭam. oḻitta uvakiyar : oḻitta : peyareccam kāraṇap poruṭṭāka vantatu.
(mē-ḷ) `veṟiyaṟi ciṟappiṉ'1 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, tarutalum eṉṟa mikiyāl, niriyallāta kōṭalum at tuṟippāṟpaṭum eṉṟurittu, `kaṟiyaṭi maṭappiṭi kāṉat talaṟak, kaḷiṟṟuk kaṉṟoḻitta vuvakiyar kaliciṟantu,..... kallā viḷiyar perumakaṉ pulli' eṉṟa aṭikaḷik kāṭṭi,...... yāṉik kaṉṟik kavarntavāṟu kāṇka eṉṟaṉar, nac.
84. mulli
(talimakaṉ pācaṟiyiruntu colliyatu.)
		malimicik kuliiya urukeḻu tiruvil
		paṇimuḻaṅ keḻili puvam vāṅkit
		2tāḻpeyaṟ perunīr valaṉērpu vaḷii
		mātiram putippap poḻitaliṉ kāṇvara
	5.	irunilaṅ kaviṉiya ēmuṟu kāli
		neruppiṉ aṉṉa ciṟukaṭ paṉṟi
		ayirkkaṇ paṭāart tuñcupuṟam putiya
		naṟuvī mulli nāṇmalar utirum
		puṟavaṭin tirunta arumuṉi iyavil
	10.	cīṟū rōḷē 3oṇṇutal yāmē
		eripuri paṉmalar piṟaḻa vāṅki
		ariñar yātta alaṅkutalip peruñcūṭu
		kaḷḷār viṉiñar kaḷantoṟum maṟukum
		taṇṇaṭi taḻīiya koṭinuṭaṅ kāreyil
	15.	aruntiṟi koṭuppavuṅ koḷḷāṉ ciṉañciṟantu
		viṉivayiṟ peyarkkun tāṉip
		puṉitār vēntaṉ pācaṟi yēmē.
- maturi eḻuttāḷaṉ.
(co-ḷ) 1-5. malimicik kuliiya urukeḻu tiruvil - malimītu vaḷinta accan tarum aḻakiya villiṉiyuṭiya, paṇi muḻaṅku eḻili - muracameṉa muḻaṅkum mēkam, puvam vāṅki - kaṭal nīri mukantukoṇṭu, valaṉ ērpu vaḷii - ulakiṉi valaṉāka eḻuntu vaḷittu, tāḻ peyal perunīr - iṟaṅkip peyyum mikka  maḻiyi, mātiram putippap poḻitaliṉ - tici yellām maṟiyap poḻitalāl, kāṇvara irunilam kaviṉiya ēmuṟu kāli - kāṭciyuṟap periya nilam aḻakupeṟṟa iṉpam eytiya ikkālattē;
10. oṇṇutal - oḷi poruntiya neṟṟiyiṉiyuṭiya nam talivi;
6-10. ayirkkaṇ paṭāart tuñcu - nuṇ maṇaliṉ
kaṇṇuḷḷa ciṟu tūṟiṉiṭattē tūṅkum, neruppiṉ aṉṉa ciṟukaṇ paṉṟi - neruppiṉi yotta ciṟiya kaṇṇiṉiyuṭiya paṉṟiyiṉ, puṟam putiya - mutuku maṟiya, naṟuvī mulli nāḷ malar utirum - naṟiya pūviṉatākiya mulliyiṉ putiya pūkkaḷ utirum, puṟavu aṭintirunta arumuṉi iyavil - kāṭu cārntirukkum ariya muṉikaḷiyuṭiya neṟiyiluḷḷa, cīṟūrōḷ - ciṟiya ūriṉkaṇ uḷḷāḷ āvaḷ;
10-17. yāmē - yāmō, eri puri paṉmalar piṟaḻa vāṅki - neruppup pōṉṟa pala malarkaḷi māṟupaṭa vittu valittu, ariñar yātta alaṅkutalip peruñcūṭu - nellariñar kaṭṭiya aciyum pakkaṅkaḷuṭiya periya neṟkaṭṭiṉik koṇṭu, kaḷār viṉiñar kaḷantoṟum maṟukum - kaḷḷiṉi uṇṭa kaḷamar kaḷantōṟum koṭu pōkum, taṇṇaṭi taḻīiya koṭi nuṭaṅku ār eyil - marutanilam cūḻnta koṭikaḷ aciyum ivvariya eyili, aruntiṟi koṭuppavum koḷḷāṉ - pakivar vaṇaṅki ariya tiṟiyākak koṭuppavum ēṟṟuk koḷḷāṉāki, ciṉañ ciṟantu - ciṉam mikku, viṉivayiṉ peyarkkum tāṉi - mēṉmēlum pōriṉkaṭ celuttum cēṉiyiṉiyuṭiya, puṉitār vēntaṉ pācaṟiyēm - māliyi aṇinta aracaṉatu pācaṟiyiṭattē yuḷḷēm.
(muṭipu) eḻili poḻitaliṉ irunilam kaviṉiya ēmuṟu kāli, oṇṇutal cīṟūrōḷ; yāmē. āreyil tiṟi koṭuppavum koḷḷāṉ viṉi vayiṟ peyarkkum tāṉip puṉitār vēntaṉ pācaṟiyēm.
(vi-ri) ēm - ēmam; pātukāvalumām. eripuri malar - tāmari, ceṅkaḻunīr. piṟaḻa eṉṟatu pūvō ṭarinta nellik kaṭṭuṅ kālattu ariyōṭu māṟa vittuk kaṭṭap peṟutalāl avi tam miṉ māṟupaṭal. eyil - araṇ, ūr. tūtuviṭak karutiya talivaṉ cīṟūrōḷ oṇṇutal eṉat taṉ varuttan tōṉṟak kūṟiṉāṉ eṉka. itaṉit tūtu kaṇṭu kūṟiya teṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
(mē-ḷ) `ēvaṉ marapiṉ'1 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, icceyyuḷi eṭuttukkāṭṭi, itu tūtu kaṇṭu varuntik kūṟiyatu eṉavum, itaṉ kaṇ taṉṉūrum arumuṉiyiyaviṟ cīṟūr eṉṟaliṉ, tāṉ kuṟunila maṉṉaṉ eṉpatu peṟṟām eṉṟum, `tāṉē cēṟalum'2 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, `itu, kuṟunila maṉṉar pōlvār ceṉṟami tōṉṟak kūṟiyatu' eṉṟum, `oṉṟāt tamariṉum' eṉṉum cūttirattu itu, `ākit tōṉṟum pāṅkōr pāṅkiṉum'3 eṉṟataṟku utāraṇamāku
meṉṟum `kūtir vēṉil'4 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, `viṉivayiṟ........ pācaṟi yēmē' eṉat taliviyi niṉivaṉa vākikku vaḻuvām; akattiṟku vaḻuvaṉṟu eṉṟum kūṟuvar, nac.
85. pāli
(talimakaṉ piriya vēṟupaṭṭa talimakaḷit tōḻi vaṟpuṟuttiyatu.)
		naṉṉutal pacappavum peruntōḷ nekiḻavum
		uṇṇā uyakkamō ṭuyircelac cāay
		iṉṉam ākavum iṅkunat tuṟantōr
		aṟavar allar avareṉap palapulantu
	5.	āḻal vāḻi tōḻi cāral
		īṉṟunāḷ ulanta meṉṉaṭi maṭappiṭi
		kaṉṟupaci kaḷiiya piṅkaṇ yāṉi
		muṟṟā mūṅkil muḷitaru pūṭṭum
		veṉvēl tiriyaṉ vēṅkaṭa neṭuvari
	10.	naṉṉāṭ pūtta nākiḷa vēṅki
		naṟuvī yāṭiya poṟivari maññi
		naṉippacuṅ kuruntiṉ nāṟuciṉi yiruntu
		tuṇippayirn takavun5 tuṉitaru taṇkār
		varutum yāmeṉat tēṟṟiya
	15.	paruvaṅ kāṇatu pāyiṉṟāl maḻiyē.
- kāṭṭūr kiḻār makaṉār kaṇṇaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 1-5. tōḻi vāḻi-; naṉṉutal pacappavum - aḻakiya neṟṟi pacantiṭavum, perun tōḷ nekiḻavum - periya tōḷ melintiṭavum, uṇṇā uyakkamoṭu uyir celac cāay - uṇṇāmiyā lāya varuttattāl uyir nīṅka melintu, iṉṉam ākavum - nām in niliyiṉamākavum, iṅku nam tuṟantōr - iṅku nammi viṭṭup pirintār nam talivar, aṟavar allar avar - eṉavē avar aṟattiṉarallar, eṉap pala pulantu āḻal - eṉṟivvāṟu palavuṅ kūṟi veṟuttut tuyaril aḻuntātē,
5-15. cāral - malic cāraṟkaṇṇē, īṉṟu nāḷ ulanta melnaṭi maṭappiṭi kaṉṟu paci kaḷiiya - īṉṟu aṇimi kaḻinta melliya naṭi vāynta iḷiya piṭiyiṉatum ataṉ kaṉṟiṉatum paciyip pōkkaṟku, piṅkaṇ yāṉi - paciya kaṇṇiṉiyuṭiya kaḷiṟu, muṟṟā mūṅkil muḷi tarupu ūṭṭum - mūṅkiliṉ muṟṟāta muḷiyiṉik koṇarntu uṇpikkum, veṉvēl tiriyaṉ vēṅkaṭa neṭuvari - veṟṟi poruntiya vēliṉiyuṭiya tiriyaṉatu vēṅkaṭameṉum nīṇṭa maliyil, naṉṉāḷ pūtta nāku iḷavēṅki - nalla nāṭkāliyiṟ  pūtta mika iḷiya vēṅki marattiṉ, naṟuvī āṭiya poṟi vari maññi - naṟiya pūkkaḷiṉ tukaḷi aḷinta poṟikaḷuṭaṉ kūṭiya varikaḷiyuṭiya mayil, naṉip pacuṅ kuruntiṉ nāṟu ciṉi yiruntu - tēṉiyuṭiya paciya kurunta marattiṉ naṟumaṇam vīcum kiḷiyiliruntu, tuṇip payirntu akavum tuṉitaru taṇkār - taṉ tuṇiyi aḻittuk kūppiṭum virintu varum kuḷirnta kārp paruvamē, yām varutum eṉat tēṟṟiya paruvam - nam talivar tām varuvōm eṉat teḷivitta paruvamākum, maḻi pāyiṉṟu - ataṟku maḻiyum paravā niṉṟatu, atukāṇ - ataṉik kāṇpāyāka.
(muṭipu) tōḻi, vāḻi! nām iṉṉam āka, nam talivar nammit tuṟantār, avar aṟavarallar, eṉap pulantu āḻal; taṇ kār, yām varutum eṉat tēṟṟiya paruvam, maḻi pāyiṉṟu; atu kāṇ.
yāṉi muḷi tarupu ūṭṭum vēṅkaṭa neṭuvari vēṅki naṟuvīyāṭiya maññi kuruntiṉ ciṉiyiruntu tuṇip payirntakavum kār eṉka.
(vi-ri) nam tuṟantōr eṉpatu nattuṟantōr eṉa vikāra
māyiṟṟu. nāḷ ulanta - nāḷ muṭinta; īṉṟaṇimi kaḻinta eṉṟapaṭi. piṭi kaṉṟu ivaṟṟiṉ paciyeṉa virittuk koḷka. vēṅki pūkkuṅ kālam maṇanāḷ ākaliṉ, naṉṉāṭ pūtta eṉṟāḷ eṉalumām. kār virivil vantuviṭum eṉpāḷ tuṉitaru taṇkār eṉavum, maḻi pāyiṉṟu eṉavum kūṟiṉāḷ. eṉavē itu vēṉiṟ kālattu iṟutiyāyiṟṟu. maññi tuṇip payirntu akavum eṉṟatu, kār kālattil pirintiruttal arumiyāl kūṭutaṟku aḻikkum eṉṟapaṭi.
86. marutam
(vāyil maṟutta tōḻikkut talimakaṉ colliyatu. talimakaḷik kūṭi iṉpuṟṟirunta talimakaṉ paṇṭu nikaḻntatu coṟṟu iṉpuṟṟiruntatūumām.)
		uḻuntutalip peyta koḻuṅkaḷi mitavi
		peruñcōṟ ṟamali niṟpa nirikāl
		taṇperum pantart tarumaṇal ñemiri
		maṉiviḷak kuṟuttu māli toṭarik
	5.	kaṉiyiruḷ akaṉṟa kaviṉpeṟu kālik
		kōḷkāl nīṅkiya koṭuveṇ ṭiṅkaḷ
		kēṭil viḻuppukaḻ nāṭali vanteṉa
		uccik kuṭattar puttakal maṇṭiyar
		potucey kampali mutucem peṇṭir
	10.	muṉṉavum piṉṉavum muṟimuṟi tarattarap
		putalvaṟ payanta titaliyav vayiṟṟu
		vāliḻi makaḷir nālvar kūṭik
		kaṟpiṉiṉ vaḻāa naṟpala utavip
		peṟṟōṟ peṭkum piṇiyi yākeṉa
	15.	nīroṭu corinta īritaḻ alari
		palliruṅ katuppiṉ nelloṭu tayaṅka
		vatuvi naṉmaṇam kaḻinta piṉṟik
		kalleṉ cummiyar ñerēreṉap pukutantu
		pēriṟ kiḻatti yākeṉat tamartara
	20.	ōriṟ kūṭiya uṭaṉpuṇar kaṅkul
		koṭumpuṟam vaḷiik kōṭik kaliṅkat
		toṭuṅkiṉaḷ kiṭanta ōrpuṟan taḻīi
		muyaṅkal viruppoṭu mukamputi tiṟappa
		añciṉaḷ uyirtta kāli yāḻaniṉ
	25.	neñcam paṭarnta teñcā turiyeṉa
		iṉṉaki irukkip piṉyāṉ viṉavaliṉ
		ceñcūṭ ṭoṇkuḻi vaṇkātu tuyalvara
		akamali uvakiyaḷ āki mukaṉikut
		toyyeṉa iṟiñci yōḷē māviṉ
	30.	maṭaṅkoḷ matiiya nōkkiṉ
		oṭuṅkīr ōti māa yōḷē.
- nallāvūr kiḻār.
(co-ḷ) 1-4. uḻuntu  talippeyta koḻuṅ kaḷimitavi - uḻuttam paruppuṭaṉ kūṭṭic camitta cevviya kuḻitali yuṭiya poṅkaloṭu, peruñ cōṟṟu amali niṟpa - periya cōṟṟut tiraḷiyi uṇṭal iṭiyaṟātu nikaḻa, niri kāl taṇ perum pantar - variciyākiya kālkaḷiyuṭiya kuḷirnta periya pantaril, tarumaṇal ñemiri - koṇarntiṭṭa maṇalip parappi, maṉi viḷakku uṟuttu - maṉiyiṉkaṇ viḷakkiṉi ēṟṟi vittu, māli toṭari - mālikaḷit toṅkaviṭṭu;
5-10. kōḷ kāl nīṅkiya koṭuveṇ tiṅkaḷ - tīya kōḷkaḷiṉ toṭarpu nīṅkappeṟṟa vaḷinta veḷḷiya tiṅkaḷi, kēṭuil viḻuppukaḻ nāḷ tali vanteṉa - kuṟṟamaṟṟa ciṟanta pukaḻiṉiyuṭiya urōkaṇi eṉum nāḷ aṭintatāka, kaṉiiruḷ akaṉṟa kaviṉ peṟu kāli - annāḷilē mikka iruḷ nīṅkiya aḻaku poruntiya viṭiyaṟ kāliyil, uccik kuṭattar putu akal maṇṭiyar - ucciyil kuṭattiṉi yuṭiyarum kiyiṉiṟ putiya akaṉṟa maṇṭi eṉum kalattiṉiyuṭiyarum ākiya, potucey kampali mutucem peṇṭir - maṇattiṉic ceytu vikkum āravāramuṭiya mutiya maṅkala makaḷir, muṉṉavum piṉṉavum muṟi muṟi tarat tara - muṉṉē taruvaṉavum piṉṉē taruvaṉavum muṟimuṟiyākat tantiṭa;
11-16. putalvaṉ payanta titali av vayiṟṟu - makaṉip peṟṟa tēmaluṭiya aḻakiya vayiṟṟiṉiyuṭiya, vāl iḻi makaḷir nālvar kūṭi - tūya aṇikaḷiyuṭiya makaḷir nālvar kūṭi niṉṟu, kaṟpiṉiṉ vaḻāa naṟpala utavi - kaṟpiṉiṉṟum vaḻuvātu naṉṟāya pala pēṟukaḷiyum tantu, peṟṟōṉ peṭkum piṇiyi ākeṉa - niṉṉi eytiya kaṇavaṉi virumpip pēṇum viruppatti yuṭiyi āka eṉṟu vāḻtti, nīroṭu corinta īr itaḻ alari - nīroṭu kūṭṭip peyta kuḷirnta itaḻkaḷiyuṭiya pūkkaḷ, pal iruṅ katuppiṉ nelloṭu tayaṅka - mikka kariya kūntalil neṟkaḷuṭaṉ viḷaṅka;
17. vatuvi naṉmaṇam kaḻinta piṉṟi - iṅṅaṉam naṉṟākiya vatuvik kaliyāṇam muṭinta piṉpu;
18-20. tamar kal eṉ cummiyar ñerēreṉap pukutantu - cuṟṟattār kalleṉṟa oliyiṉarāy virintu vantu, pēr iṟkiḻatti āka eṉat tara - periya maṉik kiḻatti āvāy eṉṟu kūṟik kūṭṭa, ōr il kūṭiya uṭaṉpuṇar kaṅkul - ōr aṟiyil uṭaṉkūṭiya puṇarccikkuriya iravil;
21-26. koṭumpuṟam vaḷii - mutukiṉi vaḷittu, kōṭik kaliṅkattu - kōṭip puṭivikkuḷ, oṭuṅkiṉaḷ kiṭanta puṟam taḻīi - oṭuṅkikkiṭanta iṭattiṉic cārntu, muyaṅkal viruppoṭu mukam puti tiṟappa - aṇiyum viruppuṭaṉ mukattiṉi mūṭiyirunta āṭiyiṉi nīkka, añciṉaḷ uyirtta kāli - añci uyirtta poḻutu, niṉ neñcam paṭarntatu eñcātu uriyeṉa - niṉ uḷḷam niṉintataṉi maṟiyātu uri eṉṟu, piṉ yāṉ viṉavaliṉ - yāṉ piṉpu viṉavutaliṉ, iṉ naki irukki - iṉiya makiḻcciyuṭaṉ kūṭiya irukkiyiṉkaṇ;
27-31. māviṉ maṭaṅ koḷ matiiya nōkkiṉ - māṉiṉ maṭattiṉik koṇṭatum cerukkiṉi yuṭiyatumāṉa nōkkiṉiyum, oṭuṅku īr ōti - oṭuṅkiya kuḷirnta kūntaliṉiyum uṭiya, māayōḷ - māmi niṟattiṉi yuṭiyāḷ;
ceñ cūṭṭu oṇ kuḻi vaṇkātu tuyalvara - civanta maṇikaḷ patitta oḷḷiya kuḻi vaḷaviya kātiṉkaṇ aciya, akam mali uvakiyaḷ āki - uḷḷam niṟinta makiḻcciyiṉaḷ āki, mukaṉ ikuttu - mukattiṉit tāḻttu, oyyeṉa iṟiñciyōḷ - virintu talivaṇaṅkiṉaḷ.
(muṭipu) vatuvi naṉ maṇam kaḻinta piṉṟi. tamartara ōriṟkūṭiya kaṅkul, kōṭik kaliṅkattu oṭuṅkiṉaḷ kiṭanta puṟam taḻīi, mukam puti tiṟappa, añciṉaḷ uyirttakāli, niṉ neñcam paṭarntatu eñcātu uriyeṉa yāṉ viṉavaliṉ, māyōḷ mukaṉ ikuttu oyyeṉa iṟiñciyōḷ.
nāḷ talivanteṉa, amali niṟpa, pantar viḷakkuṟuttu, māli toṭari, mutu cem peṇṭir tarat tara, makaḷir nālvar kaṟpiṉiṉ vaḻāa naṟpala utavip peṟṟōṟ peṭkum piṇiyi ākeṉa, nīroṭu corinta alari katuppiṉ nelloṭu tayaṅka vatuvi naṉ maṇam kaḻinta piṉṟi eṉka.
(vi-ri) mitavi - poṅkal; kummāyamumām. uṇpār iṭiyaṟāmipaṟṟi amali niṟpa eṉṟāṉ. ñemiri - parappi eṉap piṟaviṉip poruṭṭu. viḷakkuṟuttu - tūymi ceytu eṉṟumām. kaṉiyiru ḷakaṉṟakāli yeṉṟamiyāl, muṟpakkam (pūrva pakkam) eṉpatu peṟṟām. kōḷ eṉṟatu īṇṭut tīya kōḷkaḷi. urōkaṇi eṉpataṉi varuvittu, viḻuppukaḻ nāḷāvatu urōkaṇi tiṅkaḷi aṭinta nāḷ eṉṉalumām. urōkaṇiyum tiṅkaḷum kūṭiya nāḷ kaliyāṇattiṟkuc ciṟantateṉpatu, `tiṅkaṭ cakaṭam vēṇṭiya tukaṭīr kūṭṭattu ...... vatuvi maṇṇiya makaḷir'1 eṉap piṉṉar in nūluḷḷum, `vāṉūr matiyam cakaṭaṉiya vāṉattuc, cāli yorumīṉ takiyāḷik kōvalaṉ, māmutu pārppāṉ maṟivaḻi kāṭṭiṭat tīvalam ceyvatu'1 eṉac cilappatikārat tuḷḷum varutalāṉ aṟiyappaṭum. kaliyāṇam ellārum pukutaṟku uriyatākaliṉ, `potu' eṉappaṭṭatu. piṇi - viruppam; `piṇiyum pēṇum peṭpiṉ poruḷa'2 eṉpavākaliṉ. nelloṭu tayaṅka eṉṟamiyāl, nellum corintami peṟṟām. vatuvi maṇamāvatu - kuḷippāṭṭal. kōṭik kaliṅkam- putup puṭivi. ōrpuṟan taḻīi : ōr, aci: yāḻa : aci talimakaṉ tōḻikku vāyiṉ maṟuttavaḻik kūṟiyatāyiṉ talivi ekkālattum eṉpāl iṉṉatōr aṉpuṭiyaḷ; avaḷ karuttaṟiyātu kūṟukiṉṟaṉi eṉṟāṉ eṉka.
(mē-ḷ) `kaṟpeṉap paṭuvatu'3 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, ic ceyyuḷik kāṭṭi, `itaṉuḷ vatuvikku ēṟṟa karaṇaṅkaḷ nikaḻnta vāṟum, tamar koṭuttavāṟuṅ kāṇka. cuṟṟañ cūḻntu niṟṟalāṉum tamar aṟiya maṇavaṟic cēṟalāṉum kaḷavāṟ curuṅki niṉṟa nāṇ ciṟantamiyip piṉṉart talimakaṉ viṉava, avaḷ maṟumoḻi koṭātu niṉṟamiyit talivaṉ tōḻikkuk kūṟiyavāṟu kāṇka. itaṉāṉē itu kaḷaviṉvaḻi nikaḻnta kaṟpāyiṟṟu eṉṟum,' `karaṇattiṉ amintu muṭinta kāli'4 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu `mukaṉikut toyyeṉa viṟiñciyōḷē' eṉpatu karaṇattiṉ amintu muṭintatu eṉṟum kūṟiṉar, nac.
`pukumukam purital'5 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, akamali yuvakiyaḷāki mukaṉikut, toyyeṉa viṟiñci yōḷē' eṉṟatu citivu piṟarkkiṉmi eṉṉum meyppāṭṭiṟku utāraṇamākak kāṭṭi, itu talimakaṉ aṟiya meyppaṭṭateṉpatu eṉṟum, `maṟi veḷip paṭutal tamariṟ peṟutal'6 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, `tamartara, ōriṟ kūṭi yuṭaṉpuṇar kaṅkul' eṉpatu,  tamariṟ peṟutal eṉṟum urittaṉar, pērā.
87. pāli
(7viṉimuṟṟi mīḷun talimakaṉ iṭiccurattut taṉ neñciṟkuc colliyatu.)
		tīntayir kaṭinta tiraḷkāl mattam
		kaṉṟuvāy cuvippa muṉṟil tūṅkum
		paṭalip pantarp pulvēy kurampi
		nalkūr cīṟūr ellit taṅkik
	5.	kuṭumi neṟṟi neṭumarac cēval
		talikkural viṭiyal pōki muṉāatu
		kaṭuṅkaṇ maṟavar kalkeḻu kuṟumpiṉ
		eḻunta taṇṇumi iṭaṅkaṭ pāṇi
		aruñcuram celvōr neñcam tuṇṇeṉak
	10.	kuṉṟucēr kavali icikkum attam
		naṉinī ṭuḻantaṉi maṉṉē ataṉāl
		uvaviṉi vāḻiya neñcē miyaṟa
		vikucuṭar viḷaṅkum vāṉtōy viyaṉakarc
		cuṇaṅkaṇi vaṉamuli nalampā rāṭṭit
	15.	tāḻiruṅ kūntaṉaṅ kātali
		nīḷami vaṉappiṉ tōḷumā raṇintē.
- maturip pērālavāyār.
(co-ḷ) 12. vāḻiya neñcē-;
1-4. tīm tayir kaṭinta tiraḷ kāl mattam - iṉiya tayirik kaṭinta tiraṇṭa taṇṭiṉiyuṭiya mattu, kaṉṟu vāy cuvippa muṉṟil tūṅkum - kaṉṟu taṉ vāyāṟ cuvittiṭa muṟṟattē toṅkum, paṭalip pantarp pulvēy kurampi - mara niḻalākiya pantariyum pullāl vēyappeṟṟa kuṭilkaḷiyumuṭiya, nalkūr cīṟūr - vaṟumippaṭṭa ciṟiya ūriṉkaṇ, ellit taṅki - iraviṟṟaṅki;
5-6. kuṭumi neṟṟi neṭumarac cēval - ceñcūṭu poruntiya neṟṟiyiyuṭiya nīṇṭa marattiluḷḷa cēvaliṉ, talikkural viṭiyaṟ pōki - mutaṟ kural eḻunta viṭiyaṟkāliyiṟ puṟappaṭṭuc ceṉṟu;
6-11. muṉāatu - paḻami yuṭiyatākiya, kaṭuṅkaṇ maṟavar kalkeḻu kuṟumpiṉ eḻunta - vaṉkaṇmiyuṭiya maṟavaratu kaṟkaḷ poruntiya kāṭṭaraṇkaḷil eḻunta, taṇṇumi iṭaṅkaṇ pāṇi - taṇṇumip paṟiyiṉ akaṉṟa kaṇṇiṉiṉ ṟeḻum oli, aruñcuram celvōr neñcam tuṇṇeṉa icikkum - ariya cura neṟiyē celvōr neñcu naṭukkuṟa olikkum, kuṉṟu cērka vali attam - kuṉṟiṉic cārnta kavartta neṟikaḷiyuṭiya kāṭṭil, naṉi nīṭu uḻantaṉimaṉ - nī mikavum neṭitu varuntiṉi;
11-16. ataṉāl - ātalāl, mi aṟa viku cuṭar viḷaṅkum vāṉṟōy viyaṉ nakar - iruḷ nīṅka viṭiviḷakku viḷaṅkukiṉṟa vāṉ aḷāviya nam periya māḷikiyiṉkaṇ, tāḻiruṅ kūntal nam kātali cuṇaṅku aṇi vaṉamuli nalam pārāṭṭi - tāḻnta kariya kūntaliyuṭiya nam kātaliyiṉ cuṇaṅku aṇinta aḻakiya muliyiṉ nalliṉpattip pārāṭṭi, nīḷ ami vaṉappiṉ tōḷum aṇintu- nīṇṭa mūṅkil pōṉṟa aḻakiṉiyuṭiya tōḷiṉiyum aṇintu, uva iṉi - ippoḻutu makiḻvāyāka.
(muṭipu) neñcē vāḻiya! nī cīṟūr ellit taṅki viṭiyaṟ pōki attam nīṭu uḻantaṉi maṉṉē; ataṉāl viyaṉakar, nam kātali muli nalam pārāṭṭi, tōḷum aṇintu iṉi uva.
(vi-ri) paṭali - taḻipparappu. paṭalip pantar - mara niḻalākiya pantar. viṉimuṟṟi mīḷkiṉṟāṉākaliṉ ippoḻutu uva eṉṟāṉ. mi - iruḷ. viku cuṭar - viṭiyumaḷavum eriyum viḷakku; viṭiviḷakku eṉappaṭum. tōḷumār : ār, aci.
88. kuṟiñci
(iravukkuṟi vanta talimakaṉ ciṟippuṟattāṉākat tōḻi colliyatu.)
		muticcuval kalitta mūric centiṉi
		ōṅkuvaṇarp peruṅkural uṇīiya pāṅkarp
		pakuvāyp pallip pāṭōrttuk kuṟukum
		puruvip paṉṟi varutiṟam nōkkik
	5.	kaṭuṅkik kāṉavaṉ kaḻutumicik koḷīiya
		neṭuñcuṭar viḷakkam nōkki vantunam
		naṭuṅkutuyar kaḷinta naṉṉa rāḷaṉ
		ceṉṟaṉaṉ kollō tāṉē kuṉṟat
		tirumpuli tolitta peruṅki yāṉik
	10.	kavuḷmali piḻitarum kāmar kaṭāam
		iruñciṟit toḻuti yārppa yāḻcet
		tiruṅkal viṭaraḷi acuṇam ōrkkum
		kāmpamal iṟumpil pāmpupaṭat tuvaṉṟik
		koṭuviral uḷiyam keṇṭum
	15.	vaṭuvāḻ puṟṟiṉa vaḻakkaru neṟiyē.
- 1īḻattup pūtaṉtēvaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 1-7. mutic cuval kalitta - paḻaṅkolliyākiya mēṭṭu nilattil taḻitta, mūric centiṉi - koḻutta centiṉiyiṉ, ōṅku vaṇarp peruṅ kural uṇīiya - uyarnta vaḷinta periya katiri uṇṇa, pakuvāyp palli pāṅkar pāṭu ōrttu - piḷanta vāyiṉiyuṭiya palli naṟpakkattē ceyta oliyāya nimittam uṇarntu, kuṟukum - aṇuka varum, puruvip paṉṟi varutiṟam nōkki - iḷimi poruntiya paṉṟiyiṉ varum vakiyiṉi nōkki, kaṭuṅkik kāṉavaṉ - valiya kiyiṉiyuṭiya tiṉippuṉaṅ kāppōṉ, kaḻutu micik koḷīiya - paraṇmītu koḷutti vitta, neṭuñcuṭar viḷakkam nōkki - nīṇṭa cuṭariṉ oḷiyiṉi nōkki, vantu nam naṭuṅku tuyar kaḷinta naṉṉarāḷaṉ - vantu namatu naṭukkattit tarum tuyariṉi oḻitta naṉmi yāḷaṉākiya nam talivaṉ;
8-15. kuṉṟattu irumpuli tolitta peruṅki yāṉi - kuṉṟiṉkaṇṇuḷḷa periya puliyik koṉṟa periya kiyiṉiyuṭiya yāṉiyiṉ, kavuḷ malivu iḻitarum kāmar kaṭāam - kaṉṉattiliruntu peruki iḻiyum aḻakiya matanīril, iruñciṟit toḻuti ārppa - kariya ciṟakiṉiyuṭiya vaṇṭiṉ kūṭṭam olikka, yāḻ cettu - ataṉi yāḻiciyeṉak karuti, iruṅkal viṭar aḷi acuṇam ōrkkum - periya maliyiṉ piḷappāya kukiyiluḷḷa acuṇaṅkaḷ uṟṟuk kēṭkum, kāmpu amal iṟumpil - mūṅkil niṟinta ciṟu kāṭṭil, pāmpu paṭa tuvaṉṟi - pāmpu iṟantupaṭa neruṅki, koṭu viral uḷiyam keṇṭum - vaḷinta viraliṉiyuṭiya karaṭi tōṇṭum, vaṭu vāḻ puṟṟiṉa - vaṭukkaḷ poruntiya puṟṟukkaḷi yuṭiyaṉavākiya, vaḻakku aruneṟi - vaḻaṅkutaṟku ariyaneṟiyiṉkaṇ, ceṉṟaṉaṉ kol - ceṉṟāṉ kollō!
(muṭipu) paṉṟi varutiṟa ṉōkkik kāṉavaṉ kaḻutumicik koḷīiya viḷakkam nōkki vantu, nam tuyarkaḷinta naṉṉarāḷaṉ, vaḻakkaru neṟi ceṉṟaṉaṉ kollō!
(vi-ri) pāṭu - ōci. puruvi - iḷami. kaḻutu - paraṇ. puliyik koṉṟa yāṉiyum, acuṇamum, pāmpiṉ puṟṟum, karaṭiyum uṭiya koṭu neṟiyiṟ ceṉṟāṉō eṉṟu varuntiṉaḷ eṉka. it taṉmiyāṉa vaḻiyilē vantu pōkiṉṟārōveṉa iraṅkik kūṟak kēṭṭu varivāṉāvatu payaṉāka iṅṅaṉam kūṟiṉāḷ. paṉṟi palli nimittam pārttuc cellutaluṇṭeṉṉuṅ karuttu, "eymmuḷ ḷaṉṉa parūumayi reruttiṟ, ceymmēvaṟ ciṟukaṭ paṉṟi, ōṅkumali viyaṉpuṉam paṭīiyar vīṅkupoṟi, nūḻi nuḻiyum poḻutiṟ ṟāḻātu, pāṅkarp pakkattup palli paṭṭeṉa, mella mellap piṟakkē peyarntutaṉ, kallaḷip paḷḷi vatiyum nāṭa"1 eṉa naṟṟiṇiyilum vantiruttal aṟintu makiḻaṟkuriyatu.
(u-ri) tiṉi nukartaṟkup paṉṟi nimittam pārttu varukiyic ceytēyum, kāṉavaṉ atu varutiṟamaṟintu cuṭar koḷuttiṉāṟ  pōla, tāmum pēṇi vantārē yāyiṉum varavu veḷippaṭum eṉṟavāṟu.
89. pāli
(makaṭ pōkkiya cevilittāy colliyatu.)
		teṟukatir ñāyiṟu naṭuniṉṟu kāytaliṉ
		uṟupeyal vaṟanta ōṭutēr naṉantali
		urutteḻu kurala 1kuṭiñic cēval
		pulcāy viṭarakam pulampa variya
	5.	kalleṟi iciyiṉ iraṭṭum āṅkaṇ
		ciḷvīṭu kaṟaṅkum ciṟiyili vēlat
		tūḻuṟu viḷineṟ ṟutirak kāḻiyar
		kavvip parappiṉ vevvuvarp poḻiyak
		kaḷari paranta kalneṭu maruṅkiṉ
	10.	viḷarūṉ tiṉṟa vīṅkucili maṟavar
		mipaṭu2 tiṇṭōḷ malira vāṭṭip
		poṟimali kaḻuti neṭuniṟi taḻīiya
		tiruntuvāḷ vayavar aruntali tumitta
		paṭupulāk kamaḻum ñāṭpil tuṭiyikut
	15.	taruṅkalan teṟutta perumpukal valattār
		vilkeḻu kuṟumpiṟ kōḷmuṟi pakukkuṅ
		kolli yirumpuṉam neṭiya eṉṉātu
		melleṉ cēvaṭi meliya ēka
		vallunaḷ kollō tāṉē tēmpey
	20.	taḷavuṟu tīmpāl alippavum uṇṇāḷ
		iṭumaṇaṟ 3pantaruḷ iyalum
		neṭumeṉ paṇittōḷ māa yōḷē.
-maturik kāñcip pulavar.
(co-ḷ) 19-22. tēm peytu aḷavuṟu tīmpāl - tēṉic corintu kalanta iṉiya pāli, alippavum uṇṇāḷ - alit tūṭṭavum uṇṇāḷāy, iṭu maṇal pantaruḷ iyalum - maṇal parappiya pantaruḷ aṅkum iṅkum ōṭum, neṭumeṉ paṇittōḷ māayōḷ - nīṇṭa melliya mūṅkil pōlum tōḷiṉiyuṭiya māmi niṟattiṉaḷākiya uṉ makaḷ;
1-2. teṟu katir ñāyiṟu - yāvaṟṟiyum aḻikkum katirkaḷi yuṭiya ñāyiṟu, naṭu niṉṟu kāytaliṉ - mutuvēṉiṟ kālattu iṭi nāṭkaḷil niṉṟu erittaliṉ, uṟupeyal vaṟanta - mikka maḻiyālāya nīr vaṟaṇṭa, ōṭu tēr naṉantali - pēyttēr ōṭum akaṉṟa iṭattiṉiyum;
3-5. uruttu eḻu kurala kuṭiñic cēval - vekuṇṭu eḻukiṉṟa kuraliṉiyuṭiya pērāntic cēval, pul cāy viṭar akam pulampa - puṟkaḷ oḻinta veṭippiṭaṅkaḷ taṉittiṭac ceṉṟu, variya kal eṟi iciyiṉ iraṭṭum - maliyiluḷḷa kaṟkaḷ uruṇṭu viḻum pōtu eḻum ōcipōla māṟi olikkum (iṭattiṉiyum);
6-9. ciṟi yili vēlattu - ciṟiya iliyiṉiyuṭiya vēla marattiṉ, ūḻ uṟu viḷi neṟṟu utira - muṟiyāka muṟṟi viḷinta neṟṟu utira, ciḷvīṭu kaṟaṅkum - ciḷvī ṭeṉṉum vaṇṭu olikkum, kavvip parappiṉ - āravāram poruntiya parappiṉiyuṭiyatum, kāḻiyar vevvuvarppu oḻiya - vaṇṇārkaḷ eṭukkum vevviya uvar maṇ oḻiya, kaḷari paranta - kaḷar maṇ parantatumākiya, kalneṭu maruṅkiṉ - kaṟkaḷi iṭiyē koṇṭa nīṇṭa iṭattiṉiyum uṭiyatum;
10-16. viḷar ūṉ tiṉṟa vīṅku cili maṟavar - koḻuppiṉi yuṭiya ūṉittiṉṟa vicikoṇṭa ciliyiṉarāya maṟavarkaḷ, mipaṭu tiṇ tōḷ malira - karumi poruntiya valiya tōḷkaḷ pūrikka, poṟi mali kaḻuti neṭuniri taḻīiya - pārammikka kaḻutikaḷiṉ nīṇṭa nirikaḷip piṉpaṟṟi varum, tiruntu vāḷvayavar vāṭṭi aruntali tumitta - ceppamuṭiya vāḷiṉik koṇṭa vīrarkaḷāya vaṇikarkaḷi vāṭṭi avarkaḷatu ariya taliyit tuṇitta, paṭu pulā kamaḻum ñāṭpil - mikka pulavu nāṟum pōrkkaḷattē, tuṭi ikuttu - tuṭiyiṉit tāḻak koṭṭi, aruṅkalam teṟutta perum pukal valattar - ariya aṇikalaṉ kaḷit tiṟiyākap peṟṟuk kuvitta periya pōr viruppiṉiyuṭiya veṉṟiyiṉar, vilkeḻu kuṟumpil kōḷ muṟi pakukkum - viṟkaḷ poruntiya araṇiṭattē avaravar koḷḷa vēṇṭiya muṟiyē pirittuk koṭuppatumākiya;
17-19. kolli irumpuṉam neṭiya eṉṉātu - kolliyiṉi yuṭiya periya kāṭukaḷi nīṇṭa tūrattaṉa eṉṟeṇṇātu, melleṉ cēvaṭi meliya ēka vallunaḷ kollō - metteṉṟirukkum civanta aṭi varuntac cellaṟku vaṉmiyuṭiyaḷ āvaḷō?
(muṭipu) tīmpāl alippavum uṇṇāḷ pantaruḷ iyalum mā ayōḷ, kolli yirum puṉam neṭiya veṉṉātu cēvaṭi meliya ēka vallunaḷ kollō?
ōṭutēr naṉantaliyiṉiyum kalleṟi yiciyiṉ iraṭṭum iṭattiṉiyum kaṉṉeṭu maruṅkiṉiyu muṭiya kolli irumpuṉam eṉavum, kōḷ muṟi pakukkum kolli irumpuṉam eṉavum kūṭṭuka.
(vi-ri) naṭuniṉṟu kāytaliṉ : naṭu - mutuvēṉiṟ kālattiṉ naṭukkūṟākiya nāṭkaḷ. tēr - pēyttēr. uṟupeyal `paranta' eṉpatu pāṭamāyiṉ, peyal (vaṟantu) ōṭu tērāyp paraviya eṉka. ciṟiya ili - ciṟiyili eṉa vikārappaṭṭatu, vēlattu : attu, cāriyi. kaṟaṅkum kavvi eṉavum, vāṭṭit tuṇitta eṉavum kūṭṭuka. malira āṭṭi eṉap pirittu, āṭṭi eṉaṟku alittu eṉṟurittalu mām. vāḷ vayavar eṉṟataṉāl vāṇikac cāttarum vīrarāyiruppar eṉpatu peṟṟām. avaruṭaṉ pōr purintu koṉṟār eṉpatu tōṉṟavē ñāṭpil eṉṟār. tēṉ kalanta pāl cuvi vīriyaṅkaḷāṟ ciṟakkum eṉpatu `pāloṭu tēṉ kalantaṟṟē'1 eṉpatu mutaliyavaṟṟāṉ aṟiyappaṭum. tēṉ kalanta pālit tāṉum accuṟutti ūṭṭavum uṇṇātu ōṭupavaḷ eṉa avaḷatu celvac ciṟappuk kūṟiyavāṟu. celvac ciṟārum ciṟumiyarum uṇavuṇṇa maṟukkuṅkāl, uṇpikkum tāyar avarkaṭku iṉiya katikaḷ kūṟiyum accuṟuttiyum uṇpittal iyalpu eṉka.
90. neytal
(pakaṟkuṟi vantu kaṇṇuṟṟu nīṅkun talimakaṉit tōḻi 
etirppaṭṭu niṉṟu iṟceṟip paṟivuṟīiyatu.)
		mūttō raṉṉa veṇṭalip puṇari
		iḷiyō rāṭum varimaṉi citikkum
		taḷiyaviḻ tāḻik kāṉalam peruntuṟic
		cilcevit tākiya puṇarcci alareḻa
	5.	ilvayiṉ ceṟittami aṟiyāy paṉṉāḷ
		varumuli varuttā ampakaṭṭu mārpil
		terumaral uḷḷamoṭu varuntum niṉvayiṉ
		nīṅku keṉṟiyāṉ yāṅṅaṉam moḻikō
		aruntiṟaṟ kaṭavuḷ cellūrk kuṇāatu
	10.	peruṅkaṭal muḻakkiṟ ṟāki yāṇar
		irumpiṭam paṭutta vaṭuvuṭi mukattar
		2kaṭuṅkaṇ kōcar niyamam āyiṉum
		uṟumeṉak koḷḷunar allar
		naṟunutal arivi pāciḻi viliyē.
- maturi marutaṉiḷa nākaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 1-8. taliva-, mūttōr aṉṉa veṇtalip puṇari - mutiyōri otta veḷḷiya taliyiyuṭiya kaṭal, iḷiyōr āṭum varimaṉi citikkum - iḷiya makaḷir viḷiyāṭum vaṇṭal maṉiyiṉi aḻikkum, taḷi aviḻ tāḻik kāṉalam peruntuṟi - kaṭṭaviḻnta maṭalkaḷ poruntiya tāḻikaḷi yuṭiya cōli poruntiya periya kaṭaṟṟuṟikkaṇṇē, cil cevittākiya puṇarcci - cilar cevippaṭṭa māttiriyākiya kūṭṭam, alareḻa - alarāki eṅkum parava, ilvayiṉ ceṟittami aṟiyāy - tāy taliviyi illiṭattē ceṟittamiyi aṟiyāyāki, palnāḷ - palanāḷum, am pakaṭṭu mārpiṉ - niṉatu aḻakiya perumiyiyuṭiya mārpiṉāl, varumuli varuttā - vaḷarum muliyiṉi yuṭiyāḷi varutti, terumaral uḷḷamoṭu varuntum - kalaṅkum uḷḷattōṭu varuntā niṟkum, niṉvayiṉ - niṉṉiṭattē, nīṅkuka eṉṟu yāṉ yāṅṅaṉam moḻikō - ik kaḷavoḻukkat tiṉiṉṟum nīṅkuvāyāka eṉṟu yāṉ eṅṅaṉam moḻivēṉ;
9-14. naṟu nutal arivi pāciḻi vili - (ivaḷ tantimār) naṟiya nutaliṉiyuṭiya ariviyāya ivaḷatu paciya aṇikaṭku viliyāka, arun tiṟal kaṭavuḷ cellūrk kuṇāatu - ariya valikoṇṭa teyvaṅkaḷiyuṭiya cellūriṉ kīḻppāliṉatāy, peruṅ kaṭal muḻakkiṟṟāki - periya kaṭalpōlum āravārattiṉi yuṭiyatākiya, irumpu iṭampaṭutta vaṭu uṭi mukattar - paṭikkalam iṭampaṭac ceytiṭṭa vaṭukkaḷiyuṭiya mukattiṉarāya, kaṭuṅkaṭ kōcar - añcāmiyiyuṭiya kōcarkaḷ (vāḻum), yāṇar niyamam āyiṉum - putu varuvāyiyuṭiya niyamam eṉṉum ūriṉik koṭuppiṉum, uṟum eṉak koḷḷunar allar - amiyum eṉak koḷvār allar.
(muṭipu) taliva! kāṉalam peruntuṟi cil cevittākiya puṇarcci alareḻa iṟ ceṟittami aṟiyāy, paṉṉāḷ ivaḷi varutti varuntum niṉvayiṉ, (ik kaḷavoḻukkam) nīṅkuka eṉṟu yāṅṅaṉam moḻikō! arivi pāciḻi vili kōcar niyamamāyiṉum uṟumeṉak koḷḷunar allar.
(vi-ri) `mūttōraṉṉa citikkum' eṉṟatu puṇari uruviṉāl mutiyōri yottum ciṟiyariṉ ceyki yuṭiya tāyiṟṟeṉa nayampaṭak kūṟiyavāṟu. varumuli, irumpu eṉpaṉa ākupeyar. mārpu kāraṇamāka varuntutaliṉ mārpiṉ varuttā eṉṟāḷ. em varutta maṟintu nīyē varitaṟku muyalutal muṟimi yeṉpāḷ, yāṉ yāṅṅaṉam moḻikō eṉṟāḷ. āki eṉṟataṉi ākiya eṉat tirikka. yāṇar niyamam eṉak kūṭṭuka. talivaṉ taliviyip poṉṉaṇintu varital vaḻakkātaliṉ arivi pāciḻi vili eṉṟāḷ. uṟumeṉak koḷḷunar allar eṉṟamiyāl, poruḷ mikak koṭukka vēṇṭumeṉat taliviyi arumi pārāṭṭiya vāṟu. aṉṟi, vili niyamam koṭuppiṉum koḷḷārātalāl avarkaḷ aṉpiṟkum uriyiyākuka eṉṟāḷ eṉalumām.
(u-ṟi) mūttōr iḷiyōri varuttāraṉṟē, atu ceyyātu puṇari iḷiyōr maṉi citittāṟpōla, periya aṟivuṭiya nīr ciṟiyēmākiya eṅkaḷi varuttāniṉṟīr eṉṟavāṟu.
(mē-ḷ) `nāṟṟamun tōṟṟamum'1 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, ic ceyyuḷ, `poruḷ mikak koṭuttal vēṇṭumeṉṟatu' eṉṟaṉar, nac.
91. pāli
(piriviṭi melinta talimakaḷit tōḻi vaṟpuṟuttiyatu.)
		viḷaṅkupakal utaviya palkatir ñāyiṟu
		vaḷaṅkeḻu māmali payaṅkeṭat teṟutaliṉ
		aruvi yāṉṟa peruvari maruṅkil
		cūrccuṉi tuḻii nīrppayaṅ kāṇātu
	5.	pāci tiṉṟa piṅkaṇ yāṉi
		ōypacip piṭiyō ṭorutiṟaṉ oṭuṅka
		vēykaṇ ṇuṭinta veyilavir naṉantali
		arumporuḷ vēṭkiyiṉ akaṉṟaṉa rāyiṉum
		perumpē raṉpiṉar tōḻi iruṅkēḻ
	10.	irali cēkkum paraluyar patukkik
		kaṭuṅkaṇ maḻavar kaḷavuḻa veḻunta
		neṭuṅkāl āciṉi oṭuṅkāṭ ṭumpar
		vicipiṇi muḻaviṉ kuṭṭuvaṉ kāppap
		paciyeṉa vaṟiyāp paṇipayil irukkit
	15.	taṭamarup perumi tāmari muṉiyiṉ
		muṭamutir palaviṉ koḻuniḻal vatiyum
		kuṭanāṭu peṟiṉum taviralar
		maṭamāṉ nōkkiniṉ māṇalam maṟantē.
- māmūlaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 1-9. tōḻi-, viḷaṅku pakal utaviya pal katir ñāyiṟu- ulakam viḷaṅkaṟkuk kāraṇamākiya pakaliṉit tanta pala katir
kaḷiyuṭiya ñāyiṟu, vaḷam keḻu māmali payam keṭat teṟutaliṉ- vaḷam vāynta periya maliyiṉ payaṉ keṭṭoḻiyak kāytaliṉ, aruvi āṉṟa peruvari maruṅkiṉ - aruvi illiyāṉa periya pakkamaliyiṉiṭattu, cūrccuṉi tuḻii nīrppayam kāṇātu, teyvattiṉiyuṭiya cuṉiyittuḻāvi nīrākiya payaṉik kāṇātu, pāci tiṉṟa piṅkaṇ yāṉi - pāciyiṉit tiṉṟa paciya kaṇṇiṉiyuṭiya āṇ yāṉi, ōy pacip piṭiyoṭu oru tiṟaṉ oṭuṅka - ayarviṉit tarum paciṉiyuṭiya peṇ yāṉiyiṉuṭaṉ  oru pakkattē oṭuṅkik kiṭakka, vēy kaṇ uṭinta veyil avir naṉantali - mūṅkilkaḷ kaṇukkaḷ piḷanta veyil viḷaṅkum akaṉṟa pāli nilattē, arum poruḷ vēṭkiyiṉ akaṉṟaṉar āyiṉum - ariya poruḷ viruppāl nam talivar nammip pirintu ceṉṟā rāyiṉum, perum pēraṉpiṉar - mikap periya aṉpiṉarākaliṉ;
9-18. iru kēḻ irali cēkkum paral uyar patukki - kariya niṟamuṭiya āṇmāṉkaḷ taṅkum paraliyuṭiya uyarnta kaṟkuviyalil, kaṭuṅkaṇ maḻavar kaḷavu uḻavu eḻunta - vaṉkaṇmiyiṉi yuṭiya maḻavarkaḷ kaḷavākiya uḻaviṟku eḻum iṭamākiya, neṭuṅkāl āciṉi oṭuṅkāṭṭu umpar - nīṇṭa aṭiyi yuṭiya īrappalā maraṅkaḷiyuṭiya oṭuṅkāṭu eṉṉum ūrkku appāl, vicipiṇi muḻaviṉ kuṭṭuvaṉ  kāppa - iṟukap piṇitta muḻaviṉi yuṭiya kuṭṭuvaṉ eṉpāṉ purattalāl, paci eṉa aṟiyā paṇi payil irukki - paci eṉali aṟiyāta marutavaḷam mikka ūrkaḷi yuṭiya, taṭa maruppu erumi tāmari muṉiyiṉ - vaḷinta kompiṉiyuṭiya erumi (mēynta) tāmariyi veṟukku māyiṉ, muṭam mutir palaviṉ koḻu niḻal vatiyum - vaḷivu mikka palāviṉatu koḻuviya niḻaṟkaṇṇē taṅkum, kuṭanāṭu peṟiṉum - kuṭanāṭṭiṉiyē peṟuvarāyiṉum, maṭamāṉ nōkki - iḷami vāynta māṉ pōlum pārviyiṉiyuṭiyāy, niṉ māṇ nalam maṟantu taviralar - niṉatu māṇpuṟṟa nalattiṉi maṟantu āṅkut taṅkuvārallar.
(muṭipu) tōḻi! māṉōkki! nam talivar veyilavir naṉantali arum poruḷ vēṭkiyiṉ akaṉṟaṉarāyiṉum perum pēraṉpiṉarākaliṉ kuṭanāṭu peṟiṉum niṉ māṇalam maṟantu āṅkut taviralar.
(vi-ri) mārikkaṇ uṇṭa nīrik kōṭiyil umiḻum iyalpiṉatākiya maliyiṉ payaṉ keṭat teṟum eṉa vēṉiliṉ vemmi mikuti kūṟiyavāṟāyiṟṟu. āṉṟa - illiyāṉa; akaṉṟa eṉpataṉ marūu. cuṉikkaṇ cūr uṟiyum eṉpataṉi, `cuṉiyuṟiyum, cūrmakaḷ mātō eṉṉumeṉ neñcē'1 eṉpataṉāṉum aṟika. cūr, accamumām. yāṉi pāciyit tiṉṟami nīrppaci karuti yeṉka. oṭu - oruvaki maram eṉpatum, ataṉmuṉ valleḻuttuvariṉ, melimikum eṉpatum, `utimarak kiḷavi melleḻuttu mikumē'2 `oṭumarak kiḷavi yutimara viyaṟṟē' eṉpavaṟṟāl aṟiyappaṭum. īṇṭu oṭu maraṅkaḷ cūḻntamiyiṉ oṭuṅkāṭu eṉappaṭṭa teṉka. irukkiyiyuṭiya kuṭanāṭu eṉka. erumi tāmari  muṉiyiṉ palaviṉ niḻalil vatiyum eṉṟatu; marutattiṟkum kuṟiñcikkum aṇimi kūṟiyapaṭiyām. erumi, tāmari yeṉṉum marutak karupporuḷkaḷ pālikkaṇ vantaṉa; eṉṉi? `ennila maruṅkiṟ pūvum puḷḷum, annilam poḻutoṭu vārā vāyiṉum, vanta nilattiṉ payatta vākum'1 eṉpavākaliṉ eṉka.
92. kuṟiñci
(iravukkuṟic ceṉṟu talimakaḷik kaṇṇuṟṟu nīṅkun talimakaṉip 
pakaṟkuṟi nērnta vāyppāṭṭāl tōḻi varivukaṭāyatu.)
		neṭumali yaṭukkam kaṇkeṭa miṉṉip
		paṭumaḻi poḻinta pāṉāṭ kaṅkul
		kuñcaram  naṭuṅkat tākkik koṭuvaric
		ceṅkaṇ irumpuli kuḻumuñ cāral
	5.	vāral vāḻiyar aiya nēriṟi
		neṭumeṉ paṇittōḷ ivaḷum yāṉum
		kāval kaṇṇiṉam tiṉiyē nāḷi
		mantiyum aṟiyā marampayil iṟumpiṉ
		oṇceṅ kāntaḷ aviḻnta āṅkaṇ
	10.	taṇpal aruvit tāḻnīr oruciṟi
		urumuccivan teṟinta uraṉaḻi pāmpiṉ
		tirumaṇi viḷakkiṟ peṟukuvi
		iruṇmeṉ kūntal ēmuṟu tuyilē.
- maturip pālāciriyar naṟṟāmaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 1-5. aiya-, vāḻiyar-, neṭumali aṭukkam kaṇkeṭa miṉṉi - uyarnta maliyiṉ pakkaṅkaḷil kaṇṇoḷi keṭa miṉṉi, paṭu maḻi poḻinta pālnāḷ kaṅkul - mikka maḻi corinta pāti yiravākiya iruḷil, kuñcaram naṭuṅkat tākki - yāṉiyi naṭuṅkumpaṭi tākki, koṭuvari ceṅkaṇ irumpuli kuḻumum cāral- vaḷinta kōṭukaḷiyum civanta kaṇṇiṉiyumuṭiya periya puli muḻaṅkum maliccāralilē, vāral - nī vārātiruppāyāka;
5-7. nēr iṟi neṭu meṉ paṇittōḷ ivaḷum yāṉum - melliya muṉ kiyiṉiyum nīṇṭa melliya mūṅkili yotta tōḷiṉiyumuṭiya ittaliviyum yāṉum, kāval kaṇṇiṉam tiṉi - tiṉippuṉaṅ kāttalik karutiyuḷḷēm;
7-13. nāḷi - nāḷip pakaṟkaṇ, mantiyum aṟiyā maram payil iṟumpiṉ - mantikaḷum ēṟi aṟiyamāṭṭāta maraṅkaḷ ceṟinta kāṭṭil, oṇ ceṅkāntaḷ aviḻnta āṅkaṇ - oḷḷiya ceṅkāttaḷ malarnta avviṭattē, taṇ pal aruvi tāḻ nīr oru ciṟi - palavāya taṇṇiya aruvikaḷ vīḻum cuṉiyiṉ orupuṟam, urumu civantu eṟinta uraṉ aḻi pāmpiṉ tirumaṇi viḷakkil - iṭi ciṉantu tākkaliṉ vali yaḻinta pāmpiṉatu aḻakiya talimaṇiyākiya viḷakkilē, iruḷ mel kūntal ēm uṟu tuyil - taliviyiṉ iruṇṭa melliya kūntalakattu iṉpam uṟum tuyili, peṟu kuvi - nī aṭivāy.
(muṭipu) aiya! vāḻiyar! maḻi poḻinta pāṉāṭkaṅkul kuñcaram tākkip puli kuḻumum cāral vāral; ivaḷum yāṉum tiṉi kāval kaṇṇiṉam; nāḷi iṟumpil kāntaḷ aviḻnta āṅkaṇ tāḻ nīr oru ciṟi pāmpiṉ tirumaṇi viḷakkil kūntal ēmuṟu tuyil peṟukuvi.
(vi-ri) nāḷi kāval kaṇṇiṉam eṉalumām. kōṭu vāḻ kuraṅku eṉappaṭuvatum, maramēṟun toḻiliṟ ciṟantatumākiya mantiyum ēṟi aṟiyāta eṉa marattiṉ uyarcci kūṟiyavāṟu. ummi ciṟappummi. `kuraṅkaṟi vārā marampayi liṟumpil'1 eṉap piṉṉar innūliṉum, `mantiyu maṟiyā marampayi loruciṟi,'2 `mantiyu maṟiyā maraṉpayi laṭukkattu'3 eṉap piṟanūlkaḷilum varutalum kāṇka. iṉi manti, eṉpataṟku ñāyiṟu eṉap poruḷ koṇṭu, parutiyiṉ katir nuḻiyalākātavāṟu maraṅkaḷ ceṟinta kāṭu eṉṉalumām. pakaṟkuṟi nērntāḷpōṟ kūṟiṉum, kāntaḷ aviḻnta āṅkaṇ aruvit tāḻnīr oruciṟi eṉṟamiyiṉ, pūkkoyya varuvārālum aruviyāṭa varuvārālum pakaṟkuṟiyum ariteṉpatu pulappaṭutti varivu kaṭāyiṉā ḷāyiṟṟu.
93. pāli
(viṉimuṟṟi mīḷaluṟun talimakaṉ iṭiccurattut taṉ neñciṟkuc colliyatu.)
		kēḷkē ṭūṉṟavum kiḷiñar āravum
		kēḷal kēḷir keḻīiyiṉar oḻukavum
		āḷviṉik ketiriya ūkkamoṭu pukalciṟan
		tāraṅ kaṇṇi aṭupōrc cōḻar
	5.	aṟaṅkeḻu nallavi uṟanti aṉṉa
		peṟalaru naṉkalaṉ eyti nāṭum
		ceyalaruñ ceyviṉi muṟṟiṉa māyiṉ
		araṇpala kaṭanta muraṇkoḷ tāṉi
		vāṭā vēmpiṉ vaḻuti kūṭal
	10.	nāḷaṅ kāṭi nāṟum naṟunutal
		nīḷiruṅ kūntal māa yōḷoṭu
		varikuyiṉ ṟaṉṉa vāṉtōy neṭunakar
		nurimukan taṉṉa meṉpūñ cēkki
		nivanta paḷḷi neṭuñcuṭar viḷakkattu
	15.	nalaṅkēḻ ākam pūṇvaṭup poṟippa
		muyaṅkukañ ceṉmō neñcē varinutal
		vayantikaḻ pimiḻtarum vāypuku kaṭāttu
		mīḷi moympoṭu nilaṉeṟiyāk kuṟuki
		āḷkōṭ piḻiyā añcuvaru taṭakkik
	20.	kaṭumpakaṭ ṭiyāṉi neṭuntērk kōti
		tirumā viyaṉakark karuvūr muṉṟuṟit
		teṇṇīr uyarkarik kaviiya
		taṇṇāṉ poruni maṇaliṉum palavē.
- kaṇakkāyaṉār makaṉār nakkīraṉār.
(co-ḷ) 16. neñcē-;
1-3. kēḷ kēṭu ūṉṟavum - uṟaviṉarik kēṭṭiṉi nīkkit tāṅkavum, kiḷiñar āravum - kiḷikaḷāyuḷḷār uṇṇavum, kēḷal kēḷir keḻiiyiṉar oḻukavum - notumalāḷar keḻutakiyiṉarāki oḻukavum vēṇṭi, āḷviṉikku etiriya ūk kamoṭu - poruḷīṭṭum muyaṟcikku ēṟṟatāya ūkkaṅkoṇṭu, pukal ciṟantu - viruppam mikku;
4-7. āram kaṇṇi aṭupōrc cōḻar - āttimāli yaṇinta aṭum pōriṉiyuṭiya cōḻaratu, aṟamkeḻu nallavi uṟanti aṉṉa - aṟam poruntiya nalla aviyiṉiyuṭiya uṟiyūri yotta, peṟal arum naṉkalaṉ eyti - peṟutaṟku ariya nalla aṇikalaṉkaḷi eyti, nāṭum ceyal arum ceyviṉi muṟṟiṉam āyiṉ - yāvarum virumpum ceytaṟkariya poruḷcey viṉiyiṉi nām muṭittaṉam ātaliṉ;
8-11. araṇ pala kaṭanta muraṇ koḷ tāṉi - pakivar araṇkaḷ palavaṟṟi veṉṟa māṟupāṭu koṇṭa tāṉiyiṉiyuṭiya, vāṭā vēmpiṉ vaḻuti - vāṭāta vēppa māliyiṉit taritta pāṇṭiyaṉatu, kūṭal nāḷ aṅkāṭi nāṟum - maturiyiṉ kālik kaṭi vītiyeṉa maṇakkum, naṟunutal nīḷiruḷ kūntal māayōḷoṭu - naṟiya neṟṟiyiṉiyum nīṇṭa kariya kūntaliṉiyu muṭiya māmi niṟattāḷāya nam taliviyuṭaṉ;
12-15. vari kuyiṉṟaṉṉa vāṉtōy neṭunakar - maliyik kuṭin tiyaṟṟiyati yotta vāṉi aḷāviya nīṇṭa maṉiyiṉ kaṇṇē, nuri mukantaṉṉa meṉpūñ cēkki - nuriyi mukantu vittāṟ pōṉṟa melliya pūkkaḷālāya paṭukkiyiyuṭiya, nivanta paḷḷi neṭuñcuṭar viḷakkattu - ōṅkiya kaṭṭiliṉiṭattu neṭiya viḷakkiṉ oḷiyilē, nalamkēḻ ākam pūṇ vaṭu poṟippa - naṉmi poruntiya nam mārpilē taliviyiṉ mārpakattup pūṇkaḷ vaṭukkaḷic ceyya;
16-23. varinutal - vari poruntiya neṟṟiyiṉiyum, vayam tikaḻpu imiḻtarum - vali viḷaṅkutaliṉ muḻaṅkum, vāy puku kaṭāttu - vāyiṟ pukum matattiṉiyum, mīḷi moympoṭu - kūṟṟuvaṉi yotta valiyuṭaṉ, nilaṉ eṟiyā - nilattiṉkaṇṇē curuṭṭi eṟintu, kuṟuki āḷ kōḷ piḻiyā añcuvaru taṭakki - neruṅki vantu āṭkaḷik kollutalit tappāta accam varukiṉṟa periya kiyiṉiyum uṭiya, kaṭum pakaṭṭu yāṉi - kaṭiya periya yāṉip paṭiyiṉiyum, neṭun tērk kōti - neṭiya tērppaṭiyiṉiyumuṭiya cēraṉatu, tirumā viyaṉ nakark karuvūr muṉṟuṟi - celvam mikka ciṟanta akaṉṟa nakarākiya karuvūriṉ tuṟimuṉ, teḷ nīr - teḷinta nīriṉiyuṭiya, taṇ āṉ poruni-  taṇṇiya āṉ poruni eṉṉum āṟṟiṉ, uyarkari kuviiya - uyarnta karik kaṇ kuvintuḷa, maṇaliṉum pala - maṇaliṉum palavāka;
16. muyaṅkukam ceṉmō - nām muyaṅkuvōm varuvāyāka.
(muṭipu) neñcē! ūṉṟavum, āravum, oḻukavum vēṇṭi, ūkkamoṭu pukal ciṟantu, uṟanti yaṉṉa naṉkalaṉ eytic ceyviṉi muṟṟiṉam ātalāl, māyōḷoṭu meṉpūñ cēkki nivanta paḷḷiyil, cuṭar viḷakkattu, āṉ poruni maṇaliṉum pala ākam vaṭup poṟippa muyaṅkukam ceṉmō.
(vi-ri) kēḷ - kēḷir eṉṉum poruṭṭu. ūṉṟal - tāṅkal. vaṟumiyuṟṟa kiḷiñar āravum eṉka. kēḷal kēḷir - ētilār. avar aṉpuṭiyarāy oḻukavum eṉṟapaṭi. `ārum vetirum cārum pīrum, melleḻuttu mikutal meypeṟat tōṉṟum'1 eṉṉum cūttirattu, meypeṟa eṉṟa ilēcāl `āraṅkaṇṇi' eṉa ammup peṟṟatu. uṟiyūr aviyil aṟam nili peṟṟa teṉpataṉi, `maṟaṅkeḻu cōḻar uṟanti yaviyat, taṟaṅkeṭa vaṟiyā tāṅku'2 `maṟaṅkeḻu cōḻar uṟanti aviyat, taṟamniṉṟu niliyiṟ ṟākaliṉ'1 eṉa varuvaṉavaṟṟāṉum aṟika. muṟṟi eytiṉam eṉṟu māṟutalumām. `eytiya' eṉap pāṭaṅkoṇṭu, eytutaṟkeṉṟu urippāru muḷar. āyiṉ - ātalāl. mīḷi - kūṟṟuvaṉ ātali, `mīḷi yuṭampiṭit taṭakki'2 eṉpataṟku nacciṉārkkiṉiyar uritta uriyāṉ uṇarka. mīḷi - maṟamumām. mataṅ koṇṭa yāṉi kiyi nilattiṉkaṇ eṟintu varum eṉpatu, `irumpiṇart taṭakki yirunilam cērttic, ciṉantikaḻ kaṭāañ cerukki maraṅkolpu, miyal vēḻam taṭaṅkaliṉ etirtara'3 eṉpataṉāl aṟiyappaṭum. karuvūr mēṟkaṭaṟ pakkattatu eṉpārum, cōḻa nāṭṭiṉ elliyi aṭuttuḷḷa koṅku
nāṭṭuk karuvūrē eṉpārum eṉa, irutiṟappaṭuvar varalāṟṟu ārāycciyāḷar. ic ceyyuḷuḷ cōḻar, pāṇṭiyar, cērar eṉṉum mūvēntarum, avar talinakarākiya uṟanti, kūṭal, karuvūr eṉpaṉavum vantuḷḷami aṟiyaṟpāṟṟu.
(mē-ḷ) `mēviya ciṟappiṉ'4 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, `kēḷkē ṭūṉṟavum...... pukal ciṟantu' eṉpatu, `vaṇikar poruḷ vayiṟ pirintavāṟu' eṉṟum, `puṟattiṇi maruṅkiṉ'5 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, ippāṭṭu, `puṟattiṇit talivar palarāy akattiṇikkaṇ aḷavi vantatu' eṉṟum, `karaṇattiṉamintu'6 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu `kēḷkē ṭūṉṟavum kiḷiñarāravum' eṉpatu, `avvaḻip perukiya ciṟappu' eṉṟum kūṟuvar, nac.
`piṟappē kuṭimi' eṉṉum cūttirattu, `kēḷkē ṭūṉṟavum ...... pukal ciṟantu'7 eṉpuḻi iṉṉa kāraṇattiṟ pirintu pōntu viṉi muṭittaṉamāyiṉum, avaḷi `muyaṅkukam ceṉmō' eṉṟamiyiṉ taṉ āḷviṉikkut takka peṇmiyāṉ avaḷ āṟṟiyiruntāḷ eṉpatūum karutiya karuttiṉāṟ kāmak kuṟippup piṟantamiyiṉ, aḵtu āṇmiyāyiṟṟu eṉavum, `maṟi veḷippaṭuttalum,'8 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, `nalaṅkē ḻākam.... ceṉmō neñcē' eṉpatu malital eṉavum kūṟuvar, pērā.
94. mulli
(viṉimuṟṟi mīḷun talimakaṉ tērppākaṟkuc colliyatu. talimakaṉ pāṅkaṟkuc coṟṟatūu mām.)
		tēmpaṭu cimayap pāṅkarp pampiya
		kuṟiyili mucuṇṭi veṇpūk kuḻiya
		9vāṉeṉap pūtta pāṉāṭ kaṅkul
		maṟitturūut tokutta paṟippuṟa iṭiyaṉ
	5.	taṇkamaḻ mulli tōṉṟiyoṭu virii
		vaṇṭupaṭat toṭutta nīrvār kaṇṇiyaṉ
		aitupaṭu koḷḷi aṅki kāyak
		kuṟunari yuḷampuṅ kūriruḷ neṭuviḷi
		ciṟukaṭ paṉṟip peruniri kaṭiya
	10.	mutippuṉaṅ kāvalar niṉintirun tūtum
		karuṅkōṭ ṭōciyo ṭoruṅkuvan ticikkum
		vaṉpulak kāṭṭunāṭ ṭatuvē aṉpukalan
		tārvañ ciṟanta cāyal
		irumpal kūntal tiruntiḻi yūrē.
- naṉpalūrc ciṟumētāviyār.
(co-ḷ) 12-14. aṉpu kalantu ārvam ciṟanta - aṉpāl uḷḷam kalantu viruppam mikka, cāyal - meṉmit taṉmiyiyum, irum pal kūntal - kariya palavākiya kūntaliṉiyum uṭiya, tiruntu iḻi - tiruntiya iḻiyiṉiyuṭiya taliviyiṉatu, ūr - ūrāṉatu;
1-8. tēmpaṭu cimayap pāṅkarp pampiya kuviili mucuṇṭi veṇpū - tēṉ aṭi poruntiya uccimaliyiṉ pakkaliṟ ceṟinta kuvinta ilikaḷiyuṭiya mucuṇṭiyiṉ veḷḷiya pūkkaḷ, vāṉ eṉak kuḻiyap pūtta pāl nāḷ kaṅkul - vāṉamāṉatu mīṉippūttatu pōlak  kuḻintiṭap pūtta naṭuiraviṉ iruḷilē, maṟit turūu tokutta paṟippuṟa iṭiyaṉ - āṭṭuk kuṭṭikaḷic cērat tokuttu vittuḷa ōlippāyi mutukiṟ koṇṭa iṭiyaṉ, taṇkamaḻ mulli tōṉṟiyoṭu virii - taṇṇiya kamaḻum mullip pūviṉit tōṉṟip pūvuṭaṉ iṇittu, vaṇṭupaṭat toṭutta nīrvār kaṇṇiyaṉ - vaṇṭukaḷ moykkat toṭutta nīr oḻukukiṉṟa kaṇṇiyaṉāy, aitu paṭu koḷḷi aṅki kāya - meṉmi vāynta koḷḷiyiṉ tīyil akaṅki kāyntiṭa, kūr iruḷ kuṟu nari uḷampum neṭuviḷi - mikka iruḷil kuṟunari
kaḷi alittōṭṭum nīṇṭa oli;
9-12. ciṟu kaṇ paṉṟip peruniri kaṭiya - ciṟiya kaṇṇiṉi yuṭiya paṉṟiyiṉ peruṅkūṭṭatti ōṭṭaṟku, mutippuṉaṅ kāvalar - muṟṟiya tiṉippuṉam kāttiruppōr, niṉittiruntu ūtum karuṅkōṭṭu ōciyōṭu - avi varuṅkālatti eṇṇiyiruntu ūtum periya kompiṉ ōciyoṭu, oruṅkuvan ticikkum - oru cēra vantolikkum, vaṉpulak kāṭṭu nāṭṭatuvē - vaṉpulamākiya kāṭṭiṉ akattatākiya nāṭṭiṉiṭattatākum;(pāka! virintu tēriṉic celuttuvāyāka.)
(muṭipu) (pāka!) tiruntiḻiyūr, kaṅkulil iṭiyaṉ kuṟu nari uḷampum neṭuviḷi puṉaṅ kāvalar ūtum kōṭṭōci yoṭu oruṅkuvan ticikkum kāṭṭu nāṭṭatuvē. (ākaliṉ tēri virintu celuttuvāyāka.)
(vi-ri) tēm - tēṉ; tēṉiṟāl. kuḻital - taḻittal. vāṉ eṉap pūtta - vāṉ mīṉip pūttāṟpōlat tōṉṟumāṟu pūtta eṉṟu viritturikka. turūu - cemmaṟiyāṭu. maṟi - kuṭṭi. paṟi - maḻiyit taṭuppataṟku olippāyāl vaḷittu iyaṟṟiya kuṭali. tokutta iṭiyaṉ eṉa iyiyum. koḷḷiyil kiyik kāyttik koṇṭu nariyi ōṭṭuvaṉ eṉka. kuṟu nari - āṭṭu maṟiyi vuvutaṟku varuvatu. kuṟu nari, neṭu viḷi, ciṟu kaṇ, peruniri eṉpaṉa muraṇ eṉṉum aṇi. vaṉpulam - kuṟiñciyum mulliyum. aṉpu - oruvari yoruvar iṉṟiyamiyāmi. ārvam - viruppam.
talimakaṉ pāṅkaṟkuc coṟṟatu eṉpatu tuṟiyāyiṉ kaḷavuk kālattup pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭattut talivaṉ pāṅkaṟkut taliviyiṉ iṭaṅ kūṟutalām. itu mulliyiṟ kaḷavu nikaḻnta
tātaliṉ, tiṇi mayakkamām, `tiṇimayak kuṟutalum kaṭinili yilavē'1 eṉpavākaliṉ.
(mē-ḷ) `puṇartal pirital'2 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, ic ceyyuḷ, `iruttal nimittamām; ikkālam varuntuṇiyum āṟṟiṉāḷ eṉat tāṉ varuntutaliṉ' eṉṟār, nac.
95. pāli
(pōkkuṭaṉ paṭṭa talimakaḷ tōḻikkuc colliyatu.)
		pipayap 3pacantaṉṟu nutaluñ cāay
		aitā kiṉṟeṉ taḷirpuri mēṉiyum
		palarum aṟiyat tikaḻtarum avalamum
		uyirkoṭu kaḻiyiṉ allati niṉiyiṉ
	5.	evaṉō vāḻi tōḻi porikāl
		pokuṭṭari yiruppik kuvikulik kaḻaṉṟa
		āli yoppiṉ tūmpuṭit tiraḷvī
		āṟucel vampalar nīḷiṭi yaḻuṅka
		īṉal eṇkiṉ iruṅkiḷi kavarum
	10.	4curampala kaṭantōrk kiraṅkupa eṉṉār
		kuvi mēvala rāki ivvūr
		niriyap peṇṭir iṉṉā kūṟupa
		puriya alleṉ makaṭkeṉap parii
		nammuṇarn tāṟiya koḷki
	15.	aṉṉi muṉṉaryām eṉṉitaṟ paṭalē.
- ōrōṭōkattuk kantarattaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 1-5. tōḻi vāḻi-, pi payap pacantaṉṟu nutalum - eṉ neṟṟiyum mella mellap pacantatu, cāay aitu ākiṉṟu eṉ taḷir puri mēṉiyum - taḷiri yotta eṉ mēṉiyum nuṇuki mellitākiṉṟatu, palarum aṟiyat tikaḻtarum avalamum - eṉ tuyaramum palarum aṟiyumpaṭi viḷaṅkit tōṉṟum, uyirkoṭu kaḻiyiṉ allati - ivi eṉ uyirik koṇṭu pōvatallatu, niṉiyiṉ evaṉō - āyumiṭattu vēṟu eṉ ceyvaṉa;
5-10. pori kāl pokuṭṭu ari iruppi - porinta aṭiyiṉiyum koṭṭikaḷiyuṭiya ariyiṉiyumuṭiya iruppiyiṉatu, kuvi kulik kaḻaṉṟa - kuvinta kuliyiṉiṉṟum kaḻaṉṟa,  āli oppiṉ tūmpu uṭit tiraḷ vī - paṉikkaṭṭi pōlum uṭṭuḷi yiṉiyuṭiya tiraṇṭa pūkkaḷi, āṟu cel vampalar nīḷiṭi aḻuṅka - vaḻic cellum putiyar annīṇṭa neṟiyiṭattē añcip pōkkiṉit tavira, īṉal eṇkiṉ iruṅkiḷi kavarum - īṉṟa karaṭi kaḷiṉ peruṅ kūṭṭam kavarntuṇṇukiṉṟa, curam pala kaṭantōrkku iraṅkupa eṉṉār - pala curaṅkaḷiyum kaṭantu ceṉṟa tam talivar poruṭṭut taliviyar iraṅkuvarātal iyalpeṉṟu niṉiyārāy;
11-12. kuvi mēvalar āki - alar tūṟṟalē virumpuvārāki, ivvūr niriyap peṇṭir iṉṉā kūṟuva - ivvūriluḷḷa iḻinta iyalpuṭiya peṇṭir kūṟuvaṉavāya iṉṉāta coṟkaḷ;
13-15. puriya alla eṉ makaṭku eṉa - eṉ makaḷukkup poruntuvaṉa alla eṉṟu, parii - teyvattip paravi, nam uṇarntu - namatu kaḷavoḻukkattiṉi uṇarntu vittu, āṟiya koḷki aṉṉi muṉṉar - amitiyuṟṟirukkum koḷki yiṉiyuṭiya nam aṉṉi muṉ, yām eṉitaṟ paṭal - yām ik kaḷavoḻukkattiṟ paṭṭu oḻukal eṅṅaṉam iyalum?
(muṭipu) tōḻi! vāḻi! eṉ nutalum pacantaṉṟu; mēṉiyum aitākiṉṟu; avalamum tikaḻtarum; (ivi) uyirkoṭu kaḻiyiṉ allati niṉiyiṉ evaṉō? curam pala kaṭantōrkku iraṅkupa eṉṉār, kuvi mēvalarāki niriyap peṇṭir iṉṉā kūṟupa, eṉ makaṭkup puriya allaveṉa, parii āṟiya koḷki aṉṉi muṉṉar yām itaṟpaṭal eṉ?
(vi-ri) pacanta eṉṉum pāṭattiṟku, pacanta nutalum aitākiṉṟa mēṉiyum tikaḻtarum avalamum eṉap peyareccat toṭarkaḷākak koḷka. itaṟku ākiṉṟu eṉpatu vikāram. allati - allatu : īṟu tirintatu. pacappu mutaliyaṉa uyir koṇṭē kaḻiyum eṉpatu karuttu. vīyi eṇkiṉ kiḷi kavarum eṉka. īṉṟa karaṭikaḷ cīṟum iyalpiṉavākaliṉ vampalar aḻuṅkuvar eṉka. puriya alla - uyarvāvaṉa alla eṉalumām. parii - teyvattip parāvi. aṉṉi, taṉ makaḷ paḻiyoṭu paṭāta iṉpa vāḻvu peṟal vēṇṭumeṉat teyvattip paraviṉaḷ. `namatu āṟṟā nilimi yuṇarntu nammic ciṉavātu amintuḷḷa aṉṉiyiṉ muṉpu nām mēlum ivvāṟu oḻukutal poruntātu eṉṟāḷ.'
(mē-ḷ) `eñci yōrkkum eñcuta lilavē'1 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, icceyyuḷi eṭuttuk kāṭṭi, itu pōkkuṭaṉ paṭṭami talivi tōḻik kurittatu eṉṟaṉar, nac.
96. marutam
(tōḻi vāyiṉ maṟuttatu)
		naṟavuṇ maṇṭi nuṭakkaliṉ iṟavukalittup
		pūṭṭaṟu villil kūṭṭumutal teṟikkum
		paḻaṉap poyki aṭikarip pirampiṉ
		aravāy aṉṉa ammuḷ neṭuṅkoṭi
	5.	aruvi āmpal akalaṭi tuṭakki
		acivaral vāṭi tūkkaliṉ ūtuli
		vicivāṅku tōliṉ vīṅkupu ñekiḻuṅ
		kaḻaṉiyam paṭappik kāñci yūra
		oṇtoṭi yāyat tuḷḷunī nayantu
	10.	koṇṭaṉi yeṉpavōr kuṟumakaḷ atuvē
		cempoṟ cilampiṉ ceṟinta kuṟaṅkiṉ
		aṅkaluḻ māmi2 yaḵti tanti
		aṇṇal yāṉi aṭupōrc cōḻar
		veṇṇel vippiṟ paruvūrp paṟantali
	15.	iruperu vēntarum 3porutukaḷat toḻiya
		oḷiṟuvāḷ nallamark kaṭanta ñāṉṟik
		kaḷiṟukavar kampali pōla
		alarā kiṉṟatu palarvāyp paṭṭē.
-marutam pāṭiya iḷaṅkaṭuṅkō.
(co-ḷ) 1-8. naṟavu uṇ maṇṭi nuṭakkaliṉ iṟavu kalittu - kaḷḷuṇṭa kalam kaḻuvap paṭutaliṉ (annīriyuṇṭa) iṟā mīṉ cerukki, pūṭṭu aṟu villiṉ kūṭṭu mutal teṟikkum - pūṭṭiya nāṇ aṟṟa vil teṟippatupōla neṟ kūṭukaḷiṉ aṭikaḷiṟ ṟuḷḷi viḻum iṭamākiya, paḻaṉap poyki aṭikari - marutanilattup poykiyiṉ aṭikariyiluḷḷa, pirampiṉ ara vāy aṉṉa ammuḷ neṭuṅ koṭi - pirampiṉatu arattiṉ vāy pōṉṟa aḻakiya muṭkaḷiyuṭiya nīṇṭa koṭi, aruvi āmpal akal aṭi tuṭakki - nīrkkuṟi vaṟṟa āmpalatu akaṉṟa iliyiṉic cuṟṟiṭa (avviliyi), aci varal vāṭi tūkkaliṉ - acintuvarum vāṭikkāṟṟu (viṭṭu viṭṭup pukuntu) acittaliṉ, ūtu uli vici vāṅku tōliṉ vīṅkupu ñekiḻum - (avvili) ūtappeṟum kollaṉ ulikkaḷattu vicittu iḻuttuviṭum turuttiyip pōlap puṭittuc curuṅkum, kaḻaṉi am paṭappik kāñci ūra - vayalkaḷiyum tōṭṭaṅkaḷiyu muṭiya kāñci maraṅkaḷ mikka ūriyuṭiya talivaṉē!,
9-10. oṇtoṭi āyattuḷḷum - oḷḷiya toṭiyiṉiyuṭiya parattiyar kūṭṭattiṉuḷḷum, nī ōr kuṟumakaḷ nayantu koṇṭaṉi eṉpa - nī oru iḷiya makaḷi virumpi maṇantaṉi eṉṟu ūrār kūṟuvārāyiṉar;
10-18. atuvē - ak kūṟṟu, cempoṉ cilampiṉ - civanta poṉṉālāya cilampiṉiyum, ceṟinta kuṟaṅkiṉ - neruṅkiya tuṭiyiṉiyum, am kaluḻ māmi - aḻaku oḻukum māmi niṟattiṉiyum uṭiya, aḵti tanti - aḵti eṉpāṭkut tantiyarākiya, aṇṇal yāṉi aṭupōrc cōḻar - perumi taṅkiya yāṉiyiyum vellum pōriṉiyumuṭiya cōḻar, veṇṇel vippil paruvūrp paṟantali - veṇṇel viḷiyum iṭaṅkaḷi yuṭiya paruvūrp pōrk kaḷattē, iru peru vēntarum porutu kaḷattu oḻiya - tammoṭu poruta ēṉic cērar pāṇṭiyarāya iruperu vēntarkaḷum pōr ceytu kaḷattiṉiṭattē paṭṭoḻiya, oḷiṟu vāḷ nal amar kaṭanta ñāṉṟi - viḷaṅkum vāḷiṉāṟ ceyyum nalla pōriṉi veṉṟa poḻutu, kaḷiṟu kavar kampali pōla - ap paki vēntarkaḷatu kaḷiṟukaḷik kavarntu koṇṭakāli eḻunta āravāram pōla, palar vāyppaṭṭu alar ākiṉṟatu - palar vāyilum porunti yeḻuntu alar ākā niṉṟatu.
(muṭipu) ūra! nī ōr kuṟumakaḷ koṇṭaṉi yeṉpa. atu, cōḻar paruvūrp paṟantaliyil iruperu vēntarum porutu oḻiya, vāḷamark kaṭanta ñāṉṟik kaḷiṟu kavar kampali pōla alar ākiṉṟatu.
(vi-ri) maṇṭi ikkālattu monti eṉa vaḻaṅkappaṭu
kiṉṟatu. nuṭakkal - kaḻuval. annīri yuṇṭa eṉa varuvitturikka. naṟavu kalanta nīrātaliṉ uṇṭu kaḷippatāyiṟṟu. kūṭṭu mutaṟ ṟeṟikkum eṉṟatu vaḷamikuti kūṟiyavāṟām. teṟikkum ñekiḻum eṉṉum peyareccaṅkaḷ iṭappeyar koṇṭaṉa. pirampiṉ neṭuṅkoṭi eṉka. kavar kampali peyareccam kāriyap peyar koṇṭatu. kiṉṟu: iṭinili; piṟkāla vaḻakkākaliṉ, alarā kiṉṟu eṉpatu pāṭamātal vēṇṭum; aṉṟi, atu pakutip poruḷ vikuti eṉalumām.
(u-ṟi) `naṟavuṇ maṇṭi...... kāñciyūra' eṉṟatu, parattiyar cēriyic cūḻntu tiriyum pāṇaṉ kāmañ cālā iḷamiyō ḷākiya parattiyit talimakaṉōṭu kūṭṭat talimakaṉ avaḷi muyaṅkiya viṭattu avaḷ kaḷippuṟṟu, nīṅkiyavaḻi melikiṉṟāḷ eṉṟapaṭi. itaṉkaṇ poykiyip parattiyar cēriyākavum, muḷḷuṭip pirampiṉ koṭiyi ataṉic cūḻntu tiriyum neñcu valiya pāṇaṉākavum, āmpal iliyik kāmañcālāk kuṟumakaḷākavum, ataṉik koṭi tuṭakkiyataṉip pāṇaṉ avaḷit talimakaṉōṭu kūṭṭiyatākavum, vāṭiyit tali makaṉākavum, atu tūkkuntōṟum avvili vīṅkit tūkkātavaḻi nekiḻntataṉi, talimakaṉ avaḷi muyaṅkiyavaḻi avaḷ kaḷip puṟṟu, nīṅkiya vaḻi melinta taṉmiyākavum koḷka. maṟṟum, itil `naṟavuṇ maṇṭi...... kūṭṭu mutaṟ ṟeṟikkum' eṉṟatu naṟavu kalanta iḻinta nīriyuṇṭa iṟāl ac cerukkiṉāl kūṭṭumutaṟ ṟeṟittatu pōlap parattiyariṉ iḻinta iṉpatti nukarnta talivaṉum cerukkuṟṟut taṉakkup puṟampākiya vēṟiṭattē taṅkik kiṭakkiṉṟa taṉmiyākavuṅ koḷka.
(mē-ḷ) `pullutal mayakkum'1 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, `oṇṭoṭi yāyat tuḷḷunī nayantu, koṇṭaṉi yeṉpavōr kuṟumakaḷ eṉak kāmañ cālā iḷamiyōḷik kūṟiṟṟu' eṉṟaṉar, nac.
`teyva mañcal'2 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, `kaḷiṟu kavar kampali pōla, alarākiṉṟatu palar vāyp paṭṭē' eṉpatu puṟañcol māṇāk kiḷavikku utāraṇamākum eṉṟaṉar, pērā.
`orumi cuṭṭiya'3 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, `paṉmik kāku miṭaṉumā ruṇṭē' eṉpatu, orumic col paṉmic collōṭu toṭar taṟkup poruntu miṭamu muṇṭeṉpatūum paṭa niṉṟamiyāl, `aḵti tanti ...... cōḻar' eṉa orumic col paṉmic collōṭu toṭartaluṅ kaṇṭukoḷka. īṇṭu orumic col paṉmic collōṭu mayaṅkuta luṭimiyāṉ, `oruvarik kūṟum paṉmik kiḷavi' eṉpuḻi aṭaṅkāmi yaṟika eṉṟaṉar cēṉāvariyar. ac cūttirattu, `aḵti tanti........ cōḻar' eṉpuḻic cōḻarellārum aḵtikkut tantiyām muṟiyarāy niṟṟaliṉ, `tanti' eṉṉum orumi, cōḻar eṉṉum paṉmiyōṭu taṉittaṉi ceṉṟu kūṭutaliṉ vaḻuviṉṟēṉum īṟṟup paṉmipaṟṟi vaḻuvamittār eṉṟaṉar, nac.
97. pāli
(vaṟpuṟukkun tōḻikkut talimakaḷ colliyatu.)
		kaḷḷiyaṅ kāṭṭa puḷḷiyam poṟikkali
		vaṟaṉuṟal aṅkō ṭutira valaṅkaṭantu
		pulavuppuli tuṟanta kalavukkaḻi kaṭumuṭi
		iravukkuṟum palaṟa nūṟi niripakut
	5.	tiruṅkal muṭakkart tiṟṟi keṇṭum
		kolivil āṭavar pōlap 1palavuṭaṉ
		peruntali yeruviyoṭu paruntuvan tirukkum
		aruñcuram iṟanta koṭiyōrk kalkalum
		iruṅkaḻi iṟumpiṉ āyntukoṇ ṭaṟutta
	10.	nuṇaṅkukaṇ ciṟukōl vaṇaṅkiṟi makaḷiro
		ṭakavunarp puranta aṉpiṉ kaḻaltoṭi
		naṟavumakiḻ irukki naṉṉaṉ vēṇmāṉ
		vayali vēli viyalūr aṉṉaniṉ
		alarmuli yākam pulampap palaniṉin
	15.	tāḻēl eṉṟi tōḻi yāḻaveṉ
		kaṇpaṉi niṟuttal eḷitō kuravumalarn
		taṟcira nīṅkiya 2arumpata vēṉil
		aṟalavir vārmaṇal akaliyāṟ ṟaṭikarit
		tuṟiyaṇi 3marutu tokalkoḷa vōṅkik
	20.	kaliḻtaḷir aṇinta iruñciṉi māat
		tiṇartati putuppū niritta poṅkarp
		pukipuri ammañ cūra
		nukarkuyil akavum kuralkēṭ pōrkkē.
- 4māmūlaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 15. tōḻi-;
1-3. kaḷḷi am kāṭṭa puḷḷi am poṟik kali - kaḷḷik kāṭṭilēyuḷḷa puḷḷikaḷāya poṟikaḷiyuṭiya kalimāṉi, vaṟaṉ uṟal am kōṭu utira valaṅ kaṭantu - ataṉ vaṟaṭciyuṟṟa aḻakiya kompu citarntoḻiya ataṉ ōṭṭattiṉi veṉṟu paṟṟittiṉṟa, pulavup puli tuṟanta kalavuk kaḻi kaṭumuṭi - pulāl viruppuṭiya puli kiviṭṭup pōṉa mūṭṭu vāy oḻinta kaṭiya muṭināṟṟattiṉiyuṭiya taci kiṭakku miṭattē;
4-6. iravu kuṟumpu alaṟa nūṟi - iravilē kāṭṭaraṇ kaḷiluḷḷār alaṟa avarkaḷik koṉṟu, niri pakuttu - tām koṇṭa nirikaḷip pakuttuk koṇṭu, iruṅ kal muṭukkar - periya kaṟpāṟiyiṉ muṭukkilē, tiṟṟi keṇṭum - taciyiṉi aṟuttut tiṉṉum, koli vil āṭavarpōla - kolit
toḻil valla villiṉiyuṭiya veṭci vīrar pōla;
6-8. peruntali eruviyoṭu paruntu pala uṭaṉ vantirukkum - periya taliyiṉiyuṭiya kaḻukukaḷōṭu paruntukaḷ palavum oruṅkē vantu cūḻntirukkum, aruñ curam iṟanta koṭiyōrkku - ariya curaneṟiyik kaṭantu ceṉṟa koṭiyōrāya nam talivariṉ piriviṟkāka, alkalum - nāṭōṟum;
9-15. iruṅ kaḻi iṟumpiṉ - periya mūṅkilkaḷiyuṭiya ciṟu kāṭṭil, āyntu koṇṭu aṟutta - ārāyntu koṇṭu aṟuttiṭṭa, nuṇaṅku kaṇ ciṟu kōl - ciṟiya kaṇukkaḷiyuṭiya nuṇṇiya kōliṉik koṇṭa, vaṇaṅku iṟi makaḷiroṭu - vaḷinta muṉ kiyiṉiyuṭiya viṟaliyaroṭu, akavunar puranta aṉpiṉ - pāṇarkaḷip purantiṭum aṉpiṉiyum, kaḻaltoṭi naṟavu makiḻ irukki - kaḻalum toṭiyiṉiyum kaḷḷuṇṭu makiḻum irukkiyiṉiyumuṭiya, naṉṉaṉ vēṇmāṉ - naṉṉaṉ vēṇ
māṉatu, vayali vēli viyalūr aṉṉa - vayalikkoṭi paṭarnta vēlikaḷiyuṭiya viyalūriṉi yotta, niṉ alar muli ākam pulampa - niṉatu paranta muliyiyuṭiya mārpakam taṉittu varunta, pala niṉintu āḻēl eṉṟi - palavum niṉintu tuṉpattil āḻntiṭātē eṉṉāniṉṟāy; eṉ - iḵteṉṉi!,
16-23. kuravu malarntu aṟciram nīṅkiya arumpatavēṉil - kuravam malarntu muṉpaṉikkālam nīṅkap peṟṟa ariya cevviyiyuṭiya iḷavēṉiṟ kālattē, aṟal avir vār maṇal akal yāṟṟu aṭikari - aṟal viḷaṅkum nīṇṭa maṇaliyuṭiya akaṉṟa āṟṟiṉ kariyiṭattē, tuṟi aṇi marutu tokalkoḷa ōṅki- tuṟiyi aḻaku ceyyum maruta maraṅkaḷoṭu tokutiyāka uyarntu, kaliḻtaḷir aṇinta irumciṉi māattu - aḻaku oḻukum taḷirik koṇṭa periya kiḷikaḷiyuṭiya mā marattiṉ, iṇartati putup pū niritta poṅkar - kottukkaḷāka neruṅkiya putiya pūkkaḷ ceṟinta cōlikaḷilē, puki puri am mañcu ūra - pukiyiṉi yotta aḻakiya veṇ mēkaṅkaḷ tavaḻa, nukar kuyil akavum kural kēṭpōrkku - (avviṉpatti) nukarum kuyilkaḷ pāṭum  kuraliṉik kēṭpōrukku, kaṇpaṉi niṟuttal eḷitō - kaṇṇiṉ nīri niṟuttutal eḷitākuṅkol?
(muṭipu) tōḻi! aruñcuram iṟanta koṭiyōrkku viyalūraṉṉa niṉ alarmuli yākam pulampa āḻēl eṉṟi; eṉ? vēṉilil yāṟṟiṉ aṭikarip poṅkaril mañcu ūrak kuyil akavum kural kēṭpōrkkuk kaṇpaṉi niṟuttal eḷitō?
niri pakuttut tiṟṟi keṇṭum āṭavar pōla, puli kaliyiṉ valaṅ kaṭantu tuṟanta muṭikkaṇ eruviyoṭu paruntu vantirukkum aruñcuram eṉa iyiyum.
(vi-ri) kaḷḷiyam.... poṟikkali; am: acikaḷ: valam - veṉṟi, ōṭum veṉṟi, kaṭantu eṉpataṉpiṉ paṟṟik koṉṟu tiṉṟa eṉpataṉi viritturikka. muṭi - muṭi nāṟṟa muṭiya tacikku ākupeyar. muṭiyiṭattē eruviyoṭu paruntu vantirukkum eṉka. iṟumpu - pakivar eyiṟpuṟattuk kāṭumām. makaḷir - viṟaliyar. ivar kiyilē ciṟiya mūṅkiṟ kōliyuṭiya rāvareṉpatu, `veṇkaṭic ciṟu kōl akavaṉ makaḷir'1 eṉpataṉālum aṟiyappaṭum. pāṇar, kūttarākiya akavunarum kiyiṟ kōluṭiya reṉpatu piṟāṇṭu varuvaṉavaṟṟāl aṟiyappaṭum. yāḻa : aci. eṉ - evvāṟu iyalum eṉṟapaṭi. marutu - marutoṭu eṉa mūṉṟaṉurupu viritturikka. kuyil akavum kural - kuyil pēṭṭiṉi aḻikkum kural eṉṟalumām. kēṭpōrkku eṉat taṉṉip piṟarpōl vittuk kūṟiṉāḷ. iṉi, kēṭṭu vāḷāvirukkum talivar poruṭṭāka, eṉ kaṇ paṉi niṟuttal eḷitō eṉṟurittalumām.
(mē-ḷ) `naṭuvu nilittiṇiyē'2 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, ic ceyyuḷi eṭuttuk kāṭṭi, itaṉkaṇ iruvaki vēṉilum pālik kaṇ vantaṉa eṉṟār. nac.
98. kuṟiñci
(talimakaṉ ciṟippuppuṟattāṉākat tōḻi talimakaṭkuc colluvāḷāyc colliyatu; tōḻikkut talimakaḷ colliyatūumām.)
		paṉivari nivanta payaṅkeḻu kavāaṉ
		tuṉiyil koḷkiyō ṭavarnamak kuvanta
		iṉiya uḷḷam iṉṉā vāka
		muṉitaka niṟutta nalkal evvam
	5.	cūruṟi veṟpaṉ mārpuṟat taṇital
		aṟintaṉaḷ allaḷ aṉṉi vārkōl
		ceṟintilaṅ kelvaḷi nekiḻntami nōkkik
		kiyaṟu neñciṉaḷ viṉavaliṉ mutuvāyp
		poyval peṇṭir pirappuḷar pirīi
	10.	murukaṉ āraṇaṅ keṉṟaliṉ atucet
		tōvat taṉṉa viṉipuṉi nallil
		pāvi aṉṉa palarāy māṇkaviṉ
		paṇṭiyiṟ ciṟakkaveṉ makaṭkeṉap pariik
		kūṭukoḷ iṉṉiyam kaṟaṅkak kaḷaṉiḻit
	15.	tāṭaṇi ayarnta akaṉperum pantar
		veṇpōḻ kaṭampoṭu cūṭi iṉcīr
		aitami pāṇi irīikki peyarāc
		celvaṉ perumpeyar ētti vēlaṉ
		veṟiyayar viyaṉkaḷam poṟpa vallōṉ
	20.	poṟiyami pāviyiṉ tūṅkal vēṇṭiṉ
		eṉṉāṅ kollō tōḻi mayaṅkiya
		miyaṟ peṇṭirkku novva lāka
		āṭiya piṉṉum vāṭiya mēṉi
		paṇṭiyiṟ ciṟavā tāyiṉ immaṟi
	25.	alarā kāmiyō aritē yaḵtāṉ
		ṟaṟivar uṟuviya allalkaṇ ṭaruḷi
		veṟikamaḻ neṭuvēḷ nalkuvaṉ eṉiṉē
		ceṟitoṭi yuṟṟa cellalum piṟiteṉak
		kāṉkeḻu nāṭaṉ kēṭpiṉ
	30.	yāṉuyir vāḻtal ataṉiṉum aritē.
- veṟipāṭiya kāmakkaṇṇiyār.
(co-ḷ) 21. tōḻi-;
1-4. paṉivari nivanta payam keḻu kavāṉ - kuḷir vāynta maliyiṉatu uyarnta vaḷam poruntiya pakka maliyiṭattu, avar - nam talivar, tuṉiil koḷkiyōṭu - veṟuppillāta koḷkiyuṭaṉ, namakku uvanta iṉiya uḷḷam - namakkuvantaḷitta avaratu iṉiya uḷḷam, iṉṉāāka - iṉṉāvāyiṉamiyiṉ, muṉitaka niṟutta nalkal evvam - veṟukkumāṟu nili peṟuttiya nalkal varuttamāṉatu;
5-6. cūr uṟi veṟpaṉ - teyvam uṟiyum maliyiṉi yuṭiyāṉāya at talivaṉ, mārpuuṟa - mārpu uṟuvatoṉṟāṉē, taṇital - taṇivatātali, aṉṉi aṟintāḷ allaḷ - nam tāy aṟiyātavaḷāki;
6-8. vārkōl ceṟintu ilaṅku elvaḷi nekiḻntami nōkki - nīṇṭa kōṟṟoḻi laminta neruṅki viḷaṅkum oḷi poruntiya vaḷi nekiḻnta niliyiṉip pārttu, kiyaṟu neñciṉaḷ - ceyalaṟṟa uḷḷattiṉaḷāy, viṉavaliṉ - viṉavutalālē;
8-10. mutuvāy poy val peṇṭir - mutumi vāynta poy kūṟal valla kaṭṭuvicciyarāya peṇṭir, pirappu uḷarpu irīi - pirappariciyip parappi vittu, murukaṉ ār aṇaṅku eṉṟaliṉ - itu murukaṉatu ceyalāṉ vanta ariya varuttam eṉṟu kūṟaliṉ, atu cettu - ataṉi vāymiyākak karuti;
11-13. ōvattu aṉṉa viṉi puṉi nal il - ōviyatti yotta puṉinta toḻiṟ ṟiṟaṅkaḷiyuṭiya nalla maṉiyil, pāvi aṉṉa palar āy māṇ kaviṉ - pāviyip pōṉṟa palarālum ārāyappeṟum māṇpuṟṟa aḻakāṉatu, eṉ makaṭkup paṇṭiyiṟ ciṟakka eṉa - eṉ makaṭku muṉpōṟ ciṟappuṟuka eṉṟu, parii- teyvattip paravi;
14-15. kūṭu koḷ iṉṉiyam kaṟaṅkak kaḷaṉ iḻittu - iṇinta palavāya iṉiya iyaṅkaḷ ottu olikka veṟiyāṭum kaḷaṉi iyaṟṟi, āṭu aṇi ayarnta akal perum pantar - āṭutaṟ kēṟṟa aḻaku ceyta akaṉṟa periya pantalilē;
16-21. veṇ pōḻ kaṭampoṭu cūṭi - veḷḷiya paṉan tōṭṭiṉik kaṭappamalaroṭu cūṭi, iṉ cīr aitu ami pāṇi irīi - iṉiya cīr aḻakitāka aminta tāḷattoṭu porutti, celvaṉ perum peyar ētti - murukak kaṭavuḷiṉ perum pukaḻiṉit tutittu, vēlaṉ veṟi ayar viyaṉ kaḷam poṟpa - vēlaṉ veṟiyāṭum periya kaḷam aḻakupeṟa, vallōṉ poṟi ami pāviyiṉ - vallōṉ āṭṭum poṟiyaminta pāviyip pōla, tūṅkal vēṇṭiṉ - āṭutali virumpiṉ, eṉām kol - eṉ ākumō?
21-25. mayaṅkiya miyal peṇṭirkku - veṟiyāṭuṅ kaḷattuk kūṭiya mayakkam poruntiya peṇṭirkku, novvalāka āṭiya piṉṉum - tuṉpam uṇṭāka vēlaṉ āṭiya piṉṉum, vāṭiya mēṉi - eṉatu vāṭiya mēṉi, paṇṭiyiṟ ciṟavātāyiṉ - muṉpu pōlac ciṟantiṭātāyiṉ, im maṟi alar ākāmiyō aritē - ik kaḷavoḻukkam palarum tūṟṟumāṟu veḷippaṭātiruttal aritu;
25. aḵtāṉṟu - aḵtaṉṟi;
26-30. aṟivar uṟuviya allal kaṇṭaruḷi - tam talivar uṟuvitta ivvallalit terintaruḷutaliṉ, veṟi kamaḻ neṭuvēḷ nalkuvaṉ eṉiṉ - maṇaṅkamaḻum neṭuvēḷ nam muṉṉi aḻakiṉit tantaruḷvaṉ eṉiṉ, ceṟitoṭi uṟṟa cellalum piṟiteṉa - neruṅkiya vaḷiyali yaṇinta nam talivi uṟṟa tuṉpamum piṟitoṉṟāṉeytiyatu eṉa, kāṉ keḻu nāṭaṉ kēṭpiṉ - kāṭu poruntiya nāṭṭiṉiyuṭiya nam talivaṉ kēṭṭaṟiyiṉ, yāṉ uyir vāḻtal ataṉiṉum aritu - yāṉ uyir koṇṭu vāḻtal, muṟkūṟiya ataṉiṉum aritu.
(muṭipu) tōḻi (nam) evvam, aṉṉi aṟintaṉaḷ allaḷ; viṉavaliṉ peṇṭir murukaṉ āraṇaṅku eṉṟaliṉ, atu cettu, (murukaṟ) parii āṭaṇi ayarnta pantar, vēlaṉ pāviyiṟ ṟūṅkal vēṇṭiṉ eṉ ām kol? āṭiya piṉṉum vāṭiya mēṉi ciṟavātāyiṉ maṟi alarākāmi aritu; aḵtāṉṟu, neṭuvēḷ nalkuvaṉ eṉiṉ, cellalum piṟiteṉak kāṉkeḻu nāṭaṉ kēṭpiṉ, yāṉ uyir vāḻtal ataṉiṉum aritu.
