(vi-ri) uvanta - uvantu aḷiceyta. āka: kāraṇap poruṭṭāyatu. nalkal evvam - talivaṉ aruḷāl vanta evvam eṉattāṉuṟṟa nōykkup peyar kūṟiṉāḷ. allal eṉpataṉi eccamākkuka. mutuvāy - pēci mutirnta vāy eṉṟalumām. mutuvāyp peṇṭirāvār kaṭṭuvicciyar; kaṭṭuk kūṟupavar. kaṭṭāvatu, muṟattilē nelli yiṭṭuk kuṟi collutal. pirappu - arici, tiṉi mutaliyavaṟṟip pala pakutiyākak kūṭiyilātal, muṟattilātal paliyāka iṭṭu vittal. `palpirap pirīi yeṉa' (242) eṉa innūluḷḷum, `tiṉippirap pirīi'1 eṉak kuṟuntoki yilum, `kurutiyoṭu viriiya tūveḷ ḷarici, cilpalic ceytu palpirap pirīi'2 eṉat tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭiyilum varutal kāṇka. veṇ pōḻ - pōḻappaṭṭa paṉantōṭu : ākupeyar. cīr - tāḷa aṟuti. celvaṉ - murukaṉ. perum peyar - periya tirunāmamum ām. aḵtāvatu, murukaṉukkuriya āṟeḻut tarumaṟi. vēlaṉ- cevvēḷiṉ vēli ēnti niṉṟāṭupavaṉ; pūcāri. poṟi - iyantiram. vallōṉ iyaṟṟiya poṟi eṉka. vallōṉ āṭṭum pāvi yeṉiṉum poruntum. novval - nōval. ākāmi aritu : ākum eṉṟapaṭi. aḵtāṉṟu : `iṉṟi yeṉṉum viṉiyeñciṟuti'1 eṉṉum cūttirattu, `toṉṟiyal maruṅkiṉ' eṉṟataṉāl, aṉṟi eṉpatu aṉṟu eṉa āyiṟṟu eṉavum, `aṉṟu varukāli āvā kutalum'2 eṉṟataṉāl āṉṟu eṉa āyiṟṟu eṉavum koḷka. kaṇṭaruḷi - kaṇṭu aruḷ ceytu eṉṟurit
talumām. veṟi - murukaṉukku iyalpāya naṟumaṇam. `maṇaṅkamaḻ teyvat tiḷanalam kāṭṭi'3 eṉṟār nakkīraṉārum. iṉi veṟiyāṭum kaḷattup pōtaraliṉ annāṟṟam kamaḻum eṉṟalumām. cellalum piṟiteṉak kāṉkeḻu nāṭaṉ kēṭpiṉ yāṉuyir vāḻtal...... aritu eṉṟatu, taṉatu uṇmik kātalukku iḻukku uṇṭātal karuti eṉka. ic ceyyuḷ talivi kūṟṟātaṟkē peritum poruntiyatām. nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum ik karuttiṉarē yeṉpatu `kaḷavala rāyiṉum'4 eṉṉuñ cūttiravuriyāṟ peṟappaṭum. tōḻi kūṟṟāyiṉ talivikkum taṉakkum uḷḷa oṟṟumiyāl, yāṉ eṉak kūṟiṉāḷ eṉal vēṇṭum.
99. pāli
(uṭaṉ pōkiya talimakaḷit talimakaṉ maruṭṭic colliyatu.)
		vāḷvari vayamāṉ kōḷukir aṉṉa
		cemmuki yaviḻnta muṇmutir murukkiṉ
		citarār cemmal tāay matareḻil
		māṇiḻi makaḷir pūṇuṭi muliyiṉ
	5.	mukipiṇi aviḻnta kōṅkamo ṭaciinaṉi
		atiral paranta antaṇ pātiri
		utirvī añciṉi tāay etirvī
		marāa malaroṭu virāayp parāam
		aṇaṅkuṭi nakariṉ maṇanta pūviṉ
	10.	naṉṟē kāṉam nayavarum amma
		kaṇṭiciṉ vāḻiyō kuṟumakaḷ nunti
		aṭukaḷam pāynta toṭiciti maruppiṉ
		piṭimiṭi kaḷiṟṟil tōṉṟum
		kuṟuneṭun tuṇiya kuṉṟamum uṭittē.
-pālipāṭiya peruṅkaṭuṅkō.
(co-ḷ) 11. kuṟumakaḷ - iḷamaṭavāy - vāḻiyō - vāḻvāyāka;
1-3. vāḷ vari vaya māṉ tōḷ ukir aṉṉa - vāḷ pōlum varikaḷiyuṭiya valiya puliyiṉatu kollum toḻiliyuṭiya nakam pōṉṟa, cem muki aviḻnta muḷ mutir murukkiṉ - civanta muki virinta muḷ niṟinta murukka malarākiya, citar ār cemmal tāay - vaṇṭu cūḻnta vāṭiya pūkkaḷ parantu;
3-6. matar eḻil māṇ iḻi makaḷir pūṇ uṭi muliyiṉ - katirtta aḻakiṉiyum māṇpuṟṟa aṇiyiṉiyumuṭiya makaḷiratu pūṇaṇinta muliyiṉip pōṉṟa, muki piṇi aviḻnta kōṅka
moṭu naṉi atiral acii - mukikaḷ alarnta kōṅkam pūkkaḷōṭu kottukkaḷāya puṉalippū kūṭik kiṭakka;
6-7. paranta am taṇ pātiri añciṉi utir vī tāay - paraviya aḻakiya kuḷirnta pātiriyiṉatu aḻakiya ciṉiyiṉiṉṟum utirnta pūkkaḷōṭu tāvi;
7-8. etir vī marāa malaroṭu virāay - māṟupaṭṭa pūkkaḷ veṇkaṭappam pūkkaḷōṭu viravit tāvi;
8-9. parāam aṇaṅku uṭi nakariṉ maṇanta pūviṉ - paravuk kaṭaṉ pūṇṭa teyvamuṭiya kōviliṉkaṇ kalantu kiṭakkum pūkkaḷip pōla;
10-11. kāṉam naṉṟē nayavarum - ik kāṭu peritum virumpat takkatākiṉṟatu, kaṇṭiciṉ - kāṇpāka;
11-14. (maṟṟum ik kāṉam), nunti aṭukaḷam pāynta toṭi citi maruppiṉ - uṉ tanti pakivari aṭum pōrkkaḷattup pāyntu pōr ceytalāl pūṇ citinta kōṭṭiṉiyuṭiyavum, piṭimiṭi - piṭikaḷ cūḻap peṟṟavumāya, kaḷiṟṟil tōṉṟum - kaḷiṟukaḷip pōlat tōṉṟum, kuṟu neṭun tuṇiya - ciṟiyavum periyavumāya aḷaviṉiyuṭiya, kuṉṟamum uṭittu - kuṉṟaṅ kaḷiyum uṭiyatākiṉṟatu; (kāṇpāyāka.)
(muṭipu) kuṟumakaḷ, vāḻiyō! murukkiṉ cemmal tāay, muki piṇi yaviḻnta kōṅkamoṭu naṉi atiral acii, pātiri añciṉi utir vī etir vī marāa malaroṭu tāay, virāay, aṇaṅkuṭi nakariṉ maṇanta pūviṉ kāṉam naṉṟu nayavarum; (atu) piṭimiṭi kaḷiṟṟiṟ ṟōṉṟum kuṟu neṭun tuṇiya kuṉṟamum uṭittu; amma! kaṇṭiciṉ.
(vi-ri) kōḷ ukir - iriyip paṟṟiya ukireṉṟumām; kurutiyāṟ civanta ukireṉpāṉ `kōḷukir' eṉṟāṉ; puliyukir vaṇṇamum vaṭivum paṟṟi murukkiṉ cemmukikku uvamiyāyatu. mulipōlum muki yeṉka. `marāa malaroṭu virāayp parāam' eṉṟatu aḷapeṭi vaṇṇam; eṉṉi? "aḷapeṭi vaṇṇa maḷapeṭi payilum"1 eṉṟārākaliṉ. amma : uriyaci; kēṭpittaṟ poruṭṭumām. pakivaratu matiṟkatavip pāytaliṉ toṭi citinta teṉalumām. maruppiṟ kaḷiṟu piṭimiṭi kaḷiṟu eṉka.
(mē-ḷ) `oṉṟāt tamariṉum'2 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu ic ceyyuḷik kūṟi, `itu taliviyi maruṭṭiyatu' eṉṟu kūṟiṉar, nac.
100. neytal
(tōḻi varivu kaṭāyatu.)
		ariyuṟ ṟaminta āram nīvip
		puriyap pūṇṭa kōti mārpiṉi
		nallakam vaṭukkoḷa muyaṅki nīvan
		telliṉiṟ peyartal eṉakkumā riṉitē
	5.	peruntiri muḻakkamo ṭiyakkavin tirunta
		koṇṭal iraviṉ iruṅkaṭal maṭutta
		koḻumīṉ koḷpavar iruḷnīṅ koṇcuṭar
		ōṭāp pūṭki vēntaṉ pācaṟi
		āṭiyal yāṉi aṇimukat tacitta
	10.	ōṭi oṇcuṭar oppat tōṉṟum
		pāṭunart toṭutta kivaṇ kōmāṉ
		pariyuṭi naṟṟērp periyaṉ viriyiṇarp
		puṉṉiyaṅ kāṉaṟ puṟanti muṉṟuṟi
		vampa nāri yiṉaṉolit taṉṉa
	15.	ampal vāytta teyya taṇpular
		vikuṟu viṭiyal pōkiya erumi
		neytalam putumalar  3māntum
		kitiyam paṭappiem maḻuṅka lūrē.
- ulōccaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 1-4. ari uṟṟu aminta āramnīvi - naṟumaṇam kūṭṭi arikkappeṟṟu muṭinta cantaṉattip pūci, puriyap pūṇṭa kōti mārpiṉi - uyarvuṟa māliyiṉip pūṇṭa mārpiṉiyuṭiyiyāy, nī elliṉil vantu nal akam vaṭukkoḷa muyaṅkip peyartal eṉakkum iṉitu - nī iraviṉil vantu niṉatu nalla mārpu vaṭuvuṇṭāka muyaṅkip pōtal eṉakkum mika iṉitākum; (āyiṉum,)
5-7. perum tiri muḻakkamoṭu iyakku avintirunta - periya aliyiṉ oliyōṭu ataṉ alivum ōyntirunta, koṇṭal iravil - mēkañ cūḻnta iravil, iru kaṭal maṭutta koḻumīṉ koḷpavar - koḻumīṉip piṭikkum paratavar kariya kaṭalilē maṭutta ampiyiluḷḷa, iruḷ nīṅku oḷcuṭar - iruḷ nīṅkutaṟkuk kāraṇamāṉa oḷi poruntiya viḷakkam;
8-10. ōṭāp pūṭki vēntaṉ pācaṟi - piṟakkiṭāta mēṟ kōḷiṉiyuṭiya vēntaṉ pācaṟikkaṇṇē yuḷḷa, āṭu iyal yāṉi aṇimukattu acitta - aṭutal valla yāṉiyiṉ aḻakiya mukattiṟ piṇitta, ōṭi oṇ cuṭar oppat tōṉṟum - ōṭiyiṉatu oḷḷiya cuṭarpōlat tōṉṟum (iṭamākiya);
11-15. pāṭunart toṭutta kivaṇ kōmāṉ - pāṭi varuvāri vaḷittuk koḷḷum kivaṇmi vāynta kōmāṉākiya, pari uṭi naltērp periyaṉ - kutirikaḷ pūṇṭa ciṟanta tēriṉi yuṭiya periyaṉ eṉpāṉatu, viri iṇarp puṉṉi am kāṉal puṟanti muṉṟuṟi - malar virinta kottukkaḷiyuṭiya puṉṉi maraṅkaḷiyuṭiya aḻakiya cōli cūḻnta puṟiyāṟṟiṉ kaṭaṟṟuṟiyiṉkaṇṇuḷḷa, vampa nāri iṉaṉ olittaṉṉa ampal - putiya nāriyiṉ kūṭṭam olittāṟ pōṉṟa alar;
15-18. taṇ pular vikuṟu viṭiyal - taṇṇeṉṟu pulantiṭum iruḷ taṅkiya viṭiyaṟkālattē, pōkiya erumi neytal putumalar māntum - veḷic ceṉṟa erumi neytaliṉ putitāka alarnta malariṉit tiṉṉum, kiti am paṭappi em aḻuṅkal ūr - tāḻi vēlikaḷiyuṭiya aḻakiya tōṭṭaṅkaḷiyuṭiya ematu āravāram mikka ūriṉkaṇ, vāytta - eḻuntaṉa.
(muṭipu) taliva, nī elliṉil vantu peyartal emakkum iṉitu; āyiṉum, mīṉ koḷpavar iruṅkaṭal maṭutta oṇcuṭar vēntaṉ pācaṟi yāṉiyiṉ ōṭi  oṇcuṭar oppat tōṉṟum periyaṉ puṟanti muṉṟuṟi nāri iṉaṉ olittaṉṉa ampal em aḻuṅkal ūr vāyttaṉa.
(vi-ri) eṉakkumār: ummi eccap poruṭṭu; mār : aci. uvamikkaṇ yāṉi mukat tacitta ōṭi yeṉṟamiyāl, poruḷilum ataṟkēṟpa, kaṭalmaṭutta ampiyiṟ piṇitta cuṭar eṉa varuvitturikka. `pāṭunart toṭutta kivaṇ kōmāṉ' eṉṟatu, pāṭuvār poruḷ vēṭṭup piṟariṭam cellāvāṟu mikutiyāka vaḻaṅkupavaṉ eṉṟapaṭiyām. puṉṉiyil nāri yirukku meṉpatum, vampa nāri eṉṉum vaḻakkum, `niṉṟa puṉṉi nilantōy paṭuciṉi - vampa nāri cēkkum'1 eṉṉuñ ceyyuḷuḷḷuṅ kāṇka. puṟanti - puṟiyāṟu eṉpataṉ marūu. itu poṟiyāṟu eṉavum vaḻaṅkum. `naṟavumaki ḻirukki naṟṟērp periyaṉ, kaṭkamaḻ poṟiyāṟ ṟaṉṉa'1 eṉpatu kāṇka. vāytta : muṟṟuviṉi. teyya: aci. `ennila maruṅkiṟ pūvum puḷḷum'2 eṉṟa cūttirattu oṉṟeṉa muṭittalāl, piṟa karupporuḷ mayaṅkuvaṉa uḷavēṉuṅ koḷka eṉṟaliṉ, marutattu vilaṅkākiya erumiyum, ciṟupoḻutākiya vikaṟiyum neytaṟkaṇ vantaṉa.
101. pāli
(piriviṭi vēṟupaṭṭa kiḻatti tōḻikkuc colliyatu. 
tōḻi kiḻattikkuc colliyatūu mām.)
		amma vāḻi tōḻi yimmi
		naṉṟucey maruṅkil tītil eṉṉum
		toṉṟupaṭu paḻamoḻi yiṉṟupoyt taṉṟukol
		takarmarup pēyppac cuṟṟupu curinta
	5.	cuvaṉmāy pittic ceṅkaṇ maḻavar
		vāyppaki kaṭiyum maṇṇoṭu kaṭuntiṟal
		tīppaṭu ciṟukōl villoṭu paṟṟi
		nuriteri mattaṅ koḷīi niṟippuṟat
		taṭiputit toṭutōl paṟiya vēkik
	10.	kaṭipulaṅ kavarnta kaṉṟuṭik koḷḷiyar
		iṉantali peyarkkum naṉantalip peruṅkāṭ
		ṭakaliru vicumpiṟ kōṭam pōlap
		pakaliṭi niṉṟa palkatir ñāyiṟ
		ṟuruppavir pūriya 3cuḻaṉṟuvaru kōṭip
	15.	puṉkāl muruṅki ūḻkaḻi paṉmalar
		taṇkār āliyil tāvaṉa utirum
		paṉipaṭu paṉmali iṟantōrkku
		muṉitaku paṇpiyāñ ceytaṉṟō vilamē.
- māmūlaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 1. tōḻi vāḻi-, amma - nāṉ kūṟuvataṉik kēṭpāyāka!,
1-3. immi naṉṟu cey maruṅkil tītu il eṉṉum - immiyil naṉṟu ceyyumiṭattut tītu varuvatilli eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum, toṉṟu paṭu paḻamoḻi - toṉṟu toṭṭu vaḻaṅkum paḻamoḻi, iṉṟu poyttaṉṟukol - iṉṟu poyyāyatō;
4-11. takar maruppu ēyppac cuṟṟupu curinta - cemmaṟiyāṭṭuk kiṭāyiṉ kompiṉi yoppac cuṟṟik kaṭi curuṇṭa, cuvalmāy pittic ceṅkaṇ maḻavar - piṭariyi maṟikkum tali mayiriṉi yum civanta kaṇṇiṉiyumuṭiya maḻavarkaḷ, vāyp paki kaṭiyum maṇṇoṭu - vāyiṉiṉṟeḻum irumalāya pakiyiṉi eḻāmaṟ ṟīrkkum maruntāya puṟṟumaṇṇi vāyil aṭakkik koṇṭaṉarāy, kaṭum tiṟal tīp paṭu ciṟu kōl villoṭu paṟṟi - kaṭiya tiṟalvāynta tīyuṇṭām ciṟiya ampiṉi villōṭu kiyiṟpaṟṟi, nuri terimattam koḷīi - veṇṇeyyi veḷippaṭuttum kaṭiyum mattiṉik kavarntukoṇṭu, nirip puṟattu aṭi puti toṭutōl paṟiya ēki - āṉirikaḷ uḷḷaviṭattut tam aṭiyi maṟittuḷḷa ceruppukkaḷ tēyac ceṉṟu, kaṭi pulam kavarnta kaṉṟuṭik koḷḷiyar - pakivar kāval iṭattilē kavarnta kaṉṟukaḷuṭaṉ kūṭiya āṉ iṉattiyuṭiyarāy, iṉam tali peyarkkum naṉantalip peruṅkāṭṭu - avviṉattiṉi avviṭattiṉiṉṟu koṇṭu pōkum akaṉṟa iṭattiyuṭiya periya kāṭṭiṉ kaṇṇē;
12-16. akal iru vicumpiṟku ōṭampōla - akaṉṟa periya vāṉākiya kaṭaṟkaṇ ōṭampōla, pakaliṭi niṉṟa pal katir ñāyiṟṟu - pakalil vāṉiṭiyē niṉṟa pala katirkaḷiyuṭiya ñāyiṟṟiṉ, uruppu avirpu ūriya cuḻaṉṟu varu kōṭi - veppam viḷaṅkip parakkac cuḻaṉṟu varu mēl kāṟṟiṉāl, puṉ kāl muruṅki ūḻ kaḻi paṉmalar - pulliya aṭimarattiṉiyuṭiya muruṅkiyiṉ mutirntu kaḻiyum pala pūkkaḷ, taṇ kār āliyil tāvaṉa utirum - kuḷirnta kārkālattu ālaṅkaṭṭi pōlap parantaṉavāy utirum;
17-18. paṉipaṭu paṉmali iṟantōrkku - naṭukkamuṇṭākum pala malikaḷiyum tāṇṭic ceṉṟa nam talivarkku, muṉitaku paṇpuyām ceytaṉṟō ilam - veṟukkattakka ceyal yātum nām ceytilamē.
(muṭipu) tōḻi! "immi..... paḻamoḻi" iṉṟu poyttaṉṟukol! peruṅkāṭṭilē, ñāyiṟṟiṉ uruppu avirpu ūrtaliṉ, cuḻaṉṟuvaru kōṭiyāl muruṅkiyiṉ paṉmalar āliyiṉ utirum paṉmali yiṟantōrkku yām muṉitaku paṇpu ceytaṉṟō ilam.
maḻavar, maṇṇoṭu ciṟukōl villoṭu paṟṟi, koḷīi, ēki, kavarnta koḷḷiyar iṉantali peyarkkum peruṅkāṭu eṉka.
(vi-ri) vāyppaki - vāyiṉiṉ ṟuṇṭām paki; irumal; avaratu varavi veḷippaṭuttaliṉ paki eṉṟār. nuri - veṇṇey. paṟiya - kaḻala veṉṟumām. kaṉṟuṭik koḷḷiyar - kaṉṟiṉi yuṭiya āṉiṉ koḷḷiyar eṉka. vicumpiṟku ōṭam - vicumpākiya kaṭaliṉkaṇ ōṭam: aḷakkalākā virivu paṟṟi vicumpiṟkuk kaṭalum, ataṉi ūṭaṟuttuc cellutal paṟṟi ñāyiṟṟukku ōṭamum uvamiyāyiṉa. ūriya - ūrtalāl. kāṭṭiṉkaṇ malar utirum maliyeṉka. kāṭṭiṉiyuṭiya paṉmali yeṉṟalumām. maliyiṟan tōrkku muṉitaku paṇpu yām ceytilam; aṅṅaṉamākavum avar variviṉi nīṭṭikkac ceytu nammi varuttutaliṉ `naṉṟu cey maruṅkil tītuil'  eṉṉum paḻamoḻi iṉṟu poyttatu pōlum eṉak kiḻatti kavaṉṟu kūṟiṉāḷeṉka. `naṉṟu cey maruṅkil' eṉṟamiyāl ivaḷ iyaṟkip puṇarccik kālattut talivaṟku iṉpam viḷittā ḷeṉpatu peṟṟām. tōḻi kūṟṟāyiṉum poruḷituvē.
102. kuṟiñci
(iravuk kuṟikkaṇ ciṟippuṟamākat tōḻikkuc colluvāḷāyt talimakaḷ colliyatu.)
		uḷimāṉ tuppiṉ ōṅkutiṉip perumpuṉattuk
		kaḻutiṟ kāṉavaṉ piḻimakiḻntu vatinteṉa
		uritta cantiṉ ūral iruṅkatup
		pituvaral acivaḷi yāṟṟak kipeyarā
	5.	oliyal vārmayir uḷariṉaḷ koṭicci
		peruvari maruṅkil kuṟiñci pāṭak
		kuraluṅ koḷḷātu niliyiṉum peyarātu
		paṭāap piṅkaṇ pāṭupeṟ ṟoyyeṉa
		maṟampukal maḻakaḷi ṟuṟaṅkum nāṭaṉ
	10.	āra mārpiṉ ariñimi ṟārppat
		tāraṉ kaṇṇiyaṉ eḵkuṭi valattaṉ
		kāvalar aṟital ōmpip piyeṉa
		vīḻāk katavam aciyiṉaṉ pukutan
		tuyaṅkupaṭar akala muyaṅkit tōḷmaṇan
	15.	tiṉcol laḷiip peyarntaṉaṉ tōḻi
		iṉṟevaṉ kollō kaṇṭiku maṟṟavaṉ
		nalkā miyiṉ ampa lāki
		oruṅkuvan tuvakkum paṇpiṉ
		iruñcūḻ ōti oṇṇutal pacappē.
- 1maturi iḷampālāciriyaṉ cēntaṅkūttaṉ.
(co-ḷ) 15. tōḻi-;
1-9. uḷimāṉ tuppiṉ - ciṅkam pōlum valiyiṉaṉāy, tiṉip perum puṉattu ōṅku kaḻutil - periya tiṉippuṉattil ōṅkiya paraṇiṉkaṇṇē, kāṉavaṉ piḻi makiḻntu vatinteṉa - kāṉavaṉ kaḷḷuṇṭu kaḷittu iruntaṉaṉāka, uritta cantiṉ ūral irum katuppu - pūciya mayirc cāntiṉiyuṭiya paranta kariya kūntali, aituvaral acivaḷi āṟṟa - melleṉa acintu varum kāṟṟup pukuntu pulartta, oliyal vār mayir ki peyarā uḷariṉaḷ - taḻittu nīṇṭa ak kūntalit taṉ kiyāṟ peyarttuk kōtik koṇṭu, koṭicci - avaṉ maṉivi, peruvari maruṅkil - ap periya variyiṉ pakkattē, kuṟiñci pāṭa - kuṟiñcip paṇṇip pāṭāniṟka, kuralum koḷḷātu - tāṉ koṇṭa tiṉik katiriṉiyum uṭkoḷḷātu, niliyiṉum peyarātu - niṉṟa niliyiṉiṉṟum akalātu, paṭāap piṅkaṇ pāṭupeṟṟa - tuyilvarappeṟāta aḻakiya kaṇ tuyilvarap peṟṟu, maṟam pukal maḻa kaḷiṟu oyyeṉa uṟaṅkum nāṭaṉ - vīrattiṉi virumpiya iḷiya kaḷiṟu virintu tūṅkum nāṭṭiyuṭiyaṉākiya nam talivaṉ;
10-15. āram mārpiṉ ari ñimiṟu ārppa - cantaṉam pūciya taṉatu mārpiṉkaṇṇē aḻakiya vaṇṭukaḷ olippa, tāraṉ kaṇṇiyaṉ eḵku uṭi valattaṉ - tāraṉum kaṇṇiyaṉum vēl poruntiya valakkiyi yuṭiyavaṉumāki, kāvalar aṟital ōmpi - kāvalāḷar aṟitalip parikarittu, piyeṉa vīḻāk katavam aciyiṉaṉ pukutantu - tāḻ vīḻttāta kataviṉarukē taṅki melleṉap pukuntu, uyaṅku paṭar akala muyaṅkit tōḷ maṇantu - varuntum tuṉpam nīṅka aṇittut tōḷiṉik kūṭi, iṉcol aḷiip peyarntaṉaṉ - iṉ coṟkaḷik kalantu pēcic ceṉṟaṉaṉ; (aṅṅaṉamākavum ippāl,)
16-19. iṉṟu avaṉ nalkāmiyiṉ - avaṉ iṉṟu oru nāḷ vantu aruḷ ceyyāmiyiṉ, oruṅku vantu ampal āki - oruṅkē vantu alar ākumāṟu, uvakkum paṇpiṉ - makiḻcciyi viḷivikkum iyalpiṉiyuṭiya, iruñ cūḻ ōti oṇnutal pacappu - kariya kūntalāṟ cūḻappaṭṭa oḷḷiya neṟṟiyiṉ pacappiṉi, evaṉ kollō kaṇṭikum - nām kaṇṭatu eṉṉiyō?
(muṭipu) tōḻi! kāṉavaṉ piḻi makiḻntu vatinteṉa, koṭicci kuṟiñci pāṭa, kaḷiṟu kaṇpāṭu peṟṟu uṟaṅkum nāṭaṉ, muyaṅkit tōḷ maṇantu iṉ col aḷiip peyarntaṉaṉ; iṉṟu avaṉ nalkāmiyiṉ, ampal āka, nutaliṭattup pacappiṉi evaṉ kol kaṇṭikum?
(vi-ri) uḷimāṉ - piṭari mayiriṉiyuṭiya vilaṅku; ciṅkam. piḻi - kaḷ, piḻiyappaṭuvatu eṉṉum poruṭṭu. pāṭa - pāṭutalāl. pāṭutalāṟ koḷḷātu peyarātu uṟaṅkum eṉka. tār - mārpiṟ cūṭuvatu. kaṇṇi - taliyiṟ cūṭuvatu. aṟital ōmpi - aṟiyātapaṭi pātukāttu. maṟṟu : viṉimāṟṟu. ampal āki : ampal āka eṉat tirikka. uvakkum paṇpiṉ ōti eṉka; nutal eṉṟalumām. iṉi ōti eṉpataṉi āku peyarākak koṇṭu, tōḻi ōti eṉa viḷiyākkalumām. iṉṟu oru nāḷ aruḷ ceyyāmiyāl, neṟṟiyil pacappu uṇṭāyatu eṉṉi eṉṟapaṭi.
(u-ṟi) koṭicci, kāṉavaṉukku iṉpam uṇṭākap pāṭiya kuṟiñcip paṇ, yāṉiyit tiṉi yuṇṇāmaṟ ceytataṉṟi, paci oru puṟam naliya, ataṉi uṟaṅkavum ceytatupōla, ampaṟ peṇṭir, tāykku uṇarttiya alar, talivaṉ taliviyi nukarāmaṟ ceytataṉṟi, vēṭki orupuṟam naliya, avaṉ vāḷātē puṟattē ayarntirukkavum ceytatu eṉka.
(mē-ḷ) `iravukkuṟiyē...... maṉiyakam pukāk kāliyāṉa'1 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu ic ceyyuḷik kūṟi, itu maṉiyakam pukkatu eṉṟurittaṉar, nac.
103. pāli
(talimakaṉ piriviṉkaṇ talimakaḷ tōḻikkuc coṟṟatu.)
		niḻalaṟu naṉantali yeḻālēṟu kuṟitta
		katirtta ceṉṉi nuṇaṅkucen nāviṉ
		vitirtta pōlum annuṇ palpoṟik
		kāmar cēval ēmañ cēppa
	5.	muḷiyaril pulampap pōki muṉāatu
		murampaṭin tirunta mūri maṉṟat
		tatarpārt talkum ākeḻu ciṟukuṭi
		uṟiyunar pōkiya vōṅkunili viyaṉnakar2
		iṟiniḻal oruciṟip pulampayā uyirkkum
	10.	vemmuṉi yaruñcuram nīntit tamvayiṉ
		īṇṭuviṉi maruṅkiṉ mīṇṭōr maṉṉeṉa
		naḷḷeṉ yāmat tuyavuttuṇi yāka
		nammoṭu pacali nōṉṟu tammoṭu
		tāṉē3 ceṉṟa nalaṉum
	15.	nalkār kollōnām nayantici ṉōrē.
- kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉattuc ceṅkaṇṇaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 15. (tōḻi),  nām nayanticiṉōr - nammāl virumpappaṭṭa talivar;
1-5. niḻal aṟu naṉantali - niḻalaṟṟa akaṉṟa pāli nilattē, eḻāl ēṟu kuṟitta - pullūṟṟiṉāl eṟṟutal kuṟikkappaṭṭa, katirtta ceṉṉi - periya taliyiṉiyum, nuṇaṅku cen nāviṉ - nuṇukiya civanta nāviṉiyum, vitirtta pōlum am nuṇ pal poṟi - teḷittaṉa pōlum aḻakiya ciṟiya palavāya puḷḷikaḷiyu muṭiya, kāmar cēval - aḻakiya kuṟumpūḻc cēval, ēmam  cēppa - pātukāvalāṉa iṭattiṟ ceṉṟu taṅka eṇṇi, muḷi aril pulampap pōki - tāṉirunta kāyntupaṭṭa ciṟu tūṟu taṉit toḻiyap pōy;
5-10. muṉāatu murampu aṭintirunta mūri maṉṟattu - vaṉṉilattic cārntirunta paḻimiyāṉa periya maṉṟilē, atar pārttu alkum - (āṟalikka) vaḻiyip pārttut taṅki yirukkum, ā keḻu ciṟukuṭi - pacukkaḷ poruntiya cīṟūriṉkaṇṇē, uṟiyunar pōkiya ōṅki nili viyaṉnakar - uṟivōr viṭṭup pōṉa iṭamākiya uyarnta niliyiṉiyuṭiya periya maṉiyil, iṟi niḻal oru ciṟi - iṟappu niḻaliṉ oru pakkattē taṅki, pulampu ayā uyirkkum - taṉimiyāl peru mūcceṟintirukkum, vemmuṉi arum curam nīnti - koṭiya muṉiyiruppukkaḷiyuṭiya ariya curattiṉik kaṭantu;
10-15. tam vayiṉ īṇṭu viṉi maruṅkiṉ - tammiṭattu vantuṟṟa poruḷīṭṭum viṉiyakattē, mīṇṭōr eṉa - mīṇṭum cellu taluṟṟāreṉa, naḷ eṉ yāmattu uyavut tuṇi āka - naḷḷeṉṟa naṭu yāmattum ucāvum tuṇiyākum poruṭṭu, nammoṭu pacali nōṉṟu - nammoṭu itukāṟum pacaliyip poṟuttiruntu ippoḻutu, tammoṭu tāṉē ceṉṟa nalaṉum - at talivaruṭaṉ tāṉē valintu ceṉṟa nam nalaṉiyēṉum, nalkār kollō - namakku aruḷārō?
(muṭipu) (tōḻi!) nām nayanticiṉōr, aruñcuram nīnti, viṉi maruṅkiṉ mīṇṭōr eṉa nammoṭu pacali nōṉṟu tammoṭu tāṉē ceṉṟa nalaṉum nalkārkollō?
eḻālēṟu kuṟitta cēval, ēmam cēppa, pōki iṟi niḻal oru ciṟi pulampu ayāvuyirkkum aruñcuram eṉka.
(vi-ri) eḻāl - pullūṟu, ēṟu - eṟital, vavvutal; `kūrukirp paruntiṉ ēṟukuṟit torīi'1 eṉpatu kāṇka. katirtta ceṉṉi - oḷiviṭum tali eṉalumām. cēval - īṇṭuk kuṟumpūḻc cēval. mīṇṭōr - mīṇṭu ceṉṟār. ākiya eṉum pāṭattiṟkum ākum poruṭ ṭeṉpatē poruḷ. nalaṉum uyavut tuṇiyāka nammoṭu pacali nōṉṟiruntu, tammoṭu tāṉē ceṉṟatu; iṉi, nalkār kollō eṉṟu urittalumām. itaṟkuc ceṉṟatu eṉṟatu ceṉṟa eṉa vikāramāyiṟṟu eṉka.
104. mulli
(viṉimuṟṟi mīḷun talimakaṟkut tōḻi colliyatu.)
		vēntuviṉi muṭitta kālit tēmpāyn
		tiṉavaṇ ṭārkkun taṇṇaṟum puṟaviṉ
		veṉvēl iḷiyar iṉpuṟa valavaṉ
		vaḷpuvalit tūriṉ allatu muḷḷuriṉ
	5.	munnīr maṇṭilam āti āṟṟā
		naṉṉālku pūṇṭa kaṭumpari neṭuntēr
		vāṅkuciṉi poliya ēṟip putala
		pūṅkoṭi avarip poyyataḷ aṉṉa
		uḷḷil vayiṟṟa veḷḷi veṇmaṟi
	10.	māḻki yaṉṉa tāḻperuñ ceviya
		puṉtalic ciṟārō ṭukaḷi maṉṟuḻik
		kaviyili āriṉ aṅkuḻi kaṟikkum
		cīṟūr palapiṟak koḻiya māli
		iṉitucey taṉiyāl enti vāḻiya
	15.	paṉivār kaṇṇaḷ palapulan tuṟiyum
		āytoṭi yarivi kūntal
		pōtukural aṇiya vēytan tōyē.
- maturi marutaṉiḷa nākaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 14. enti - em talivaṉē!,
1-7. vēntu viṉi muṭitta kāli - vēntaṉ viṉiyi muṭitta kālattē, tēm pāyntu iṉa vaṇṭu ārkkum - ticikaḷtōṟum paṟantu kūṭṭamāya vaṇṭukaḷ āravārikkum, taṇ naṟum puṟavil - kuḷirnta naṟiya mulli nilattē, veṉvēl iḷiyar iṉpuṟa - veṉṟi poruntiya vēliṉiyuṭiya vīrar iṉpam aṭiya, valavaṉ vaḷpu valittu ūriṉ allatu - pākaṉ kaṭivāḷattiṉi iḻuttup piṭittuc celuttiṉ allatu, muḷuṟiṉ munnīr maṇṭilam āti āṟṟā - tāṟṟukkōl tīṇṭiṉ kaṭal cūḻnta ulakē āti eṉum celaviṟkup pōtāta, kaṭumpari nalnālku pūṇṭa neṭuntēr - mikka vēkamuṭiya nalla nāṉku kutirikaḷ pūṭṭappeṟṟa nīṇṭa tēril, vāṅku ciṉi poliya ēṟi - vaḷinta koṭiñci poliya ēṟi;
7-13. putala pū koṭi avarip poy ataḷ aṉṉa - putalkaḷiṟ paṭarnta pūviyuṭiya avarik koṭiyiluḷḷa poyt tōl pōṉṟa, uḷil vayiṟṟa - uḷḷīṭillāta vayiṟṟiṉavum, māḻki yaṉṉa tāḻperum ceviya - mayaṅkik kiṭantālotta tāḻntu toṅkum periya ceviyiṉavum ākiya, veḷḷi veṇmaṟi - veḷḷāṭṭiṉ veḷḷiya kuṭṭi, puṉtalic ciṟārōṭu ukaḷi - puṟkeṉṟa taliyiṉiyuṭiya ciṟuvarkaḷōṭu kutittuc ceṉṟu, maṉṟuḻik kavi ili āriṉ am kuḻi kaṟikkum - maṉṟiṉ kaṇṇē kaviyāya iliyiṉiyuṭiya āttiyiṉ aḻakiya taḷirik kaṭikkum, ciṟu ūr pala piṟakku oḻiya - ciṟiya ūrkaḷ pala piṉṉē oḻintiṭa vantu;
13-17. māli - im mālik kālattē, paṉivār kaṇṇaḷ pala pulantu uṟiyum - nīrvaṭiyum kaṇṇiṉaḷāyp palavaṟṟiyum eṇṇi veṟuttu uṟiyāniṟkum, āytoṭi arivi - ārāynta vaḷiyaliyuṭiya talivi, aṇiya - aṇintukoḷḷumāṟu, kural kūntal - avaḷuṭiya kottākiya kūntalil, pōtu vēytantōy - malaric cūṭṭiṉi (ākavē), iṉitu ceytaṉi - iṉiya toṉṟic ceytaṉiyāvāy, vāḻiya - nī vāḻvāyāka.
(muṭipu) enti! vēntu viṉi muṭitta kāli, kaṭumpari neṭuntēr ēṟi, cīṟūr pala piṟakku oḻiya (vantu,) māli, pulantu uṟiyum arivi aṇiya kūntal pōtu vēytan
tōy, ataṉāl, iṉitu ceytaṉi, vāḻiya!
(vi-ri) tēm - tici, `avaṉ maṟitēem nōkki'1 eṉpatu kāṇka; tēṉumām. vaḷpu - vār, kaṭivāḷam. muḷ - tāṟṟu muḷ. āti - kutiriyiṉ nērōṭṭam. nālku - nāṉku; peyart tiricol leṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar; `pālpuri puravi nālkuṭaṉ pūṭṭi' 2 eṉpataṉ uri kāṇka. ciṉi - tēriṉ uṟuppākiya koṭiñci. poy ataḷ - uḷḷīṭiṉṟit tōl māttiramāyirukkum kāy. talivaṉi enti yeṉṟu kūṟuvatu, `aṉṉi yeṉṉi yeṉṟalu muḷavē' eṉṉuñ cūttirattu ummiyāl taḻuvappaṭum. maṟi, ciṟārō ṭukaḷik kuḻi kaṟikkum cīṟūr pala piṟakkoḻiya eṉṟatu, kārp paruvattil, talivaṉ mīṇṭu varum vaḻiyāṉatu, avaṉukku iṉpatti viḷittu, talivipāl virintēkat tūṇṭiyatik kuṟittavāṟu.
(mē-ḷ) `veṟiyaṟi ciṟappiṉ'1 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, vañcik kaṇṇum potuviyal varuvaṉa uḷa eṉṟu kūṟi, atu `vēntu viṉi muṭitta' eṉṉum akappāṭṭiṉuḷ, cuṭṭi oruvar peyar kūṟā vañci potuviyalāy vantavāṟu kāṇka, eṉṟum, `viṉivayiṟ pirintōṉ'2 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, ic ceyyuḷik kāṭṭi, itaṉuḷ viṉimuṭitta kālit tēriḷiyar celaviṟ kēṟpa ūrātu kōlūṉṟiṉ ulakiṟantaṉa celaviṟkup paṟṟāta kutirit tērēṟi iṭiccurattil taṅkātu mālikkālattu vantu pūccūṭṭiṉi;  iṉitu ceytaṉi; enti vāḻiya, eṉat tōḻi kūṟiyavāṟu kāṇka eṉṟum urittaṉar, nac.
105. pāli
(makaṭ pōkkiya tāy colliyatu.)
		akalaṟi malarnta arumpumutir vēṅki
		oḷḷilit toṭali tii melleṉa
		nalvari nāṭaṉ taṟpā rāṭṭa
		yāṅkuval lunaḷkol tāṉē tēmpeytu
	5.	maṇicey maṇṭit tīmpāl ēnti
		īṉāt tāyar maṭuppavum uṇṇāḷ
		niḻaṟkayat taṉṉa nīḷnakar varippiṉ
		emmuṭic celvamum uḷḷāḷ poymmaruṇṭu
		pantupuṭip paṉṉa pāṇip pallaṭic
	10.	cilparik kutirip palvēl eḻiṉi
		keṭalaruri tuppiṉ viṭutoḻiṉ muṭimār
		kaṉiyeri naṭanta kalkāy kāṉattu
		viṉival ampiṉ viḻuttoṭi maṟavar
		tēmpiḻi naṟuṅkaḷ makiḻiṉ muṉikaṭantu
	15.	vīṅkumeṉ curiya ēṟṟiṉan tarūum
		mukitali tiṟanta vēṉil
		pakitali maṇanta pallatarc celavē.
- tāyaṅkaṇṇaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 4-8. maṇi cey maṇṭit tīmpāl tēm peytu ēnti - maṇikaḷiḻitta poṟkalattil iṉiya pāloṭu tēṉ kalantu ēnti, īṉāt tāyar maṭuppavum uṇṇāḷ - cevilit tāyar ūṭṭavum uṇṇāḷāya eṉmakaḷ, niḻaṟ kayattu aṉṉa nīḷ nakar varippil - niḻaṟkaṇṇuḷḷa kuḷampōlak kuḷirnta nīṇṭa māḷikiyiṭattēyuḷḷa, em uṭic celvamum uḷḷāḷ - ematu periya celvattiyum niṉiyāḷāy;
1-3. akal aṟi malarnta arumpu mutir vēṅki - akaṉṟa pāṟiyil arumpu mutirntu malarnta vēṅkiyiṉ, oḷilit toṭali tii - oḷḷiya ili virāviya māliyiṉi aṇintu, melleṉa - metteṉa, nalvari nāṭaṉ taṉ pārāṭṭa - nalla mali nāṭaṉākiya talivaṉ taṉṉip pārāṭṭi vara;
8. poy maruṇṭu - ap poyyi meyyeṉa mayaṅki;
9-17. pantu puṭippaṉṉa pāṇip pallaṭi - pantu puṭikkap paṭṭu eḻuvatotta tāḷattuṭaṉ kūṭiya pala aṭiyīṭṭiṉiyum, cil pari - cilavakic celaviṉiyumuṭiya, kutiri - kutiri kaḷiyum, palvēl - pala vēṟpaṭiyiṉiyu muṭiya, keṭalarum tuppiṉ eḻiṉi - keṭutalillāta vali poruntiya eḻiṉi yeṉpāṉ, viṭu toḻil muṭimār - ēviviṭṭa toḻili muṭikkum poruṭṭu, kaṉi eri naṭanta kalkāy kāṉattu - mikka eri parattaliṉ pāṟikaḷ kāyntirukkum kāṭṭiṉkaṇ, viṉi val ampiṉ viḻuttoṭi maṟavar - pōrttiṟam vāynta ampiṉatu tappāta toṭuttali yuṭiya vīrarkaḷ, tēmpiḻi naṟuṅkaḷ makiḻiṉ - piḻinta tēṉāṟ caminta naṟiya kaḷḷuṇṭa makiḻcciyiṉāl, muṉi kaṭantu - pakivar pōr muṉiyi veṉṟa, vīṅku meṉ curiya ēṟu iṉam  tarūum - parutta melliya maṭiyiṉiyuṭiyavum ēṟukaḷōṭu kūṭiyavu mākiya āṉiṉattik koṇarntu tarum, paki tali maṇanta - pakivaruṭaṉ porutal kūṭiya, pal atar - palavākiya neṟikaḷil, mukitali tiṟanta vēṉil - mali muḻiñcukaḷ veṭittaṟ kētuvākiya vēṉiṟ kālattē, celavu cellutali;
4. yāṅku vallunaḷ kol - eṅṅaṉam vallaḷ āyiṉaḷē!
(muṭipu) īṉāttāyar tīmpāl tēm peytu maṭuppavum uṇṇāḷ, emmuṭic celvamum uḷḷāḷ, vari nāṭaṉ taṟ pārāṭṭa, poy maruṇṭu paki talimaṇanta pallataril, vēṉil celavu yāṅku vallunaḷ kol!
eḻiṉi viṭu toḻil muṭimār maṟavar, muṉi kaṭantu ēṟṟiṉam tarūum paki tali maṇanta pallatar eṉka.
(vi-ri) melleṉa eṉṟataṉic celavu eṉpataṉōṭu iyittu urittalumām. naṟṟāyiṉ vēṟu paṭukkac ceviliyi, īṉāt tāyar eṉṟār. cilpari - āti, maṇṭilam mutaliya celavu vaki. viṭutoḻil - ēviya toḻil; pakivar āṉiṉattik kavarntu varum toḻil; pakivar kavarnta taṉatu niriyi mīṭkum toḻilumām. kaḷ makiḻiṉ - kaḷḷiyuṇṭu makiḻntāl eṉṟumām. vēṟṟu iṉam eṉap pirittu, pakip pulattu iṉam eṉṟalumām. muki - malimuḻiñcu. ēṟṟiṉam tarūum atar, paki talimaṇanta atar eṉat taṉittaṉi kūṭṭuka.
106. marutam
(talimakaḷ taṉṉip puṟaṅkūṟiṉāḷākak kēṭṭa paratti 
avaṭkup pāṅkāyiṉār kēṭpac colliyatu.)
		eriyakin taṉṉa tāmarip paḻaṉattup
		poriyakin taṉṉa poṅkupala ciṟumīṉ
		veṟikoḷ pācaṭi yuṇīiyar pippayap
		paṟitapu mutuciral acipuvan tirukkun
	5.	tuṟikē ḻūraṉ peṇṭutaṉ koḻunaṉi
		nammoṭu pulakkum eṉpa nāmatu
		ceyyā māyiṉum uyyā miyiṉ
		ceṟitoṭi teḷirppa vīcic ciṟitavaṇ
		ulamantu varukam ceṉmō tōḻi
	10.	yoḷi ṟuvāṭ ṭāṉik koṟṟac ceḻiyaṉ
		veḷiṟil kaṟpiṉ maṇṭamar aṭutoṟum
		kaḷiṟupeṟu valcip pāṇaṉ eṟiyum
		taṇṇumik kaṇṇiṉ aliiyartaṉ vayiṟē.
- ālaṅkuṭi vaṅkaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 9. tōḻi-;
1-6. eri akintaṉṉa tāmarip paḻaṉattu - tī kiḷit terintāṟ pōlum tāmarip pūkkaḷiyuṭiya vayalil, pori akintaṉṉa poṅku pala ciṟu mīṉ uṇīiyar - neṟpori mutaliyaṉa teṟittāṟ pōṉṟu viḷaṅkum pala ciṟiya mīṉkaḷi uṇṇum poruṭṭu, veṟi koḷ pācaṭi - maṇaṅkoṇṭa paciya iliyil, paṟitapu mutuciral - paṟattal oḻinta mutiya ciccilip paṟavi, pippaya acipu vantirukkum - mella mella acintu vantirukkum, tuṟikēḻ ūraṉ peṇṭu - tuṟi poruntiya ūraṉiṉ maṉivi, taṉ koḻunaṉi nammoṭu pulakkum eṉpa - taṉ kaṇavaṉi nammoṭu kūṭṭi veṟuttup pēcukiṉṟāḷ eṉpar;
6-7. nām atu ceyyām āyiṉum uyyāmiyiṉ - nām ataṟkētuvāya toṉṟum ceyyām āyiṉum avaḷ kūṟum paḻiyiṉiṉṟum nīṅka māṭṭāmiyiṉ;
10-13. oḷiṟu vāḷ tāṉi koṟṟac ceḻiyaṉ - viḷaṅkum vāṭpaṭiyiṉiyuṭiya veṟṟi poruntiya pāṇṭiyaṉ, veḷiṟu il kaṟpiṉ maṇṭu amar aṭutoṟum - kuṟṟamillāta paṭip
payiṟciyoṭu kūṭi neruṅkiya pōril aṭuntōṟum, kaḷiṟu peṟu valcip pāṇaṉ eṟiyum - kaḷiṟāya uṇaviṉip peṟum pāṇaṉ aṭikkum, taṇṇumik kaṇṇiṉ - mattaḷattiṉ kaṇpōla, taṉ vayiṟu ali iyar - avaḷ taṉ vayiṟṟi alikkumpaṭi;
8-9. ceṟi toṭi teḷirppa vīci - ceṟinta vaḷi olittiṭak kiyi vīci, ciṟitu avaṇ ulamantu varukam - ciṟitu poḻutu avviṭattē ulāvi varuvōm, ceṉmō - varuvāyāka.
(muṭipu) tōḻi! tuṟi kēḻ ūraṉ peṇṭu, taṉ koḻunaṉi nammoṭu pulakkum eṉpa; nām atu ceyyā māyiṉum uyyāmiyiṉ, ceḻiyaṉ amar aṭutoṟum pāṇaṉ eṟiyum taṇṇumik kaṇṇiṉ, taṉ vayiṟu aliiyar, ciṟitu avaṇ ulamantu varukam, ceṉmō.
(vi-ri) teḷirttal - olittal. ceṉmō - vārāy eṉṟapaṭi. mō; muṉṉili yaci. kaṟpu - kalvi, paṭikkalap payiṟci.
(u-ṟi) mutumiyāl paṟakkamāṭṭāta ciral, mīṉukku aṇmiyil pācaṭi mītiruntum ataṉik kavaramāṭṭāmalum ēṉi yiḷañciral kavartaṟkup poṟāmalumiruttal pōl, mutumiyāl eḻucci kuṉṟiya talivi, taṉ maṉiyakattē kaṇavaṉik
koṇṭiruntum avaṉi vaḷittukkoḷḷa māṭṭāmalum, ēṉi yiḷam paruvamuṭiyār taḻuvutaṟkup poṟāmalum irukkiṉṟāḷ eṉpatu.
(mē-ḷ) `paratti vāyil'1  eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, `ceṟitoṭi teḷirppa vīcic ciṟitivaṇ - ulamantu varukañ ceṉmō tōḻi' eṉṟakkāl talivikkup pāṅkāyiṉār kēṭpac collik kūṟal vēṇṭu meṉpatu eṉṟaṉar, pērā.


107. pāli
(tōḻi talimakaḷ kuṟippaṟintu vantu talimakaṟkuc colliyatu.)
		nīcela vayarak kēṭṭoṟum palaniṉin
		taṉpiṉ neñcat tayāappoṟi melinta
		eṉṉakat tiṭumpi kaḷimār niṉṉoṭu
		karuṅkal viyalaṟik kiṭappi vayiṟutiṉ
	5.	ṟirumpuli tuṟanta ēṟṟumāṉ uṇaṅkal
		neṟicel vampalar uvantaṉar āṅkaṇ
		olikaḻi nelliṉ ariciyō ṭōrāṅ
		kāṉilip paḷḷi aḷipey taṭṭa
		vāṉiṇam urukkiya vāal veṇcōṟu
	10.	pukararit tēkkiṉ akalili māntum
		kallā nīṇmoḻik katanāy vaṭukar
		vallāṇ arumuṉi nīnti allāntu
		ukumaṉū ṟañcum orukāṟ paṭṭat
		tiṉṉā ēṟṟat tiḻukki muṭaṅkūrn
	15.	torutaṉit toḻinta uraṉuṭi nōṉpaka
		ṭaṅkuḻi iruppi aṟivāy vāṉpuḻal
		pulluḷic ciṟāar villiṉ nīkki
		marikaṭin tūṭṭum variyakac cīṟūr
		māli iṉṟuṇi yākik kālip
	20.	pacunaṉi naṟuvīp parūuppara luṟippa
		maṇamaṉi kamaḻum kāṉam
		tuṇiyī rōtiyeṉ tōḻiyum varumē.
- kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉattuk kārikkaṇṇaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 22. (taliva,) tuṇi īr ōti eṉ tōḻiyum - kaṭiyotta kuḷirnta kūntaliyuṭiya eṉ tōḻiyum;
1-3. nī celavu ayarak kēṭṭoṟum - nī uṭaṉkoṇṭu cellutali virumpa avviruppiṉik kēṭkuntoṟum, aṉpiṉ neñcattup pala niṉintu - aṉpiṉiyuṭiya neñcattu (muṉpu nikaḻnta) palavaṟṟiyum eṇṇi (ataṉoṭu), ayāap poṟi melinta eṉ akattu iṭumpi kaḷimār - varuttattit tāṅkuvatāl melintuḷa eṉ neñcattuḷḷa tuṉpiṉi nīkkum poruṭṭu;
4-12. irumpuli karuṅkal viyal aṟi kiṭappi - periya puliyāṉatu kariya kaṟkaḷiyuṭiya akaṉṟa pāṟiyil kiṭatti, vayiṟu tiṉṟu tuṟanta ēṟṟumāṉ uṇaṅkal - vayiṟu niṟiyat tiṉṟu viṭṭup pōṉa māṉēṟṟiṉ kāynta taciyi, neṟi cel vampalar- vaḻic celvōrāya putiyar, uvantaṉar āṅkaṇ - kaṇṭu makiḻn taṉarāy (ataṉi) avviṭattē, oli kaḻi nelliṉ ariciyōṭu ōrāṅku - taḻitta mūṅkiliṉ viḷinta nelliṉ ariciyuṭaṉ oruṅkē kūṭṭi, āṉilip paḷḷi aḷipeytu aṭṭa - āyar cēriyiluḷḷa tayiriṉip peytu camitta, vāl niṇam urukkiya vāl veṇcōṟu - veḷḷiya niṇattiṉi urukacceyta mika veḷḷiya cōṟṟiṉi, pukar arit tēkkiṉ akal ili māntum - puḷḷi poruntiya ariyiṉiyuṭiya tēkka marattiṉatu akaṉṟa iliyil vittu uṇṇuvatum, kallā nīḷmoḻik kata nāy vaṭukar - kalviyillāta neṭumoḻi kūṟum ciṉam mikka nāyiyuṭiya vaṭukaratu, val āṇ arumuṉi - valiya āṇmi viḷaṅkum ariya pōr muṉiyāvatumākiya curattiṉi, nīnti - kaṭantu ceṉṟu;
13-15. uku maṇ ūṟu añcum oru kāl paṭṭattu - iṭintu viḻum maṇṇiṉ iṭiyūṟṟiṉi añcuvatāṉa orē tuṟiyiṉi yuṭiya ōṭiyiluḷḷa, iṉṉā ēṟṟattu iḻukki muṭam kūrntu - iṉṉātatākiya ēṟṟaṅkoṇṭa neṟiyil vaḻukki viḻuntu mikka muṭampaṭṭu, oru taṉittu oḻinta uraṉ uṭi nōṉ pakaṭu - taṉṉan taṉiyē oḻintu kiṭakkum uṭalvali vāynta poṟukkun taṉmiyuṭiya pakaṭṭiṉi;
16-21. pul uḷic ciṟār - pulliya kuṭumiyiṉiyuṭiya ciṟuvar, am kuḻi iruppi aṟi vāy vāṉ puḻal - aḻakiya taḷiriyuṭiya iruppiyiṉ aṟṟa vāyiṉiyum veḷḷiya tuḷiyiṉiyumuṭiya pūvi, villiṉ nīkki - villiṉāl utirttu, mari kaṭintu ūṭṭum - ataṉit tiṉṉavarum marimāṉit  turantu uṇpikkum, vari akac cīṟūr - variyiṭattē yuḷḷa ciṟiya ūrkaḷil, māli iṉtuṇi āki - mālik kālattē niṉakku iṉiya tuṇiyākit taṅki, kāli pacu naṉi naṟu vī - kālip pōtil putiya tēṉiyuṭiya naṟiya pūkkaḷ, parūup paral uṟippa - periya pāṟik kaṟkaḷil utirntu kiṭattalāṉ, maṇa maṉi kamaḻuṅ kāṉam - maṇam nikaḻum maṉi pōṉṟu nāṟuṅ kāṭṭil;
3. niṉṉoṭu - niṉṉuṭaṉ;
22. varum - vārā niṟpaḷ.
(muṭipu) taliva! nī celavu ayarak kēṭṭoṟum eṉ tōḻiyum, pala niṉintu, eṉṉakattu iṭumpi kaḷimār, vampalar veṇcōṟu māntuvatum vaṭukar arumuṉiyāvatu mākiya curattiṉi nīnti variyakac cīṟūr māli iṉṟuṇi yāki, kāli maṇamaṉi kamaḻum kāṉam niṉṉoṭu varum.
(vi-ri) vayiṟu tiṉṟu - vayiṟu niṟiyat tiṉṟu. aṭṭa veṇcōṟu eṉavum, vāl niṇa murukkiya veṇcōṟu eṉavum kūṭṭuka. aṭutalāl niṇam urukiya teṉka. vampalar veṇcōṟu māntum muṉi eṉavum, vaṭukar aṭumuṉi eṉavum taṉittaṉi iyiyum, pakaṭu - pakaṭṭiṉi. orukāṟ paṭṭam - orē tuṟiyiṉiyuṭiya ōṭi. puḻal, pūviṟku ākupeyar. māli iṉtuṇi yāki eṉpataṟku - mālip poḻutil nī iṉiya tuṇi yākiyālē eṉṟurittalumām. itaṟku āki eṉṟataṉi āka eṉat tirikka.
(u-ṟi) ōṭiyilē nīruṇṭu atiṉiṉṟum ēṟamāṭṭāta muṭampaṭṭa uraṉuṭip pakaṭṭiṟkuc ciṟār iruppip pūvi marattiṉiṉṟum pirittu mariyiṉik kaṭintu ūṭṭiṉāṟpōla, kaḷavoḻukkamākiya iṉpatti nukarntu, ivvoḻukkat tiṉiṉṟum nīṅki varivoṭu puka māṭṭātu varuntum niṉakkuk kuṟṟēvaṉ makaḷākiya yāṉ talimakaḷic cuṟṟattiṉiṉṟum pirittu notumalar variviṉi māṟṟi uṭaṉ pōkkiṟku oruppaṭuttā niṉṟēṉ eṉpatu.
108. kuṟiñci
(talimakaṉ ciṟippuṟattāṉākat talimakaṭkuc 
colluvāḷāyt tōḻi colliyatu.)
		puṇarntōr puṉkaṇ aruḷalum uṇarntōrk
		kottaṉṟu maṉṉāl evaṉkol muttam
		varimutal citaṟi yavi1pōl yāṉip
		pukarmukam poruta putunīr āli
	5.	paḷiṅkucori vatupōl pāṟi varippak
		kārkatam paṭṭa kaṇṇakaṉ vicumpiṉ
		viṭupoṟi ñekiḻiyil koṭipaṭa miṉṉip
		paṭumaḻi poḻinta pāṉāṭ kaṅkul
		āruyirt tuppil kōṇmā vaḻaṅkum
	10.	iruḷiṭit tamiyaṉ varutal yāvatum
		aruḷāṉ vāḻi tōḻi alkal
		viravuppoṟi maññi verīi yaraviṉ
		aṇaṅkuṭi yaruntali pivirip pavipōl
		kāyā meṉciṉi tōya nīṭip
	15.	paḵṟuṭup peṭutta alaṅkukulik kāntaḷ
		aṇimalar naṟuntā tūtun tumpi
		kiyāṭu vaṭṭil tōṉṟum
		miyāṭu ceṉṉiya malikiḻa vōṉē.
- taṅkāṟ poṟkollaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 11. tōḻi vāḻi-,
12-18. viravup poṟi maññi verīi - puḷḷikaḷ viraviya mayilik kaṇ ṭañci, araviṉ aṇaṅkuṭi arum tali pi virippavi pōl - pāmpiṉ varuttattic ceyyum ariya nañciṉiyuṭiya talikaḷ paṭatti virippaṉa pōla, kāyā meṉciṉi tōya - kāyāviṉ melliya ciṉikaḷ tōya, nīṭip pal tuṭuppu eṭutta alaṅku kulik kāntaḷ aṇi malar - nīṇṭu tūkkiya pala tuṭuppukaḷ pōlum aciyum kulikaḷiyuṭiya kāntaḷatu aḻakiya malariṉ, naṟum tātu ūtum tumpi - naṟiya tātiṉik kuṭintuṇṇum vaṇṭukaḷ, ki āṭu vaṭṭil tōṉṟum - kiyiṉkaṇ vittu āṭum vaṭṭup pōlat tōṉṟum, mi āṭu ceṉṉiya mali kiḻavōṉ - mēkam tavaḻum cimiyaṅkaḷiyuṭiya malikku uriyaṉākiya nam talivaṉ;
2-11. muttam vari mutal citaṟiyavi pōl - malittaliyil muttukkaḷ citaṟiyavi pōla viḷaṅkum, yāṉip pukar mukam poruta putu nīr āli - yāṉiyiṉ puḷḷiyiṉiyuṭiya mukattil mōti vīḻnta putiya ālaṅkaṭṭi, paḷiṅku corivatu pōl pāṟi varippa - paḷiṅkiṉic corivatu pōlap pāṟiyil vīḻntu kōlam ceyya, kār katam paṭṭa kaṇ akal vicumpil - mukilkaḷ ciṉanteḻuntaṉavāya iṭamakaṉṟa vāṉil, viṭu poṟi ñekiḻiyiṉ koṭi paṭa miṉṉi - poṟiviṭukiṉṟa koḷḷi pōl oḻuṅkupaṭa miṉṉi, paṭu maḻi poḻinta pāṉāḷ kaṅkul - mikka maḻiyic corinta naṭu nāḷākiya iravil, ār uyir tuppiṉ - ariya uyirp poruḷākiya uṇaviṉiyuṭiya, kōṇmā vaḻaṅkum iruḷiṭi - kollum toḻiluṭiya vilaṅkukaḷ tiriyum iruḷiṭiyē, alkal tamiyaṉ varutal yāvatum aruḷāṉ - nāṭōṟum taṉiyē varutalāṉē ciṟitum aruḷ ceykiṉṟāṉallaṉ;
1-2. puṇarntōr puṉkaṇ aruḷalum - tammic cārntōratu tuṉpiṉip pōkkiyaruḷalum, uṇarntōrkku ottaṉṟu - aṟivuṭiyārkkup poruntiyatākumē, maṉ - nam talivaṉ aṅṅaṉam ceytilaṉ, evaṉkol - eṉṉiyō?
(muṭipu) mali kiḻavōṉākiya nam talivaṉ pāṉāṭ kaṅkul kōṇmā vaḻaṅkum iruḷiṭi alkal tamiyaṉ varutalāṉē yāvatum aruḷāṉ; puṇarntōr puṉkaṇ aruḷalum uṇarntōrkku ottaṉṟu maṉ; evaṉ kol?
(vi-ri) puṉkaṇ aruḷalum - puṉkaṇṇi nīkki yaruḷalum eṉka; maṉ : oḻiyici. variyiṭattuc citaṟiya muttam pōl yāṉi mukattiṟ poruta āli eṉpatum, maññi verīi araviṉ tali pi virippavi pōl kāyā meṉ ciṉi tōya nīṭi eṭutta kulik kāntaḷ eṉpatum, ki yāṭu vaṭṭil pōlak kāntaḷ malari yūtum tumpi tōṉṟum eṉpatum iraṭṭik kiḷaviyākiya uvamaṅkaḷām. āli - nīrkkaṭṭi; nīr eṉa muṉ vantamiyiṉ īṇṭup peyar māttiriyāka niṉṟatu. mukil kumuṟi eḻuntatik katampaṭṭatu eṉṟār. kōṇmā; ciṅkam mutaliyaṉa; oruvaki vilaṅkumām. eṉṉi? `accap poruḷāvaṉa : vaḷḷeyiṟ ṟarimā vāḷvari vēṅki, muḷḷeyiṟ ṟaravē muḻaṅkaḻaṟ centī, īṟṟā matamā ēka pātam, kūṟṟaṅ kōṇmā kuṉṟuṟi yacuṇam eṉṟu collap paṭṭaṉa pōlvaṉa' eṉṟār pērāciriyar ākaliṉ. varutal - varutalāl eṉa urupu virikka. kāyāviṉ meṉciṉikku maññiyum, tūkkiya kāntaṭ kulikkup pāmpu paṭam virittatum uvamam. tumpikku vaṭṭu, vaṇṇam, vaṭivu, toḻil eṉṉum mūṉṟum paṟṟiya uvamiyām.
(u-ṟi) kaṇṭār virumpattakka kāntaṭkuli añcattakka araviṉ paṭam pōlum eṉṟatu, nammāl virumpattakka talivaṉ varuki, āṟṟatu tīmiyāl, namakku accatti viḷivikkiṉṟatu eṉṟu talivaṉ uṇarumāṟu tōḻi kūṟiyapaṭiyām.
(mē-ḷ) `viraviyum varūum marapiṉa veṉpa'1 eṉṉum cūttirattu, `kāyā meṉciṉi....... malikiḻa vōṉē' eṉpuḻi, āṭutaṟṟoḻil paṟṟiyum, vaṭivupaṟṟiyum, vaṇṇampaṟṟiyum (uvami) vantatu eṉpar, pērā.
109. pāli
(iṭiccurattut talimakaṉ taṉ neñciṟkuc colliyatu.)
		pallitaḻ meṉmalar uṇkaṇ nalyāḻ
		narampicit taṉṉa iṉṟīṅ kiḷavi
		nalanal korutti irunta ūrē
		kōṭuḻu kaḷiṟṟiṉ toḻuti yīṇṭik
	5.	kāṭukāl yātta nīṭumarac cōli
		viḻiveḷil āṭuṅ kaḻivaḷar naṉantali
		2veṇṇuṉi yampiṉ viciyiṭa vīḻntōr
		eṇṇuvaram paṟiyā uvaliṭu patukkic
		curaṅkeḻu kavali kōṭpāṟ paṭṭeṉa
	10.	vaḻaṅkunar maṭinta attam iṟantōr
		kipporuḷ illiyāyiṉum 3meykkoṇ
		ṭiṉṉuyir cekāar viṭṭakal tappaṟkup
		peruṅkaḷiṟṟu maruppoṭu variyataḷ iṟukkum
		aṟaṉil vēntaṉ āḷum
	15.	vaṟaṉuṟu kuṉṟam palavilaṅ kiṉavē.
- kaṭuntoṭik kāviṉār.
(co-ḷ) 1-3. pal itaḻ mel malar uṇ kaṇ - pala itaḻ kaḷiyuṭiya melliya malar pōṉṟa miyuṇṭa kaṇṇiṉiyum, nalyāḻ narampu icittaṉṉa iṉ tīm kiḷavi - nalla yāḻiṉ narampu olippatu pōṉṟa mika iṉiya moḻiyiṉiyum uṭiya, nalam nalku orutti - nalaṉellām taravalla oppaṟṟa nam kātali, irunta ūr - irukkum ūrāṉatu;
4-10. kōṭu uḻu kaḷiṟṟiṉ toḻuti īṇṭi - tam kompiṉāl pakiyāyavaṟṟik kutti uḻum kaḷiṟṟiyāṉikaḷiṉ kūṭṭam kūṭa, kāṭu kāl yātta nīṭu maraccōli - kāṭākap paranta nīṇṭa maraṅkaḷiyuṭiya cōliyiluḷḷa, viḻi veḷil āṭum kaḻivaḷar  naṉantali - oṉṟi yoṉṟu viḻinta aṇilkaḷ kūṭi āṭum mūṅkil vaḷarnta akaṉṟaviṭattē, veḷ nuṉi ampiṉ vici iṭa vīḻntōr - niṇam tōytalāl veḷḷiya muṉiyiṉiyuṭiya ampiṉ vēkam paṭa iṟantōrkaḷiṉ, eṇṇu varampu aṟiyā uval iṭu patukki - eṇṇiṉ elli aṟiya lākāta taḻiyiṭṭu mūṭiya patukkikaḷiyuṭiya, curam keḻu kavali kōḷpāl paṭṭeṉa - curattiṟ poruntiya kavar neṟikaḷ āṟalikkum pakutiyiṟ koḷḷap peṟṟatāka, vaḻaṅkunar maṭinta attam - vaḻippōvār illiyākiya ac cura neṟiyil;
10-15. iṟantōr kip poruḷ illi yāyiṉum - aṟiyātu vantōr kiyiṟ poruḷ illiyāyiṉum, mey koṇṭu iṉ uyir cekār viṭṭu akal tappaṟku - avartam meyyip paṟṟik koṇṭu avar iṉiya uyiriṉik kollārāy viṭṭu akaṉṟu vanta piḻikkāka, perum kaḷiṟṟu maruppoṭu vari ataḷ iṟukkum - periya kaḷiṟṟiṉ kompoṭu puliyiṉ vari poruntiya tōlit taṇṭamākat taṉ ēvalari iṟukkac ceyyum, aṟaṉil vēntaṉ āḷum vaṟaṉ uṟu kuṉṟam pala vilaṅkiṉa - aṟaṉillāta aracaṉ āḷum vaṟaṭciyuṟṟa kuṉṟukaḷ pala kuṟukkiṭṭuḷḷaṉavē; (eṉṟu talivaṉ taṉ neñciṟkuk kūṟiṉāṉ).
(muṭipu) nalam nalku orutti irunta ūr, aṟaṉil vēntaṉ āḷum kuṉṟam pala vilaṅkiṉa.
cōliyi yuṭiya akaṉṟa iṭattil vīḻntōratu patukkikaḷ poruntiya curaṅ keḻu kavali kōṭpāṟ paṭṭeṉa, vaḻaṅkunar maṭinta attam eṉṟum, attam iṟantōr uyir cekāar viṭṭakal tappaṟku maruppoṭu vari ataḷ iṟukkum aṟaṉil vēntaṉ eṉṟum kūṭṭuka.
(vi-ri) īṇṭi - īṇṭa eṉat tirikka. kāl yāttal - parattal. kōṭpāl tīṅkiṉ pakuti; eṉṟatu āṟalikkum pakuti iṟukkum - ēvalari iṟukkac ceyyum eṉa viritturikka. aṟaṉil vēntaṉ eṉṟatu - kāṭṭiṉi āḷum talivaṉi. kuṉṟam pala vilaṅkiṉa vātaliṉ ittuṇiyum pōntu iṉi vaṟitē mīḷutal ollā teṉṟāṉ eṉka.
(mē-ḷ) `veṟiyaṟi ciṟappiṉ'1 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, `aṟaṉil vēnta ṉāḷum, vaṟaṉuṟu kuṉṟam pala vilaṅkiṉavē' eṉa kāṭṭut talivaṉi nāṭṭut talivaṉ peyarāṟ kūṟiṉar eṉṟaṉar, nac.
110. neytal
(tōḻi cevilittāykku aṟattoṭu niṉṟatu.)
		aṉṉi aṟiyiṉum aṟika alarvāy
		ammeṉ cēri kēṭpiṉuṅ kēṭka
		piṟitoṉ ṟiṉmi aṟiyak kūṟik
		koṭuñcuḻip pukāart teyvam nōkkik
	5.	kaṭuñcūḷ tarukuval niṉakkē2 kāṉal
		toṭali yāyamoṭu kaṭaluṭaṉ āṭiyuñ
		ciṟṟil iḻittum ciṟucōṟu kuviiyum
		varuntiya varuttam tīra yāñciṟi
		tiruntaṉa māka eyta vantu
	10.	taṭameṉ paṇittōḷ maṭanal līrē
		ellum elliṉ ṟacivumika uṭiyēṉ
		mellilip parappiṉ viruntuṇ ṭiyāṉumik
		kalleṉ ciṟukuṭit taṅkiṉmaṟ ṟevaṉō
		eṉamoḻin taṉaṉē oruvaṉ avaṟkaṇ
	15.	ṭiṟiñciya mukattem puṟañcērpu porunti
		ivinumak kuriya valla iḻinta
		koḻumīṉ valci eṉṟaṉam iḻumeṉa
		neṭuṅkoṭi nuṭaṅkum nāvāy tōṉṟuva
		kāṇā mōveṉak kāliṟ citiyā
	20.	nillātu peyarnta pallō ruḷḷum
		eṉṉē kuṟitta nōkkamoṭu naṉṉutāl
		oḻikō yāṉeṉa aḻitakak kūṟi
		yāṉpeyar keṉṉa nōkkit tāṉtaṉ
		neṭuntērk koṭiñci paṟṟi
	25.	niṉṟōṉ pōlum 3iṉṟumeṉ kaṭkē.
- pōntip pacaliyār.
(co-ḷ) 5-9. toṭali āyamoṭu kaṭal uṭaṉ āṭiyum - mālipōṉṟa āyattārōṭu kaṭalil oruṅku viḷiyāṭiyum, kāṉal ciṟṟil iḻittum - kaṭaṟkaric cōliyil ciṟu vīṭu kaṭṭiyum, ciṟu cōṟu kuviiyum - ciṟu cōṟṟi aṭṭuk kuvittum, varuntiya varuttam tīra - iṅṅaṉam varuntiya varuttam nīṅka, yām ciṟitu iruntaṉamāka - yām ciṟitu iḷippāṟi yiruntēmāka (atupōtu);
9-14. oruvaṉ eyta vantu - oru talivaṉ emmiṭam aṇuka vantu, taṭameṉ paṇit tōḷ maṭa nallīrē - periya melliya mūṅkil pōlum tōḷiṉiyuṭiya maṭappam vāynta nallīrē, ellum elliṉṟu - pakalum oḷi iḻantatu, acivu mika uṭiyēṉ - taḷarcci mikavum uṭiyēṉ, mel ilip parappiṉ viruntu uṇṭu yāṉum - yāṉum melliya ilip parappiṉ nīyir iṭum viruntuṇaviṉi uṇṭu, ik kal eṉ ciṟu kuṭit taṅkiṉ evaṉō - intak kalleṉṟa āravāramuṭiya ciṟukuṭiyil taṅkiṉāl eytum kuṟi yātu? eṉa moḻintaṉaṉ - eṉṟu kūṟiṉaṉ;
14-17. avaṉ kaṇṭu iṟiñciya mukattem - avaṉik kaṇṭu kaviḻnta mukattēmāy, puṟam cērpu porunti - maṟivāṉa iṭattē cērntiruntu, iḻum eṉa - iḻumeṉṟa melliya kuralil, ivi numakku uriya alla - ivvuṇavu numakku ēṟṟaṉa alla, iḻinta koḻumīṉ valci eṉṟaṉam - iḻivāya koḻumīṉālāya uṇavu eṉṟu kūṟiṉēm: (piṉṉar;)
18-22. neṭuṅkoṭi nuṭaṅkum nāvāy tōṉṟuva - nīṇṭa koṭikaḷ aciyāniṟkum nāvāykaḷ tōṉṟuvaṉavaṟṟi, kāṇāmō eṉa- yām kāṇpē mallēmō eṉṟu kūṟi, kāliṉ citiyā - em ciṟṟil ciṟu cōṟukaḷik kālāṟ citittu viṭṭu, nillātu peyarnta pallōruḷḷum -aṅku nillātu pōya palaruḷḷum, eṉṉē kuṟitta nōkkamoṭu - avaṉ eṉṉiyē kuṟittu nōkkiya pārviyoṭu, nal nutāl oḻikō yāṉ eṉa aḻitakak kūṟi - nalla neṟṟiyiṉi yuṭiyāḷē cellukō eṉṟu eṉ neñcam aḻintiṭak kūṟa;
23-25. yāṉ peyarka eṉṉa - yāṉ celka eṉṉalum, nōkki - (peyarātu eṉṉi) nōkkiyavaṉāy, tāṉ taṉ neṭun tērk koṭiñci paṟṟi - avaṉ taṉatu nīṇṭa tēriṉ koṭiñciyiṉip paṟṟik koṇṭu, niṉṟōṉ - niṉṟaṉaṉ, iṉṟum eṉ kaṭkup pōlum - iṉṟum eṉ kaṇ muṉ niṟpatu anniliyē pōlum;
1-5. aṉṉi aṟiyiṉum aṟika - itaṉi aṉṉi aṟiyiṉum aṟivāḷāka, alar vāy a meṉ cēri kēṭpiṉum kēṭka - alar kūṟum vāyiṉiyuṭiya am melliya cēriyiṉar kēṭpiṉum kēṭṭiṭuka, piṟitu oṉṟum iṉmi aṟiyak kūṟi - vēṟoṉṟum illāmiyi nī yaṟiyak kūṟi, koṭuñ cuḻip pukārt teyvam nōkki - vaḷinta cuḻikaḷ mēviya pukāriṭattuḷḷa teyvatti nōkki, niṉakkuk kaṭuñcūḷ tarukuvaṉ - niṉakkuk kaṭiya cūḷ ceytu taruvaṉ.
(muṭipu) kāṉal toṭali āyamoṭu varuntiya varuttam tīra yām ciṟitu iruntaṉam; oruvaṉ, acivu mika uṭiyēṉ, viruntuṇṭu ic ciṟu kuṭit taṅkiṉ evaṉō eṉa moḻintaṉaṉ; iḻinta koḻumīṉ valci eṉṟaṉam; nillātu peyarnta pallōruḷḷum eṉṉē kuṟitta nōkkamoṭu oḻikō yāṉeṉa aḻitakak kūṟi, yāṉ peyarka eṉṉa, koṭiñci paṟṟi niṉṟōṉ pōlum iṉṟum eṉ kaṭkē; (itaṉi) aṉṉi aṟiyiṉum aṟika; cēri kēṭpiṉum kēṭka. piṟitoṉṟiṉmi aṟiyak kūṟip pukārt teyvam nōkki niṉakkuk kaṭuñcūḷ tarukuvaṉ.
(vi-ri) `aṉṉi eṉṟatu naṟṟāyi. cēri: ākupeyar. taliviyuṟṟa nōykkuk kāraṇam piṟitoṉṟu miṉmiyi eṉka, piṟitoṉṟum eṉṉum muṟṟummi tokkatu. maṟṟevaṉō: maṟṟu yāṉ eṉṉūrkkuc celluva toḻintu eṉap poruḷ tarutaliṉ viṉimāṟṟu. eṉṉē - eṉṉiyē. kūṟi, kūṟa eṉat tirikka. niṉṟōṉ pōlum : niṉṟōṉ eṉa muṟṟākki anniliyē pōlum eṉa viritturikka. eṉṟu meṉ makaṭkē eṉpatu pāṭamāyiṉ, aṅṅaṉam niṉṟavaṉē eṉṟum eṉ makaṭku uriyaṉ pōlumeṉa aṉṉi aṟiyiṉum aṟika eṉa iyitturikka.
kaṭuñcūḷ eṉpataṉi, kaṭi eṉṉum uriccol muṉṉēṟṟup poruḷil vantataṟku eṭuttukkāṭṭi, muṉṉēṟṟu, puṟattilaṉṟit `teyva mutalāyiṉavaṟṟiṉ muṉṉiṉṟu teḷittal'1 eṉpar, cēṉā.
mēlellām āyattāriyum taliviyiyum uḷappaṭuttik kūṟiya tōḻi piṉṉar `nillātu peyarnta pallē muḷḷum'2 eṉṉātu, pallōruḷḷu meṉat taṉṉi vēṟu pirittuk kūṟiyatu avarellām talivaṉukku iraṅkātu ceṉṟamiyum, tāṉ maṭṭum taṉittu niṉṟamiyum tōṉṟak kūṟiyatākum eṉpar, nac.
(mē-ḷ) `māyōṉ mēya'3 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, neytaṉilattil nuḷiyarkku valivaḷam tappiṉ am makaḷir kiḷiyuṭaṉ kuḻīic cuṟavukkōṭu naṭṭup paravukkaṭaṉ koṭuttaliṉ āṇṭu varuṇaṉ veḷippaṭum eṉṟu kūṟi, koṭuñcuḻip pukāart teyvam nōkkik kaṭuñcūḷ tarukuval eṉa varum eṉṟum `meytoṭṭup payiṟal'1 eṉṉum cūttirattu, `mellilip parappiṉ viruntuṇṭi yāṉumik, kalleṉ ciṟukuṭit taṅkiṉ maṟṟevaṉō' eṉa varuvatu `ūrum pērum keṭutiyum piṟavum.... kūṟi' eṉṟa cūttirap pakutiyil, piṟavum eṉṟataṉāṟ koḷka eṉṟum, ac cūttirattut `taṇṭātu irappiṉum' eṉṉum pakutikkaṇ, `ellum.... oruvaṉ eṉat `talivaṉ iravukkuṟi vēṇṭiyataṉit tōḻi kūṟiyavāṟu kāṇka' eṉṟum, `eḷitta lēttal'2 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, `ellum.... oruvaṉ' itu eḷittal eṉṟum, accūttirattu, piṟitoṉṟiṉmi..... kaṭuñcū ṭarukuva ṉiṉakkē' itu talippāṭu eṉṟum kūṟuvar, nac.
`kaṇṇiṉuñ ceviyiṉum' eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, `oḻikō yāṉeṉa aḻitakak kūṟi'3 eṉpuḻi, talimakaṉ maṉattu nikaḻnta aḻivellām oḻikō yāṉ eṉṟa uriyāṉē uṇarntamiyiṉ atu ceviyuṇarvu eṉappaṭum eṉṟu kūṟiṉar, pērā.
111. pāli
(talimakaṉ piriviṉkaṇ tōḻi talimakaḷi yāṟṟuvittatu.)
		uḷḷāṅ kuvattal cellār kaṟuttōr
		eḷḷal neñcat tēeccol nāṇi
		varuvar vāḻi tōḻi yaraca
		yāṉi koṇṭa tukiṟkoṭi pōla
	5.	alantali ñemiyattu valanta cilampi
		ōṭik kuṉṟattuk kōṭiyoṭu tuyalvara
		maḻiyeṉa maruṇṭa mammar palavuṭaṉ
		oykaḷi ṟeṭutta nōyuṭi neṭuṅki
		tokucoṟ kōṭiyar tūmpiṉ uyirkkum
	10.	attak kēḻal aṭṭa naṟkōḷ
		cennāy ēṟṟi kammeṉa īrppak
		kuruti yārum eruvic ceñcevi
		maṇṭama raḻuvat tellik koṇṭa
		puṇtēr viḷakkiṉ tōṉṟum
	15.	viṇtōy piṟaṅkal maliyiṟan tārē.
- pālipāṭiya peruṅkaṭuṅkō.
(co-ḷ) 3. tōḻi vāḻi-;
1-2. uḷḷāṅku uvattal cellār - tamakkup poruḷ uḷḷa aḷaviṟku makiḻta lilarāy, kaṟuttōr eḷḷal neñcattu ēec col nāṇi - pakivar ikaḻum neñcattuṭaṉ kūṟum ampu pōlum colliṟku nāṇiṉarāy;
3-6. araca yāṉi koṇṭa tukiṟkoṭi pōla - paṭṭattu yāṉi taṉmītu koṇṭa tukiṟkoṭi pōla, ōṭik kuṉṟattu - ōṭi eṉṉum kuṉṟattuḷḷa, alantali ñemiyattu - kāynta taliyiṉiyuṭiya ñemi marattiṉmītu, valanta cilampi - piṉṉiya cilampiyiṉ kūṭāṉatu, kōṭiyoṭu tuyalvara - mēl kāṟṟāl aciya;
7-15. maḻi eṉa maruṇṭa - ataṉi mēkam eṉa mayaṅkiya, mammar oy kaḷiṟu pala uṭaṉ - mayakkattiṉiyuṭiya iḷitta kaḷiṟukaḷ palavum oruṅkē, eṭutta nōyuṭi neṭuṅ ki - tūkkiya varuttattiṉiyuṭiya nīṇṭa kikaḷ, toku col kōṭiyar tūmpiṉ uyirkkum attam - pukaḻiṉit tiraṭṭik kūṟum kūttaratu tūmpiṉip pōlat tōṉṟi olikkum kāṭṭiluḷḷa, kēḻal aṭṭa nalkōḷ cennāy ēṟṟi - āṇ paṉṟiyiṉik koṉṟa taṉ iriyiṉi naṉku paṟṟikkoḷḷum tiṟaṉuṭiya cennāyiṉ ēṟṟi, kam eṉa īrppa - virivāka ataṉi iḻuttiṭa, kuruti ārum eruvic ceñcevi - vaḻiyelām oḻukiya kurutiyiṉip parukum paruntiṉ civanta cevikaḷ, maṇṭu amar aḻuvattu ellik koṇṭa puṇtēr viḷakkiṉ tōṉṟum - mikka pōrkkaḷap parappil irāppoḻutil kikkoṇṭa vīrarkaḷatu marumattup puṇkaḷi ārāyum viḷakkukaḷip pōṉṟu tōṉṟum, viṇtōy piṟaṅkal mali iṟantōr - vāṉit tōyum viḷakkattiyuṭiya maliyiṉik kaṭantu poruḷ īṭṭac ceṉṟōrāya nam talivar;
3. varuvar - viriya varuvar (eṉat tōḻi taliviyi āṟṟuvittāḷ.)
(muṭipu) tōḻi vāḻi! uvattal cellār, ēec col nāṇi maliyiṟantōr varuvar.
(vi-ri) uḷḷāṅku - uḷḷapaṭi, ēeccol - perumitattuṭaṉ kūṟum collumām. alantatali alantali yeṉa vikārappaṭṭatu. ñemiyattu, attu : cāriyi. ōṭik kuṉṟattiṟku aracayāṉiyum, ñemiyattu valanta cilampik kūṭṭiṟkut tukiṟ koṭiyum uvamam. uṇṭaṟku nīriṉṟi melinta kaḷiṟu mēluḷḷa cilampikkūṭṭi mēkameṉa maruṇṭu atil nīruṇṭaṟkuk kiyi mēlē eṭutta teṉka. kōṭiyar tūmpiṉ oliyum yāṉiyiṉ uyirppoliyum oru taṉmiyaṉa eṉpatu, `kaṇṇiṭi viṭutta kaḷiṟṟuyirt tūmpiṉ'1 eṉpataṉālum aṟika. uyirkkum attam eṉavum, attattil kēḻalaṭṭa ēṟṟi īrppa ataṉ kurutiyi ārum eruvi eṉavum iyiyum. kuruti paṭinta attam amarkkaḷam pōlāka, eruvic ceñcevi āṇṭuḷḷa viḷakkup pōlat tōṉṟum.
kammeṉa : ticic col.
(u-ṟi) cilampik kūṭṭiṉi mēkameṉak karutum yāṉiyik kāṇum talivar, tām poruḷallataṉip poruḷeṉak karutiyataṉi uṇarntu viriya mīḷvar eṉṟapaṭi.
112. kuṟiñci
(iravukkuṟi vanta talimakaṉi yetirppaṭṭu niṉṟu 
tōḻi colli varivu kaṭāyatu.)
		1kūṉal eṇkiṉ kuṟunaṭit toḻuti
		citali ceyta cennilip puṟṟiṉ
		maṇpuṉi neṭuṅkō ṭuṭiya vāṅki
		irinaciip parikkum arināṭ kaṅkul
	5.	īṉṟaṇi vayavuppiṇap pacitteṉa maṟappuli
		oḷiṟēntu maruppiṉ kaḷiṟaṭṭuk kuḻumum
		paṉiyiruñ cōli yemiyam eṉṉāy
		tīṅkucey taṉiyē yīṅkuvan tōyē
		nāḷiṭip paṭiṉeṉ ṟōḻi vāḻāḷ
	10.	tōḷiṭi muyakkam nīyum veyyi
		2kaḻiyak kātalar āyiṉum cāṉṟōr
		paḻiyoṭu varūum iṉpam veḵkār
		variyiṉ evaṉō vāṉtōy veṟpa
		kaṇakkali ikukkum kaṟiyivar cilampiṉ
	15.	maṇapparuṅ kāmam puṇarntami aṟiyār
		toṉṟiyal marapiṉ maṉṟal ayarap
		peṇkōḷ oḻukkam kaṇkoḷa nōkki
		notumal viruntiṉam pōlavivaḷ
		putunāṇ oṭukkamum kāṇkuvam yāmē.
- neytaṟ cāyttuytta āvūr kiḻār.
(co-ḷ) 13. vāṉtōy veṟpa - vāṉi aḷāvum maliyi yuṭiya talivaṉē!,
1-4. kūṉal kuṟunaṭi eṇkiṉ toḻuti - kūṉiya mutukiṉiyum kuṟukakkuṟuka aṭiyiṭṭu naṭakkum naṭiyiṉiyum uṭiya karaṭik kūṭṭam, citali ceyta cennilip puṟṟiṉ - kaṟiyāṉ eṭutta civanta niliyiṉiyuṭiya puṟṟiṉatu, maṇpuṉi neṭuṅkōṭu uṭiya vāṅki - maṇṇāṟ puṉinta nīṇṭa ucci uṭintiṭap peyarttu, iri naciip parikkum arināḷ kaṅkul - puṟṟāñcōṟāya iriyiṉi virumpic cūḻntu koṇṭirukkum naṭunāḷ iravil;
5-8. īṉṟa aṇi vayavu piṇa pacitteṉa - kuṭṭiyi īṉṟa aṇmiyiṉiyuṭiya vēṭkiyiyuṭiya peṇpuli pacittatāka, maṟappuli - taṟukaṇmiyiyuṭiya āṇpuli, oḷiṟu ēntu maruppiṉ kaḷiṟu aṭṭuk kuḻumum - oḷi vīcukiṉṟa ēntiya kōṭṭiṉiyuṭiya āṇ yāṉiyik koṉṟu muḻaṅkukiṉṟa, paṉi irumcōli - kuḷirnta kariya cōliyil, emiyam eṉṉāy - yām tamiyēm eṉṟu niṉiyāyāy, tīṅku ceytaṉiyē īṅku vantōyē - īṅku varutaliṉ nī tīṅku ceytāy āvāy;
9-10. nāḷ iṭippaṭiṉ eṉ tōḻi vāḻāḷ - nī vārātu oru nāḷ iṭiyī ṭuṟiṉum eṉtōḻi uyir vāḻāḷkāṇ, tōḷiṭi muyakkam nīyum veyyi - aṅṅaṉē nāḷum eṉ tōḻiyiṉ tōḷiṉ kaṇ muyaṅkutali nīyum virumputaluṭiyi;
11-12. cāṉṟōr kaḻiyak kātalar āyiṉum - cāṉṟōrāvār mikak kātal koṇṭār āyiṭiṉum, paḻiyoṭu varūum iṉpam veḵkār - paḻiyuṭaṉ kūṭi varum iṉpiṉi virumpār ākaliṉ;
13. variyiṉ evaṉ - nī varintu koḷḷiṉ kuṟiyāvatu eṉṉi? (aṅṅaṉam varintiṭiṉ;)
14-16. kaṇam kali ikukkum kaṟi ivar cilampil - kūṭṭa mākiya kalimāṉkaḷ tāḻa olikkum miḷakuk koṭi paṭarnta maliccāralil, maṇappu arum kāmam puṇarntami aṟiyār - eytutaṟku ariya kāmattāl nīr kūṭiya kaḷavoḻukkattiṉi aṟiyāta emar, toṉṟu iyal marapiṉ maṉṟal ayara - toṉṟu toṭṭu varum muṟippaṭi vatuvi nikaḻttiṭa;
17-19. peṇkōḷ oḻukkam kaṇkoḷa nōkki - nī ivaḷi varintukoḷḷum avvoḻukkattiṉik kaṇṇāra nōkki, yām notumal viruntiṉam pōla - yām ayalēmākiya putiyēm pōla, ivaḷ putunāṇ oṭukkamum kāṇkuvam - ivaḷ putiya nāṇālākiya oṭukkattiṉiyum kāṇpē maṉṟō?
(muṭipu) ari nāṭ kaṅkul puli kaḷiṟu aṭṭuk kuḻumum cōliyil īṅku vantōy tīṅku ceytaṉi; nāḷ iṭippaṭiṉ eṉ tōḻi vāḻāḷ; tōḷiṭi muyakkam nīyum veyyi; cāṉṟōr paḻiyoṭu varūum iṉpam veḵkār; variyiṉ evaṉ? maṉṟal ayara ap peṇkōḷoḻukkatti nōkki yām notumal viruntiṉam pōla ivaḷ putu nāṇ oṭukkamum kāṇkuvam.
(vi-ri) `kūraleṇkiṉ' eṉṟa pāṭattiṟku, vaḷinta uṭampiṉiyuṭiya karaṭiyiṉ eṉpatē poruḷākum. aṇi - aṇimiyuṭiya. piṇā eṉpatu piṇa eṉṟāyatu, `kuṟiyata ṉiṟutic ciṉikeṭa vukaram - aṟiya varutal ceyyuḷu ḷurittē'1 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, aṟiya eṉṉum ilēcāṟ koḷḷappaṭum. kaḻiyāk kātalar eṉpatu pāṭamāyiṉ, nīṅkāta kātaliyuṭiyār eṉka. maṉṟal nikaḻttum tamarāvār, irumutu kuravarum cāṉṟōru mutalāyavar.
(u-ṟi) karaṭiyiṉ kūṭṭam citali ceyta puṟṟiṉ kōṭṭiṉi yuṭittu, atiluḷḷa puṟṟām paḻañ cōṟṟi yuṇṭum, mēlum ataṉi virumpic cūḻntāṟ pōla, nī emar kāvalic citittut taliviyi nukarntum mīṭṭum ak kaḷa voḻukkattiyē virumpip pakaliṉum iraviṉum cūḻavārāniṉṟāy eṉpatu.
(mē-ḷ) `nāṟṟamum tōṟṟamum'2 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, `āṟṟatu tīmi yaṟivuṟu kalakkamum' eṉpataṟku ippāṭṭu utāraṇa mākum eṉṟum, `poḻutu māṟuṅ kāppum'3 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu `naṉmiyun tīmiyum piṟitiṉik kūṟalum' eṉṟa pakutikku, `kaḻi peruṅ kātala rāyiṉuñ cāṉṟōr, paḻiyoṭu varūu miṉpam veḵkār' eṉap piṟarmēl vittut talivaṉi aṟivu koḷuttiṉamiyiṉ vaḻuvāy amintatu eṉṟum, `paḻiyoṭu varūu miṉpam veḵkār' eṉavē, pukaḻoṭu varūum iṉpam veḵkuva reṉak koḷḷa vittaliṉ, naṉmiyun tīmiyum piṟitiṉmēl vittuk kūṟiṟṟām eṉṟum kūṟiṉar, nac.
`kalvi taṟukaṇ'4 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, `kaḻiyāk kātala rāyiṉuñ cāṉṟōr, paḻiyoṭu varūu miṉpam veḵkār' eṉpatu pukaḻ paṟṟip piṟanta perumitam' eṉpar, pērā.
113. pāli
(talimakaṉ piriviṉkaṇ talimakaḷ tōḻikkuc colliyatu.)
		naṉṟal kāliyum naṭpiṟ kōṭār
		ceṉṟu vaḻippaṭūun tiripil cūḻcciyil
		puṉṟali maṭappiṭi akavunar perumakaṉ
		5amarvīcu vaṇmakiḻ aḵtip pōṟṟik
	5.	kāppukki niṟutta palvēṟ kōcar
		iḷaṅkaḷ kamaḻum neytalam ceruviṉ
		vaḷaṅkeḻu naṉṉā ṭaṉṉaveṉ tōḷmaṇan
		taḻuṅkal mūtūr alareṭut taraṟṟa
		nalkātu tuṟanta kātalar eṉṟum
	10.	kalporūu meliyāp 1pāṭiṉōṉ aṭiyaṉ
		2alku vaṉcurip peyta valciyaṉ
		ikantaṉa vāyiṉum iṭam pārttup pakivar
		ōmpiṉar uṟiyum kūḻkeḻu kuṟumpil
		kuviyimil viṭiya vēṟṟā oyyum
	15.	kaṉiyiruñ curuṇik kaṉikāḻ neṭuvēl
		viḻavayarnta taṉṉa koḻumpal tiṟṟi
		eḻāap pāṇaṉ naṉṉāṭ ṭumpar
		neṟicel vampalark koṉṟa tevvar
		eṟipaṭi kaḻīiya cēyaric ciṉṉīr
	20.	aṟutuṟi yayirmaṇal paṭukarip pōkic
		cēyar eṉṟalil ciṟumi yuṟṟaveṉ
		kiyaṟu neñcat tevvam nīṅka
		aḻāam uṟitalum uriyam parāri
		alaṅkal añciṉik kuṭampi pulleṉap
	25.	pulampeyar maruṅkil puḷḷeḻun tāṅku
		meyyiva ṇoḻiyap pōkiavar
		ceyviṉi maruṅkil celīiyareṉ uyirē.
- kallāṭaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 1-5. naṉṟu al kāliyum naṭpil kōṭār - (naṭṭār ākkamiṉṟik) kēṭuṟṟa kāliyum naṭput taṉmiyiṟ ṟiriyārāy, ceṉṟu vaḻippaṭūum tiripu il cūḻcciyil - an naṭṭārpāṟ ceṉṟu avar kuṟippiṟ paṭum māṟupāṭillāta aṟivuṭimiyāl, akavunar perumakaṉ - kūttarip purakkum talivaṉāy, puṉtali maṭappiṭi amar vīcu vaṇmakiḻ - (avarkaṭkup) pulliya taliyiṉiyuṭiya iḷiya piṭiyiṉi amariṉkaṇ aḷikkum vaṇmiyālākiya makiḻvuṭiya, aḵti pōṟṟi  - aḵti eṉpāṉip pātukāttu, kāppuk ki niṟutta - avaṉik kāval mikka iṭattē nili niṟuttiya, palvēl kōcar - pala vēṟpaṭiyiṉi yuṭiya kōcareṉpavaratu;
6-7. iḷaṅ kaḷ kamaḻum neytalañ ceṟuviṉ - putiya kaḷ kamaḻum neytalañ ceṟu eṉum, vaḷam keḻu nal nāṭu aṉṉa - vaḷam poruntiya nalla nāṭṭi yotta;
7-9. eṉ tōḷ maṇantu  - eṉ tōḷiṉik kūṭi, aḻuṅkal mūtūr alar eṭuttu araṟṟa - āravāramuṭiya paḻiya ūr alariṉi mikuttuk kūṟa, nalkātu tuṟanta kātalar - aruḷātu viṭṭēkiya nam kātalar;
9-11. eṉṟum kal porūu meliyāp pāṭuiṉ nōṉ aṭiyaṉ - eññāṉṟum kallip porutu melivuṟāta ōci iṉiya valiya aṭiyiṉi yuṭiyavaṉum, alku vaṉ curi peyta valciyaṉ - mikka valiya mūṅkiṟ kuḻāyiṟ peyta uṇaviṉi yuṭiyavaṉum;
12-17. ikantaṉavāyiṉum - taṉ nāṭṭelliyik kaṭantu cēymikkaṇṇa vāyiṉum, iṭam yārttu - kavarum cevvi pārttu, pakivar ōmpiṉar uṟiyum kūḻkeḻu kuṟumpil - pakivar āviṉip pātukāttu uṟiyum uṇavu mikka araṇkaḷiṟ ceṉṟu, kuvi imil viṭiya vēṟṟu ā oyyum - tiraṇṭa imiliyuṭiya viṭikaḷuṭaṉ kūṭiya pakippulattu ākkaḷik kavarntu celuttum, kaṉi irum curuṇi kaṉi kāḻ neṭuvēl - ceṟinta kariya pūṇiyum ney kaṉinta taṇṭiyumuṭiya nīṇṭa vēliṉiyum, viḻavu ayarntaṉṉa koḻum pal tiṟṟi - viḻāc ceytālotta koḻumi yākiya pala uṇavukaḷiyumuṭiya, eḻāap pāṇaṉ naṉṉāṭṭu umpar - pakivarkkup puṟakkiṭāta pāṇaṉ eṉpāṉatu nalla nāṭṭiṟku appāṟpaṭṭa;
18-22. neṟicel vampalark koṉṟa tevvar - vaḻiyiṟ pōkum putiyarkaḷik koṉṟa āṟalippōr, eṟi paṭi kaḻīiya cēy aric cil nīr - tāṅkaḷ eṟinta paṭikkalattik kaḻuviya civanta niṟamuṭiya arittōṭum ciṉṉīriyuṭiya, aṟu tuṟi ayir maṇal paṭukari pōki - makkaḷ iyakkam aṟṟa tuṟiyākiya nuṇmaṇal poruntiya kariyiṉit tāṇṭi, cēyar eṉṟaliṉ - cēymikkaṇ uḷḷār eṉṟu palarum kūṟaliṉ, ciṟumiyuṟṟa eṉ kiyaṟu neñcattu evvam nīṅka - nōyuṟṟa eṉ ceyalaṟṟa neñcattut tuṉpam oḻiya;
23-27. paru ari alaṅkal am ciṉik kuṭampi pul eṉa- parutta ariyiṟ kiḷitta aciyum aḻakiya kiḷiyiluḷḷa taṉ kūṭupolivaṟṟoḻiya, pulam peyar maruṅkil puḷ eḻuntāṅku - tāṉ peyarntu pōka eṇṇiya pulattiṭattup paṟavi puṟappaṭṭuc ceṉṟāṟ pōla, mey ivaṇ oḻiya - eṉatu mey iṅkut taṉit toḻiya, eṉ uyir pōki-eṉ uyir pirintu puṟappaṭṭu, avar ceyviṉi maruṅkil celīiyar - avar viṉi ceyyumiṭattiṟ celvatāka, aḻāam uṟitalum uriyam - ataṉāṉē nām aḻātēmāy uṟitaṟku uriyam āvēm.
(muṭipu) tōḻi! kōcar naṉṉāṭaṉṉa eṉ tōḷ maṇantu tuṟanta kātalar pāṇaṉ naṉṉāṭṭumpar, paṭukari pōkic cēya reṉṟaliṉ evvam nīṅka eṉ uyir pōki avar viṉicey maruṅkiṟ celīiyar; aḻāam uṟitalum uriyam.
aṭiyaṉum valciyaṉumākiya vēṟṟā oyyum pāṇaṉ eṉka.
(vi-ri) kūttar mutalāyiṉārkku yāṉi mutaliyavaṟṟi amarkkaḷattil aḷittali, `māṟṟār uṟumuraṇ citittaṉiṉ nōṉṟāḷ vāḻttik, kāṇku vanticiṟ kaḻaṟoṭi yaṇṇal........ maḻiyiṉum perumpayam poḻiti yataṉāṟ, paciyuṭi yokkali yorīiya, icimēn tōṉṟaṉiṉ pācaṟi yāṉē'1 eṉpataṉāl aṟika. māvīcu eṉpatu pāṭamāyiṉ, piṭiyoṭu kaḷiṟṟiṉi vaḻaṅkum eṉka. neytalañ ceṟuvākiya naṉṉā ṭeṉka. iṉcāriyi al vaḻikkaṇ vantatu. pāṭu iṉ nōṉ aṭiyaṉ  ōci iṉiya valiya aṭiyi yuṭiyavaṉ; ceruppaṇintu paralil naṭattaliṉ ōci iṉiya eṉṟār. paraṭṭiṉō ṉaṭi eṉṟa pāṭattiṟkup paraṭṭiṉiyuṭiya valiya aṭi yeṉka. viṭiya vēṟṟā - vēṟṟuppulattu viṭi
kaḷōṭu kūṭiya ākkaḷ. aṭiyaṉ valciyaṉ ākiya pāṇaṉ eṉavum, vēṟṟā oyyum pāṇaṉ eṉavum, vēliyum tiṟṟiyiyum uṭiya pāṇaṉ eṉavum, taṉittaṉi kūṭṭuka. `valimiku moympiṟ pāṇaṉ' eṉavum `vaṭāatu valvēṟ pāṇaṉ naṉṉāṭu' eṉavum innūluṭ piṟāṇṭum varutaliṟ pāṇaṉeṉpāṉ tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ vaṭakkaṇ irunta oru kāṭṭut talivaṉ āvaṉ eṉka. kātalar cēya reṉṟaliṉ eṉak kūṭṭuka. uyir uṭampi viṭṭup pōtaṟkup puḷ kuṭampiyi viṭṭuccellutal uvamamātali, `cēkki maraṉoḻiyac cēṇīṅku puṭpōla, yākki tamark koḻiya nīttu'2 eṉpataṉā laṟika. evvam nīṅka, aḻām uṟitalum uriyam eṉṟatu, uyir ceṉṟa kālattu niliyik karutiyatu.
114. mulli
(viṉimuṟṟi mīḷun talimakaṉ tērppākaṟkuc colliyatu.)
		kēḷāy ella tōḻi vēlaṉ 
		veṟiyayar kaḷattuc ciṟupala tāaya
		viravuvī uṟitta īrnaṟum puṟaviṉ
		uravukkatir maḻuṅkiya kalcēr ñāyiṟu
	5.	aravunuṅku matiyiṉ aiyeṉa maṟiyum
		ciṟupuṉ māliyum uḷḷār avareṉa
		nampulan tuṟiyum evvam nīṅka
		nūlaṟi valava, kaṭavumati uvakkāṇ
		neṭuṅkoṭi nuṭaṅkum vāṉtōy purici
	10.	yāmaṅ koḷpavar nāṭṭiya naḷicuṭar
		vāṉaka mīṉiṉ viḷaṅkit tōṉṟum
		aruṅkaṭik kāppiṉ aruñvaru mūtūrt
		tirunakar aṭaṅkiya mācil kaṟpiṉ
		arimatar maḻikkaṇ amipuri paṇittōḷ
	15.	aṇaṅkucāl ariviyik kāṇkuvam
		polampaṭik kalimāp pūṇṭa tērē.
-..........................
(co-ḷ) 8. nūl aṟi valava - parinūl valla pākaṉē!,
1-7. ella tōḻi kēḷāy - ellā ēṭi tōḻiyē yāṉ kūṟuvataṉik kēṭpāyāka; vēlaṉ veṟi ayar kaḷattu tāya - vēlaṉ veṟiyāṭum kaḷattil paranta, ciṟu pala viravu vī - ciṟiya palavāya kalanta pūkkaḷpōla, uṟitta īr naṟum puṟaviṉ - pūkkaḷ utirnta kuḷirnta naṟiya mulli nilattē, uravu katir maḻuṅkiya kalcēr ñāyiṟu - valiya katiriṉ vemmi kuṟinta mēṟku maliyic cērnta ñāyiṟu, aravu nuṅku matiyiṉ aiyeṉa maṟiyum - pāmpu viḻuṅkum tiṅkaḷeṉa mella maṟikiṉṟa, ciṟu pul māliyum uḷḷār avar  eṉa - ciṟiya pulliya mālikkālattum nam talivar nammi niṉintilarē eṉṟivvāṟu nam talivi taṉ tōḻiyi nōkkikkūṟi, nam pulantu uṟiyum evvam nīṅka - nammi veṟuttu uṟiyum tuṉpam nīṅkumāṟu;
8-15. neṭuṅkoṭi nuṭaṅkum vāṉtōy purici - neṭiya koṭi aciyum vāṉit tōyum matiliṉkaṇ, yāmam koḷpavar nāṭṭiya naḷicuṭar - irāppoḻutik kāttiruppōr ēṟṟiya neruṅkiya viḷakkukkaḷ, vāṉaka mīṉiṉ viḷaṅkit tōṉṟum - vāṉiṭattēyuḷḷa mīṉpōla viḷakkamuṟṟuk kāṇappeṟum, arukaṭi kāppiṉ - aṇukutaṟkuriya mikka kāvaliṉiyuṭiya, añcu varum mūtūr - pakivarkku accattiṉit tarum mutiya ūriluḷḷa, tirunakar aṭaṅkiya - celvamuḷḷa maṉiyil tayaṅkiya, mācu il kaṟpiṉ - kuṟṟamaṟṟa kaṟpiṉiyum, ari matar maḻikkaṇ - cevvari paṭarnta matartta kuḷirnta kaṇkaḷiyum, ami puri paṇittōḷ - mūṅkili otta parutta tōḷiṉiyum uṭiya, aṇaṅkucāl ariviyik kāṇkuvam - teyvampōṉṟa ciṟanta nam taliviyik kāṇpōm;
16. polampaṭik kali mā pūṇṭa tēr - poṉṉālāya cēṇattiṉi yaṇinta cerukkiṉiyuṭiya kutirikaḷ pūṭṭap peṟṟa tēriṉi;
8. uvakkāṇ kaṭavumati - uṅkē celuttuvāyāka.
(muṭipu) valava! nam talivi tōḻiyi nōkki, `ciṟupuṉ māliyum uḷḷār avar' eṉak kūṟi, pulantu uṟiyum evvam nīṅka, mūtūril tirunakar aṭaṅkiya ariviyik kāṇkuvam! uvakkāṇ tēr kaṭavumati.
puriciyil nāṭṭiya naḷicuṭar vāṉaka mīṉiṉ viḷaṅkit tōṉṟum mūtūr eṉka.
(vi-ri) ella - ellā; keḻutakip potuccol: kal - atta mali. katir vemmi maḻuṅkiṉamiyāl matipōl eṉṟār. ciṟitu ciṟitāka maṟital kuṟittu aravu nuṅku mati eṉṟār. aiyeṉa - viyakkum paricu eṉṟumām. puṉ māli - varuttattic ceyyum māliyumām. nam pulantu eṉpatu vikāramāyiṟṟu. nūl - kutiriyuḷa maṟiyum nūl.  uvakkāṇ - uṅkē; mūtūrkkaṇ eṉṟapaṭi. kaṭikkāppu : oruporuṭ paṉmoḻi. aṭaṅkiya kaṟpiṉ eṉakkoṇṭu, āṟiya kaṟpiṉiyuṭiya eṉṟurittalumām. aṇaṅku cāl - aruntati pōlum kaṟpuṭiya eṉṟalumām.
115. pāli
(piriviṭi vaṟpuṟukkun tōḻikkut talimakaḷ colliyatu.)
		aḻiyā viḻaviṉ añcuvaru mūtūrp
		paḻiyilar āyiṉum palarpuṟaṅ kūṟum
		ampal oḻukkamum ākiya veñcoṟ
		cēriyam  peṇṭir eḷḷiṉum eḷḷuka
	5.	nuṇpūṇ erumi kuṭanāṭ ṭaṉṉeṉ
		āynalam toliyiṉum tolika eṉṟum
		nōyila rākanam kātalar vāyvāḷ
		evvi vīḻnta ceruviṟ pāṇar
		kitoḻu marapiṉmuṉ parittiṭūup paḻicciya
	10.	vaḷḷuyir vaṇarmarup paṉṉa oḷḷiṇarc
		cuṭarppūṅ koṉṟi ūḻuṟu viḷineṟṟu
		aṟimicit tāam attam nīḷiṭip
		piṟimaruḷ vāṉkōṭ ṭaṇṇal yāṉic
		ciṉamiku muṉpiṉ 1vāmāṉ añci
	15.	iṉaṅkoṇ ṭoḷikkum añcuvaru kavali
		naṉṉar āykaviṉ toliyac cēynāṭṭu
		nannīt tuṟiyum poruṭpiṇik
		kūṭā miyiṉ nīṭi yōrē.
- māmūlaṉār.
(co-ḷ) (tōḻi!)
1-4. aḻiyā viḻaviṉ añcuvarum mūtūr - eṉṟum nīṅkāta viḻāviṉiyuṭiya pakivarkku accam tarum im mutiya ūriṉ kaṇṇē, palar - palariyum, paḻiyilarāyiṉum - avar paḻi yoṉṟum ilarāyiṉum, puṟaṅkūṟum ampal oḻukkamum - puṟañcoṟ kūṟutalākiya ampal oḻukkattiṉiyum, ākiya vem col - vevviya colliyum  uṭiyārākiya, cēri peṇṭir eḷḷiṉum eḷḷuka - cērip peṇṭir nammi ikaḻiṉum ikaḻuka;
5-6. nuṇpūṇ erumi kuṭa nāṭu aṉṉa - nuṇṇiya toḻiṟ pāṭaminta pūṇiṉiyuṭiya erumi yeṉpāṉatu kuṭa nāṭṭiṉiyotta, eṉ āy nalam toliyiṉum tolika - eṉatu aḻakiya nalam toliyiṉum tolivatāka;
7-12. vāy vāḷ evvi vīḻnta ceruvil - tappāta vāṭpaṭi yiṉiyuṭiya evvi eṉpāṉ vīḻnta pōrkkaḷattē, pāṇar pāṇarkaḷ, kitoḻu marapiṉ - kiyāṟ ṟoḻum muṟimiyoṭu, muṉ paḻicciya - muṉpellām parāviya (yāḻiṉ), vaḷ uyir vaṇar maruppu - vaḷam poruntiya oliyiyuṭiya vaḷinta kōṭṭiṉi, parittu iṭūu aṉṉa - oṭittup pōkaṭṭāl otta, oḷ iṇar cuṭar pū koṉṟi - oḷi poruntiya kottukkaḷiyuṭiya cuṭarum pūkkaḷiyuṭiya koṉṟiyiṉatu, ūḻ uṟu viḷi neṟṟu - muṟiyāka muṟṟi viḷinta neṟṟukkaḷ, aṟimicit tāam attam nīḷ iṭi - pāṟi mītu parantu kiṭakkum curattiṉ nīṇṭa neṟiyiluḷḷa,
13-15. piṟi maruḷ vāṉ kōṭṭu aṇṇal yāṉi - piṟiyiṉi yotta veḷḷiya kōṭṭiṉiyuṭiya perumi taṅkiya yāṉikaḷiyum, ciṉam miku muṉpiṉ vām māṉ añci - ciṉam mikka valimiyiyum tāvum kutirikaḷiyumuṭiya añci eṉpāṉ, iṉam koṇṭu oḷikkum añcu varu kavali - pakivaratu āṉiṉaṅkaḷik kippaṟṟi maṟittiṭum accantōṉṟum kavartta neṟikaḷilē;
16-18. naṉṉar āykaviṉ toliya - naṉṟākiya namatu aḻaku toliya, nam nīttuc cēy nāṭṭu uṟiyum poruṭpiṇi - nammip pirintu neṭun tūrattiluḷḷa nāṭṭil vatitaṟkētuvāya poruḷīṭṭum ceyal, kūṭāmiyiṉ nīṭiyōr - uriya kālattiṉuḷ muṟṟāmiyiṉ kālam tāḻntiruppōr ākiya;
6-7. nam kātalar eṉṟum nōyilar āka - nam kātalar eṉṟum nōyiṉṟi iṉitiruppārāka.
(muṭipu) mūtūril veñcoṟ peṇṭir eḷḷiṉum eḷḷuka; eṉ āy nalam toliyiṉum tolika; evvi vīḻnta ceruvil pāṇar muṟittu iṭūup paḻicciya vaṇar maruppu aṉṉa koṉṟi neṟṟu tāam attam nīḷiṭi, añci iṉam koṇṭoḷikkum kavaliyil, kaviṉ toliya nīttuc cēynāṭṭuṟiyum poruṭpiṇi kūṭāmiyiṉ nīṭiyōr ākiya nam kātalar eṉṟum nōyilarāka.
(vi-ri) palar - palarum; `palar pukaḻ ñāyiṟu' eṉpuḻip pōla muṟṟummi tokkatu. cēri am peṇṭir: am, cāriyi. pāṇar yāḻilē teyvam uṟital paṟṟiyum, tamakku uṇavaḷikkum utaviyuṭimi paṟṟiyum yāḻiṉit toḻutu paravuvār eṉavum, evvi iṟantapiṉ iṉit tam pāṭṭiṉik kēṭṭup paricil aḷippār ilareṉṉum varutta mikutiyāl yāḻiṉi muṟittup pōkaṭṭār eṉavum koḷka. maruppu aṉṉavāka neṟṟu aṟimicit tāam eṉka. itaṉāl pāṇar muṟittup pōkaṭṭa maruppum palaveṉpatu peṟṟām. vayamāṉ añci eṉpatu pāṭamāyiṉ, ciṅkam pōlum valiyuṭiya añci eṉṟurikka. yāṉi ciṉa miku muṉpiṉ vayamāṉ añci iṉaṅkoṇ ṭoḷikkum eṉpatu pāṭamāyiṉ, yāṉiyāṉatu ciṅkattik kaṇṭañci taṉ iṉattik koṇṭoḷikkum kavali eṉka.
(mē-ḷ) `maṅkala moḻiyum viiya moḻiyum'1 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, `nōyila rākanaṅ kātalar' eṉak kūṟuvatu, nammi aṟaṉaṉṟit tuṟattaliṉ tīṅku varumeṉṟu añci vāḻttiyatu; namporuṭṭut tīṅku varumeṉa niṉittaliṉ vaḻuvāy amintatu eṉṟaṉar, nac.
116. marutam
(tōḻi talimakaṉi vāyil maṟuttatu.)
		eriyakin taṉṉa tāmari yiṭiyiṭi
		arintukāl kuvitta cennel viṉiñar
		kaṭkoṇṭu maṟukuñ cākā ṭaḷaṟṟuṟiṉ
		āykarum paṭukkum pāypuṉal ūra
	5.	periya nāṇiṉi maṉṟa poriyeṉap
		puṉkaviḻ akaṉṟuṟip poliya oṇṇutal
		naṟumalark kāṇvaruṅ kuṟumpal kūntal
		māḻi nōkkiṟ kāḻiyal vaṉamuli
		eḵkuṭi eḻilnalat toruttiyoṭu *1neruni
	10.	vikupuṉal ayarntaṉi yeṉpa atuvē
		poypuṟam 2potintiyām karappavum kiyikantu
		alarā kiṉṟāl tāṉē malartār
		miyaṇi yāṉi maṟappōrc ceḻiyaṉ
		poyyā viḻaviṉ kūṭaṟ paṟantali
	15.	uṭaṉiyin teḻunta iruperu vēntar
		kaṭalmaruḷ perumpaṭi kalaṅkat tākki
		iraṅkici muracam oḻiyap parantavar.
		ōṭupuṟam kaṇṭa ñāṉṟi
		āṭukoḷ viyaṉkaḷat tārppiṉum peritē.
-paraṇar.
(co-ḷ) 1-5. eri akinta aṉṉa tāmari iṭiyiṭi - neruppuk kappuviṭ ṭerivatu pōṉṟa tāmarip pūkkaḷiṉ iṭiyiṭiyē, cennel kāl arintu kuvitta viṉiñar - cenneṟṟāḷi arintu ariariyākap pōkaṭṭa nellarivōr, kaṭ koṇṭu maṟukum cākāṭu - taṅkaṭkuk kaḷḷik koṇṭu palakālum tiriyum vaṇṭi, aḷaṟṟu uṟiṉ - cēṟṟiṟ patintiṭiṉ, āykarumpu aṭukkum pāypuṉal ūra - ataṉip pōkkac ciṟanta karumpukaḷi aṭukki iṭimaṭukkum pāyum puṉal vaḷa mikka ūraṉē, periya nāṇili maṉṟa - uṟutiyāka nī peritum nāṇillātavaṉāvāy;
5-10. pori eṉap puṉku aviḻ akal tuṟi poliya -poripōlap puṉkampū malarum akaṉṟa nīrttuṟi polivuṟa, oḷnutal - oḷi poruntiya neṟṟiyiṉiyum, naṟumalar kāṇvarum kuṟum pal kūntal - naṟiya malarkaḷ vēynta kāṇṭaṟkiṉiya kuṟiya palavāya kūntaliṉiyum, māḻi nōkkiṉ - māvaṭup pōṉṟa kaṇṇiṉiyum, kāḻ iyal vaṉa muli - muttuvaṭam aciyum aḻakiya muliyiṉiyum, eḵku uṭi eḻil nalattu oruttiyoṭu - nuṇṇiya aḻakiṉ nalattiṉiyumuṭiya oru parattiyōṭu, neruni vikupuṉal ayarntaṉi eṉpa - nēṟṟu iṭiyaṟātu oḻukum puṉalil viḷiyāṭ ṭayarntaṉi eṉap palaruṅ kūṟuvar;
10-11. atuvē - atutāṉ, poypuṟam potintu yām karappavum - ataṉip poy yeṉṟu puṟattē mūṭi yām maṟikkavum, kiyi kantu - emceyaliṉik kaṭantu;
12-19. malar tār - malarnta pūmāliyiṉiyum, mi aṇi yāṉi - taliyil mi aṇinta yāṉiyiṉiyum uṭiya, maṟappōr - maṟam poruntiya pōril valla, ceḻiyaṉ - pāṇṭiyaṉatu, poyyā viḻaviṉ kūṭal paṟantali - eṉṟum nīṅkāta viḻaviṉiyuṭiya kūṭaṟ pōrkkaḷattil, uṭaṉ iyintu eḻunta iru peru vēntar - tammuḷ oruṅku iyintu eḻunta cōḻa cēra aracarkaḷatu, kaṭalmaruḷ perum paṭi - kaṭal pōṉṟa peruntāṉikaḷi, kalaṅkat tākki - kalaṅkum paricu tākki, iraṅku ici muracam oḻiyap parantu avar ōṭupuṟam kaṇṭa ñāṉṟi - olikkum oliyiṉiyuṭiya muracu oḻintu kiṭakka avar parantu ōṭum puṟakkoṭiyik kaṇṭa nāḷil, āṭu koḷviyaṉ kaḷattu ārppiṉum peritu - veṟṟi koṇṭa periya kaḷattiṉkaṇ eḻunta āravārattiṉum peritāka;
12. alar ākiṉṟu - alarākiṉṟatu.
(muṭipu) pāy puṉalūra! periya nāṇili maṉṟa; eḻil nalat toruttiyoṭu neruni vikupuṉal ayarntaṉi eṉpa; atu yām karappavum kiyikantu, ceḻiyaṉ kūṭaṟ paṟantali, iruperu vēntar perumpaṭi kalaṅkat tākki avar puṟaṅkaṇṭa ñāṉṟi, kaḷattu eḻunta ārppiṉum peritu alar ākiṉṟu.
(vi-ri) āy karumpu - melliya karumpumām. karumpu aṭukkum eṉṟatu maruta nilattiṉ vaḷamikuti kūṟiyapaṭi. māḻi - iḷami eṉṟum, aḻa keṉṟumām. eḻil nalam - eḻucciyum nalamumām. yāṉikku neṟṟiyil añcaṉam aṇital marapātaliṉ, miyaṇi yāṉiyeṉṟār.
(u-ṟi) nellariyum viṉiñar tam toḻili viṭuttu iḻintakaḷ vaṇṭiyiṉ āḻcciyip pōkkaṟkuc ciṟanta karumpiṉic citippatu pōla, nī illaṟañ ceytalāya niṉ oḻukkattiṉik kiviṭṭu, iḻinta parattiyiṉ iṉpatti nukartaṟkuc ciṟanta taliviyi varuttukiṉṟāy eṉpatu.
(mē - ḷ) `kiḻavōṉ viḷiyāṭ ṭāṅku maṟṟē'1 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, `eḵkuṭi yeḻiṉalat toruttiyoṭu.... alarā kiṉṟāṟ ṟāṉē' eṉpatu, talivi piṟar alar kūṟiyavaḻik kāmañ ciṟantu pulantavāṟu eṉṟum, `vāyiṟ kiḷavi veḷippaṭak kiḷattal' 1  eṉṉum cūttirattu, `eriyakin taṉṉa tāmari yiṭi yiṭi....... yāppiṉum peritē' eṉpataṉuḷ, `nāṇili maṉṟa' eṉat tōḻi kūṟi alarā kiṉṟāl eṉa veḷippaṭak kiḷattaliṉ vaḻuvāy amintatu eṉṟum kūṟiṉar, nac.
117. pāli
(makaṭ pōkkiya cevilittāy colliyatu.)
		muvaloṭu malarnta mākkural nocciyum
		avvari alkul āyamum uḷḷāḷ
		ētilaṉ poymmoḻi nampi ērvi(ṉi)
		vaḷaṅkeḻu tirunakar pulampap pōki
	5.	veruvaru kavali āṅkaṇ aruḷvarak
		karuṅkāl ōmi yēṟi veṇṭalip
		paruntupeṭi payirum pāḻnāṭ ṭāṅkaṇ
		polantoṭi teḷirppa vīcic cēvaṭic
		cilampunaka iyalic ceṉṟeṉ makaṭkē.
	10.	cāntuḷar vaṇarkural vāri vakivakuttu
		yāṉpōtu tuṇippat takaram maṇṇāḷ
		taṉṉō raṉṉa takiveṅ kātalaṉ
		veṟikamaḻ paṉmalar puṉiyap piṉṉuviṭac
		ciṟupuṟam putiya neṟiputāḻn taṉakol
	15.	neṭuṅkāl māat tūḻuṟu veṇpaḻam
		koṭuntāḷ yāmi pārppoṭu kavarum
		poyki cūḻnta poyyā 2 yāṇar
		vāṇaṉ ciṟukuṭi vaṭāatu
		tīnīrk kāṉyāṟṟu aviraṟal pōṉṟē.
- ..............................
(co-ḷ) 1-2. muvaloṭu malarnta mākkural nocciyum- mullip pūvōṭu malarnta kariya kottiṉiyuṭiya nocciyiyum, avvari alkul āyamum uḷḷāḷ - aḻakiya vari poruntiya alkuliṉiyuṭiya āyattiṉiyum niṉiyāḷāyum;
10-11. cāntu uḷar vaṇar kural vāri - mayirc cāntu pūciya vaḷinta kottākiya kūntali vāri, vaki vakuttu - vakippaṭuttu, yāṉ pōtu tuṇippa - yāṉ malarkaḷi iṇikkappuka, takaramum maṇṇāḷ - ataṟku uṭaṉpaṭṭut takaramum pūcikkoḷḷāḷāyum;
3-4. ētilaṉ poy moḻi nampi - piṟaṉoruvaṉ poym moḻiyiṉi virumpi, ēr viṉi vaḷamkeḻu tiru nakar pulampap pōki - aḻakiya toḻiṉalam vāynta vaḷam poruntiya celva mikka māḷiki taṉit toḻiyap puṟappaṭṭu;
5-9. veru varu kavali āṅkaṇ - añcattakka kavartta neṟikaḷāka avviṭaṅkaḷil, veṇtalip paruntu - veḷḷiya taliyiṉiyuṭiya paruntu, karuṅkāl ōmi ēṟi - kariya aṭiyiṉiyuṭiya ōmi marattiṉ mītēṟi, aruḷ vara - irakkam uṇṭāka, peṭi payirum pāḻ nāṭṭu āṅkaṇ - taṉatu peṭiyiṉi aḻikkum pāḻāṉa nāṭṭiṭattē, polam toṭi teḷirppa vīci - taṉatu poṟṟoṭi yolikkak kiyiṉi vīciyum, cēvaṭic cilampu naka iyali - civanta aṭiyiṟ cilampukaḷ viḷaṅka ulāviyum, ceṉṟa eṉ makaṭku - pōy viṭṭa eṉ makaḷukku;
12-13. taṉ ōraṉṉa taki vem kātalaṉ - taṉṉiyē otta takimiyiyum viruppattiyumuṭiya kātalaṉākiya talivaṉ, veṟi kamaḻ paṉmalar puṉiyap piṉṉuviṭa - maṇam kamaḻum palavaki malarkaḷi vit taṇiceyaṟkup piṉṉutalic ceyya (avaḷ kūntal),
15-19. neṭuṅ kāl māattu ūḻ uṟu veṇ paḻam - neṭiya aṭiyiṉiyuṭiya māmarattiṉatu muṟiyāka muṟṟiya veḷḷiya paḻattiṉi, koṭu tāḷ yāmi pārppoṭu kavarum - vaḷinta kāliṉiyuṭiya yāmi taṉ pārppōṭu kavarntuṇṇum, poyki cūḻnta poyyā yāṇar- poykikaḷ cūḻntuḷḷa eṉṟum aṟāta putu varuvāykaḷiyuṭiya, vāṇaṉ ciṟukuṭi vaṭāatu - vāṇaṉatu ciṟukuṭi eṉṉum ūrkku vaṭakkiṉkaṇ ṇuḷḷa, tīm nīrk kāṉyāṟṟu avir aṟal pōṉṟu - iṉiya nīriṉiyuṭiya kāṭṭāṟṟiṉ viḷaṅkum aṟal pōṉṟu;
14. ciṟupuṟam putiya neṟipu tāḻntaṉa kol - piṭar maṟiya neṟintu toṅkukiṉṟaṉavō?
(muṭipu) nocciyiṉiyum āyattiṉiyum niṉiyāḷā yum, takaram maṇṇāḷāyum, ētilaṉ poymmoḻi nampi, nakar pulampappōki, pāḻ nāṭṭāṅkaṇ, vīciyum iyaliyuñ ceṉṟa eṉ makaṭku, kātalaṉ piṉṉuviṭa, avaḷ kūntal vāṇaṉ ciṟukuṭi vaṭāatu kāṉyāṟṟu aṟal pōṉṟu ciṟupuṟam putiya neṟipu tāḻntaṉakol?
(vi-ri) aruḷ vara peṭikku aruḷ; kaṇṭārkku aruḷ vara eṉalumām. kiyi vīci eṉka. taṉṉō raṉṉa - aṉpu, oḻukkam mutaliyavaṟṟāl taṉṉi yotta.
118. kuṟiñci
(ceṟippaṟivuṟīi iravum pakalum vāra leṉṟu varivu kaṭāayatu.)
		kaṟaṅkuveḷ 1aruvi piṟaṅkumalik kavāaṉ
		tēṅkamaḻ iṇara vēṅki cūṭit
		toṇṭakap paṟiccīr peṇṭiroṭu virii
		maṟukiṟ ṟūṅkum ciṟukuṭip pākkattu
	5.	2iyaṉmuru koppiṉi vayanāy piṟpaṭap
		pakalvariṟ kavvi añcutum ikalkoḷa
		irumpiṭi kaṉṟoṭu viriiya kayavāyp
		peruṅki 3 yāṉik kōḷpiḻit tirīiya
		aṭupuli vaḻaṅkum āriruḷ naṭunāḷ
	10.	taṉiyi varutal ataṉiṉum añcutum
		eṉṉā kuvaḷkol tāṉē paṉṉāḷ
		puṇarkuṟi ceyta pularkural ēṉal
		kiḷitaṭi pāṭalum oḻintaṉaḷ
		aḷiyaḷ tāṉiṉ aḷiyala tilaḷē.
- kapilar
(co-ḷ) 1-6. kaṟaṅku veḷ aruvi piṟaṅku malik kavāaṉ - olikkum veḷḷiya aruvikaḷ viḷaṅkum maliyiṉ cāraṟ kaṇṇē, tēm kamaḻ iṇara vēṅki cūṭi - tēṉ kamaḻum kottuk kaḷiyuṭiya vēṅkip pūkkaḷic cūṭi, toṇṭakap paṟic cīr - toṇṭakam eṉṉum paṟiyiṉ tāḷattiṟkiciya, peṇṭiroṭu virii - āṭavar peṇṭiroṭu kalantu, maṟukiltūṅkum ciṟukuṭip pākkattu - terukkaḷil āṭum ematu ciṟukuṭip pākkattiṉkaṇṇē, iyal muruku oppiṉi - iyaṅkum murukaṉi oppāyāki, vayanāy piṟpaṭap pakalvariṉ- valiya nāy piṉṉē varap pakaṟkaṇ nī variṉ, kavvi añcutum - ūrār kūṟum alarkku añcukiṟōm;
6-10. irumpiṭi kaṉṟōṭu viriiya - kariya piṭiyōṭum kaṉṟōṭuṅ kūṭiya, kayavāy peruṅki yāṉik kōḷ piḻittu- akaṉṟa vāyiṉiyum nīṇṭa kiyiṉiyumuṭiya yāṉiyik koḷḷutal piḻittu, ikalkoḷa irīiya - ataṉāl avaṟṟip pakimi porunta maṟintirukkumpaṭi ceyta, aṭupuli vaḻaṅkum āriruḷ naṭunāḷ - kollum puli tiriyum ariya iruḷ cūḻnta naḷḷiravil, taṉiyi varutal ataṉiṉum añcutum - nī taṉiyiyāki varutali ataṉik kāṭṭiṉum añcukiṉṟōm;
11-14. paṉṉāḷ puṇarkuṟi ceyta pularkural ēṉal - pala nāḷum puṇarum pakaṟkuṟiyākak koṇṭa muṟṟiya katiriyuṭiya tiṉip puṉattilē, kiḷi kaṭi pāṭalum oḻintaṉaḷ - kiḷiyi ōṭṭutaṟkup pāṭum pāṭalum nīṅkiṉaḷ, eṉ ākuvaḷ kol - iṉi eṉ ākuvaḷō, aḷiyaḷ niṉ aḷi alatu ilaḷē - iraṅkat takkāḷāya eṉ tōḻi niṉ aruḷi yaṉṟip piṟitoru paṟṟuk kōṭum ilaḷ.
(muṭipu) taliva, ciṟukuṭip pākkattu iyal muruku oppiṉi pakalvariṉ kavvi añcutum; aṭupuli vaḻaṅkum āriruḷ naṭunāḷ taṉiyi varutal ataṉiṉum añcutum, ivaḷ, ēṉal kiḷikaṭi pāṭalum oḻintaṉaḷ; eṉ ākuvaḷ kol? aḷiyaḷ niṉ aḷiyalatu ilaḷ.
(vi-ri) aruvip piṟaṅkumali eṉpatu pāṭamāyiṉ aruviyiyuṭiya viḷaṅkum mali yeṉka. ikal muruku eṉpatu pāṭamāyiṉ, pakivaroṭu māṟupāṭu koṇṭa murukaṉ eṉka. mikalkoḷa eṉap pirittu, tarukku amiya eṉṟurittalumām. irīiya - ōṭumpaṭi ceyta eṉṟumām. yāṉi kōṭ piḻittu eṉpatu pāṭamāyiṉ, yāṉiyāṉatu koḷḷutal piḻittu irīiya eṉka. vaḻaṅkum āriruḷ naṭunāḷ eṉpatu pāṭamākak koḷḷiṉ, vaḻaṅkum neṟiyiṉ kaṇ iruḷiyuṭiya naṭunāḷ eṉka.
pakalvariṟ kavvi yañcutum eṉṟataṉālē pakaṟkuṟi vilakkiyum, āriruḷ naṭunāḷ varutal añcutum eṉṟataṉāl iravukkuṟi vilakkiyum, ēṉalkiḷikaṭi pāṭalum oḻintaṉaḷ eṉṟataṉāl ceṟippu aṟivuṟuttiyum, niṉ aḷiyala tilaḷ eṉṟataṉāl varintu koḷḷal vēṇṭum eṉṟāḷām.
(mē-ḷ) `poḻutu māṟum'1 eṉṟa cūttirattup `pakal vāral' eṉṟataṟkup `pakalvariṟ kavvi yañcutum' eṉpataṉi eṭuttuk kāṭṭiṉar, nac.
119. pāli
(celavuṇarttiya tōḻikkut talimakaḷ coṟṟatu. tōḻi tali makaṭkuc coṟṟatūu mām.)
		nutalun tōḷun titali yalkulum
		vaṇṇamum vaṉappum variyum vāṭa
		varuntuvaḷ ivaḷeṉat tiruntupu nōkki
		varivunaṉ ṟeṉṉā takaliṉum avarvaṟitu
	5.	āṟucel mākkaḷ aṟutta piraṇṭi
		ēṟupeṟu pāmpiṉ pintuṇi kaṭuppa
		neṟiyayal tiraṅkum attam veṟikoḷa
		umaṇcāt tiṟanta oḻikal aṭuppiṉ
		nōṉcili maḻavar ūṉpuḻuk kayarum
	10.	curaṉvaḻak kaṟṟa teṉṉā turañciṟantu
		neytal uruviṉ aitilaṅ kakalilit
		toṭiyami pīlip polinta kaṭiki
		maṭiyami tiṇcuri mākkāḻ vēloṭu
		taṇiyamar aḻuvam tammoṭu tuṇippat
	15.	tuṇikuvar kollō tāmē 1tuṇikoḷa
		maṟappuli uḻanta vacipaṭu ceṉṉi
		uṟunōy varuttamo ṭuṇīiya maṇṭip
		paṭimuḻam ūṉṟiya neṭunal yāṉi
		kitōyt tuyirkkum vaṟuñcuṉi
	20.	mitōy cimiya malimuta lāṟē.
- kuṭavāyiṟ kīrattaṉār.
(co-ḷ) (tōḻi!)
1-4. avar - nam talivar, varivu naṉṟu eṉṉātu akaliṉum - varintu kōṭal takkatu eṉṉātu pirintiṭiṉum, nutalum tōḷum titali alkulum vaṇṇamum vaṉappum variyum vāṭa - neṟṟiyum tōḷum tēmaliyuṭiya alkulum niṟamum aḻakum variyum vāṭa, varuntuvaḷ ivaḷ eṉat tiruntupu nōkki - ivaḷ varuntuvāḷ eṉa naṉku ārāyntuṇarntu;
15-20. maṟappuli uḻanta tuṇikoḷa vacipaṭu ceṉṉi - maṟattiyuṭiya puliyuṭaṉ porutu uḻantamiyāl tuṇṭamākap piḷavupaṭṭa ceṉṉiyiṉkaṇ, uṟu nōy varuttamoṭu - uṟṟa nōyālāya varuttattuṭaṉ, uṇīiya maṇṭi - nīr uṇṭaṟku virintu ceṉṟu, paṭi muḻam ūṉṟiya neṭu nal yāṉi - maṇṇil muḻaṅkāli maṭittūṉṟiya neṭiya nalla yāṉiyāṉatu, ki tōyttu uyirkkum vaṟuñ cuṉi - taṉatu kiyāl tōyttum nīriṉmiyāl perumūc ceṟiyum vaṟiya cuṉiyiṉiyuṭiya, mi tōy cimiya mali mutal āṟu - mēkam paṭiyum ucciyiṉiyuṭiya maliyiṭattuc cellum neṟiyāya;
4-7. āṟu cel mākkaḷ aṟutta piraṇṭi - neṟiyiṟ cellum makkaḷ aṟuttup pōkaṭṭa piraṇṭik koṭi, ēṟu peṟu pāmpiṉ pin tuṇi kaṭuppa - iṭiyāṟ ṟākkutal peṟṟa pāmpiṉatu paciya tuṇṭupōla, neṟi ayal vaṟitu tiraṅkum attam - vaḻiyiṉ pakkattē payaṉiṉṟi vataṅkik kiṭakkum kāṭṭil;
7-10. umaṇ cāttu iṟanta oḻi kal aṭuppil - uppu vaṇikar kūṭṭam viṭṭoḻinta kal aṭuppil, nōṉ cili maḻavar - valiya villiyuṭiya maḻavar, veṟi koḷa ūṉ puḻukku ayarum curaṉ - nāṟṟamuṇṭāka ūṉip puḻukki yuṇṇum iṭaṅkaḷiyuṭiya curamāṉatu, vaḻakku aṟṟatu eṉṉātu - peṇṭiroṭu iyaṅkutaṟku uriyatallatu eṉṟu niṉiyātu;
10-15. uram ciṟantu - ūkkam mikku, neytal uruviṉ aitu ilaṅku akal ili - neytaṟpūp pōlum uruviṉiyuṭiya aḻakitāka viḷaṅkum akaṉṟa iliyiṉiyum, toṭi ami pīlip polinta kaṭiki - toṭuttal aminta mayiṟṟōkiyāl viḷakkamuṟṟa kāmpiṉiyum, maṭi ami tiṇ curi - mūṭṭuvāy aminta tiṇṇiya curiyiṉiyum, mā kāḻ vēloṭu - kariya taṇṭiṉiyu muṭiya vēlē tuṇiyāka, taṇi amar aḻuvam - pakiyit taṇivikkac cellum pōrkkaḷattiṟku, tammoṭu tuṇippa - nāmum tammoṭu tuṇiyākic cella, tuṇikuvar kollō - tuṇivarō?
(muṭipu) tōḻi! nam talivar varivu naṉṟeṉṉātu akaliṉum, varuntuvaḷ ivaḷ eṉat tiruntupu nōkki malimutal āṟu āya curaṉ vaḻakkaṟṟa teṉṉātu, urañ ciṟantu amar aḻuvam tammoṭu tuṇiyākak koṇṭu cellat tuṇikuvar kollō?
piraṇṭi tiraṅkum attattil aṭuppil maḻavar ūṉpuḻuk kayarum curaṉ eṉka.
(vi-ri) vaṟitu tiraṅkum eṉavum, veṟikoḷa ūṉ puḻukkayarum eṉavum kūṭṭuka. ēṟu - iṭi yēṟu eṉavum, eṟital eṉavum iraṭṭuṟa moḻitalākak koḷka. cāttu - vaṇikar tiraḷ. tuṉikoḷa eṉṉum pāṭattiṟku veṟuppuṇṭāka eṉṟurikka.
(u-ṟi) `āṟu cellum mākkaḷāl aṟuttup pōkaṭap paṭṭup piraṇṭi vaṟitē tiraṅkuvatupōlat talivarāṟ ṟuṟakkappaṭṭu em aḻaku payaṉpaṭaliṉṟiyē kaḻiyā niṉṟatu' `cāttarāṟ ṟuṟakkappaṭṭa kallaṭuppu maḻavarkku ūṉpuḻuk kayarntu makiḻtaṟkuk karuviyāṉāṟ pōlat talivarāṟ ṟuṟakkappaṭṭa yām ampaṟ peṇṭirkku alartūṟṟi makiḻaṟku ilakkāyiṉōm.'
`puliyoṭuḻantu ceṉṉiyiṭattu vaṭuppaṭṭu varuntiya yāṉi nīruṇṇac cuṉiyiṭattu muḻa mūṉṟik kiyit tōyttum nīr peṟā tuyirttāṟpōl, aṉṉiyiṉ koṭuñ collālum ampaṟ peṇṭiriṉ alarālum neñcu puṇṇuṟṟa yām, annōy tīra variyumāṟu talimakaṉ kaḻalkaḷip paṇintu vēṇṭiyum aruḷ peṟāmal uyirkkiṉṟōm' eṉṟāḷām. (irācakōpāli yaṅkār.)
120. neytal
(tōḻi pakaṟkuṟikkaṇ talimakaḷi iṭattuyttu vantu talimakaṉi 
etirppaṭṭu niṉṟu colliyatu.)
		neṭuvēḷ mārpiṉ āram pōlac
		cevvāy vāṉan tīṇṭimīṉ aruntum
		piṅkāṟ 1kokkiṉa niripaṟi yukappa
		elli pippayak kaḻippik kuṭavayiṉ
	5.	kalcērn taṉṟē palkatir ñāyiṟu
		matareḻil maḻikkaṇ kaluḻa ivaḷē
		perunāṇ aṇinta ciṟumeṉ cāyal
		māṇalam citiya ēṅki yāṉātu
		aḻaltoṭaṅ kiṉaḷē peruma ataṉāl
	10.	kaḻiccuṟā eṟinta puṭṭāḷ attiri
		neṭunīr iruṅkaḻip parimelin tacii
		valvil iḷiyaro ṭellic cellātu
		cērntaṉi celiṉē citikuva tuṇṭō
		peṇṇi ōṅkiya veṇmaṇaṟ paṭappi
	15.	aṉṟil akavum āṅkaṇ
		ciṟukural neytalem peruṅkaḻi nāṭṭē.
- nakkīraṉār.
(co-ḷ) 1-5. neṭuvēḷ mārpiṉ āram pōla - murukak kaṭavuḷ mārpiṉiṭattu muttārampōla, cevvāy vāṉam tīṇṭi - cevvāṉattiṭam porunti, mīṉ aruntum piṅkāl kokku iṉam niripaṟi ukappa - mīṉi aruntum paciya kāliyuṭiya kokkiṉatu iṉam variciyākap paṟattal uyarntiṭa, elli pip payak kaḻippi - pakaṟpoḻuti mella mellap pōkki, pal katir ñāyiṟu kuṭavayiṉ kal cērntaṉṟu - pala katirkaḷiyuṭiya ñāyiṟu mēṟṟiciyil maṟiyum maliyi aṭintatu;
6-9. ivaḷē perunāṇ aṇinta ciṟumeṉ cāyal - mikka nāṇik koṇṭa ciṟiya melliya cāyaliṉiyuṭiya ivaḷ, māṇ nalam citiya- māṇpuṟṟa aḻaku keṭa, ēṅki - ēkkamuṟṟu, matar eḻil maḻikkaṇ kaluḻa - matartta aḻakiṉiyuṭiya kuḷirnta kaṇ kalaṅkiṭa, āṉātu aḻal toṭaṅkiṉaḷē - amiyātu aḻutalit toṭaṅkiyuḷḷāḷ;
9-12. peruma - taliva!, ataṉāl - ataṉālum, kaḻiccuṟā eṟinta puṇ tāḷ attiri - uppaṅkaḻiyiluḷḷa cuṟāmīṉ eṟitalāluṟṟa puṇṇuṟṟa tāḷiṉiyuṭiya kōvēṟu kaḻuti, neṭunīr irukaḻi pari melintu - nīṇṭa nīriṉiyuṭiya kariya kaḻiyil cellutal melintiṭalālum, val vil iḷiyaroṭu ellic cellātu acii - valiya villiṉiyuṭiya niṉ ēvalaroṭu ivviraviṟ cellātu iḷippāṟa;
14-16. peṇṇi ōṅkiya veṇmaṇal paṭappi - paṉi maraṅkaḷ ōṅkiya veḷḷiya maṇal paranta tōṭṭaṅkaḷil, aṉṟil akavum āṅkaṇ - aṉṟil taṉ tuṇiyi aḻikkum avviṭattē, ciṟu kural neytal em peruṅ kaḻi nāṭṭu - ciṟiya pūṅkottiṉi yuṭiya neytal poruntiya ematu periya kaḻi cūḻnta nāṭṭiṉ kaṇ;
13. cērntaṉi celiṉē citikuvatu uṇṭō - nī taṅkic celliṉ keṭuvatoṉṟu uḷatō?
(muṭipu) peruma! pal katir ñāyiṟu kal cērntaṉṟu; ivaḷ aḻal toṭaṅkiṉaḷ; ataṉālum, attiri parimelin tiṭalālum iḷiyaroṭu ellic cellātu acii, em peruṅkaḻi nāṭṭuc cērntaṉi celiṉ, citikuvatu uṇṭō?
(vi-ri) murukak kaṭavuḷ mikka cenniṟamuṭiya reṉpatu cēy, cevvēḷ eṉṉum peyarkaḷālum, "palarpukaḻ ñāyiṟu kaṭaṟ kaṇṭāṅku"1 "pavaḻat taṉṉa mēṉi"2 eṉpavaṟṟāṉum aṟiyappaṭum. cevvāṉattiṟku neṭuvēḷiṉ cemmēṉiyum, avvāṉatti yoṭṭi variciyākap paṟantu cellum kokkiṉattiṟku avaratu mārpilaṇinta muttāramum uvamamāyiṉa; āṟṟuppaṭiyāl muruka vēḷi yēttiya āciriyar nakkīraṉāratu aṉpu kaṉinta tiruvuḷḷattil cevvāṉiyum ataṉi yoṭṭip paṟantu cellum kokkiṉ niriyiyum kaṇṭuḻic cevvēḷiṉ tirumēṉiyum avaratu mārpiṉ muttāramum tōṉṟuvatu iyalpēyām. kokkiṉ niri eṉpatu pāṭamāyiṉ kokkiṉ kūṭṭam eṉka. ukappu - uyarvu, paṟi yukappa - uyarntu paṟakka veṉak koḷka. cāyal ivaḷ eṉak kūṭṭuka. melintu eṉpataṉi meliya veṉat tirikka. aṉṟil akavum āṅkaṇ eṉṟataṉāl talivaṉ varāviṭil talivikkuṇṭām ētam kuṟikkappaṭṭatu. "elli........ aṉṟil, tuṇiyoṉṟu piriyiṉun tuñcā kāṇ"1 eṉpataṉ uri kāṇka.
itu neytaliṟ kaḷavu.  "tiṇimayak kuṟutaluṅ kaṭinili yilavē"2 eṉpataṉāl amintatu.
(mē-ḷ) `nāṟṟamum tōṟṟamum'3 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, tōḻi talivaṉi vēḷāṇ peruneṟi vēṇṭikkōṭaṟku ic ceyyuḷi eṭuttuk kāṭṭiṉar, iḷam.
`vikuṟu viṭiyal'4 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu ic ceyyuḷ, pakaṟ kuṟikkaṇ iṭattuyttut talivaṉi etirppaṭṭu urittatu eṉṟum, īṇṭu neytaṟku eṟpāṭu vantateṉṟum, `poḻutum  āṟum kāppum'5 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, `valvi liḷiyaroṭu........... peruṅkaḻi nāṭṭē' eṉpatu, `iraviṉum pakaliṉum nī vā' eṉat tōḻi kūṟuvataṟku utāraṇamākum eṉṟum, iṅṅaṉam kaḷavu aṟivuṟumeṉṟu añcātu varuka eṉṟaliṉ vaḻuvēṉum talivaṉ varuttam paṟṟik kūṟaliṉ amittār eṉṟum, `tērum yāṉiyum kutiriyum, piṟavum ūrntaṉar iyaṅkalum, uriyaṉ eṉpa'6 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, `piṟa' āvaṉa kōvēṟu kaḻutiyum civikiyum mutaliyaṉavām eṉṟum, `kaḻiccuṟā veṟinta puṭṭā ḷattiri.......... parimelin tacii' eṉa varutal ataṟku utāraṇamāku meṉṟum, iṉṉum iccūttirattu (iyaṅkalum eṉṟa) ummiyāl iḷiyarōṭu vantu taṉittuk kūṭutaluṅ koḷka; itaṟku `valvi liḷiyaroṭu...... citikuva tuṇṭō' eṉpatu utāraṇamāku meṉṟum kūṟiṉar, nac.
kaḷiṟṟiyāṉi niri muṟṟum.
akanāṉūṟu - kaḷiṟṟiyāṉi niri

			akattiyar tēvārattiraṭṭu 
1930 	-	pipravari 11,12,13,14 ākiya nāṭkaḷil ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻaka aṟakkaṭṭaḷi cārpāka tolkāppiya ārāycci coṟpoḻivu. (inta coṟpoḻivu ituvari veḷivarātavi kiyeḻuttup paṭiyākak  kiṭittatu. mutaṉ mutalāka  veḷivarukiṟatu.   nāvalar nāṭṭār tamiḻurikaḷ tokuti eṇ - 17 il 15-vatu kaṭṭuriyil pārkka .)
